7/13/2026, 6:42:57 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 3 — Redis Client Lifecycle in Jersey/JAX-RS**. Fokusnya sekarang bukan fitur Redis, tetapi cara membuat integrasi yang aman: bootstrap, DI, connection ownership, timeout, health check, dan shutdown.

# Seri 3 - Redis Client Lifecycle in Jersey/JAX-RS

Status: **Seri 3 dari 22 seri lanjutan**.  
Setelah seri ini selesai, tersisa **19 seri**.

---

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas cara mengelola **Redis client lifecycle** dalam aplikasi **Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice**:

```text
Application start
  ↓
Load Redis config
  ↓
Create Redis client
  ↓
Bind client/service into HK2/CDI
  ↓
Use via application service
  ↓
Expose health/metrics
  ↓
Graceful shutdown
```

Target desainnya:

```text
- Redis client dibuat sekali, bukan per request
- Dependency injection jelas
- Timeout eksplisit
- Connection/pool tidak bocor
- Request path tidak menggantung saat Redis bermasalah
- Blocking listener/queue tidak mencampuri request command
- Shutdown menutup Redis client dengan benar
```

Jersey mendukung custom injection/lifecycle extension melalui `AbstractBinder` yang didaftarkan ke `ResourceConfig`, sehingga Redis service dapat dibuat sebagai dependency aplikasi, bukan dibuat manual di setiap resource class. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/ioc.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Lifecycle Mental Model

Jangan berpikir seperti ini:

```text
Setiap HTTP request
  ↓
new Redis client
  ↓
execute command
  ↓
close client
```

Itu buruk karena:

```text
- connection overhead tinggi
- TCP/TLS handshake bisa mahal
- pool tidak efektif
- latency naik
- Redis server kebanjiran connection
- sulit dimonitor
```

Model yang benar:

```text
Application startup
  ↓
Create Redis client / pool / connection factory
  ↓
Register as singleton
  ↓
Inject Redis adapter/service
  ↓
Use per request
  ↓
Application shutdown closes client
```

---

## 3. Layering yang Direkomendasikan

Struktur umum:

```text
JAX-RS Resource
  ↓
Application Service
  ↓
Redis Use Case Service
  ├── CacheStore
  ├── RateLimiter
  ├── IdempotencyStore
  ├── DistributedLockService
  ├── RedisStreamConsumer
  └── RedisHealthCheck
  ↓
Redis Client Adapter
  ↓
Jedis / Lettuce / Redisson
```

Contoh package:

```text
com.company.orders
├── api
│   └── OrderResource.java
├── application
│   └── OrderCommandService.java
├── redis
│   ├── RedisConfig.java
│   ├── RedisLifecycle.java
│   ├── RedisBinder.java
│   ├── RedisCacheStore.java
│   ├── RedisIdempotencyStore.java
│   ├── RedisRateLimiter.java
│   └── RedisHealthResource.java
└── persistence
    ├── OrderMapper.java
    └── OrderRepository.java
```

Rule utama:

```text
JAX-RS Resource tidak boleh tahu Jedis/Lettuce/Redisson.
Resource hanya tahu application service.
Application service hanya tahu port/interface.
Redis client detail hanya hidup di adapter layer.
```

---

## 4. Lifecycle State Machine

```text
[NOT_CREATED]
   ↓ load config
[CONFIG_LOADED]
   ↓ create client
[CLIENT_CREATED]
   ↓ validate connectivity
[READY]
   ↓ serve requests
[DEGRADED]
   ↕ reconnect / timeout / partial failure
[STOPPING]
   ↓ close listener, worker, pool, client
[STOPPED]
```

### State Meaning

| State | Makna | Action |
|---|---|---|
| `NOT_CREATED` | App belum membuat Redis client | Load config |
| `CONFIG_LOADED` | Redis host/port/auth/TLS/timeout sudah tersedia | Create client |
| `CLIENT_CREATED` | Client object sudah dibuat | Optional ping/readiness check |
| `READY` | Redis dependency bisa dipakai | Serve requests |
| `DEGRADED` | Redis timeout/down/failover | Apply failure policy |
| `STOPPING` | App sedang shutdown | Stop worker/listener dulu |
| `STOPPED` | Redis resources sudah ditutup | No command allowed |

---

## 5. Configuration Model

Buat config eksplisit, jangan menyebar env var di banyak class.

```java
public record RedisConfig(
    String host,
    int port,
    String username,
    String password,
    boolean tlsEnabled,
    int database,
    Duration connectTimeout,
    Duration commandTimeout,
    Duration socketTimeout,
    int poolMaxTotal,
    int poolMaxIdle,
    int poolMinIdle,
    Duration poolMaxWait,
    String keyPrefix
) {}
```

### Config Source Priority

```text
1. Environment variable / Kubernetes Secret / ConfigMap
2. System property
3. Application YAML/properties
4. Safe local default only for dev/test
```

Contoh env var:

```text
REDIS_HOST=redis-master.redis.svc.cluster.local
REDIS_PORT=6379
REDIS_USERNAME=app_user
REDIS_PASSWORD=...
REDIS_TLS_ENABLED=true
REDIS_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_MS=300
REDIS_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS=500
REDIS_KEY_PREFIX=order-svc:prod
```

### Jangan hardcode ini

```java
private static final String REDIS_PASSWORD = "secret";
```

---

## 6. Dependency Injection dengan Jersey/HK2

### 6.1 ResourceConfig

```java
public final class OrderApplication extends ResourceConfig {

    public OrderApplication() {
        packages("com.company.orders.api");

        RedisConfig redisConfig = RedisConfigLoader.fromEnvironment();

        register(new RedisBinder(redisConfig));
        register(new HealthResource());
    }
}
```

Jersey custom injection extension perlu didaftarkan ke `ResourceConfig`; ini cocok untuk mengikat Redis service sebagai singleton dependency aplikasi. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/ioc.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

### 6.2 Binder Pattern

```java
import jakarta.inject.Singleton;
import org.glassfish.hk2.utilities.binding.AbstractBinder;

public final class RedisBinder extends AbstractBinder {

    private final RedisConfig config;

    public RedisBinder(RedisConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        RedisLifecycle lifecycle = RedisLifecycle.create(config);

        bind(lifecycle).to(RedisLifecycle.class).in(Singleton.class);
        bind(lifecycle.cacheStore()).to(CacheStore.class).in(Singleton.class);
        bind(lifecycle.idempotencyStore()).to(IdempotencyStore.class).in(Singleton.class);
        bind(lifecycle.rateLimiter()).to(RateLimiter.class).in(Singleton.class);
        bind(lifecycle.healthCheck()).to(RedisHealthCheck.class).in(Singleton.class);
    }
}
```

Catatan penting:

```text
- Binding singleton harus benar-benar thread-safe.
- Jangan bind raw mutable connection sembarangan.
- Bind service abstraction, bukan library client langsung, kecuali untuk internal infra package.
```

---

## 7. Startup Strategy

Ada dua pilihan startup:

| Strategy | Perilaku | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|
| **Fail-fast** | App gagal start jika Redis tidak bisa connect | Redis kritikal: idempotency, login protection, lock critical |
| **Lazy/degraded start** | App tetap start, Redis health `degraded` | Redis non-critical cache |

### Rekomendasi

```text
Cache-only Redis:
  boleh lazy/degraded

Auth/session/rate-limit critical:
  prefer fail-fast atau fail-closed per endpoint

Idempotency untuk command penting:
  fail-fast atau fallback DB idempotency table

Queue worker:
  app boleh start, worker reconnect loop
```

---

## 8. Jedis Lifecycle Pattern

Jedis cocok untuk synchronous request path. Redis docs menyebut Jedis sebagai synchronous Java client, dan production guidance Redis menyarankan connection pooling agar tidak perlu membuka/menutup connection sendiri terus-menerus. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/clients/jedis/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 8.1 Jedis Adapter Shape

```java
public final class JedisRedisLifecycle implements AutoCloseable {

    private final JedisPooled jedis;
    private final RedisCacheStore cacheStore;
    private final RedisIdempotencyStore idempotencyStore;
    private final RedisRateLimiter rateLimiter;
    private final RedisHealthCheck healthCheck;

    public JedisRedisLifecycle(RedisConfig config) {
        this.jedis = createJedis(config);
        this.cacheStore = new RedisCacheStore(jedis, config);
        this.idempotencyStore = new RedisIdempotencyStore(jedis, config);
        this.rateLimiter = new RedisRateLimiter(jedis, config);
        this.healthCheck = new RedisHealthCheck(jedis);
    }

    private static JedisPooled createJedis(RedisConfig config) {
        // Pseudocode: actual constructor depends on selected Jedis version.
        // Keep config centralized and version-specific code isolated here.
        return new JedisPooled(config.host(), config.port());
    }

    public CacheStore cacheStore() {
        return cacheStore;
    }

    public IdempotencyStore idempotencyStore() {
        return idempotencyStore;
    }

    public RateLimiter rateLimiter() {
        return rateLimiter;
    }

    public RedisHealthCheck healthCheck() {
        return healthCheck;
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        jedis.close();
    }
}
```

### 8.2 Jedis Lifecycle Rules

```text
[ ] Create pooled client once per app
[ ] Do not create Jedis connection per request
[ ] Set connection/socket/command timeout explicitly
[ ] Keep pool size aligned with HTTP thread pool
[ ] Use dedicated connection for Pub/Sub or blocking queue patterns
[ ] Close pool on application shutdown
```

---

## 9. Lettuce Lifecycle Pattern

Lettuce cocok sebagai balanced default karena bisa sync, async, dan reactive. Redis docs menyebut Lettuce sebagai advanced Java client dengan synchronous, asynchronous, dan reactive connections. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/clients/lettuce/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Redis docs untuk Lettuce menyebut pendekatan umum: buat satu `RedisClient`, reuse untuk membuat connection, dan connection dapat dimultiplex sehingga beberapa command dapat berjalan concurrent lewat satu atau sedikit connection. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/clients/lettuce/connect/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 9.1 Lettuce Adapter Shape

```java
import io.lettuce.core.RedisClient;
import io.lettuce.core.RedisURI;
import io.lettuce.core.api.StatefulRedisConnection;
import io.lettuce.core.api.sync.RedisCommands;

public final class LettuceRedisLifecycle implements AutoCloseable {

    private final RedisClient client;
    private final StatefulRedisConnection<String, String> connection;
    private final RedisCommands<String, String> commands;

    private final RedisCacheStore cacheStore;
    private final RedisIdempotencyStore idempotencyStore;
    private final RedisRateLimiter rateLimiter;
    private final RedisHealthCheck healthCheck;

    public LettuceRedisLifecycle(RedisConfig config) {
        RedisURI uri = RedisURI.builder()
            .withHost(config.host())
            .withPort(config.port())
            .withDatabase(config.database())
            .withTimeout(config.commandTimeout())
            .build();

        this.client = RedisClient.create(uri);
        this.connection = client.connect();
        this.commands = connection.sync();

        this.cacheStore = new RedisCacheStore(commands, config);
        this.idempotencyStore = new RedisIdempotencyStore(commands, config);
        this.rateLimiter = new RedisRateLimiter(commands, config);
        this.healthCheck = new RedisHealthCheck(commands);
    }

    public CacheStore cacheStore() {
        return cacheStore;
    }

    public IdempotencyStore idempotencyStore() {
        return idempotencyStore;
    }

    public RateLimiter rateLimiter() {
        return rateLimiter;
    }

    public RedisHealthCheck healthCheck() {
        return healthCheck;
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        connection.close();
        client.shutdown();
    }
}
```

### 9.2 Thread-safety Rule

Lettuce reference documentation describes Lettuce as scalable and thread-safe; multiple threads may share one connection if they avoid blocking and transactional operations such as `BLPOP` and `MULTI`/`EXEC`. ([Redis](https://redis.github.io/lettuce/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Praktisnya:

```text
Shared Lettuce connection:
  OK untuk GET/SET/HGET/HSET/INCR/EVAL ringan

Dedicated connection:
  Pub/Sub
  BLPOP/BRPOP
  XREAD BLOCK
  MULTI/EXEC transaction-heavy flow
  long-running script
```

---

## 10. Redisson Lifecycle Pattern

Redisson cocok jika service banyak memakai distributed object seperti lock, semaphore, map, queue, atau scheduler abstraction. Redisson mendeskripsikan dirinya sebagai Java client untuk Redis/Valkey yang menambahkan distributed Java collections, objects, dan service implementations. ([GitHub](https://github.com/redisson/redisson?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 10.1 Redisson Adapter Shape

```java
import org.redisson.Redisson;
import org.redisson.api.RedissonClient;
import org.redisson.config.Config;

public final class RedissonRedisLifecycle implements AutoCloseable {

    private final RedissonClient redisson;

    private final RedisDistributedLockService lockService;
    private final RedisSemaphoreService semaphoreService;
    private final RedisQueueService queueService;

    public RedissonRedisLifecycle(RedisConfig redisConfig) {
        Config config = new Config();

        config.useSingleServer()
            .setAddress(toRedisAddress(redisConfig))
            .setDatabase(redisConfig.database())
            .setConnectTimeout((int) redisConfig.connectTimeout().toMillis())
            .setTimeout((int) redisConfig.commandTimeout().toMillis());

        this.redisson = Redisson.create(config);

        this.lockService = new RedisDistributedLockService(redisson, redisConfig);
        this.semaphoreService = new RedisSemaphoreService(redisson, redisConfig);
        this.queueService = new RedisQueueService(redisson, redisConfig);
    }

    private static String toRedisAddress(RedisConfig config) {
        String scheme = config.tlsEnabled() ? "rediss://" : "redis://";
        return scheme + config.host() + ":" + config.port();
    }

    public DistributedLockService lockService() {
        return lockService;
    }

    public DistributedSemaphoreService semaphoreService() {
        return semaphoreService;
    }

    public RedisQueueService queueService() {
        return queueService;
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        redisson.shutdown();
    }
}
```

Redisson configuration docs show programmatic configuration using `Config`, including server mode and Redis/Redis-over-SSL address schemes such as `redis://` and `rediss://`. ([Redisson](https://redisson.pro/docs/configuration/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Redisson FAQ also states a Redisson instance should be treated as a shared singleton that starts/stops with the application, and shutdown disconnects active connection pools and stops event loops. ([Redisson](https://redisson.pro/docs/faq/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 11. Application Shutdown

Shutdown order matters.

```text
1. Stop accepting new HTTP traffic
2. Mark readiness DOWN
3. Stop queue consumers / stream consumers
4. Stop Pub/Sub / keyspace notification listeners
5. Wait briefly for in-flight requests
6. Close Redis connections/pools/client
7. Stop app process
```

### Why this order?

Kalau Redis client ditutup sebelum worker/listener berhenti:

```text
- consumer bisa error loop
- background thread bisa mencoba reconnect saat app shutdown
- in-flight request bisa gagal tidak terkontrol
```

### Interface sederhana

```java
public interface ManagedLifecycle extends AutoCloseable {
    void start();
    void stop();
    boolean isReady();

    @Override
    void close();
}
```

---

## 12. Health Check Design

Pisahkan **liveness** dan **readiness**.

| Check | Tujuan | Redis ikut? |
|---|---|---|
| Liveness | Apakah proses JVM masih hidup? | Biasanya tidak |
| Readiness | Apakah app siap menerima traffic? | Ya, jika Redis dependency critical |
| Dependency health | Status Redis detail | Ya |
| Startup probe | Apakah bootstrap selesai? | Tergantung mode |

### Health State

```java
public enum DependencyStatus {
    UP,
    DEGRADED,
    DOWN
}
```

### Health Result

```java
public record RedisHealth(
    DependencyStatus status,
    Duration latency,
    String message,
    Instant checkedAt
) {}
```

### Health Check Implementation

```java
public final class RedisHealthCheck {

    private final RedisPing redisPing;
    private final Duration timeoutBudget;

    public RedisHealthCheck(RedisPing redisPing, Duration timeoutBudget) {
        this.redisPing = redisPing;
        this.timeoutBudget = timeoutBudget;
    }

    public RedisHealth check() {
        Instant start = Instant.now();

        try {
            String result = redisPing.ping(timeoutBudget);
            Duration latency = Duration.between(start, Instant.now());

            if ("PONG".equalsIgnoreCase(result)) {
                return new RedisHealth(
                    DependencyStatus.UP,
                    latency,
                    "Redis responded to PING",
                    Instant.now()
                );
            }

            return new RedisHealth(
                DependencyStatus.DEGRADED,
                latency,
                "Unexpected Redis PING result",
                Instant.now()
            );

        } catch (Exception ex) {
            return new RedisHealth(
                DependencyStatus.DOWN,
                Duration.between(start, Instant.now()),
                ex.getClass().getSimpleName(),
                Instant.now()
            );
        }
    }
}
```

### Readiness Policy

```text
If Redis is cache-only:
  readiness can stay UP, but dependency health = DEGRADED

If Redis protects auth/rate-limit/idempotency:
  readiness may become DOWN or selected endpoints fail-closed

If Redis powers queue workers only:
  HTTP readiness may stay UP, worker health DOWN
```

---

## 13. Timeout Budget

Redis timeout harus lebih kecil dari HTTP timeout.

Contoh:

```text
HTTP request timeout:       2_000 ms
Service internal budget:    1_500 ms
PostgreSQL query timeout:   800 ms
Redis command timeout:      100-300 ms
Redis connect timeout:      300-500 ms
```

### Per-use-case Timeout

| Use case | Timeout | Failure behavior |
|---|---:|---|
| Cache `GET` | 50-200 ms | fallback DB |
| Cache `SET` | 50-200 ms | log warning, continue |
| Rate limiter | 50-150 ms | fail-open/fail-closed by endpoint |
| Idempotency | 100-300 ms | fail-closed or DB fallback |
| Lock acquire | explicit wait budget | return busy/retryable |
| Health check | 100-500 ms | degraded/down |
| Stream blocking read | long timeout allowed | worker only |
| Pub/Sub listener | persistent | dedicated connection |

Rule:

```text
Redis retry + timeout total tidak boleh melebihi request budget.
```

---

## 14. Retry Policy

Jangan retry Redis secara agresif di request path.

### Request-path retry

```text
Allowed:
  - 0 or 1 retry
  - only for transient connection failure
  - jittered tiny backoff
  - only if operation is safe/idempotent

Not allowed:
  - retry storm
  - retry EVAL that may have executed but timed out unless idempotent
  - retry lock release blindly
```

### Worker retry

Worker boleh lebih sabar, tetapi tetap bounded:

```text
- reconnect loop with exponential backoff
- max backoff cap
- log rate limiting
- metric on reconnect
- no busy loop
```

---

## 15. Connection Ownership

### Jangan

```java
public class OrderResource {
    private final RedisClient redisClient;

    @GET
    public Response get() {
        redisClient.connect(); // buruk
        ...
    }
}
```

### Lebih baik

```java
public final class OrderQueryService {
    private final CacheStore cacheStore;
    private final OrderRepository orderRepository;

    public OrderDto getOrder(String tenant, String orderId) {
        return cacheStore.get(orderCacheKey(tenant, orderId), OrderDto.class)
            .orElseGet(() -> loadFromDbAndCache(tenant, orderId));
    }
}
```

### Ownership Rule

| Object | Owner |
|---|---|
| Redis config | Application bootstrap |
| Redis client/pool | Redis lifecycle component |
| Redis command abstraction | Redis adapter |
| Cache/idempotency/rate limiter | Redis use-case service |
| Business decision | Application/domain service |
| HTTP mapping | JAX-RS resource/filter |

---

## 16. Dedicated Connections untuk Listener dan Blocking Operation

Jangan campur command biasa dengan blocking operation.

```text
Redis normal command connection:
  GET, SET, HGET, HSET, INCR, EVAL

Dedicated Pub/Sub connection:
  SUBSCRIBE, PSUBSCRIBE

Dedicated stream consumer:
  XREAD BLOCK, XREADGROUP BLOCK

Dedicated queue connection:
  BLPOP, BRPOP

Dedicated transaction path:
  WATCH, MULTI, EXEC if high frequency
```

Lettuce reference explicitly warns that shared connections are suitable when avoiding blocking and transactional operations such as `BLPOP` and `MULTI`/`EXEC`; that maps directly to this separation rule. ([Redis](https://redis.github.io/lettuce/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 17. JAX-RS Filters: Rate Limiter Example Boundary

Redis rate limiter biasanya dipasang di `ContainerRequestFilter`.

```java
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION)
public final class RateLimitFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private final RateLimiter rateLimiter;

    @Inject
    public RateLimitFilter(RateLimiter rateLimiter) {
        this.rateLimiter = rateLimiter;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) {
        RateLimitDecision decision = rateLimiter.check(
            RateLimitSubject.from(requestContext),
            RateLimitPolicy.standardApi()
        );

        if (!decision.allowed()) {
            throw new TooManyRequestsException(decision.retryAfter());
        }
    }
}
```

Important boundary:

```text
Filter boleh memakai RateLimiter abstraction.
Filter tidak boleh tahu Redis key, Lua script, Jedis/Lettuce/Redisson.
```

---

## 18. Exception Mapping

Jangan biarkan exception Redis mentah bocor ke resource response.

### Internal exception

```java
public final class RedisDependencyException extends RuntimeException {
    private final RedisUseCase useCase;
    private final boolean retryable;

    public RedisDependencyException(
        RedisUseCase useCase,
        boolean retryable,
        Throwable cause
    ) {
        super("Redis dependency failure: " + useCase, cause);
        this.useCase = useCase;
        this.retryable = retryable;
    }
}
```

### Failure Mapping

| Redis use case | Redis failure | HTTP behavior |
|---|---|---|
| Cache get | timeout | fallback DB, still 200 if DB OK |
| Cache set | timeout | still 200, metric warning |
| Rate limit public read API | timeout | fail-open or 503 depending policy |
| Rate limit login | timeout | fail-closed / 503 |
| Idempotency POST | timeout | 503 retryable |
| Lock acquire | timeout | 409/423/503 depending domain |
| Job queue | timeout | worker retry, no HTTP response |
| Permission cache | timeout | fallback policy DB/service |

### JAX-RS ExceptionMapper

```java
@Provider
public final class RedisDependencyExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<RedisDependencyException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(RedisDependencyException ex) {
        ErrorResponse body = new ErrorResponse(
            "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE",
            "Temporary dependency failure"
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE)
            .entity(body)
            .build();
    }
}
```

---

## 19. Key Prefix Lifecycle

Redis key prefix harus disediakan oleh config dan tidak boleh dibuat ad-hoc.

```java
public final class RedisKeyFactory {

    private final String prefix;

    public RedisKeyFactory(RedisConfig config) {
        this.prefix = config.keyPrefix();
    }

    public String cacheOrder(String tenantId, String orderId) {
        return prefix + ":tenant:" + tenantId + ":order-cache:" + orderId + ":v1";
    }

    public String idempotency(String tenantId, String endpoint, String key) {
        return prefix + ":tenant:" + tenantId + ":idem:" + endpoint + ":" + key;
    }

    public String rateLimit(String tenantId, String subject, String window) {
        return prefix + ":tenant:" + tenantId + ":rate:" + subject + ":" + window;
    }
}
```

Contoh prefix:

```text
order-svc:prod
case-svc:staging
auth-svc:prod
```

Hindari:

```text
user:123
lock:abc
cache:order
```

---

## 20. Metrics yang Harus Dibuat di Adapter Layer

Minimal metrics:

```text
redis.command.latency
redis.command.error.count
redis.command.timeout.count
redis.connection.state
redis.pool.active
redis.pool.idle
redis.pool.wait.time
redis.cache.hit
redis.cache.miss
redis.cache.set.error
redis.rate_limit.allowed
redis.rate_limit.denied
redis.idempotency.hit
redis.idempotency.conflict
redis.lock.acquire.success
redis.lock.acquire.timeout
redis.listener.reconnect.count
redis.stream.pending.count
```

### Label/Tag yang berguna

```text
service
environment
redis_use_case
command_family
result
exception_class
```

Jangan label dengan raw key:

```text
redis_key=order-svc:prod:tenant:abc:...
```

Itu bisa menyebabkan cardinality tinggi dan risiko PII.

---

## 21. Logging

### Log yang baik

```json
{
  "event": "redis_command_failed",
  "service": "order-service",
  "useCase": "IDEMPOTENCY",
  "commandFamily": "EVAL",
  "durationMs": 241,
  "exception": "RedisCommandTimeoutException",
  "retryable": true,
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

### Jangan log

```text
- raw Redis password
- full Redis URL with credentials
- raw key yang mengandung user/customer id sensitif
- full cached payload
- token/session/OTP value
```

---

## 22. Kubernetes Lifecycle

### Readiness

```text
/readiness
  - app initialized
  - DB ready if critical
  - Redis ready if critical
  - worker optional depending service type
```

### Liveness

```text
/liveness
  - JVM alive
  - event loop not deadlocked
  - no dependency PING required by default
```

### Shutdown

```text
preStop hook
  ↓
mark readiness down
  ↓
sleep small drain window
  ↓
SIGTERM
  ↓
application graceful shutdown
  ↓
close Redis client
```

### Kubernetes Design Rule

```text
Jangan pakai liveness probe untuk Redis PING.
Kalau Redis outage sementara membuat liveness fail,
Kubernetes bisa me-restart semua pod dan memperparah outage.
```

---

## 23. Docker / Local Development

Untuk local dev:

```yaml
services:
  redis:
    image: redis:8
    ports:
      - "6379:6379"
    command: ["redis-server", "--appendonly", "yes"]
```

Tapi untuk production:

```text
- jangan expose Redis publik
- gunakan auth/ACL
- gunakan TLS bila network policy menuntut
- gunakan managed Redis atau HA topology
- gunakan resource limit dan persistence policy jelas
```

---

## 24. Testing Lifecycle

### Unit Test

Mock interface:

```java
CacheStore cacheStore = mock(CacheStore.class);
```

Test application logic tanpa Redis.

### Integration Test

Gunakan Redis nyata via Testcontainers/local container.

```text
Test:
[ ] app starts with Redis available
[ ] app fails/degrades as expected when Redis unavailable
[ ] timeout handled
[ ] cache fallback DB works
[ ] Redis client closes cleanly
[ ] Pub/Sub listener reconnect works
[ ] stream consumer stops on shutdown
```

### Failure Test

```text
- Redis stopped before app start
- Redis stopped during request
- Redis slow / network latency
- Redis auth failure
- Redis failover
- Redis max connection pressure
- Redis memory full / eviction
```

---

## 25. Common Lifecycle Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Redis client dibuat per request | Latency dan connection explosion | Singleton lifecycle |
| Redis client di resource class | Coupling HTTP dengan infra | Adapter/service abstraction |
| Timeout default | Request menggantung | Explicit timeout budget |
| Pub/Sub pakai connection command biasa | Command bisa terganggu | Dedicated listener connection |
| Blocking read di request thread | Request thread habis | Worker thread khusus |
| Liveness probe tergantung Redis | Restart storm | Redis di readiness/dependency health |
| Shutdown tidak close client | Thread/connection leak | `AutoCloseable` lifecycle |
| Retry tanpa batas | Outage amplification | bounded retry + backoff |
| Pool size ditebak | Latency spike | align dengan concurrency + metrics |
| Raw key di logs/metrics | PII/cardinality risk | mask/hash/tag semantic only |
| Domain service tahu Lettuce/Jedis | Vendor lock-in internal | port/adapter |

---

## 26. Minimal Production Template

```text
Redis Integration Checklist

Configuration:
[ ] host/port/db/auth/tls from env/secret/config
[ ] command timeout explicit
[ ] connect timeout explicit
[ ] key prefix explicit
[ ] pool/connection settings explicit

Lifecycle:
[ ] client created once at startup
[ ] registered via HK2/CDI binder
[ ] no per-request client creation
[ ] close on shutdown
[ ] listener/worker stopped before client close

Usage:
[ ] resource uses service abstraction
[ ] Redis library hidden in adapter
[ ] blocking operations use dedicated connection
[ ] Pub/Sub uses dedicated connection
[ ] key factory centralized

Resilience:
[ ] fallback/fail-open/fail-closed policy per use case
[ ] bounded retry
[ ] timeout lower than HTTP request budget
[ ] health check separates liveness/readiness
[ ] Redis-down behavior tested

Observability:
[ ] latency/error/timeout metrics
[ ] cache hit/miss metrics
[ ] pool/connection metrics
[ ] structured logs
[ ] no raw secret/key/payload logging
```

---

## 27. Recommended Reference Architecture

```text
OrderApplication extends ResourceConfig
  └── register RedisBinder
        └── RedisLifecycle singleton
              ├── Redis client / pool / connection
              ├── RedisKeyFactory
              ├── RedisCacheStore
              ├── RedisIdempotencyStore
              ├── RedisRateLimiter
              ├── RedisHealthCheck
              └── RedisShutdownHook

OrderResource
  └── OrderCommandService
        ├── IdempotencyStore
        ├── OrderRepository/MyBatis
        ├── CacheStore
        ├── WorkflowStarter/Camunda
        └── EventPublisher/Kafka
```

---

## 28. Senior Engineer Review Questions

Gunakan ini saat review desain/PR:

```text
[ ] Siapa pemilik Redis client lifecycle?
[ ] Apakah client dibuat singleton?
[ ] Apakah timeout explicit dan masuk akal?
[ ] Apakah HTTP timeout budget menghitung Redis timeout?
[ ] Apakah Redis dependency critical atau optional?
[ ] Apakah readiness policy sesuai criticality?
[ ] Apakah liveness bebas dari dependency Redis?
[ ] Apakah Pub/Sub/Streams/blocking command memakai dedicated connection?
[ ] Apakah failure behavior berbeda untuk cache, limiter, idempotency, lock?
[ ] Apakah resource class bebas dari Jedis/Lettuce/Redisson?
[ ] Apakah shutdown order benar?
[ ] Apakah metrics dan logs cukup untuk incident debugging?
[ ] Apakah Redis-down test ada?
```

---

## 29. Ringkasan Praktis

```text
Redis client lifecycle yang benar:
  dibuat saat app start
  dikonfigurasi terpusat
  di-bind sebagai singleton
  dipakai melalui adapter/use-case service
  diberi timeout eksplisit
  dimonitor via metrics
  dipisahkan antara command biasa dan blocking/listener
  ditutup saat shutdown

Redis client lifecycle yang buruk:
  dibuat per request
  dipakai langsung dari Resource
  timeout default
  listener mencampur connection command biasa
  shutdown tidak jelas
  health check salah tempat
```

Untuk stack Java 17 + Jersey + MyBatis + PostgreSQL + Camunda + Kubernetes, desain lifecycle ini jauh lebih penting daripada memilih library Redis tertentu.

## Response:
7/13/2026, 6:44:58 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 4 — Key Naming, Namespace Governance & TTL Strategy**. Fokusnya: membuat Redis keyspace yang rapi, aman untuk multi-tenant/env, mudah di-debug, dan tidak menjadi sumber stale data atau memory leak.

# Seri 4 - Key Naming, Namespace Governance & TTL Strategy

Status: **Seri 4 dari 22 seri lanjutan**.  
Setelah seri ini selesai, tersisa **18 seri**.

---

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Redis keyspace itu **flat namespace**. Secara teknis Redis tidak punya folder/hierarchy seperti filesystem; struktur seperti `order-svc:prod:tenant-42:cache:order:123` hanyalah convention di level aplikasi. Karena itu, key naming harus diperlakukan sebagai **kontrak arsitektur**, bukan detail kecil implementasi.

Tujuan seri ini:

```text
- membuat Redis key mudah dibaca dan di-debug
- mencegah collision antar service/env/tenant
- mengontrol TTL dan stale data
- menghindari memory leak karena key tanpa expiry
- mendukung cache invalidation
- mendukung Redis Cluster hash-slot planning
- menghindari PII/secret bocor lewat key
- memudahkan incident response
```

GitLab Redis development guideline juga menekankan bahwa Redis adalah flat namespace, sehingga nama key perlu dirancang untuk menghindari collision; convention umum adalah elemen dipisahkan dengan colon. ([GitLab Docs](https://docs.gitlab.com/development/redis/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model: Redis Key adalah API Internal

Redis key bukan cuma string.

Redis key mewakili:

```text
ownership
environment
tenant boundary
domain boundary
data classification
versioning
lifecycle
TTL policy
operational searchability
cluster slot behavior
```

Contoh buruk:

```text
user:123
order:456
lock:job
cache:abc
session:xyz
```

Masalahnya:

```text
- service owner tidak jelas
- environment tidak jelas
- tenant tidak jelas
- versi schema tidak jelas
- purpose tidak jelas
- sulit dihapus selektif
- raw identifier bisa sensitif
```

Contoh lebih baik:

```text
order-svc:prod:tenant:42:cache:order-summary:v1:order:456
auth-svc:prod:tenant:42:session:v2:user-hash:8f92aa
case-svc:staging:tenant:42:lock:case-rebuild:v1:case:991
```

---

## 3. Recommended Key Shape

Format dasar:

```text
{service}:{environment}:{tenant-scope}:{purpose}:{domain}:{version}:{identifier}
```

Atau lebih eksplisit:

```text
{service}:{env}:tenant:{tenantId}:{purpose}:{domain}:{version}:{entity}:{id}
```

Contoh:

```text
order-svc:prod:tenant:t-001:cache:order-summary:v1:order:o-9001
auth-svc:prod:tenant:t-001:rate-login:v1:user:u-123:window:202607131030
case-svc:prod:tenant:t-001:idem:create-case:v1:key:sha256-abc
workflow-svc:prod:tenant:t-001:lock:external-task:v1:task:task-123
```

### Recommended Segments

| Segment | Contoh | Wajib? | Fungsi |
|---|---|---:|---|
| `service` | `order-svc` | Ya | Owner key |
| `env` | `prod`, `staging`, `dev` | Ya | Mencegah environment collision |
| `tenant` | `tenant:t-001` | Ya jika multi-tenant | Isolation |
| `purpose` | `cache`, `lock`, `rate`, `idem`, `stream` | Ya | Menjelaskan pola penggunaan |
| `domain` | `order-summary`, `permission`, `case-task` | Ya | Domain object/context |
| `version` | `v1`, `v2` | Sangat disarankan | Schema/key migration |
| `identifier` | `order:o-9001` | Ya | Entity/request/window target |

---

## 4. Diagram Keyspace Governance

```text
Redis Keyspace
│
├── service owner
│   ├── order-svc
│   ├── auth-svc
│   ├── case-svc
│   └── workflow-svc
│
├── environment
│   ├── prod
│   ├── staging
│   └── dev
│
├── tenant scope
│   ├── tenant:t-001
│   ├── tenant:t-002
│   └── global
│
├── purpose
│   ├── cache
│   ├── rate
│   ├── idem
│   ├── lock
│   ├── session
│   ├── queue
│   ├── stream
│   ├── zschedule
│   ├── pubsub
│   └── presence
│
├── lifecycle policy
│   ├── mandatory TTL
│   ├── optional TTL
│   ├── no TTL but bounded
│   └── forbidden no TTL
│
└── operations
    ├── SCAN pattern
    ├── metrics by key family
    ├── deletion runbook
    ├── migration/versioning
    └── incident debugging
```

---

## 5. Key Family Catalog

Setiap service sebaiknya punya **Key Family Catalog**. Ini semacam registry internal untuk Redis key.

| Key family | Pattern | Data structure | TTL | Source of truth | Owner |
|---|---|---|---:|---|---|
| Order summary cache | `order-svc:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:cache:order-summary:v1:order:{orderId}` | String/JSON | 5 min | PostgreSQL | order-svc |
| Idempotency create order | `order-svc:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:idem:create-order:v1:key:{keyHash}` | Hash/String | 48 h | PostgreSQL unique constraint | order-svc |
| API rate limit | `api-gw:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:rate:v1:api-key:{apiKeyHash}:route:{routeHash}:window:{minute}` | String counter | 1-2 min | Policy config | api-gw |
| User session | `auth-svc:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:session:v2:sid:{sessionHash}` | Hash | 30 min sliding | Auth DB | auth-svc |
| Permission snapshot | `authz-svc:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:cache:permission:v3:user:{userHash}:policy:{policyVersion}` | String/JSON | 1-5 min | Policy DB | authz-svc |
| Case claim lock | `case-svc:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:lock:case-claim:v1:case:{caseId}` | String token | 10-60 sec | PostgreSQL | case-svc |
| Worker semaphore | `worker-svc:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:semaphore:external-call:v1:provider:{provider}` | String/Set/ZSet | lease-based | Runtime policy | worker-svc |
| Delayed job schedule | `job-svc:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:zschedule:retry:v1` | Sorted set | bounded trim | Job metadata DB/stream | job-svc |
| Redis stream | `job-svc:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:stream:email-task:v1` | Stream | trim policy | Producer service | job-svc |
| Presence | `realtime-svc:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:presence:v1:user:{userHash}:device:{deviceHash}` | String | 30-120 sec | Runtime heartbeat | realtime-svc |

Key family catalog harus ada sebelum Redis dipakai luas. Tanpa catalog, Redis akan berubah menjadi “hidden database” yang sulit diaudit.

---

## 6. Purpose Naming Standard

Gunakan vocabulary tetap.

| Purpose | Untuk | Contoh key |
|---|---|---|
| `cache` | Derived/read cache | `order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:order:v1:order:o1` |
| `rate` | Rate limit counter/window | `api:prod:tenant:t1:rate:v1:user:u1:window:202607131030` |
| `idem` | Idempotency key | `order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:idem:create-order:v1:key:k1` |
| `lock` | Distributed lock/lease | `case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:lock:case-claim:v1:case:c1` |
| `session` | Session/token state | `auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:session:v1:sid:s1` |
| `otp` | OTP/nonce | `auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:otp:v1:user:u1:purpose:login` |
| `quota` | Quota/capacity counter | `api:prod:tenant:t1:quota:v1:client:c1` |
| `stream` | Redis Stream | `job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:invoice-task:v1` |
| `group` | Consumer group metadata if custom | `job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:group:invoice-worker:v1` |
| `zschedule` | Delayed schedule sorted set | `job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:zschedule:retry:v1` |
| `pubsub` | Pub/Sub channel name | `notify-svc:prod:tenant:t1:pubsub:user-event:v1` |
| `presence` | Heartbeat/presence | `realtime-svc:prod:tenant:t1:presence:v1:user:u1` |
| `flag` | Feature/config cache | `config-svc:prod:tenant:t1:flag:v1:feature:f1` |

Hindari vocabulary yang ambigu:

```text
tmp
misc
data
object
state
thing
redis
cache2
newcache
```

---

## 7. Versioning Strategy

Versioning perlu karena key format dan value schema bisa berubah.

### Key Version

```text
order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:order-summary:v1:order:o1
order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:order-summary:v2:order:o1
```

Gunakan ketika:

```text
- value JSON schema berubah incompatible
- key dimension berubah
- TTL policy berubah drastis
- serialization format berubah
- compression/encryption berubah
```

### Value Version

```json
{
  "schemaVersion": 2,
  "orderId": "o-9001",
  "status": "SUBMITTED",
  "updatedAt": "2026-07-13T10:30:00+07:00"
}
```

Gunakan ketika:

```text
- consumer lama dan baru coexist
- blue/green deployment
- rolling deployment
- stream/job payload versioning
```

### Rule

```text
Key version = routing / isolation version.
Value version = deserialization / compatibility version.
```

---

## 8. Tenant & Environment Isolation

Untuk multi-tenant system:

```text
{service}:{env}:tenant:{tenantId}:{purpose}:{domain}:{version}:...
```

Untuk global key:

```text
{service}:{env}:global:{purpose}:{domain}:{version}:...
```

Contoh:

```text
config-svc:prod:global:flag:v1:feature:new-checkout
authz-svc:prod:tenant:t-001:cache:permission:v3:user:u-123
```

### Jangan campur tenant

Buruk:

```text
permission:user:123
```

Kalau user id tidak global unique, tenant collision mungkin terjadi.

Lebih baik:

```text
authz-svc:prod:tenant:t-001:cache:permission:v3:user:u-123
```

### Operational Benefit

Dengan tenant prefix yang konsisten, incident response lebih mudah:

```text
SCAN MATCH authz-svc:prod:tenant:t-001:cache:permission:v3:*
```

Untuk produksi, gunakan `SCAN`, bukan `KEYS`. Redis docs menyatakan `KEYS` sebaiknya hanya untuk debugging/special operation dan tidak digunakan dalam regular application code karena bisa merusak performa pada database besar; `SCAN` dirancang untuk incremental iteration. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/keys/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9. PII / Secret Safety in Key Names

Redis key sering muncul di:

```text
- logs
- metrics
- slowlog
- APM traces
- admin CLI
- memory dump
- backup
- incident screenshot
```

Karena itu, jangan taruh raw sensitive data di key.

### Hindari

```text
auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:session:email:john.doe@company.com
auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:otp:phone:+628123456789
payment-svc:prod:card:4111111111111111
```

### Lebih aman

```text
auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:session:user-hash:sha256-abcd
auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:otp:phone-hash:sha256-efgh
payment-svc:prod:token:card-hash:sha256-ijkl
```

### Rule

```text
Identifier di key boleh membantu operasi,
tetapi jangan membuat key menjadi tempat bocor PII/secret.
```

Untuk domain case/regulatory, ini penting karena case id, party id, national id, customer identity, dan enforcement target bisa sensitif.

---

## 10. TTL Strategy: Core Model

TTL bukan sekadar teknis Redis. TTL adalah **policy consistency, security, dan memory management**.

Redis `EXPIRE` mendukung opsi seperti `NX`, `XX`, `GT`, dan `LT`, yang memungkinkan expiry hanya diset dalam kondisi tertentu, misalnya hanya ketika key belum punya expiry atau hanya ketika expiry baru lebih besar/kecil dari expiry saat ini. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/expire/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Redis `SET` juga mendukung opsi expiration seperti `EX`, `PX`, `EXAT`, `PXAT`, dan `KEEPTTL`, plus condition options seperti `NX` dan `XX`. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/set/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### TTL Categories

| Category | TTL | Contoh | Policy |
|---|---:|---|---|
| Ephemeral security | Detik-menit | OTP, nonce, login challenge | Wajib TTL pendek |
| Request control | Menit-jam | idempotency, rate window | Wajib TTL |
| Cache | Detik-jam | query result, permission snapshot | Wajib/strongly recommended |
| Presence | Detik | online heartbeat | Wajib TTL pendek |
| Lock/lease | Detik | distributed lock | Wajib TTL |
| Queue/stream | Berdasarkan trim/retention | stream job | Bukan TTL per key, tapi retention policy |
| Config cache | Menit | feature/config | TTL + invalidation |
| Permanent reference | No TTL optional | rarely, bounded metadata | Harus justified |

---

## 11. TTL Policy per Use Case

| Use case | Recommended TTL | Kenapa |
|---|---:|---|
| API response cache | 30 sec-10 min | Freshness vs DB load |
| Expensive aggregate cache | 1-30 min | Tergantung staleness tolerance |
| Permission/authz cache | 30 sec-5 min | Stale permission berisiko tinggi |
| Feature flag cache | 1-10 min | Source tetap config service |
| User session | 15 min-24 h | Tergantung security policy |
| OTP | 2-10 min | Security |
| Login attempt counter | 5-30 min | Abuse window |
| Rate limit fixed window | Window + buffer | Hindari key langsung hilang sebelum observability |
| Idempotency key | 24-72 h | Melindungi client retry |
| Distributed lock | 5-60 sec | Harus lebih lama dari critical section, tapi tidak terlalu lama |
| Worker lease | 30 sec-5 min | Harus renewable/fail-safe |
| Presence heartbeat | 30-120 sec | Detect offline cepat |
| Cache stampede lock | 5-30 sec | Hanya guard rebuild singkat |
| Delayed retry zset item | Sampai processed + trim | Butuh cleanup worker |
| Redis Stream | `MAXLEN`/retention | Trim eksplisit |

---

## 12. TTL Decision Tree

```text
Apakah data boleh hidup permanen?
├── Tidak
│   └── TTL wajib
│
└── Ya
    ↓
Apakah data bisa direbuild?
    ├── Ya
    │   └── TTL strongly recommended
    │
    └── Tidak
        ↓
        Apakah Redis adalah source of truth?
        ├── Ya
        │   └── Reconsider design; gunakan PostgreSQL/Kafka/Camunda
        └── Tidak
            └── Butuh cleanup/retention policy eksplisit
```

Rule praktis:

```text
Default Redis key harus punya TTL,
kecuali ada alasan jelas dan documented untuk tidak punya TTL.
```

---

## 13. Absolute TTL vs Sliding TTL

### Absolute TTL

TTL dihitung sejak key dibuat.

```text
session created at 10:00
TTL 30 min
expires at 10:30
```

Cocok untuk:

```text
- OTP
- nonce
- idempotency key
- cache freshness
- rate limit window
```

### Sliding TTL

TTL diperpanjang setiap kali ada aktivitas.

```text
session accessed at 10:20
TTL refreshed to expire at 10:50
```

Cocok untuk:

```text
- user session
- presence
- temporary user activity state
```

Risk sliding TTL:

```text
- data bisa hidup terlalu lama
- security exposure meningkat
- memory retention lebih sulit diprediksi
```

Untuk sliding TTL, biasanya perlu **absolute max lifetime** di value:

```json
{
  "createdAt": "2026-07-13T10:00:00+07:00",
  "lastAccessedAt": "2026-07-13T10:20:00+07:00",
  "absoluteExpiresAt": "2026-07-13T18:00:00+07:00"
}
```

---

## 14. TTL Jitter

Kalau banyak key dibuat bersamaan dengan TTL sama, mereka bisa expire bersamaan dan memicu cache stampede.

Buruk:

```text
set all product cache TTL = 300 seconds
```

Lebih baik:

```text
TTL = baseTTL + random jitter
TTL = 300s + random(0..60s)
```

Contoh:

```java
Duration ttlWithJitter(Duration base, Duration maxJitter) {
    long jitterMs = ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextLong(maxJitter.toMillis() + 1);
    return base.plusMillis(jitterMs);
}
```

Gunakan untuk:

```text
- cache query populer
- permission snapshot massal
- config cache
- product/catalog cache
```

Jangan gunakan jitter untuk:

```text
- OTP yang policy expiry-nya harus presisi
- lock lease
- strict rate limit window
```

---

## 15. Expiry Semantics yang Harus Dipahami

Beberapa command Redis bisa memengaruhi TTL.

### Risk: overwrite menghapus TTL

Jika key di-overwrite tanpa menjaga TTL, TTL bisa hilang tergantung command/pola yang dipakai. Karena itu, saat update value yang harus tetap expire, gunakan opsi expiry explicit atau `KEEPTTL` jika memang sesuai. Redis `SET` mendukung opsi expiry dan `KEEPTTL`. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/set/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Buruk:

```text
SET session:abc newValue
```

Kalau TTL hilang, session bisa menjadi permanent.

Lebih baik:

```text
SET session:abc newValue EX 1800
```

Atau bila ingin mempertahankan TTL:

```text
SET session:abc newValue KEEPTTL
```

### TTL invariant

Untuk setiap key family, definisikan:

```text
TTL invariant:
  key ini tidak boleh hidup tanpa expiry
```

Lalu test:

```text
TTL key > 0
```

---

## 16. Eviction Policy dan TTL

TTL berbeda dari eviction.

```text
TTL:
  kapan key expire berdasarkan waktu

Eviction:
  apa yang Redis hapus saat memory limit tercapai
```

Redis menggunakan `maxmemory-policy` untuk menentukan apa yang terjadi saat memory limit tercapai; policy tersedia untuk memilih apakah Redis menghapus key tertentu atau menolak write saat memory penuh. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/reference/eviction/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Design Implication

| Redis workload | Eviction policy yang masuk akal | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| Pure cache | `allkeys-lru` / `allkeys-lfu` | Semua key boleh dihapus |
| Mixed cache + state | Hati-hati | Pisahkan instance lebih baik |
| Session/idempotency/lock | `noeviction` atau dedicated instance | Jangan biarkan state penting ter-evict |
| Queue/stream | Dedicated instance | Eviction bisa merusak processing |
| Rate limiter | Bisa volatile policy | Key punya TTL |

Rule yang sangat penting:

```text
Jangan campur cache yang boleh di-evict dengan state yang tidak boleh hilang
dalam Redis instance yang eviction policy-nya agresif.
```

---

## 17. Key Namespace untuk Redis Cluster

Redis Cluster membagi key ke hash slots. Untuk multi-key operation, key biasanya harus berada di slot yang sama. Redis Cluster spec menjelaskan penggunaan hash tags `{...}` agar bagian tertentu dari key digunakan untuk hash slot. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/operate/oss_and_stack/reference/cluster-spec/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Tanpa hash tag

```text
order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cart:user:u1
order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cart-meta:user:u1
```

Di cluster, kedua key bisa masuk slot berbeda.

### Dengan hash tag

```text
order-svc:prod:{tenant:t1:user:u1}:cart:v1
order-svc:prod:{tenant:t1:user:u1}:cart-meta:v1
```

Keduanya dipaksa ke slot sama berdasarkan isi `{tenant:t1:user:u1}`.

### Gunakan hash tag hanya jika perlu

Cocok untuk:

```text
- multi-key transaction/script
- cart + metadata
- lock + state yang harus dicek bersama
- per-aggregate atomic update
```

Jangan pakai satu hash tag terlalu luas:

```text
order-svc:prod:{tenant:t1}:...
```

Kalau semua key tenant besar masuk satu slot, bisa hot slot.

---

## 18. Key Length Trade-off

Key terlalu pendek:

```text
u:1
```

Sulit di-debug dan rawan collision.

Key terlalu panjang:

```text
very-long-service-name:production-environment:tenant:very-long-tenant-identifier:...
```

Memboroskan memory karena Redis menyimpan key di memory.

Prinsip:

```text
Cukup deskriptif untuk operasi,
cukup pendek untuk volume tinggi.
```

### Untuk high-volume key

Contoh rate limit atau presence bisa sangat banyak. Gunakan singkatan standar yang masih jelas:

```text
api:prod:t:t1:rate:v1:u:u123:w:202607131030
rt:prod:t:t1:pres:v1:u:u123:d:d456
```

Tapi jangan membuat format unreadable tanpa registry.

---

## 19. Key Factory Pattern di Java

Jangan buat key dengan string concat tersebar.

Buruk:

```java
String key = "order:" + orderId;
```

Lebih baik:

```java
public final class RedisKeyFactory {

    private final String service;
    private final String environment;

    public RedisKeyFactory(String service, String environment) {
        this.service = service;
        this.environment = environment;
    }

    public String orderSummaryCache(String tenantId, String orderId) {
        return String.join(":",
            service,
            environment,
            "tenant", safe(tenantId),
            "cache",
            "order-summary",
            "v1",
            "order", safe(orderId)
        );
    }

    public String idempotencyKey(String tenantId, String operation, String keyHash) {
        return String.join(":",
            service,
            environment,
            "tenant", safe(tenantId),
            "idem",
            safe(operation),
            "v1",
            "key", safe(keyHash)
        );
    }

    public String lockCaseClaim(String tenantId, String caseId) {
        return String.join(":",
            service,
            environment,
            "tenant", safe(tenantId),
            "lock",
            "case-claim",
            "v1",
            "case", safe(caseId)
        );
    }

    private static String safe(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Redis key segment cannot be blank");
        }

        if (value.contains(":") || value.contains("{") || value.contains("}")) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsafe Redis key segment: " + value);
        }

        return value;
    }
}
```

### Kenapa `safe()` penting?

```text
- mencegah accidental delimiter injection
- mencegah cluster hash tag rusak
- mencegah blank segment
- membuat key predictable
```

---

## 20. TTL Policy Object di Java

Jangan hardcode TTL di banyak tempat.

```java
public final class RedisTtlPolicy {

    public Duration orderSummaryCacheTtl() {
        return Duration.ofMinutes(5);
    }

    public Duration permissionSnapshotTtl() {
        return Duration.ofMinutes(2);
    }

    public Duration idempotencyTtl() {
        return Duration.ofHours(48);
    }

    public Duration loginAttemptTtl() {
        return Duration.ofMinutes(15);
    }

    public Duration otpTtl() {
        return Duration.ofMinutes(5);
    }

    public Duration lockLeaseTtl() {
        return Duration.ofSeconds(30);
    }

    public Duration cacheStampedeLockTtl() {
        return Duration.ofSeconds(10);
    }
}
```

Lebih advanced:

```java
public record TtlSpec(
    Duration base,
    Duration maxJitter,
    boolean mandatory,
    boolean sliding
) {}
```

---

## 21. Cache Key Design

### Cache key harus memasukkan semua input yang memengaruhi hasil

Buruk:

```text
order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:orders:v1
```

Kalau query punya filter/status/page/sort, hasil bisa tercampur.

Lebih baik:

```text
order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:orders:v1:status:OPEN:page:1:size:50:sort:created-desc
```

Untuk query kompleks, hash query canonical form:

```text
order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:order-search:v1:qhash:sha256-a81fd
```

Simpan metadata opsional:

```json
{
  "queryHash": "sha256-a81fd",
  "queryShape": "status,page,size,sort",
  "createdAt": "2026-07-13T10:30:00+07:00",
  "ttlSeconds": 300
}
```

### Jangan lupa auth context

Untuk permission-sensitive query, cache key harus memasukkan:

```text
- tenant
- user/role/policy version
- data scope
- query params
```

Contoh:

```text
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-search:v2:user:uHash:policy:p17:qhash:q123
```

Kalau tidak, user bisa menerima data yang seharusnya tidak terlihat.

---

## 22. Idempotency Key Design

Pattern:

```text
{service}:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:idem:{operation}:v1:key:{idempotencyKeyHash}
```

Contoh:

```text
order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:idem:create-order:v1:key:sha256-abc
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:idem:submit-case:v1:key:sha256-def
```

Isi value harus menyimpan:

```json
{
  "requestHash": "sha256-request-body",
  "status": "PROCESSING",
  "resultRef": null,
  "createdAt": "2026-07-13T10:30:00+07:00"
}
```

TTL:

```text
24-72 jam umum untuk API command,
tergantung retry behavior client dan domain risk.
```

Invariant:

```text
Same idempotency key + same request hash = replay previous result
Same idempotency key + different request hash = conflict
```

---

## 23. Lock Key Design

Pattern:

```text
{service}:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:lock:{purpose}:v1:{entity}:{id}
```

Contoh:

```text
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:lock:case-claim:v1:case:c991
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:lock:daily-rebuild:v1:job:case-sla
```

Value harus token unik:

```text
ownerPod=case-svc-abc123
ownerThread=worker-7
token=uuid
acquiredAt=...
leaseMs=30000
```

Minimal:

```text
uuid-random-token
```

Rule:

```text
- lock wajib TTL
- release harus compare token
- lease time harus eksplisit
- critical section harus idempotent atau protected by DB constraint
```

---

## 24. Rate Limit Key Design

Fixed window:

```text
api-gw:prod:tenant:t1:rate:v1:api-key:akHash:route:rHash:window:202607131030
```

Sliding window dengan sorted set:

```text
api-gw:prod:tenant:t1:rate-sliding:v1:api-key:akHash:route:rHash
```

Token bucket:

```text
api-gw:prod:tenant:t1:bucket:v1:api-key:akHash:route:rHash
```

TTL:

```text
fixed window key TTL = window duration + buffer
sliding window TTL = max window + buffer
bucket TTL = inactivity expiration
```

### Why route hash?

Raw route bisa panjang dan mengandung path variables:

```text
/orders/123/items/456
```

Lebih baik canonical route:

```text
POST /orders/{orderId}/items/{itemId}
```

Lalu hash:

```text
route:sha256-...
```

---

## 25. Session / Token Key Design

Pattern:

```text
auth-svc:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:session:v2:sid:{sessionHash}
auth-svc:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:token-revoked:v1:jti:{jtiHash}
auth-svc:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:otp:v1:user:{userHash}:purpose:{purpose}
```

TTL:

```text
session = sliding + absolute max lifetime
revoked token = until token original expiry
OTP = short absolute TTL
```

Jangan simpan raw JWT, raw session id, OTP, phone, email, atau token di key.

---

## 26. Redis Streams Key Design

Stream key:

```text
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task:v1
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:case-timeout:v1
```

Consumer group name:

```text
email-worker-v1
case-timeout-worker-v1
```

DLQ stream:

```text
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task-dlq:v1
```

Retry stream atau zset:

```text
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:zschedule:email-task-retry:v1
```

TTL strategy:

```text
Streams biasanya tidak pakai TTL per entry.
Gunakan XTRIM / MAXLEN / retention policy.
```

Boundary:

```text
Stream key jangan punya TTL kalau masih dipakai aktif.
Retention dilakukan dengan trimming, bukan expire seluruh stream mendadak.
```

---

## 27. Keyspace Notification Key Design

Untuk keyspace notification, key naming menentukan event yang bisa difilter secara application-level.

Contoh expired key untuk timeout ringan:

```text
workflow-svc:prod:tenant:t1:timeout:case-reminder:v1:case:c991
```

Ketika expired, subscriber bisa membaca nama key dari event lalu parse domain context.

Tapi hati-hati:

```text
Keyspace notification memakai Pub/Sub.
Event bisa hilang saat subscriber disconnect.
```

Redis docs menyatakan keyspace notification disabled by default karena memakai CPU tambahan, dan karena bergantung pada Pub/Sub, notification yang terjadi saat client disconnect tidak diterima oleh client tersebut. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/using-commands/keyspace/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Maka keyspace notification harus punya fallback:

```text
- reconciliation scanner
- DB query for due items
- sorted-set scheduler
- Camunda timer
- Kafka/outbox event
```

---

## 28. Operational Scan Pattern

Redis production code tidak boleh memakai `KEYS`.

Buruk:

```text
KEYS order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:*
```

Lebih baik:

```text
SCAN 0 MATCH order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:* COUNT 1000
```

Redis docs menyebut `SCAN` melakukan incremental iteration sehingga bisa dipakai di production tanpa downside command seperti `KEYS` yang dapat memblokir server lama pada dataset besar. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/using-commands/keyspace/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Operational Use Cases

| Operation | Pattern |
|---|---|
| Delete cache tenant tertentu | `SCAN MATCH service:prod:tenant:t1:cache:*` lalu `UNLINK` |
| Inspect rate limiter | `SCAN MATCH api:prod:tenant:t1:rate:*` |
| Debug lock | `GET service:prod:tenant:t1:lock:*` via scan-limited |
| Count key family | Sampling atau scan job terbatas |
| Migration cleanup | Scan old `v1` keys after rollout |

Untuk delete banyak key, prefer `UNLINK` dibanding `DEL` jika ingin asynchronous memory reclaim.

---

## 29. Cache Invalidation dengan Version Key

Ada dua pendekatan:

### Delete specific key

```text
DEL order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:order-summary:v1:order:o1
```

Cocok untuk entity single.

### Version bump

Key data:

```text
authz-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:permission:v3:user:u1:policy:p17
```

Policy version berubah dari `p17` ke `p18`, maka key lama tidak dipakai lagi.

Cocok untuk:

```text
- permission cache
- config cache
- policy cache
- aggregate cache
```

Kelebihan:

```text
- tidak perlu delete semua key lama segera
- rolling deployment lebih aman
- stale data otomatis orphaned lalu expired
```

Syarat:

```text
- TTL harus ada
- version source harus authoritative
```

---

## 30. Namespace Registry Format

Contoh registry sederhana:

```yaml
redisKeyFamilies:
  - name: order-summary-cache
    owner: order-svc
    pattern: "order-svc:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:cache:order-summary:v1:order:{orderId}"
    dataStructure: string
    valueFormat: json
    ttl: PT5M
    ttlMandatory: true
    sourceOfTruth: postgresql.orders
    piiInKey: false
    piiInValue: possible
    encryptionRequired: false
    evictionSafe: true
    failureMode: fallback-db

  - name: create-order-idempotency
    owner: order-svc
    pattern: "order-svc:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:idem:create-order:v1:key:{keyHash}"
    dataStructure: hash
    valueFormat: json
    ttl: PT48H
    ttlMandatory: true
    sourceOfTruth: postgresql.orders.unique_client_request_id
    piiInKey: false
    piiInValue: no
    evictionSafe: partially
    failureMode: fail-closed-or-db-fallback
```

Ini bisa disimpan di repo sebagai documentation/config.

---

## 31. Governance Rules

### Rule 1 - No raw Redis key outside key factory

```text
Semua key harus dibuat oleh RedisKeyFactory.
```

### Rule 2 - Every key family must have owner

```text
Owner = service/team yang bertanggung jawab atas lifecycle dan cleanup.
```

### Rule 3 - TTL must be documented

```text
Tidak boleh ada key family baru tanpa TTL decision.
```

### Rule 4 - Sensitive identifiers must be hashed

```text
Email, phone, national id, token, session id, external customer id sensitif → hash.
```

### Rule 5 - No wildcard delete without dry run

```text
SCAN first, count/sample, approve, then UNLINK.
```

### Rule 6 - Pub/Sub/notification channel names follow same governance

```text
Channel name juga keyspace-like contract.
```

### Rule 7 - Version before incompatible change

```text
Kalau schema/key dimension berubah, buat v2.
```

### Rule 8 - Cluster hash tag harus explicit

```text
Gunakan {...} hanya untuk key yang memang perlu co-location.
```

---

## 32. Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Masalah | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| `user:{id}` | Owner/env/tenant tidak jelas | Prefix lengkap |
| Raw email/phone di key | PII bocor | Hash identifier |
| Key tanpa TTL untuk cache | Memory leak | TTL mandatory |
| TTL tersebar hardcoded | Sulit audit | Central `RedisTtlPolicy` |
| `KEYS *` di production | Bisa block Redis | Gunakan `SCAN` |
| Cache key tidak memasukkan query params | Data tercampur | Canonical query hash |
| Cache permission tanpa policy version | Stale authorization | Include policy version |
| Lock key tanpa TTL | Deadlock | Lease TTL |
| Stream key diberi TTL sembarang | Queue hilang | Retention via trim |
| Semua key cluster pakai `{tenant}` | Hot slot | Hash tag granular |
| Nama key terlalu cryptic | Sulit incident response | Standar singkatan |
| Nama key terlalu panjang untuk high volume | Memory overhead | Balance readability/size |
| Tidak ada registry | Redis jadi hidden database | Key family catalog |

---

## 33. Senior Engineer Checklist

Sebelum approve Redis key baru:

```text
[ ] Key dibuat via RedisKeyFactory
[ ] Pattern mengikuti {service}:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:...
[ ] Purpose jelas: cache/rate/idem/lock/session/stream/etc.
[ ] Owner service jelas
[ ] Data structure jelas
[ ] TTL jelas
[ ] TTL mandatory atau optional terdokumentasi
[ ] Source of truth jelas
[ ] Failure mode jelas
[ ] Key tidak mengandung raw PII/secret
[ ] Value schema punya version jika perlu
[ ] Cache key memasukkan semua query/auth context
[ ] Lock key punya lease TTL
[ ] Stream punya retention/trim policy
[ ] Keyspace notification punya fallback/reconciliation
[ ] Cluster hash tag dipakai hanya bila perlu
[ ] Operational scan/delete pattern aman
[ ] Metrics tidak memakai raw key sebagai label
```

---

## 34. Ringkasan Praktis

```text
Redis key naming yang baik:
  owner jelas
  env jelas
  tenant jelas
  purpose jelas
  version jelas
  identifier aman
  TTL jelas
  bisa di-scan operasional
  bisa dihapus selektif
  tidak bocor PII
  siap untuk migration

Redis key naming yang buruk:
  pendek tapi ambigu
  raw identifier sensitif
  tidak ada TTL
  tidak ada owner
  tidak ada version
  tidak bisa dioperasikan saat incident
```

Kalimat paling penting:

> Redis key adalah kontrak lifecycle. Kalau key tidak punya owner, TTL, source of truth, dan deletion strategy, key itu akan menjadi technical debt yang hidup di memory.
