Saya lanjut ke **Seri 5 — Redis Data Structures for Microservices**. Fokusnya: memilih struktur Redis berdasarkan pattern microservice, bukan sekadar menghafal command.

# Seri 5 - Redis Data Structures for Microservices

Status: **Seri 5 dari 22 seri lanjutan**.  
Setelah seri ini selesai, tersisa **17 seri**.

---

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Redis kuat bukan karena hanya cepat, tetapi karena menyediakan beberapa **data structure primitive** yang bisa langsung dipakai untuk pattern microservice:

```text
String      -> cache value, counter, lock token, idempotency state
Hash        -> object-like state, session metadata, field update
Set         -> uniqueness, membership, deduplication
Sorted Set  -> ranking, delayed job, sliding window, scheduler
List        -> simple queue / stack
Stream      -> append-only event/job log with consumer group
Bitmap      -> compact boolean flags / activity tracking
HyperLogLog -> approximate cardinality
Pub/Sub     -> realtime signal, not durable state
JSON        -> hierarchical document, if Redis JSON capability tersedia
Bloom       -> probabilistic membership, if Redis probabilistic capability tersedia
```

Redis docs sendiri membedakan tipe seperti **Strings, Hashes, Lists, Sets, Sorted Sets, Streams**, dan beberapa tipe lain seperti JSON/probabilistic structures tergantung capability yang tersedia. Jadi pemilihan struktur harus berdasarkan **query pattern, lifecycle, atomicity, TTL, durability expectation, dan operational cost**, bukan hanya “semua disimpan sebagai JSON string”. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/data-types/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Diagram Pemilihan Data Structure

```text
Redis Data Structure Decision
│
├── Single value?
│   ├── cache blob / token / lock / counter
│   └── String
│
├── Object with fields?
│   ├── session metadata / idempotency status / user snapshot
│   └── Hash
│
├── Need uniqueness only?
│   ├── dedup / membership / role members / processed IDs
│   └── Set
│
├── Need ordering by score?
│   ├── delayed job / leaderboard / sliding window / priority
│   └── Sorted Set
│
├── Need FIFO/LIFO simple queue?
│   ├── lightweight queue / stack
│   └── List
│
├── Need append-only log + consumer group?
│   ├── job queue / event-ish processing / retry / pending recovery
│   └── Stream
│
├── Need compact yes/no flags?
│   ├── active days / feature exposure / seen flags
│   └── Bitmap
│
├── Need approximate unique count?
│   ├── unique visitors / unique API callers
│   └── HyperLogLog
│
├── Need realtime signal only?
│   ├── websocket fanout / invalidation signal
│   └── Pub/Sub
│
└── Need probabilistic membership?
    ├── probably-seen-before / duplicate pre-check
    └── Bloom Filter if available
```

---

## 3. Big Rule: Pilih Berdasarkan Access Pattern

Kesalahan umum di Redis adalah menyimpan semua hal sebagai JSON string karena terasa mudah. Itu boleh untuk cache sederhana, tetapi buruk kalau Anda perlu update field tertentu, membership check, sorted range query, sliding window, atau worker queue.

| Pertanyaan desain | Struktur yang biasanya cocok |
|---|---|
| Butuh ambil/set satu blob? | String |
| Butuh update sebagian field? | Hash |
| Butuh cek apakah item sudah ada? | Set / Bloom Filter |
| Butuh urutan berdasarkan waktu/skor/prioritas? | Sorted Set |
| Butuh queue sederhana FIFO/LIFO? | List |
| Butuh job/event dengan ack/retry/consumer group? | Stream |
| Butuh count integer atomic? | String counter |
| Butuh online/offline heartbeat? | String dengan TTL / Set + TTL item design |
| Butuh rate limiting sliding window? | Sorted Set |
| Butuh delayed retry/scheduler? | Sorted Set |
| Butuh realtime fanout? | Pub/Sub |
| Butuh durable-ish async processing? | Stream |

Redis Streams adalah **append-only data structure**; `XADD` menambahkan entry baru ke stream, dan entry berisi field-value pairs. Ini membuat Streams lebih cocok untuk job/event ringan dibanding Pub/Sub jika Anda butuh consumer group dan acknowledgement. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/data-types/streams/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4. String

### Mental Model

Redis String adalah struktur paling dasar: satu key menyimpan satu value berupa text/binary. Ini cocok untuk cache blob, token, counter, lock token, idempotency marker, feature flag value, atau serialized DTO.

`SET` mendukung opsi penting seperti `NX`, `XX`, `EX`, `PX`, `EXAT`, `PXAT`, dan `KEEPTTL`, sehingga satu command bisa menggabungkan write condition dan expiration. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/set/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Use Case

| Use case | Contoh key | Value |
|---|---|---|
| Cache DTO | `order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:order:v1:order:o1` | JSON |
| Counter | `api:prod:tenant:t1:rate:v1:user:u1:window:202607131030` | `42` |
| Lock token | `case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:lock:claim:v1:case:c1` | UUID token |
| Idempotency state simple | `order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:idem:create:v1:key:k1` | JSON state |
| Feature flag | `config-svc:prod:global:flag:v1:new-checkout` | `enabled` |

### Command Pattern

```text
SET key value EX 300
GET key
INCR key
SET lockKey token NX PX 30000
GETDEL key
```

### Cocok Untuk

```text
- cache-aside simple
- idempotency state kecil
- rate limit fixed window
- distributed lock token
- OTP/nonce
- session pointer
- feature flag single value
```

### Tidak Cocok Untuk

```text
- object yang sering update field tertentu
- membership besar
- ranking/range query
- queue dengan retry/ack
- multi-field analytics state
```

### Jersey/JAX-RS Pattern

```text
ContainerRequestFilter
  -> RateLimiter
     -> INCR fixed-window key
     -> EXPIRE if first hit
```

Atau:

```text
OrderCommandService
  -> IdempotencyStore
     -> SET idemKey PROCESSING NX EX 48h
```

---

## 5. Hash

### Mental Model

Hash menyimpan field-value pairs di bawah satu key. Cocok untuk object kecil-menengah yang perlu update field tertentu tanpa rewrite seluruh JSON blob.

Redis `HSET` membuat atau memodifikasi field dalam hash; `HGET` membaca field; `HGETALL` membaca semua field. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/hset/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Use Case

| Use case | Contoh key | Field |
|---|---|---|
| Session metadata | `auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:session:v1:sid:s1` | `userId`, `createdAt`, `lastSeenAt` |
| Idempotency state | `order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:idem:create-order:v1:key:k1` | `status`, `requestHash`, `resultRef` |
| Worker lease metadata | `job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:lease:v1:worker:w1` | `pod`, `heartbeat`, `version` |
| Permission snapshot metadata | `authz-svc:prod:tenant:t1:perm-meta:v1:user:u1` | `policyVersion`, `cachedAt` |

### Command Pattern

```text
HSET sessionKey userId u123 createdAt 2026-07-13T10:00:00+07:00
HGET sessionKey userId
HGETALL sessionKey
EXPIRE sessionKey 1800
```

### Cocok Untuk

```text
- session state
- idempotency state machine
- small object metadata
- partial field update
- status tracking
```

### Tidak Cocok Untuk

```text
- deeply nested object
- large arbitrary document
- high-cardinality unbounded fields
- field-level TTL jika server/client belum mendukungnya
```

Catatan update: Redis command docs saat ini mencantumkan command seperti `HSETEX`, `HTTL`, dan hash-field expiration related commands. Namun untuk production, pastikan versi Redis server, managed Redis provider, dan Java client benar-benar mendukung command tersebut sebelum menjadikannya desain utama. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6. Set

### Mental Model

Set adalah koleksi unordered berisi unique strings. Cocok untuk membership, uniqueness, deduplication, dan operasi intersection/union/difference.

Redis docs menyebut Set cocok untuk melacak item unik, merepresentasikan relasi seperti user dengan role tertentu, dan melakukan operasi set seperti intersection, union, dan difference. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/data-types/sets/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Use Case

| Use case | Contoh key | Member |
|---|---|---|
| Dedup processed request | `worker-svc:prod:tenant:t1:set:processed:v1:date:20260713` | `requestIdHash` |
| Role membership cache | `authz-svc:prod:tenant:t1:set:role-users:v1:role:admin` | `userIdHash` |
| Active connection IDs | `realtime-svc:prod:tenant:t1:set:connections:v1:user:u1` | `connectionId` |
| Feature rollout cohort | `config-svc:prod:tenant:t1:set:feature-users:v1:feature:f1` | `userIdHash` |
| Unique IP temporary | `api:prod:tenant:t1:set:unique-ip:v1:window:20260713` | `ipHash` |

### Command Pattern

```text
SADD key member
SISMEMBER key member
SREM key member
SCARD key
SINTER key1 key2
```

`SADD` menambahkan member ke set dan tidak menambahkan duplikat yang sudah ada. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/sadd/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Cocok Untuk

```text
- membership check
- uniqueness
- deduplication
- relation cache
- cohort cache
```

### Tidak Cocok Untuk

```text
- ordered ranking
- delayed scheduling
- per-member TTL native
- huge unbounded membership tanpa cleanup
```

### Important Caveat

Redis Set tidak punya TTL per member. TTL berlaku di level key. Kalau perlu expiry per item, gunakan:

```text
- Sorted Set dengan score = expiry timestamp
- String per item dengan TTL
- Stream dengan retention
```

---

## 7. Sorted Set / ZSet

### Mental Model

Sorted Set adalah set unik yang setiap member punya score. Redis menjaga member tetap terurut berdasarkan score. Ini sangat berguna untuk ranking, delayed job, sliding window, scheduler, priority queue, dan timeout scanning.

Redis docs mendefinisikan sorted set sebagai koleksi unique strings yang menjaga urutan berdasarkan score; `ZADD` menambahkan atau mengupdate member dengan score. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/data-types/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Use Case

| Use case | Score | Member |
|---|---:|---|
| Delayed job | executeAt timestamp | jobId |
| Retry scheduler | retryAt timestamp | taskId |
| Sliding rate limit | request timestamp | requestId |
| Leaderboard | score/rank value | userId |
| SLA timeout index | dueAt timestamp | caseId |
| Expiring membership | expiresAt timestamp | memberId |
| Priority queue | priority score | jobId |

### Command Pattern

```text
ZADD retryQueue retryAtMillis jobId
ZRANGEBYSCORE retryQueue -inf now LIMIT 0 100
ZREM retryQueue jobId
ZCARD retryQueue
ZCOUNT retryQueue min max
```

### Cocok Untuk

```text
- delayed job
- timeout scanner
- sliding window rate limiter
- ranking
- priority queue
- expiring membership
```

### Tidak Cocok Untuk

```text
- durable job processing tanpa ack
- high-volume event log panjang
- arbitrary object storage
```

### Pattern: Delayed Job

```text
Producer:
  ZADD job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:zschedule:email-retry:v1 retryAt jobId

Worker loop:
  ZRANGEBYSCORE queue -inf now LIMIT 0 100
  for each jobId:
    acquire job lock
    load job payload
    process
    ZREM queue jobId
```

### Failure Risk

```text
Worker mengambil due job
  ↓
Worker crash sebelum ZREM atau setelah side effect
```

Guardrail:

```text
- job processing idempotent
- use lock/lease
- store attempt count
- keep job payload durable enough
- DLQ after max retry
```

---

## 8. List

### Mental Model

List adalah linked list berisi string values. Redis docs menyebut List sering dipakai untuk stack, queue, dan background worker queue management. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/data-types/lists/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Use Case

| Use case | Command style |
|---|---|
| Simple FIFO queue | `LPUSH` producer + `RPOP` consumer |
| Simple stack | `LPUSH` + `LPOP` |
| Blocking worker | `BLPOP` / `BRPOP` |
| Small buffer | `LPUSH` + `LTRIM` |

### Command Pattern

```text
LPUSH queueKey payload
RPOP queueKey
BLPOP queueKey 5
LTRIM queueKey 0 999
```

`LPUSH` menambahkan value di head list dan membuat list jika key belum ada. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/lpush/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Cocok Untuk

```text
- queue sangat sederhana
- ephemeral internal work
- local-ish async worker
- stack/buffer kecil
```

### Tidak Cocok Untuk

```text
- job queue yang butuh ack/retry kuat
- consumer group
- replay
- pending recovery
- audit
```

### List vs Stream

| Need | List | Stream |
|---|---:|---:|
| Simple FIFO | Ya | Ya |
| Consumer group | Tidak natural | Ya |
| Ack | Manual | Native via group pending/ack |
| Replay | Lemah | Lebih baik |
| Pending recovery | Manual | Ada pending entries |
| Operational visibility | Terbatas | Lebih kaya |

Untuk job queue production yang butuh retry dan recovery, **Streams biasanya lebih baik daripada List**.

---

## 9. Stream

### Mental Model

Stream adalah append-only log berisi entries. Setiap entry punya ID dan field-value pairs. Dengan consumer group, worker bisa membaca entry, memproses, lalu acknowledge.

Redis docs menjelaskan `XREADGROUP` membaca message baru atau historis dari stream untuk consumer dalam group, dan dapat block sampai message tersedia. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/xreadgroup/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Dokumentasi Redis streaming juga menjelaskan `XACK` digunakan setelah consumer memproses entry agar Redis dapat menghapus entry tersebut dari pending list group. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/use-cases/streaming/nodejs/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Use Case

| Use case | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Background job internal | email task, report generation |
| Retryable worker queue | external API call worker |
| Notification event ringan | internal notification with replay |
| Integration buffer kecil | REST command to async worker |
| Audit-lite non-critical | short retention activity log |
| Camunda helper queue | external task helper, dedup worker |

### Command Pattern

```text
XADD streamKey * type email to user@example.com template welcome
XGROUP CREATE streamKey groupName $ MKSTREAM
XREADGROUP GROUP groupName consumerName COUNT 10 BLOCK 5000 STREAMS streamKey >
XACK streamKey groupName entryId
XPENDING streamKey groupName
XCLAIM / XAUTOCLAIM ...
XTRIM streamKey MAXLEN ~ 100000
```

### Cocok Untuk

```text
- async job dengan worker group
- retry/pending recovery
- low-to-medium volume internal event
- bounded retention event stream
```

### Tidak Cocok Untuk

```text
- enterprise event backbone skala besar
- long retention cross-service replay
- schema governance besar
- exactly-once business delivery
- audit/legal ledger
```

### Stream vs Kafka

| Aspek | Redis Stream | Kafka |
|---|---|---|
| Setup | Lebih ringan | Lebih berat |
| Consumer group | Ada | Ada |
| Retention panjang | Terbatas/harus hati-hati | Natural |
| Replay besar | Terbatas | Kuat |
| Schema governance | Manual | Lebih mature ecosystem |
| Cross-domain event backbone | Bisa tapi hati-hati | Lebih cocok |
| Internal job queue | Cocok | Bisa, tapi sering overkill |

---

## 10. Bitmap

### Mental Model

Bitmap bukan tipe data terpisah secara konseptual, melainkan operasi bit pada String. Cocok untuk menyimpan banyak boolean flag secara compact.

### Use Case

| Use case | Bit offset | Value |
|---|---:|---|
| User active day | day-of-year | 0/1 |
| Feature exposure | user index/hash bucket | 0/1 |
| Daily login marker | user numeric index | 0/1 |
| Seen/unseen compact marker | item index | 0/1 |

### Command Pattern

```text
SETBIT key offset 1
GETBIT key offset
BITCOUNT key
BITOP AND output key1 key2
```

### Cocok Untuk

```text
- compact boolean tracking
- daily activity
- exposure flags
- coarse analytics
```

### Tidak Cocok Untuk

```text
- user ID tidak bisa dipetakan ke numeric offset stabil
- perlu per-item metadata
- perlu TTL per bit
- perlu query kompleks
```

### Caveat

Bitmap bisa sangat memory-efficient, tetapi offset yang terlalu besar bisa membuat Redis allocate string besar. Jangan pakai raw large numeric ID langsung sebagai offset tanpa mapping.

---

## 11. HyperLogLog

### Mental Model

HyperLogLog menyimpan estimasi jumlah unique item dengan memory kecil. Cocok untuk approximate cardinality, bukan exact membership.

### Use Case

| Use case | Key | Input |
|---|---|---|
| Unique API callers per day | `api:prod:hll:unique-callers:20260713` | user/api key hash |
| Unique visitors | `web:prod:hll:uv:20260713` | visitor hash |
| Unique tenants active | `platform:prod:hll:active-tenants:20260713` | tenant id |

### Command Pattern

```text
PFADD key member
PFCOUNT key
PFMERGE output key1 key2
```

### Cocok Untuk

```text
- approximate unique count
- analytics counter ringan
- cardinality monitoring
```

### Tidak Cocok Untuk

```text
- exact billing
- exact quota
- exact compliance count
- membership check
```

Rule:

```text
Kalau angka dipakai untuk billing, enforcement, legal, atau quota final,
jangan pakai HyperLogLog sebagai sumber kebenaran.
```

---

## 12. Pub/Sub

### Mental Model

Pub/Sub bukan storage structure, tetapi communication primitive. Cocok untuk signal realtime yang boleh hilang.

Use case:

```text
- websocket fanout antar pod
- local cache invalidation signal
- operational notification ringan
- “wake up worker” signal
```

Tidak cocok untuk:

```text
- durable event
- job queue
- audit
- workflow transition final
```

Redis Pub/Sub memakai delivery **at-most-once**, artinya message bisa hilang jika subscriber tidak menerima saat publish. Ini penting untuk membedakan Pub/Sub dari Stream/Kafka. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/data-types/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 13. JSON

### Mental Model

Redis JSON berguna bila Anda memakai Redis Stack/Redis module/capability yang mendukung JSON document. Ini memungkinkan penyimpanan dan manipulasi struktur hierarchical lebih natural dibanding String JSON biasa.

Namun untuk aplikasi Java/Jersey enterprise, jangan otomatis memilih Redis JSON hanya karena payload Anda JSON.

### Cocok Untuk

```text
- document cache yang perlu partial path update
- hierarchical config
- search/index capability jika Redis Stack digunakan
```

### Tidak Cocok Untuk

```text
- Redis OSS deployment yang tidak menyediakan JSON capability
- object sederhana yang cukup Hash/String
- domain object yang seharusnya di PostgreSQL
```

### Decision Rule

```text
String JSON:
  cocok untuk cache blob read-mostly

Hash:
  cocok untuk flat object fields

Redis JSON:
  cocok jika butuh hierarchical document operation dan deployment mendukungnya
```

Redis docs membandingkan JSON sebagai tipe untuk structured hierarchical arrays/key-value objects, tetapi pastikan capability ini tersedia di environment on-prem/AWS/Azure yang dipakai. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/data-types/compare-data-types/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 14. Bloom Filter

### Mental Model

Bloom filter adalah probabilistic membership structure. Ia bisa menjawab “mungkin ada” atau “pasti tidak ada”, dengan trade-off false positive.

Redis docs menyebut Bloom filter sebagai probabilistic data structure yang mengecek apakah elemen ada di set dengan memory kecil, mengorbankan presisi demi efisiensi. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/data-types/probabilistic/bloom-filter/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Use Case

```text
- pre-check duplicate request ID
- probably-seen-before event
- avoid expensive DB lookup for definitely-not-present item
- cache penetration protection
```

### Tidak Cocok Untuk

```text
- exact deduplication final
- billing
- permission
- legal/compliance decision
```

### Pattern

```text
Request comes in
  ↓
Bloom says definitely not seen
  ↓
Skip expensive lookup or proceed fast path

Bloom says maybe seen
  ↓
Check PostgreSQL/Redis Set/Idempotency store
```

Bloom filter harus dipakai sebagai **optimization**, bukan final correctness mechanism.

---

## 15. Data Structure Selection Matrix

| Pattern Microservice | Recommended Redis structure | Kenapa |
|---|---|---|
| Cache-aside DTO | String JSON | Simple get/set |
| Cache object fields | Hash | Partial field update |
| Idempotency key | String or Hash | State machine + TTL |
| Fixed-window rate limit | String counter | `INCR` + TTL |
| Sliding-window rate limit | Sorted Set | Timestamp score |
| Token bucket | String/Hash + Lua | Atomic refill/check |
| Distributed lock | String | Token + TTL |
| Distributed semaphore | String/Set/ZSet + Lua / Redisson | Shared permits |
| Dedup set | Set | Unique membership |
| Dedup with expiry per item | Sorted Set | Score as expiry |
| Delayed job | Sorted Set | Score as due timestamp |
| Simple queue | List | FIFO/LIFO |
| Worker queue with ack | Stream | Consumer group + pending |
| WebSocket fanout | Pub/Sub or Stream | Signal vs replay |
| Keyspace notification reaction | Pub/Sub-based notification | Best-effort event |
| Presence | String TTL / Set | Heartbeat |
| Leaderboard | Sorted Set | Score ranking |
| Unique visitor estimate | HyperLogLog | Approx cardinality |
| Feature rollout cohort | Set / Bitmap | Membership or compact flag |
| Cache penetration guard | Bloom Filter | Probabilistic membership |
| Activity bitmap | Bitmap | Compact boolean flags |
| Config document cache | String/Hash/JSON | Depends on access pattern |

---

## 16. Redis Data Structure dalam Jersey/JAX-RS Architecture

Struktur aplikasi yang sehat:

```text
JAX-RS Resource
  ↓
Application Service
  ↓
Use-case Port
  ├── CacheStore
  ├── IdempotencyStore
  ├── RateLimiter
  ├── LockService
  ├── JobQueue
  ├── PresenceStore
  └── PermissionCache
  ↓
Redis Adapter
  ├── String commands
  ├── Hash commands
  ├── Set commands
  ├── Sorted Set commands
  ├── Stream commands
  └── Lua scripts
```

Jangan membuat domain service memilih command Redis langsung. Domain service harus berpikir dalam use case:

```java
rateLimiter.check(subject, policy)
idempotencyStore.checkOrStart(key, hash)
permissionCache.getSnapshot(user, policyVersion)
jobQueue.enqueue(task)
```

Bukan:

```java
redis.zadd(...)
redis.hset(...)
redis.xadd(...)
```

---

## 17. Pattern Detail: Cache DTO

### Struktur

```text
String
```

### Key

```text
order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:order-summary:v1:order:o1
```

### Value

```json
{
  "schemaVersion": 1,
  "orderId": "o1",
  "status": "SUBMITTED",
  "total": 150000,
  "cachedAt": "2026-07-13T10:00:00+07:00"
}
```

### TTL

```text
1-10 menit, tergantung freshness tolerance
```

### Guardrail

```text
- source of truth PostgreSQL
- delete/update cache setelah write
- include schema version
- fallback DB saat Redis timeout
```

---

## 18. Pattern Detail: Idempotency Store

### Struktur

```text
Hash or String JSON
```

### Hash Example

```text
HSET idemKey
  requestHash sha256-abc
  status PROCESSING
  createdAt 2026-07-13T10:00:00+07:00
  resultRef ""
EXPIRE idemKey 172800
```

### State

```text
PROCESSING
COMPLETED
FAILED_RETRYABLE
FAILED_FINAL
```

### Kenapa Hash?

Hash berguna jika status/resultRef perlu diupdate tanpa rewrite seluruh JSON.

### Guardrail

```text
- same key + different requestHash = conflict
- final invariant tetap dijaga unique constraint PostgreSQL
- TTL 24-72 jam
```

---

## 19. Pattern Detail: Rate Limiting

### Fixed Window

```text
String counter
INCR key
EXPIRE key window+buffer
```

Cocok untuk simple API quota.

### Sliding Window

```text
Sorted Set
ZADD key now requestId
ZREMRANGEBYSCORE key -inf now-window
ZCOUNT key now-window now
```

Cocok untuk rate limiting yang lebih smooth.

### Token Bucket

```text
String/Hash + Lua
fields:
  tokens
  lastRefillAt
```

Cocok untuk quota yang ingin mengizinkan burst terbatas.

### Guardrail

```text
- command harus atomic
- Lua untuk check-update multi-step
- key include tenant/user/route
- failure mode jelas: fail-open atau fail-closed
```

---

## 20. Pattern Detail: Delayed Job

### Struktur

```text
Sorted Set
```

### Key

```text
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:zschedule:retry-email:v1
```

### Member

```text
jobId
```

### Score

```text
executeAt epoch millis
```

### Worker Flow

```text
ZRANGEBYSCORE retryQueue -inf now LIMIT 0 100
  ↓
try acquire lock for jobId
  ↓
load payload
  ↓
process
  ↓
ZREM retryQueue jobId
```

### Caveat

Sorted Set sendiri bukan ack-based queue. Kalau butuh pending recovery dan consumer group, gunakan Stream.

---

## 21. Pattern Detail: Job Queue with Stream

### Struktur

```text
Stream
```

### Key

```text
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task:v1
```

### Entry

```text
XADD stream * taskId t1 type SEND_EMAIL recipientHash h123 attempt 0
```

### Consumer

```text
XREADGROUP GROUP email-worker-v1 pod-abc COUNT 10 BLOCK 5000 STREAMS stream >
```

### Ack

```text
XACK stream email-worker-v1 entryId
```

### Guardrail

```text
- idempotent worker
- pending recovery
- retry count
- dead-letter stream
- stream trimming policy
```

---

## 22. Pattern Detail: Distributed Coordination

Redis coordination sering memakai kombinasi:

```text
String -> lock token
Sorted Set -> lease index / semaphore expiry
Hash -> owner metadata
Lua -> atomic check/update
```

### Lock

```text
SET lockKey token NX PX leaseMs
```

### Safe release

```text
Lua:
  if GET lockKey == token
  then DEL lockKey
  else return 0
```

### Semaphore

```text
ZSET permits:
  score = lease expiry time
  member = owner token
```

Flow:

```text
remove expired permits
count active permits
if count < max:
  add owner token with expiry
else:
  reject / wait
```

### Guardrail

```text
- lease expiry
- ownership token
- fencing token for critical writes
- DB constraint for final invariant
```

---

## 23. Pattern Detail: Presence / Online State

Ada dua pendekatan umum.

### Per-device TTL key

```text
realtime-svc:prod:tenant:t1:presence:v1:user:u1:device:d1 = online
TTL 60s
```

Kelebihan:

```text
- expiry natural
- simple heartbeat
```

Kekurangan:

```text
- mencari semua online user perlu scan/index tambahan
```

### Set of active connections

```text
SADD realtime-svc:prod:tenant:t1:set:online-users:v1 user:u1
```

Kelebihan:

```text
- membership global mudah
```

Kekurangan:

```text
- tidak ada TTL per member
- butuh cleanup saat disconnect/heartbeat expired
```

Pattern yang lebih kuat sering menggabungkan:

```text
String TTL per connection
+
Set index per tenant/user
+
cleanup worker
```

---

## 24. Pattern Detail: Permission Cache

### Pilihan 1 - String JSON

Cocok kalau permission snapshot dibaca utuh.

```text
authz-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:permission:v3:user:u1:policy:p17
```

### Pilihan 2 - Set

Cocok kalau perlu membership check:

```text
authz-svc:prod:tenant:t1:set:user-permissions:v3:user:u1
```

Command:

```text
SISMEMBER key "case:approve"
```

### Pilihan 3 - Hash

Cocok kalau permission punya metadata:

```text
HSET permissionKey case.approve true case.assign false policyVersion p17
```

### Guardrail

```text
- TTL pendek
- include policy version
- invalidation saat permission berubah
- fallback source of truth
```

---

## 25. Pattern Detail: Cache Penetration Protection

Problem:

```text
Request mencari entity yang tidak ada
  ↓
Cache miss
  ↓
DB hit
  ↓
Tetap tidak ada
  ↓
Repeated attack/random IDs membebani DB
```

Solusi:

| Teknik | Redis structure |
|---|---|
| Negative cache | String |
| Known IDs | Set |
| Probabilistic known IDs | Bloom Filter |

### Negative Cache

```text
SET order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache-negative:order:v1:order:o999 "__NULL__" EX 60
```

### Guardrail

```text
- TTL negative cache harus pendek
- jangan negative-cache authorization-sensitive result sembarangan
- invalidasi kalau entity bisa muncul setelah create
```

---

## 26. Pattern Detail: Leaderboard / Ranking

### Struktur

```text
Sorted Set
```

### Commands

```text
ZADD leaderboard score userId
ZRANGE leaderboard 0 9 REV WITHSCORES
ZRANK leaderboard userId
ZSCORE leaderboard userId
```

`ZSCORE` membaca score member dalam sorted set. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/zscore/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Cocok Untuk

```text
- ranking
- priority
- score-based retrieval
```

### Tidak Cocok Untuk

```text
- audit of score changes
- financial/legal ranking decision without durable source
```

---

## 27. Pattern Detail: Workflow / Camunda Helper State

Redis boleh membantu Camunda, tetapi tidak menggantikan process state.

| Camunda helper use case | Redis structure |
|---|---|
| External task dedup | String/Hash idempotency |
| Temporary correlation lookup | String/Hash |
| Worker concurrency cap | Semaphore via ZSet/Lua or Redisson |
| Retry delay helper | Sorted Set |
| Lightweight task queue | Stream |
| Notification to websocket | Pub/Sub |

Guardrail:

```text
- process instance state tetap di Camunda
- business state tetap di PostgreSQL
- Redis hanya temporary coordination/cache
```

---

## 28. Common Mistakes

| Mistake | Kenapa salah | Pilihan lebih baik |
|---|---|---|
| Semua disimpan sebagai JSON String | Tidak bisa query/update efisien | Hash/Set/ZSet sesuai pattern |
| Pub/Sub dijadikan queue | Message bisa hilang | Stream/List/Kafka |
| List dijadikan job queue critical | Ack/retry manual | Stream |
| Set dipakai untuk expiring members | TTL hanya per key | Sorted Set score expiry |
| Sorted Set dipakai untuk durable event log | Tidak natural untuk ack/replay | Stream/Kafka |
| Stream tanpa trim | Memory tumbuh terus | `XTRIM`/retention |
| Hash berisi ribuan field unbounded | Hidden memory growth | Partition/TTL/DB |
| Bitmap pakai huge raw ID sebagai offset | Memory bengkak | ID remapping |
| HyperLogLog untuk exact count | Approximate only | DB/Set/counter exact |
| Bloom untuk final dedup | False positive possible | DB unique constraint/Set |
| Lock pakai Set tanpa TTL | Deadlock | String `SET NX PX` |
| Rate limit multi-command tanpa atomicity | Race condition | Lua/transaction |

---

## 29. Data Structure Governance Table

| Data Structure | Harus punya TTL? | Bisa jadi source of truth? | Production guardrail |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| String cache | Ya | Tidak | fallback DB |
| String lock | Ya | Tidak | token + TTL + safe release |
| String counter | Ya untuk window | Tergantung | atomic command |
| Hash session | Ya | Biasanya tidak | sliding/absolute TTL |
| Hash idempotency | Ya | Auxiliary | DB unique constraint |
| Set membership cache | Ya/optional | Tidak untuk final authz | source version |
| Sorted Set scheduler | Cleanup wajib | Tidak sendirian | idempotent worker |
| List queue | Biasanya | Tidak untuk critical | simple only |
| Stream queue | Trim/retention | Auxiliary | ack/retry/DLQ |
| Bitmap | Berdasarkan window | Tidak | bounded offset |
| HyperLogLog | Berdasarkan window | Tidak | approximate only |
| Pub/Sub | N/A | Tidak | loss-tolerant |
| Bloom Filter | Berdasarkan rebuild policy | Tidak | false positive aware |

---

## 30. Decision Checklist

Sebelum memilih Redis data structure:

```text
[ ] Apa access pattern utama?
[ ] Apakah butuh exact lookup, membership, range, order, atau append log?
[ ] Apakah perlu TTL per key atau per item?
[ ] Apakah data bisa rebuild?
[ ] Apakah kehilangan data Redis aman?
[ ] Apakah butuh ack/retry?
[ ] Apakah butuh ordering?
[ ] Apakah butuh atomic multi-step update?
[ ] Apakah key bisa tumbuh tanpa batas?
[ ] Apakah ada retention/trim policy?
[ ] Apakah ada source of truth non-Redis?
[ ] Apakah operation akan aman di Redis Cluster?
[ ] Apakah command blocking dipisah dari request path?
[ ] Apakah value mengandung PII?
```

---

## 31. Practical Mapping untuk Stack Anda

| Stack area | Redis structure yang sering dipakai | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| Jersey filter | String counter / ZSet | rate limit |
| Jersey resource/service | String/Hash | cache/idempotency |
| MyBatis/PostgreSQL | String JSON / Hash | derived cache |
| Camunda worker helper | String lock / Stream / ZSet | dedup/retry |
| Kafka boundary | Stream or Pub/Sub only for local/internal | Kafka tetap event backbone |
| Auth/authz | Hash/String/Set | session, permission snapshot |
| Docker/Kubernetes | String TTL / Hash | pod heartbeat, leader guard |
| WebSocket/realtime | Pub/Sub / Stream / Set | fanout and presence |
| Scheduler/job | ZSet / Stream | delayed and ack-based work |

---

## 32. Recommended Default Choices

Kalau ragu, gunakan default ini:

```text
Cache DTO:
  String JSON

Small mutable state:
  Hash

Dedup/membership:
  Set

Time-based scheduling:
  Sorted Set

Simple queue:
  List, hanya untuk non-critical

Production worker queue:
  Stream

Realtime signal:
  Pub/Sub

Sliding window:
  Sorted Set

Fixed window:
  String counter

Distributed lock:
  String + SET NX PX + token

Distributed semaphore:
  Redisson or ZSet/Lua

Approx unique count:
  HyperLogLog

Probably-seen-before:
  Bloom Filter if available
```

---

## 33. Senior Engineer Review Questions

```text
[ ] Kenapa memilih struktur ini, bukan String JSON?
[ ] Apakah struktur ini mendukung query/update pattern dengan efisien?
[ ] Apakah data structure punya lifecycle/TTL/retention?
[ ] Apakah ada risiko unbounded growth?
[ ] Apakah ada fallback/source of truth?
[ ] Apakah command yang dipakai atomic?
[ ] Apakah blocking operation keluar dari request path?
[ ] Apakah Stream punya consumer group, ack, retry, DLQ?
[ ] Apakah Sorted Set scheduler punya cleanup dan idempotent worker?
[ ] Apakah Set butuh per-member expiry? Kalau ya, kenapa bukan ZSet?
[ ] Apakah approximate structure dipakai hanya untuk approximate decision?
[ ] Apakah semua key mengikuti namespace governance?
```

---

## 34. Ringkasan Praktis

```text
String:
  single value, cache, counter, lock, token

Hash:
  object-like state, session, idempotency fields

Set:
  unique membership, dedup, relation cache

Sorted Set:
  score-based order, delayed job, sliding window, ranking

List:
  simple queue/stack, non-critical worker

Stream:
  append-only job/event log, consumer group, ack, retry

Bitmap:
  compact boolean flags

HyperLogLog:
  approximate unique count

Pub/Sub:
  realtime signal, not durable

Bloom:
  probabilistic membership, optimization only
```

Kalimat kunci:

> Redis data structure harus dipilih dari access pattern dan failure semantics. Salah memilih struktur sering tidak terlihat saat development, tetapi akan muncul sebagai stale data, memory growth, race condition, queue loss, atau incident debugging yang sulit.

## Response:
7/13/2026, 6:50:17 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 6 — Cache-Aside Pattern with PostgreSQL/MyBatis**. Fokusnya: cache eksplisit di service layer, bukan cache “ajaib” yang tersembunyi di mapper, supaya consistency dan invalidation tetap bisa dikendalikan.

# Seri 6 - Cache-Aside Pattern with PostgreSQL/MyBatis

Status: **Seri 6 dari 22 seri lanjutan**.  
Setelah seri ini selesai, tersisa **16 seri**.

---

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas **cache-aside pattern** untuk aplikasi:

```text
Java 17+
Jersey/JAX-RS
MyBatis
PostgreSQL
Redis
Docker/Kubernetes
```

Fokusnya bukan sekadar “cara pakai Redis untuk cache”, tetapi bagaimana membuat cache yang:

```text
- eksplisit di service layer
- tidak menyembunyikan business consistency
- aman terhadap stale data
- cocok dengan MyBatis transaction boundary
- punya TTL dan invalidation jelas
- bisa fallback ke PostgreSQL
- mudah di-debug saat incident
```

Cache-aside adalah pola ketika aplikasi membaca cache terlebih dahulu; jika miss, aplikasi mengambil data dari database lalu mengisi cache. Redis menjelaskan pola ini sebagai cache yang diisi **on demand**, sedangkan Microsoft Azure Architecture Center menekankan bahwa aplikasi bertanggung jawab menjaga cache tetap selaras dengan data store. ([Redis](https://redis.io/tutorials/howtos/solutions/microservices/caching/)) ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/architecture/patterns/cache-aside))

---

## 2. Core Mental Model

```text
PostgreSQL / MyBatis
= source of truth
= transaksi bisnis
= relational integrity
= auditability

Redis
= derived read model
= performance accelerator
= temporary copy
= boleh stale dalam batas tertentu
= harus bisa rebuild
```

Rule utama:

```text
Kalau Redis hilang, sistem harus tetap benar.
Kalau Redis stale, dampaknya harus diketahui.
Kalau PostgreSQL berubah, cache harus punya jalan untuk expire atau invalidated.
```

Cache-aside tidak menjamin cache selalu konsisten dengan data store; Microsoft secara eksplisit mencatat bahwa aplikasi harus punya strategi untuk mendeteksi dan menangani stale data, dan bahwa data sensitif/security-related bisa tidak cocok untuk cache. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/architecture/patterns/cache-aside))

---

## 3. Diagram Cache-Aside

```text
Client
  ↓
JAX-RS Resource
  ↓
Application Service
  ↓
CacheStore.get(cacheKey)
  │
  ├── Cache HIT
  │     ↓
  │   Deserialize DTO
  │     ↓
  │   Return response
  │
  └── Cache MISS
        ↓
      MyBatis Repository
        ↓
      PostgreSQL
        ↓
      Map entity/result → Cache DTO
        ↓
      Redis SET with TTL
        ↓
      Return response
```

Write path:

```text
Command Request
  ↓
JAX-RS Resource
  ↓
Application Service
  ↓
PostgreSQL transaction via MyBatis
  ↓
Commit success
  ↓
Invalidate Redis keys
  ↓
Return response
```

Urutan write penting: update data store dulu, lalu hapus cache. Jika cache dihapus sebelum database commit/update selesai, request lain bisa mengalami miss, membaca data lama dari database, lalu mengisi ulang cache dengan data lama. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/architecture/patterns/cache-aside))

---

## 4. Kenapa Cache Eksplisit di Service Layer?

MyBatis punya local cache dan second-level cache. Dokumentasi MyBatis menjelaskan bahwa setiap `SqlSession` memiliki local cache, query dalam session yang sama bisa memakai cache tersebut, dan local cache dibersihkan saat `update`, `commit`, `rollback`, dan `close`. MyBatis juga menyebut adanya second-level cache. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

Selain itu, MyBatis punya project `mybatis-redis` sebagai Redis support untuk MyBatis Cache. Project summary resminya menyebut `mybatis-redis` sebagai “Redis support for MyBatis Cache”. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/redis-cache/summary.html))

Namun untuk microservice production, saya lebih merekomendasikan **explicit service-layer cache** untuk domain penting.

### MyBatis Cache vs Explicit Redis Cache

| Pendekatan | Cocok untuk | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| MyBatis local cache | Repeated query dalam satu `SqlSession` | Jangan modifikasi object hasil query karena reference bisa dipakai ulang dalam session |
| MyBatis second-level cache | Query cache sederhana per mapper | Invalidation bisa terlalu implicit |
| MyBatis Redis cache plugin | Cache mapper-level tanpa banyak code aplikasi | Sulit mengontrol domain-specific TTL, tenant, policy version, invalidation graph |
| Explicit Redis cache di service layer | Microservice dengan domain/invalidation kompleks | Butuh code lebih banyak, tapi lebih defensible |

Untuk sistem regulatory/case management/order platform, cache biasanya harus memahami:

```text
- tenant
- authorization context
- query parameter
- policy version
- business state version
- aggregate dependency
- stale tolerance
```

Hal-hal ini sering lebih aman dikelola eksplisit di service layer daripada tersembunyi di mapper cache.

---

## 5. Recommended Layering

```text
JAX-RS Resource
  ↓
Application Service
  ↓
Query Service / Command Service
  ↓
Cache Port
  ↓
Redis Adapter

Application Service
  ↓
Repository Port
  ↓
MyBatis Mapper
  ↓
PostgreSQL
```

Contoh struktur package:

```text
com.company.caseapp
├── api
│   └── CaseResource.java
├── application
│   ├── CaseQueryService.java
│   └── CaseCommandService.java
├── cache
│   ├── CacheStore.java
│   ├── CaseCacheKeys.java
│   ├── CaseCachePolicy.java
│   └── RedisCaseCacheStore.java
├── persistence
│   ├── CaseRepository.java
│   ├── MyBatisCaseRepository.java
│   └── CaseMapper.java
└── domain
    └── Case.java
```

Rule:

```text
Resource tidak tahu Redis.
Mapper tidak tahu Redis.
Domain tidak tahu Redis.
Application/query service mengatur kapan cache dipakai.
Redis adapter hanya menjalankan operasi cache.
```

---

## 6. Read Path: Cache Hit

```text
GET /cases/{caseId}
  ↓
CaseQueryService.getCase(...)
  ↓
cacheKey = caseCacheKeys.caseSummary(tenant, caseId, authzVersion)
  ↓
Redis GET
  ↓
Cache hit
  ↓
Deserialize CaseSummaryCacheDto
  ↓
Return CaseSummaryResponse
```

Pseudo-code:

```java
public CaseSummaryResponse getCaseSummary(
    TenantId tenantId,
    UserContext user,
    CaseId caseId
) {
    String key = cacheKeys.caseSummary(
        tenantId,
        caseId,
        user.policyVersion()
    );

    Optional<CaseSummaryCacheDto> cached =
        cacheStore.getJson(key, CaseSummaryCacheDto.class);

    if (cached.isPresent()) {
        metrics.cacheHit("case-summary");
        return mapper.toResponse(cached.get());
    }

    metrics.cacheMiss("case-summary");
    return loadCaseSummaryFromDbAndCache(tenantId, user, caseId, key);
}
```

---

## 7. Read Path: Cache Miss

```java
private CaseSummaryResponse loadCaseSummaryFromDbAndCache(
    TenantId tenantId,
    UserContext user,
    CaseId caseId,
    String key
) {
    CaseSummaryRow row = caseRepository.findSummaryById(
        tenantId,
        caseId,
        user.dataScope()
    ).orElseThrow(NotFoundException::new);

    CaseSummaryCacheDto dto = CaseSummaryCacheDto.from(row);

    cacheStore.setJson(
        key,
        dto,
        ttlPolicy.caseSummaryTtlWithJitter()
    );

    return mapper.toResponse(dto);
}
```

Important:

```text
- DB query tetap enforce tenant dan authorization/data scope
- cache DTO bukan domain entity mutable
- key memasukkan auth/policy dimension jika hasil bergantung pada permission
- Redis SET memakai TTL
- Redis failure tidak boleh menggagalkan read jika DB berhasil, kecuali cache critical
```

Redis `SET` mendukung expiration options seperti `EX`, `PX`, `EXAT`, `PXAT`, dan `KEEPTTL`; gunakan expiry saat menulis cache supaya key tidak hidup permanen. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/set/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8. Write Path: Update lalu Invalidate

```text
PUT /cases/{caseId}
  ↓
CaseCommandService.updateCase(...)
  ↓
Begin DB transaction
  ↓
MyBatis update PostgreSQL
  ↓
Commit
  ↓
Invalidate related Redis keys
  ↓
Return response
```

Pseudo-code:

```java
public void updateCaseStatus(
    TenantId tenantId,
    UserContext user,
    CaseId caseId,
    CaseStatus newStatus
) {
    transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
        CaseRow existing = caseRepository.findForUpdate(tenantId, caseId)
            .orElseThrow(NotFoundException::new);

        authorizationService.assertCanUpdate(user, existing);

        caseRepository.updateStatus(
            tenantId,
            caseId,
            newStatus,
            existing.version()
        );
    });

    cacheInvalidator.invalidateCaseAfterWrite(tenantId, caseId);
}
```

### Kenapa invalidate setelah commit?

Jika invalidation dilakukan sebelum commit, kemungkinan sequence buruk:

```text
T1 delete cache
T2 read cache miss
T2 read DB old value
T2 set old value into Redis
T1 commit new value
Redis sekarang berisi old value
```

Karena itu, default aman:

```text
DB commit success
  ↓
delete/invalidate cache
```

Tetap ada race kecil pada high concurrency, tetapi ini jauh lebih baik daripada delete-before-commit.

---

## 9. Cache Key untuk MyBatis Query

Cache key harus mencerminkan semua input query.

### Query sederhana by ID

```text
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-summary:v1:case:c991
```

### Query bergantung permission

```text
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-summary:v1:case:c991:policy:p17
```

### Query list/search

Untuk query dengan filter banyak, buat canonical query hash.

```text
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-search:v2:user:uHash:policy:p17:qhash:sha256-abc
```

Canonical input:

```json
{
  "tenant": "t1",
  "userDataScope": "region:west",
  "policyVersion": "p17",
  "filters": {
    "status": ["OPEN", "ESCALATED"],
    "priority": ["HIGH"],
    "createdFrom": "2026-07-01",
    "createdTo": "2026-07-13"
  },
  "page": 1,
  "size": 50,
  "sort": "createdAt:desc"
}
```

Hash canonical JSON-nya, bukan raw string acak dari request.

### Jangan cache search query jika:

```text
- hasil sangat user-specific dan permission-sensitive
- hit ratio rendah
- query params terlalu variatif
- data berubah sangat sering
- hasil besar
- pagination/sort membuat key explosion
```

---

## 10. Cache DTO: Jangan Simpan Entity MyBatis Mentah

Buruk:

```text
Redis value = serialized CaseEntity dari MyBatis
```

Risiko:

```text
- field internal ikut tersimpan
- lazy/partial mapping bisa membingungkan
- perubahan mapper merusak cache
- object mutable
- schema tidak eksplisit
```

Lebih baik:

```java
public record CaseSummaryCacheDto(
    int schemaVersion,
    String caseId,
    String status,
    String priority,
    String assigneeId,
    Instant updatedAt,
    long dbVersion,
    Instant cachedAt
) {}
```

Tambahkan:

```text
schemaVersion
dbVersion / rowVersion
cachedAt
sourceUpdatedAt
```

Ini membantu debugging stale cache.

---

## 11. TTL Strategy untuk Cache-Aside

Microsoft menekankan bahwa lifetime cache harus cocok dengan pola akses; TTL terlalu pendek membuat aplikasi terus mengambil dari data store, sementara TTL terlalu panjang meningkatkan stale data. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/architecture/patterns/cache-aside)) Redis `EXPIRE` memberikan TTL ke key; tanpa expire, key akan hidup sampai dihapus eksplisit. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/expire/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Cache type | TTL rekomendasi | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Case summary | 30 sec-5 min | Bergantung freshness requirement |
| Static reference data | 30 min-24 h | Bisa version-based |
| Permission snapshot | 30 sec-5 min | Risiko stale authorization |
| Config/feature flag | 1-10 min | Source tetap config DB/service |
| Expensive aggregate | 1-15 min | Pakai jitter |
| Negative cache | 15 sec-2 min | Jangan terlalu lama |
| Search result | 15 sec-5 min | Hati-hati key explosion |
| External API lookup | 1-60 min | Sesuaikan SLA external source |

Rule:

```text
TTL adalah keputusan domain, bukan angka default global.
```

---

## 12. TTL Jitter untuk Menghindari Stampede

Tanpa jitter:

```text
10.000 key dibuat jam 10:00 dengan TTL 5 menit
semua expire sekitar 10:05
PostgreSQL tiba-tiba kena spike
```

Dengan jitter:

```text
TTL = 5 menit + random 0..60 detik
expire tersebar
```

Pseudo-code:

```java
public Duration withJitter(Duration base, Duration maxJitter) {
    long jitterMs = ThreadLocalRandom.current()
        .nextLong(maxJitter.toMillis() + 1);
    return base.plusMillis(jitterMs);
}
```

Gunakan untuk:

```text
- cache populer
- aggregate mahal
- permission snapshot massal
- reference data
```

Jangan gunakan untuk:

```text
- strict security expiry
- lock lease
- rate limit window
```

---

## 13. Negative Cache

Negative cache menyimpan fakta bahwa data tidak ditemukan.

Problem tanpa negative cache:

```text
GET /cases/non-existing-id
  ↓
Redis miss
  ↓
DB miss
  ↓
attacker/client mengulang random ID
  ↓
DB terus terbebani
```

Pattern:

```text
SET case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache-neg:case:v1:case:c999 "__NULL__" EX 60
```

Pseudo-code:

```java
Optional<CaseSummaryResponse> getCase(String id) {
    String key = cacheKeys.caseSummary(id);
    String negKey = cacheKeys.caseSummaryNegative(id);

    if (cacheStore.exists(negKey)) {
        throw new NotFoundException();
    }

    Optional<CaseSummaryCacheDto> cached = cacheStore.getJson(key, CaseSummaryCacheDto.class);
    if (cached.isPresent()) {
        return Optional.of(mapper.toResponse(cached.get()));
    }

    Optional<CaseSummaryRow> row = repository.findSummary(id);

    if (row.isEmpty()) {
        cacheStore.setString(negKey, "__NULL__", Duration.ofSeconds(60));
        throw new NotFoundException();
    }

    ...
}
```

Guardrail:

```text
- TTL pendek
- invalidate negative cache saat create
- jangan negative-cache hasil authorization denial sebagai “not found” terlalu lama
- jangan simpan detail sensitif di negative value
```

---

## 14. Cache Stampede Protection

Cache stampede terjadi saat banyak request miss bersamaan lalu semuanya menghantam PostgreSQL.

### Problem

```text
Popular key expires
  ↓
1000 requests arrive
  ↓
all miss Redis
  ↓
all query PostgreSQL
  ↓
DB spike
```

### Solution A - Lock-on-miss

```text
Request miss
  ↓
Try acquire rebuild lock
  ├── success: load DB, set cache, release lock
  └── fail: wait briefly, retry cache, or return stale fallback
```

Lock key:

```text
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:lock:cache-rebuild:v1:case:c991
```

Pseudo-code:

```java
public CaseSummaryResponse getWithStampedeProtection(String key) {
    Optional<CaseSummaryCacheDto> cached = cacheStore.getJson(key, CaseSummaryCacheDto.class);
    if (cached.isPresent()) {
        return mapper.toResponse(cached.get());
    }

    String lockKey = cacheKeys.rebuildLock(key);
    Optional<LockToken> token = lockService.tryAcquire(lockKey, Duration.ofSeconds(5));

    if (token.isPresent()) {
        try {
            Optional<CaseSummaryCacheDto> doubleCheck =
                cacheStore.getJson(key, CaseSummaryCacheDto.class);

            if (doubleCheck.isPresent()) {
                return mapper.toResponse(doubleCheck.get());
            }

            CaseSummaryCacheDto dto = loadFromDb();
            cacheStore.setJson(key, dto, ttlPolicy.caseSummaryTtlWithJitter());
            return mapper.toResponse(dto);
        } finally {
            lockService.release(lockKey, token.get());
        }
    }

    sleepSmallJitter();
    return cacheStore.getJson(key, CaseSummaryCacheDto.class)
        .map(mapper::toResponse)
        .orElseGet(() -> loadDirectlyFromDbWithoutCaching());
}
```

Guardrail:

```text
- lock TTL pendek
- release verify ownership token
- double-check cache setelah lock acquired
- fallback DB jika lock wait gagal
- jangan menahan HTTP thread terlalu lama
```

### Solution B - Stale-While-Revalidate

Simpan data stale sebentar sambil refresh async.

Value:

```json
{
  "payload": { "...": "..." },
  "freshUntil": "2026-07-13T10:05:00+07:00",
  "staleUntil": "2026-07-13T10:10:00+07:00"
}
```

Flow:

```text
fresh -> return
stale but allowed -> return stale + trigger refresh
expired beyond stale -> block/reload
```

Cocok untuk:

```text
- dashboard
- reference data
- expensive aggregate
```

Tidak cocok untuk:

```text
- authorization decision sensitif
- payment status final
- regulatory decision
```

---

## 15. Invalidation Strategy

### A. Delete Exact Key

Cocok untuk entity by ID.

```text
DEL case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-summary:v1:case:c991
```

### B. Versioned Key

Cocok untuk permission/config/policy.

```text
authz-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:permission:v3:user:u1:policy:p17
authz-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:permission:v3:user:u1:policy:p18
```

Saat policy version berubah, key lama tidak dipakai lagi dan expire alami.

### C. Dependency Index

Jika satu write memengaruhi banyak cache key, simpan index.

```text
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:set:cache-index:case:v1:case:c991
  members:
    case-summary key
    case-detail key
    case-search qhash key
```

Saat update case:

```text
SMEMBERS index
UNLINK keys
DEL index
```

Caveat:

```text
- index sendiri harus TTL
- index bisa stale
- jangan terlalu besar
```

### D. Broad Pattern Delete dengan SCAN

Untuk incident atau admin operation:

```text
SCAN MATCH case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-search:v2:*
UNLINK matched keys
```

Jangan pakai `KEYS` di production.

---

## 16. Transaction Boundary dengan MyBatis

Pola umum:

```java
transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
    mapper.updateCase(...);
    mapper.insertAudit(...);
    mapper.updateSla(...);
});

// outside transaction:
cacheInvalidator.invalidateCase(...)
```

Kenapa outside transaction?

```text
Redis bukan bagian dari PostgreSQL transaction.
Kalau Redis delete dilakukan di tengah transaction lalu DB rollback,
cache sudah terhapus padahal data tidak berubah.
```

Namun invalidation setelah commit juga punya risiko:

```text
DB commit sukses
app crash sebelum invalidate
cache lama tetap hidup sampai TTL
```

Mitigasi:

```text
- TTL jangan terlalu panjang
- gunakan outbox event untuk invalidation penting
- gunakan version key untuk domain sensitif
- cache DTO menyimpan dbVersion/updatedAt
```

Untuk consistency yang lebih kuat:

```text
PostgreSQL transaction:
  update data
  insert outbox event CACHE_INVALIDATE_CASE
commit

Outbox worker:
  read event
  invalidate Redis
  mark event processed
```

---

## 17. Outbox-Based Cache Invalidation

Pattern:

```text
Command Service
  ↓
PostgreSQL transaction
  ├── update business tables
  └── insert cache_invalidation_outbox
  ↓
commit
  ↓
Outbox worker
  ↓
delete Redis keys
```

Outbox table:

```sql
CREATE TABLE cache_invalidation_outbox (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    aggregate_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    processed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
    attempt_count INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);
```

Cocok untuk:

```text
- cache invalidation penting
- cross-service cache invalidation
- write path tidak boleh tergantung Redis
- butuh retry jika Redis down
```

Redis bukan bagian dari DB transaction, jadi outbox membuat invalidation menjadi recoverable.

---

## 18. Cache Consistency Levels

| Level | Deskripsi | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Best-effort TTL only | Cache expire sendiri | Public reference data |
| Delete-on-write | Setelah write, delete key | Entity detail |
| Versioned key | Key include version | Permission/config |
| Outbox invalidation | Invalidation durable di DB | Critical domain cache |
| Write-through | Write DB dan cache sinkron | Read-after-write kuat, complexity naik |
| No cache | Selalu DB | Sensitive/security-critical |

Default saya untuk stack Anda:

```text
Entity detail:
  delete-on-write + TTL

Permission/authz:
  short TTL + policy version + explicit invalidation

Search result:
  short TTL only or selective invalidation

Critical command state:
  do not rely on cache

Reference data:
  longer TTL + version key
```

---

## 19. Cache-Aside untuk Query List/Search

List/search lebih sulit daripada by-ID cache.

Problem:

```text
Case status berubah dari OPEN ke CLOSED.
Cache search OPEN masih mengandung case tersebut.
Cache search CLOSED belum mengandung case tersebut.
```

Opsi:

### Option 1 - Short TTL only

Cocok jika staleness bisa diterima.

```text
case search TTL = 30-60 sec
```

### Option 2 - Invalidate by index

Simpan mapping case → search keys yang pernah berisi case.

Caveat: mahal dan kompleks.

### Option 3 - Cache only IDs, hydrate detail separately

```text
search result cache = list of case IDs
case detail cache = per case
```

Ketika case detail berubah, detail cache lebih mudah invalidated. Search membership tetap bisa stale sebentar.

### Option 4 - Jangan cache search

Jika filter sangat variatif dan hit ratio rendah, jangan cache.

Decision:

```text
Cache list/search hanya jika:
- query populer
- hit ratio tinggi
- result size bounded
- staleness acceptable
- invalidation strategy jelas
```

---

## 20. Cache dan Authorization

Ini area rawan.

Buruk:

```text
case-svc:prod:cache:case:c991
```

Jika response berbeda berdasarkan user permission, key ini bisa membocorkan data.

Lebih aman:

```text
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-summary:v1:case:c991:user:uHash:policy:p17
```

Atau cache hanya data non-sensitive, lalu authorization tetap dilakukan per request.

Pattern yang lebih defensible:

```text
1. Authz check ke policy/source
2. Cache only allowed projection
3. Include policy version/data scope in key
4. TTL pendek
```

Rule:

```text
Jika response tergantung user permission, cache key harus memasukkan permission context
atau cache harus berada setelah authorization dengan projection aman.
```

---

## 21. Cache dan PostgreSQL Row Version

Tambahkan version di tabel penting:

```sql
ALTER TABLE cases
ADD COLUMN version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
ADD COLUMN updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now();
```

Saat update:

```sql
UPDATE cases
SET status = #{status},
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND case_id = #{caseId}
  AND version = #{expectedVersion};
```

Cache DTO:

```json
{
  "schemaVersion": 1,
  "caseId": "c991",
  "status": "OPEN",
  "dbVersion": 17,
  "sourceUpdatedAt": "2026-07-13T10:30:00+07:00",
  "cachedAt": "2026-07-13T10:31:00+07:00"
}
```

Benefit:

```text
- stale cache mudah dideteksi
- response bisa include ETag/version
- optimistic concurrency lebih jelas
- debugging incident lebih mudah
```

---

## 22. Cache Key untuk Aggregate

Aggregate cache punya dependency banyak.

Contoh:

```text
case detail page depends on:
- cases
- case_tasks
- case_notes summary
- assignee profile
- SLA status
- permission projection
```

Key:

```text
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-detail:v2:case:c991:caseVer:17:policy:p17
```

Jika `caseVer` berubah, key berubah. Tetapi jika task/note berubah, perlu aggregate version.

Opsi:

```text
case_aggregate_version table
  tenant_id
  case_id
  aggregate_version
  updated_at
```

Setiap perubahan child entity menaikkan aggregate version.

Key:

```text
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-detail:v2:case:c991:aggVer:52:policy:p17
```

Ini sering lebih aman daripada mencoba delete semua kombinasi cache.

---

## 23. Redis Failure Policy

| Cache operation | Redis failure behavior |
|---|---|
| GET cache | fallback DB |
| SET cache after DB load | log warning, return DB result |
| DEL after write | retry via outbox if important |
| Negative cache SET | ignore if fail |
| Stampede lock | fallback direct DB or short wait |
| Permission cache GET | fallback policy source |
| Cache health down | degrade, not necessarily liveness down |

Rule:

```text
Cache Redis failure tidak boleh membuat read path gagal jika PostgreSQL tersedia,
kecuali cache tersebut bukan sekadar cache tapi bagian security/control plane.
```

---

## 24. Observability

Metrics minimal:

```text
redis.cache.hit.count
redis.cache.miss.count
redis.cache.set.count
redis.cache.set.error.count
redis.cache.delete.count
redis.cache.delete.error.count
redis.cache.deserialize.error.count
redis.cache.stale.detected.count
redis.cache.fallback_db.count
redis.cache.negative.hit.count
redis.cache.stampede_lock.acquire.count
redis.cache.stampede_lock.timeout.count
```

Labels:

```text
service
env
cache_family
result
exception_class
```

Jangan label dengan raw key karena cardinality dan PII risk.

Log structured:

```json
{
  "event": "cache_deserialize_failed",
  "cacheFamily": "case-summary",
  "schemaVersionExpected": 2,
  "exception": "JsonMappingException",
  "action": "delete-cache-and-fallback-db",
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

---

## 25. Handling Deserialization Error

Jika cache value tidak bisa dibaca:

```text
Redis GET success
  ↓
JSON deserialize error
  ↓
Delete corrupted key
  ↓
Fallback DB
  ↓
Set fresh cache
```

Pseudo-code:

```java
try {
    return cacheStore.getJson(key, CaseSummaryCacheDto.class);
} catch (CacheDeserializationException ex) {
    metrics.cacheDeserializeError("case-summary");
    cacheStore.deleteQuietly(key);
    return Optional.empty();
}
```

Jangan return 500 hanya karena cache corrupt jika DB masih bisa menjawab.

---

## 26. Cache Warmup / Priming

Cache-aside biasanya lazy loading. Redis menjelaskan cache-aside sebagai pola yang mengisi cache saat data diminta, sehingga tidak perlu tahu data mana yang perlu di-cache sejak awal. ([Redis](https://redis.io/tutorials/howtos/solutions/microservices/caching/))

Warmup berguna jika:

```text
- traffic spike predictable
- reference data kecil
- config data sering dipakai
- dashboard populer
```

Jangan warmup semua data besar.

Pattern:

```text
Application startup
  ↓
warmup only small reference/config cache
  ↓
serve traffic
```

Untuk Kubernetes, hati-hati:

```text
10 pods start bersamaan
  ↓
semua warmup
  ↓
DB spike
```

Mitigasi:

```text
- staggered startup
- leader-only warmup
- limit concurrency
- warmup via background job
```

---

## 27. Cache Local + Redis Two-Level

Kadang ada local in-memory cache + Redis.

```text
Request
  ↓
Local cache
  ↓ miss
Redis
  ↓ miss
PostgreSQL
```

Kelebihan:

```text
- latency lebih rendah
- mengurangi Redis load
```

Risiko:

```text
- local cache antar pod inconsistent
- invalidation lebih sulit
- memory per pod naik
```

Gunakan hanya untuk:

```text
- reference/config kecil
- TTL sangat pendek
- data non-sensitive
```

Microsoft juga mencatat bahwa local cache private per application instance bisa cepat inconsistent, sehingga perlu expiry lebih sering atau distributed cache. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/architecture/patterns/cache-aside))

---

## 28. MyBatis-Specific Notes

### Local cache MyBatis

MyBatis local cache bekerja per `SqlSession` dan dibersihkan pada update/commit/rollback/close. Ini bagus untuk nested query dan repeated query dalam session, tetapi bukan distributed cache antar pod. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

### Second-level cache

Second-level cache MyBatis bisa berguna, termasuk dengan Redis extension, tetapi hati-hati:

```text
- scope biasanya mapper/namespace
- invalidation cenderung terkait statement/update mapper
- domain cache dependency bisa lebih kompleks daripada mapper boundary
- sulit memasukkan user permission/policy version jika tidak didesain eksplisit
```

### Rekomendasi

```text
Gunakan MyBatis local cache sebagai behavior internal MyBatis.
Gunakan service-layer Redis cache untuk domain/read-model cache.
Hindari mapper-level Redis cache untuk data yang authorization-sensitive,
multi-tenant complex, atau aggregate dependency-nya rumit.
```

---

## 29. Cache-Aside with MyBatis Example

### Mapper

```java
public interface CaseMapper {
    CaseSummaryRow findSummaryById(
        @Param("tenantId") String tenantId,
        @Param("caseId") String caseId,
        @Param("dataScope") String dataScope
    );

    int updateStatus(
        @Param("tenantId") String tenantId,
        @Param("caseId") String caseId,
        @Param("status") String status,
        @Param("expectedVersion") long expectedVersion
    );
}
```

### Repository

```java
public final class MyBatisCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;

    public Optional<CaseSummaryRow> findSummaryById(
        TenantId tenantId,
        CaseId caseId,
        DataScope dataScope
    ) {
        try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession()) {
            CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);
            return Optional.ofNullable(
                mapper.findSummaryById(
                    tenantId.value(),
                    caseId.value(),
                    dataScope.value()
                )
            );
        }
    }
}
```

### Query Service

```java
public final class CaseQueryService {

    private final CaseRepository repository;
    private final CacheStore cacheStore;
    private final CaseCacheKeys cacheKeys;
    private final CaseCachePolicy cachePolicy;

    public CaseSummaryResponse getSummary(
        TenantId tenantId,
        UserContext user,
        CaseId caseId
    ) {
        String key = cacheKeys.caseSummary(
            tenantId,
            caseId,
            user.policyVersion()
        );

        Optional<CaseSummaryCacheDto> cached =
            cacheStore.getJson(key, CaseSummaryCacheDto.class);

        if (cached.isPresent()) {
            return CaseSummaryResponse.from(cached.get());
        }

        CaseSummaryRow row = repository
            .findSummaryById(tenantId, caseId, user.dataScope())
            .orElseThrow(NotFoundException::new);

        CaseSummaryCacheDto dto = CaseSummaryCacheDto.from(row);

        cacheStore.setJson(
            key,
            dto,
            cachePolicy.caseSummaryTtl()
        );

        return CaseSummaryResponse.from(dto);
    }
}
```

### Command Service

```java
public final class CaseCommandService {

    private final TransactionRunner tx;
    private final CaseRepository repository;
    private final CaseCacheInvalidator cacheInvalidator;

    public void updateStatus(
        TenantId tenantId,
        UserContext user,
        CaseId caseId,
        CaseStatus status,
        long expectedVersion
    ) {
        tx.run(() -> {
            CaseRow current = repository.findForUpdate(tenantId, caseId)
                .orElseThrow(NotFoundException::new);

            authorizationService.assertCanUpdate(user, current);

            repository.updateStatus(
                tenantId,
                caseId,
                status,
                expectedVersion
            );
        });

        cacheInvalidator.invalidateCase(tenantId, caseId);
    }
}
```

---

## 30. Cache Invalidation Example

```java
public final class CaseCacheInvalidator {

    private final CacheStore cacheStore;
    private final CaseCacheKeys cacheKeys;

    public void invalidateCase(TenantId tenantId, CaseId caseId) {
        cacheStore.delete(cacheKeys.caseSummaryAnyPolicy(tenantId, caseId));
        cacheStore.delete(cacheKeys.caseDetailAnyPolicy(tenantId, caseId));

        // For search/list caches:
        // Prefer short TTL, version key, or outbox-driven broad invalidation.
    }
}
```

Catatan: Redis delete wildcard tidak bisa dilakukan dengan `DEL pattern`. Jika butuh hapus banyak key, gunakan index atau SCAN job terkontrol.

---

## 31. Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Kenapa buruk | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Cache semua query MyBatis otomatis | Invalidation dan auth context kabur | Cache eksplisit di service layer |
| Cache key tidak include tenant | Data bocor lintas tenant | Include tenant segment |
| Cache key tidak include permission context | Data bocor lintas user/scope | Include policy/user/scope atau cache projection aman |
| TTL terlalu panjang untuk data volatile | Stale data | Short TTL + invalidation |
| TTL terlalu pendek untuk expensive query | DB tetap berat | TTL + jitter + stampede protection |
| Delete cache sebelum DB commit | Bisa repopulate old value | Commit dulu, lalu invalidate |
| Cache search kompleks tanpa strategy | Key explosion/stale list | Short TTL atau no cache |
| Simpan entity MyBatis mentah | Schema rapuh | Cache DTO |
| Redis error menggagalkan read biasa | Availability turun | Fallback DB |
| Cache corrupt menghasilkan 500 | User impact tidak perlu | Delete cache + fallback DB |
| No metrics hit/miss | Tidak tahu cache efektif | Observability wajib |
| Raw key di metric label | Cardinality/PII risk | Use cache family label |

---

## 32. Decision Matrix: Apa yang Layak Di-cache?

| Data | Cache? | Alasan |
|---|---:|---|
| Case summary read-mostly | Ya | Frequent read, bounded stale |
| Case final decision | Tidak sebagai derived truth | Audit/consistency penting |
| Permission snapshot | Ya, hati-hati | TTL pendek + policy version |
| Search result sangat variatif | Biasanya tidak | Low hit ratio |
| Reference data | Ya | Stable |
| Workflow process state | Tidak | Camunda source |
| External API lookup | Ya | Protect external latency/cost |
| Aggregate dashboard | Ya | Expensive query |
| Payment/order final state | Hati-hati | Harus DB source |
| Feature flag/config | Ya | TTL + version |
| User session | Redis boleh, tapi bukan cache-aside | Ini state store, bukan derived cache |

---

## 33. Senior Engineer Checklist

Sebelum approve cache Redis:

```text
[ ] Source of truth jelas
[ ] Cache key include tenant
[ ] Cache key include auth/policy context jika perlu
[ ] Cache DTO bukan entity mentah
[ ] TTL jelas
[ ] TTL punya jitter jika high-volume
[ ] Negative cache dipakai dengan TTL pendek jika perlu
[ ] Stampede protection untuk hot expensive key
[ ] Write path invalidate setelah DB commit
[ ] Critical invalidation punya outbox/retry
[ ] Redis GET failure fallback DB
[ ] Redis SET failure tidak merusak response
[ ] Deserialization failure delete cache + fallback DB
[ ] Search/list cache punya key explosion control
[ ] Metrics hit/miss/error ada
[ ] Tidak ada raw PII/secret di key/log/metric
[ ] MyBatis local/second-level cache behavior dipahami
```

---

## 34. Ringkasan Praktis

```text
Cache-aside:
  app read Redis
  if hit -> return
  if miss -> read PostgreSQL via MyBatis
  set Redis with TTL
  return

Write path:
  update PostgreSQL first
  commit
  invalidate Redis

Redis cache:
  derived
  disposable
  TTL-aware
  stale-bounded
  observable

PostgreSQL:
  source of truth
  transaction boundary
  consistency boundary
```

Kalimat kunci:

> Cache-aside yang baik bukan hanya mempercepat read. Ia membuat batas yang jelas antara kebenaran data di PostgreSQL dan salinan sementara di Redis.

- [MyBatis 3 | Java API – mybatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html)
