7/13/2026, 6:53:21 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 7 — Cache Invalidation, Stampede Protection & Negative Cache**. Seri ini memperdalam bagian tersulit dari caching: bukan cara mengisi cache, tapi cara memastikan cache tidak menjadi sumber stale data, DB spike, atau bug authorization.

# Seri 7 - Cache Invalidation, Stampede Protection & Negative Cache

Status: **Seri 7 dari 22 seri lanjutan**.  
Setelah seri ini selesai, tersisa **15 seri**.

---

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini memperdalam tiga problem utama dalam Redis caching:

```text
1. Cache invalidation
   Bagaimana memastikan cache tidak terlalu lama stale setelah PostgreSQL berubah.

2. Cache stampede protection
   Bagaimana mencegah banyak request menghantam PostgreSQL bersamaan saat cache miss/expire.

3. Negative cache
   Bagaimana mencegah request terhadap data tidak ada membebani database berulang-ulang.
```

Di aplikasi **Java Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL**, caching yang buruk biasanya bukan gagal karena Redis lambat, tetapi karena:

```text
- cache key tidak memasukkan tenant/auth context
- invalidation tidak sinkron dengan transaksi DB
- TTL terlalu panjang
- hot key expire bersamaan
- cache miss massal menghantam DB
- negative cache menyimpan hasil “not found” terlalu lama
- wildcard deletion dilakukan sembarangan
- tidak ada observability hit/miss/stale
```

Cache-aside sendiri tidak menjamin konsistensi penuh antara cache dan data store; aplikasi tetap bertanggung jawab menangani stale data dan sinkronisasi perubahan data. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/architecture/patterns/cache-aside?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text
PostgreSQL
= source of truth
= final business state
= transaction boundary

Redis cache
= derived read model
= boleh hilang
= boleh stale dalam batas tertentu
= harus punya TTL/invalidation
```

Kalimat paling penting:

```text
Cache invalidation bukan operasi tambahan setelah fitur selesai.
Cache invalidation adalah bagian dari model konsistensi fitur.
```

Kalau fitur Anda tidak bisa menjawab “kapan cache ini menjadi tidak valid?”, berarti fitur itu belum siap di-production-kan.

---

## 3. Diagram Besar

```text
READ PATH
Client
  ↓
JAX-RS Resource
  ↓
Query Service
  ↓
Redis GET
  ├── HIT
  │     ↓
  │   return cached projection
  │
  └── MISS
        ↓
      stampede guard?
        ↓
      MyBatis Repository
        ↓
      PostgreSQL
        ↓
      Redis SET with TTL
        ↓
      return response

WRITE PATH
Client
  ↓
JAX-RS Resource
  ↓
Command Service
  ↓
PostgreSQL transaction via MyBatis
  ↓
commit success
  ↓
invalidate Redis cache
  ↓
return response
```

Default yang aman:

```text
Write PostgreSQL first.
Commit.
Then invalidate Redis.
```

Jika invalidate dilakukan sebelum commit, request lain bisa miss, membaca data lama dari DB, lalu mengisi cache dengan data lama.

---

## 4. Cache Invalidation: Problem Sebenarnya

Misal cache:

```text
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-summary:v1:case:c991
```

Lalu ada update:

```text
case c991 status: OPEN -> CLOSED
```

Cache lama sekarang salah.

Pertanyaannya:

```text
Cache mana saja yang terdampak?
```

Jawabannya bisa lebih dari satu:

```text
- case summary cache
- case detail cache
- case search result cache untuk status OPEN
- case search result cache untuk status CLOSED
- dashboard aggregate
- assignee workload summary
- SLA summary
- permission-filtered projection
- websocket notification state
```

Itulah kenapa invalidation sulit: perubahan satu entity bisa memengaruhi banyak **read model**.

---

## 5. Invalidation Strategy Map

| Strategy | Cara kerja | Cocok untuk | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| **TTL only** | Cache expire sendiri | Data low-risk, read-heavy, stale acceptable | Data bisa stale sampai TTL habis |
| **Delete exact key** | Hapus key spesifik setelah write | Entity by ID | Tidak cukup untuk list/search/aggregate |
| **Versioned key** | Key mengandung version | Permission/config/reference data | Old key orphan sampai TTL |
| **Dependency index** | Simpan mapping entity → cache keys | Aggregate/list yang perlu invalidasi selektif | Index bisa besar/stale |
| **Outbox invalidation** | DB commit menulis event invalidasi, worker hapus Redis | Critical invalidation, Redis down safe | Lebih kompleks |
| **Event-driven invalidation** | Publish event lalu service hapus cache | Cross-service cache | Butuh delivery/retry semantics |
| **No cache** | Jangan cache | Data sangat sensitif/volatile | Performance DB lebih berat |

---

## 6. Strategy 1 - TTL Only

### Pola

```text
Read miss
  ↓
Load DB
  ↓
SET cache EX 60
  ↓
Data stale maksimal sekitar 60 detik
```

Redis `SET` mendukung opsi expiration seperti `EX`, `PX`, `EXAT`, `PXAT`, dan `KEEPTTL`, jadi penulisan cache sebaiknya selalu membawa expiry eksplisit. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/set/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Cocok untuk

```text
- dashboard ringan
- reference data yang tidak terlalu sensitif
- search result yang berubah sering tapi stale acceptable
- aggregate non-critical
```

### Tidak cocok untuk

```text
- authorization decision sensitif
- final payment/order status
- regulatory decision
- workflow state final
- data yang harus read-after-write strongly consistent
```

### Rule

```text
TTL-only adalah consistency contract:
"User mungkin melihat data lama sampai TTL habis."
```

Kalau kontrak itu tidak bisa diterima, jangan pakai TTL-only.

---

## 7. Strategy 2 - Delete Exact Key

### Pola

```text
DB update committed
  ↓
DEL / UNLINK exact cache key
```

Contoh:

```text
UNLINK case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-summary:v1:case:c991
```

Cocok untuk read model by ID:

```text
GET /cases/{caseId}
GET /orders/{orderId}
GET /users/{userId}/profile
```

### Java shape

```java
public final class CaseCacheInvalidator {

    private final CacheStore cacheStore;
    private final CaseCacheKeys keys;

    public void invalidateCaseSummary(TenantId tenantId, CaseId caseId) {
        cacheStore.delete(keys.caseSummary(tenantId, caseId));
        cacheStore.delete(keys.caseDetail(tenantId, caseId));
    }
}
```

### Kelemahan

Delete exact key tidak menyelesaikan:

```text
- list/search result cache
- aggregate dashboard
- permission-specific projection
- multiple policy versions
- multiple page/sort/filter combinations
```

Maka untuk entity by ID, exact delete bagus. Untuk aggregate/list, perlu strategy tambahan.

---

## 8. Strategy 3 - Versioned Key

Versioned key membuat cache lama tidak dipakai lagi ketika version berubah.

### Contoh permission cache

```text
authz-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:permission:v3:user:u123:policy:p17
authz-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:permission:v3:user:u123:policy:p18
```

Ketika policy version naik dari `p17` ke `p18`, aplikasi otomatis membaca key baru. Key lama akan expire natural.

### Cocok untuk

```text
- permission snapshot
- feature flag/config
- reference data
- policy-driven projection
- aggregate dengan aggregateVersion
```

### Pattern dengan DB version

```text
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-detail:v2:case:c991:aggVer:52:policy:p17
```

Jika child entity berubah, `aggregateVersion` dinaikkan di PostgreSQL.

### Kelebihan

```text
- tidak perlu delete semua kombinasi key lama
- rolling deployment lebih aman
- stale cache tidak dipakai jika version source benar
```

### Syarat

```text
- version source harus authoritative
- old key wajib punya TTL
- key explosion harus dimonitor
```

---

## 9. Strategy 4 - Dependency Index

Dependency index menyimpan daftar cache key yang bergantung pada entity tertentu.

### Contoh

```text
Index key:
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:set:cache-index:case:v1:case:c991

Members:
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-summary:v1:case:c991
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-detail:v1:case:c991
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-search:v2:qhash:aaa
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-search:v2:qhash:bbb
```

Saat case berubah:

```text
SMEMBERS indexKey
UNLINK each member
UNLINK indexKey
```

### Cocok untuk

```text
- aggregate page
- search result populer
- dashboard cache
- read model yang dependency-nya bisa dilacak
```

### Risiko

```text
- index bisa tumbuh besar
- index bisa stale
- index harus punya TTL
- cache key bisa hilang tapi index masih menyimpan referensi
- write path lebih berat
```

### Guardrail

```text
Index TTL harus sedikit lebih panjang dari TTL cache yang di-index.
```

Contoh:

```text
case search cache TTL = 60 seconds
dependency index TTL = 120 seconds
```

---

## 10. Strategy 5 - Outbox Invalidation

Problem:

```text
DB commit sukses.
Aplikasi crash sebelum Redis delete.
Cache lama tetap hidup sampai TTL.
```

Untuk data yang invalidation-nya penting, gunakan outbox.

### Flow

```text
Command Service
  ↓
PostgreSQL transaction
  ├── update business table
  └── insert cache_invalidation_outbox
  ↓
commit
  ↓
Outbox worker
  ↓
delete Redis keys
  ↓
mark outbox processed
```

### Table

```sql
CREATE TABLE cache_invalidation_outbox (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    aggregate_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    processed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
    attempt_count INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    last_error TEXT NULL
);
```

### Cocok untuk

```text
- critical cache invalidation
- Redis outage tolerance
- cross-service invalidation
- compliance-sensitive stale risk
```

### Worker behavior

```text
Read unprocessed outbox rows
  ↓
Delete/invalidate Redis
  ↓
Mark processed
```

Jika Redis down, event tetap ada di PostgreSQL dan bisa retry.

---

## 11. Strategy 6 - Broad SCAN-Based Cleanup

Untuk admin/incident/migration:

```text
SCAN MATCH case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-search:v2:* COUNT 1000
UNLINK matched keys
```

Redis `SCAN` melakukan incremental iteration atas keyspace, sedangkan `SSCAN`, `HSCAN`, dan `ZSCAN` melakukan iterasi incremental untuk Set, Hash, dan Sorted Set. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/scan/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Jangan lakukan

```text
KEYS case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:*
```

`KEYS` di production berisiko memblokir Redis pada dataset besar. Pakai SCAN untuk operasi bertahap.

### Guardrail

```text
- dry-run count/sample dulu
- limit COUNT
- gunakan UNLINK untuk deletion besar
- rate limit cleanup job
- log pattern yang dihapus
- jangan jalankan dari request path
```

---

## 12. Race Condition: Delete After Commit Masih Bisa Stale

Sequence:

```text
T1 read cache miss
T1 read DB old value

T2 update DB
T2 commit
T2 delete cache

T1 set cache old value
```

Hasil: cache berisi old value lagi.

Mitigasi:

### A. Short TTL

Mengurangi durasi stale.

### B. Cache value membawa DB version

Saat set cache, bandingkan version jika memungkinkan.

```json
{
  "caseId": "c991",
  "status": "OPEN",
  "dbVersion": 17,
  "cachedAt": "..."
}
```

### C. Versioned key

Jika key memasukkan `dbVersion` atau `aggregateVersion`, old value tidak menimpa key baru.

```text
case-detail:case:c991:aggVer:17
case-detail:case:c991:aggVer:18
```

### D. Delayed double delete

Pola:

```text
commit
delete cache
wait small delay
delete cache again
```

Caveat: ini best-effort dan menambah kompleksitas. Untuk correctness tinggi, lebih baik versioned key/outbox.

---

## 13. Cache Stampede

Cache stampede terjadi saat hot key expire/miss dan banyak request melakukan DB load bersamaan.

```text
Hot cache key expires
  ↓
1000 concurrent requests
  ↓
1000 Redis misses
  ↓
1000 PostgreSQL queries
  ↓
DB spike / timeout cascade
```

Ini sering muncul pada:

```text
- dashboard populer
- permission cache massal
- reference data global
- product/catalog cache
- case summary yang sering dibuka banyak user
```

---

## 14. Stampede Protection Strategy Map

| Strategy | Cara kerja | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|
| TTL jitter | Sebarkan waktu expiry | Banyak key dengan TTL sama |
| Lock-on-miss | Satu request rebuild, lainnya menunggu/fallback | Hot expensive key |
| Double-check after lock | Setelah dapat lock, cek cache lagi | Semua lock-on-miss |
| Stale-while-revalidate | Return stale sementara refresh async | Dashboard/reference data |
| Early refresh | Refresh sebelum expire | Hot key |
| Request coalescing local | Gabungkan request dalam satu pod | High concurrency per pod |
| Pre-warm | Isi cache sebelum traffic | Reference/hot data predictable |
| No cache for low-hit key | Hindari overhead | Query sangat variatif |

---

## 15. TTL Jitter

Tanpa jitter:

```text
100.000 key dibuat jam 10:00
TTL 300 detik
semua expire sekitar 10:05
```

Dengan jitter:

```text
TTL = 300s + random(0..60s)
```

### Java

```java
public Duration ttlWithJitter(Duration base, Duration maxJitter) {
    long jitterMs = ThreadLocalRandom.current()
        .nextLong(maxJitter.toMillis() + 1);

    return base.plusMillis(jitterMs);
}
```

### Cocok untuk

```text
- cache-aside result
- reference data
- aggregate cache
- permission snapshot
```

### Jangan untuk

```text
- OTP
- strict token expiry
- lock lease
- exact rate limit window
```

---

## 16. Lock-on-Miss

### Flow

```text
Cache miss
  ↓
Try acquire rebuild lock
  ├── success
  │     ↓
  │   double-check cache
  │     ↓
  │   load PostgreSQL
  │     ↓
  │   set cache
  │     ↓
  │   release lock
  │
  └── fail
        ↓
      wait briefly
        ↓
      retry cache
        ↓
      fallback DB or return stale
```

### Lock command

```text
SET lockKey token NX PX 10000
```

Redis `SET` mendukung kombinasi conditional set seperti `NX` dan expiry seperti `PX`, sehingga cocok untuk lock token sederhana dengan lease time eksplisit. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/set/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Safe release

Jangan:

```text
DEL lockKey
```

Karena bisa menghapus lock milik owner lain jika lease expired lalu owner baru sudah acquire.

Gunakan compare token via Lua:

```lua
if redis.call("GET", KEYS[1]) == ARGV[1] then
  return redis.call("DEL", KEYS[1])
else
  return 0
end
```

---

## 17. Lock-on-Miss Java Shape

```java
public CaseSummaryResponse getCaseSummary(TenantId tenantId, CaseId caseId) {
    String cacheKey = keys.caseSummary(tenantId, caseId);

    Optional<CaseSummaryCacheDto> cached =
        cacheStore.getJson(cacheKey, CaseSummaryCacheDto.class);

    if (cached.isPresent()) {
        return mapper.toResponse(cached.get());
    }

    String lockKey = keys.cacheRebuildLock(cacheKey);

    Optional<LockToken> token = lockService.tryAcquire(
        lockKey,
        Duration.ofMillis(50),
        Duration.ofSeconds(10)
    );

    if (token.isPresent()) {
        try {
            Optional<CaseSummaryCacheDto> secondCheck =
                cacheStore.getJson(cacheKey, CaseSummaryCacheDto.class);

            if (secondCheck.isPresent()) {
                return mapper.toResponse(secondCheck.get());
            }

            CaseSummaryCacheDto dto = loadFromDb(tenantId, caseId);

            cacheStore.setJson(
                cacheKey,
                dto,
                ttlPolicy.caseSummaryTtlWithJitter()
            );

            return mapper.toResponse(dto);

        } finally {
            lockService.release(lockKey, token.get());
        }
    }

    sleepSmallJitter();

    Optional<CaseSummaryCacheDto> afterWait =
        cacheStore.getJson(cacheKey, CaseSummaryCacheDto.class);

    if (afterWait.isPresent()) {
        return mapper.toResponse(afterWait.get());
    }

    return mapper.toResponse(loadFromDb(tenantId, caseId));
}
```

### Guardrail

```text
- lock wait time harus pendek
- lease time harus bounded
- double-check cache setelah lock acquired
- fallback behavior jelas
- jangan block request thread terlalu lama
```

---

## 18. Stale-While-Revalidate

Stale-while-revalidate memisahkan:

```text
fresh window
stale-but-servable window
expired window
```

### Value shape

```json
{
  "schemaVersion": 1,
  "payload": {
    "caseId": "c991",
    "status": "OPEN"
  },
  "freshUntil": "2026-07-13T10:05:00+07:00",
  "staleUntil": "2026-07-13T10:10:00+07:00",
  "dbVersion": 17,
  "cachedAt": "2026-07-13T10:00:00+07:00"
}
```

### Flow

```text
Cache hit and now < freshUntil
  -> return fresh

Cache hit and freshUntil <= now < staleUntil
  -> return stale
  -> trigger async refresh guarded by lock

Cache hit but now >= staleUntil
  -> treat as miss
  -> synchronous reload or fail/fallback

Cache miss
  -> reload
```

### Cocok untuk

```text
- dashboard
- reports
- reference data
- expensive aggregate
- low-risk list/search
```

### Tidak cocok untuk

```text
- permission revocation
- security/session validity
- final payment status
- regulatory decision state
```

---

## 19. Early Refresh

Early refresh mencegah hot key expire saat traffic tinggi.

### Pattern

```text
TTL = 10 minutes
If remaining TTL < 1 minute
  maybe trigger async refresh
```

Redis `EXPIRE` mendukung opsi seperti `NX`, `XX`, `GT`, dan `LT`, yang berguna saat ingin mengubah expiry hanya dalam kondisi tertentu. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/set/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Pseudocode

```java
if (cached.isPresent() && cached.remainingTtl().compareTo(Duration.ofMinutes(1)) < 0) {
    refreshExecutor.trySubmit(() -> refreshWithLock(cacheKey));
}

return cachedValue;
```

### Guardrail

```text
- refresh async harus rate-limited
- tetap pakai rebuild lock
- jangan semua request memicu refresh
- gunakan probabilistic refresh untuk hot key
```

---

## 20. Negative Cache

Negative cache menyimpan hasil “tidak ditemukan” agar random/malicious repeated lookup tidak terus menghantam PostgreSQL.

### Problem

```text
GET /cases/random-id-1
  ↓
Redis miss
  ↓
PostgreSQL miss
  ↓
return 404

Repeated random IDs
  ↓
DB terus bekerja
```

### Pattern

```text
SET case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:neg:case:v1:case:c999 "__NULL__" EX 60
```

### Flow

```text
Check positive cache
  ↓ miss
Check negative cache
  ├── hit -> return 404
  └── miss -> query DB
             ├── found -> set positive cache
             └── not found -> set negative cache short TTL
```

---

## 21. Negative Cache Java Shape

```java
public CaseSummaryResponse getCaseSummary(TenantId tenantId, CaseId caseId) {
    String positiveKey = keys.caseSummary(tenantId, caseId);
    String negativeKey = keys.caseSummaryNegative(tenantId, caseId);

    Optional<CaseSummaryCacheDto> positive =
        cacheStore.getJson(positiveKey, CaseSummaryCacheDto.class);

    if (positive.isPresent()) {
        return mapper.toResponse(positive.get());
    }

    if (cacheStore.exists(negativeKey)) {
        throw new NotFoundException();
    }

    Optional<CaseSummaryRow> row =
        repository.findSummaryById(tenantId, caseId);

    if (row.isEmpty()) {
        cacheStore.setString(
            negativeKey,
            "__NULL__",
            Duration.ofSeconds(60)
        );
        throw new NotFoundException();
    }

    CaseSummaryCacheDto dto = CaseSummaryCacheDto.from(row.get());

    cacheStore.setJson(
        positiveKey,
        dto,
        ttlPolicy.caseSummaryTtl()
    );

    return mapper.toResponse(dto);
}
```

---

## 22. Negative Cache Guardrail

| Risk | Contoh | Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|
| Entity dibuat setelah negative cache | Create case `c999`, tapi negative cache masih ada | Delete negative key saat create |
| Authorization denial dicache sebagai not found | User dapat 404 walau permission berubah | TTL sangat pendek, include policy context |
| Attack random ID membuat banyak negative key | Memory bengkak | TTL pendek, rate limit, key cap |
| Sensitive ID muncul di key | PII leak | Hash identifier |
| DB outage dicache sebagai not found | Salah secara fatal | Jangan negative-cache dependency failure |

### Rule

```text
Negative cache hanya untuk "confirmed not found",
bukan untuk "DB timeout", "permission denied", atau "upstream error".
```

---

## 23. Positive vs Negative Cache Key

Contoh:

```text
Positive:
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:case-summary:v1:case:c991

Negative:
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:neg:case-summary:v1:case:c991
```

Kenapa dipisah?

```text
- observability lebih jelas
- TTL bisa berbeda
- invalidation lebih mudah
- menghindari sentinel value bercampur dengan real payload
```

TTL:

```text
Positive cache: 1-5 menit
Negative cache: 15-60 detik
```

---

## 24. Cache Invalidation untuk Create

Create flow khusus karena perlu menghapus negative cache.

```text
POST /cases c991
  ↓
DB insert commit
  ↓
delete negative cache for c991
  ↓
delete/list invalidation if relevant
```

Pseudo-code:

```java
public void createCase(CreateCaseCommand command) {
    transaction.run(() -> {
        repository.insert(command);
        auditRepository.insert(...);
    });

    cacheStore.delete(keys.caseSummaryNegative(command.tenantId(), command.caseId()));
    cacheInvalidator.invalidateCaseLists(command.tenantId());
}
```

Kalau tidak, request setelah create bisa tetap menerima 404 dari negative cache.

---

## 25. Cache Invalidation untuk Update

Update flow:

```text
DB update commit
  ↓
delete exact positive cache
  ↓
delete aggregate/list cache or bump version
  ↓
optionally publish invalidation event
```

Contoh:

```java
public void updateCaseStatus(UpdateCaseStatusCommand command) {
    transaction.run(() -> {
        repository.updateStatus(command);
        outbox.insert(CacheInvalidationEvent.caseUpdated(command.caseId()));
    });

    cacheInvalidator.invalidateCase(command.tenantId(), command.caseId());
}
```

Untuk critical invalidation, jangan hanya delete inline. Gunakan outbox agar retryable jika Redis sedang down.

---

## 26. Cache Invalidation untuk Delete

Delete flow:

```text
DB delete/soft-delete commit
  ↓
delete positive cache
  ↓
set or delete negative cache?
```

Pilihan:

### A. Delete positive only

Request berikutnya miss lalu DB not found lalu negative cache terisi.

### B. Set negative cache langsung

```text
SET negKey "__NULL__" EX 60
```

Cocok jika delete langsung diikuti banyak read terhadap id sama.

Guardrail:

```text
Untuk soft-delete, pastikan query DB dan cache projection konsisten:
deleted entity tidak boleh muncul sebagai active.
```

---

## 27. Cache Invalidation untuk List/Search

List/search cache paling sulit.

### Problem

```text
Case c991 berubah OPEN -> CLOSED.

Terdampak:
- search status=OPEN
- search status=CLOSED
- dashboard open count
- assignee queue
- SLA queue
```

### Option A - Short TTL only

```text
Search cache TTL = 30-60 detik
```

Cocok jika stale list acceptable.

### Option B - Cache IDs only

```json
{
  "caseIds": ["c991", "c992", "c993"],
  "cachedAt": "...",
  "queryHash": "..."
}
```

Detail tetap diambil dari per-ID cache/DB. Ini mengurangi stale detail, walau membership list masih bisa stale.

### Option C - Dependency index

Saat search result berisi `c991`, masukkan search key ke index `case:c991`.

### Option D - Aggregate version

Search key memasukkan version dari domain/query scope.

```text
case-search:v2:scopeVersion:891:qhash:abc
```

### Option E - Jangan cache search

Sering ini keputusan paling benar jika query terlalu variatif.

---

## 28. Permission/Authz Cache Invalidation

Permission cache rawan stale.

### Pattern

```text
authz-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:permission:v3:user:u123:policy:p17
```

Ketika policy berubah:

```text
policyVersion p17 -> p18
```

Key lama tidak dipakai.

### Guardrail

```text
- TTL pendek
- include policy version
- include tenant
- include user/role/scope
- explicit invalidation saat role/permission berubah
- fallback ke policy DB/service
```

Jangan cache authorization result dengan key terlalu umum:

```text
BAD:
canApprove:user:u123 = true

BETTER:
authz-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:decision:v1:user:u123:policy:p17:resource:case:c991:action:approve
```

Namun decision-level cache bisa meledak jumlahnya. Lebih umum: cache permission snapshot, bukan setiap decision.

---

## 29. Local Cache + Redis Invalidation

Jika ada local in-memory cache per pod:

```text
Pod local cache
  ↓
Redis
  ↓
PostgreSQL
```

Invalidation harus menghapus:

```text
- local cache di semua pod
- Redis cache
```

Pattern umum:

```text
DB commit
  ↓
delete Redis cache
  ↓
publish invalidation signal
  ↓
all pods delete local cache
```

Redis Pub/Sub boleh dipakai untuk local cache invalidation signal, tetapi ingat Pub/Sub bukan durable. Jika pod disconnect, invalidation signal bisa hilang.

Untuk local cache, mitigasi:

```text
- TTL local sangat pendek
- local cache hanya untuk low-risk data
- Redis tetap source shared cache
- local invalidation signal best-effort
```

---

## 30. Keyspace Notification Bukan Pengganti Invalidation

Redis keyspace notification bisa memberi tahu event seperti key expired/deleted, tetapi jangan jadikan mekanisme utama untuk business invalidation.

Kenapa?

```text
- berbasis Pub/Sub
- event bisa hilang saat subscriber disconnect
- disabled by default di Redis
- menambah CPU overhead
```

Pakai untuk:

```text
- cleanup ringan
- local cache reaction
- monitoring/debug
- best-effort expired notification
```

Jangan untuk:

```text
- audit event
- workflow state transition
- guaranteed delayed job
- permission revocation final
```

---

## 31. Invalidation dan Redis Cluster

Di Redis Cluster, multi-key operation bisa bermasalah jika key berada di hash slot berbeda. Untuk dependency index yang perlu operasi multi-key atomik, pertimbangkan hash tag.

Contoh:

```text
case-svc:prod:{tenant:t1:case:c991}:cache:case-detail:v1
case-svc:prod:{tenant:t1:case:c991}:set:cache-index:v1
```

Guardrail:

```text
- jangan pakai hash tag terlalu luas seperti {tenant:t1} untuk semua key
- hanya co-locate key yang perlu operasi multi-key
- hindari hot slot
```

---

## 32. Redis Command Choices

| Purpose | Command |
|---|---|
| Set cache with TTL | `SET key value EX seconds` |
| Set lock | `SET lockKey token NX PX ms` |
| Preserve TTL on overwrite | `SET key value KEEPTTL` |
| Set TTL only if needed | `EXPIRE key seconds NX/XX/GT/LT` |
| Delete small key | `DEL key` |
| Delete many/large keys | `UNLINK key...` |
| Iterate keyspace | `SCAN` |
| Index members | `SADD`, `SMEMBERS`, `SREM` |
| Delayed expiry per item | `ZADD`, `ZRANGEBYSCORE`, `ZREM` |

Redis `EXPIRE` options `NX`, `XX`, `GT`, and `LT` are useful when expiry mutation itself must be conditional. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/set/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 33. Failure Policy

| Operation | Redis failure behavior |
|---|---|
| Cache GET | Fallback DB |
| Cache SET after DB read | Return DB result; log/metric warning |
| Inline invalidation after write | Retry briefly; if critical, write outbox |
| Outbox invalidation | Retry later |
| Stampede lock acquire | Short wait; fallback DB/stale |
| Negative cache SET | Ignore if fail |
| Negative cache GET | Fallback DB |
| Local invalidation Pub/Sub | Rely on short TTL fallback |

Rule:

```text
Cache failure should reduce performance, not corrupt business state.
```

Exception: jika Redis cache dipakai sebagai security control plane, misalnya session/token/rate-limit critical, itu bukan sekadar cache dan harus punya fail-closed/fallback policy sendiri.

---

## 34. Observability

Metrics wajib:

```text
cache.hit.count
cache.miss.count
cache.negative.hit.count
cache.negative.set.count
cache.set.error.count
cache.delete.count
cache.delete.error.count
cache.deserialize.error.count
cache.fallback_db.count
cache.stampede.lock_acquired.count
cache.stampede.lock_timeout.count
cache.stale_served.count
cache.refresh_async.count
cache.refresh_async.error.count
cache.outbox.pending.count
cache.outbox.retry.count
```

Labels aman:

```text
service
env
cache_family
operation
result
exception_class
```

Jangan pakai raw Redis key sebagai metric label.

### Log contoh

```json
{
  "event": "cache_invalidation_failed",
  "cacheFamily": "case-summary",
  "tenantHash": "t-hash",
  "entityType": "case",
  "entityIdHash": "case-hash",
  "exception": "RedisTimeoutException",
  "recovery": "outbox_retry",
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

---

## 35. Testing Matrix

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Cache hit returns cached DTO | Basic correctness |
| Cache miss loads DB and sets Redis | Cache-aside happy path |
| Redis GET timeout falls back DB | Availability |
| Redis SET fails after DB read | Response tetap sukses |
| Update invalidates exact key | Stale reduction |
| Create deletes negative cache | Avoid false 404 |
| Delete sets/deletes appropriate cache | Delete consistency |
| Concurrent miss only one DB load | Stampede guard |
| Lock owner release safe | Avoid deleting other lock |
| Negative cache TTL expires | Avoid permanent false not found |
| Permission version changes key | Authz stale control |
| Outbox retries invalidation | Redis outage recovery |
| Deserialization error deletes cache | Self-healing corrupt cache |
| SCAN cleanup dry-run | Operational safety |

---

## 36. Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| TTL panjang tanpa invalidation | Stale data lama | Delete-on-write/version key |
| Invalidate sebelum DB commit | Cache bisa diisi data lama | Commit dulu |
| Cache search kompleks tanpa TTL pendek | List stale/key explosion | TTL pendek/no cache |
| Negative-cache DB timeout | False 404 | Negative cache hanya confirmed not found |
| Lock-on-miss tanpa safe release | Hapus lock owner lain | Token + Lua compare-delete |
| Semua request menunggu lock lama | Thread starvation | Wait pendek + fallback |
| Pub/Sub untuk guaranteed invalidation | Event bisa hilang | Outbox/Kafka/DB version |
| `KEYS *` untuk cleanup | Redis freeze | SCAN + UNLINK |
| Raw key di logs | PII/cardinality risk | Hash/mask |
| Cache DTO tanpa version | Deserialization brittle | schemaVersion |
| No metrics | Tidak tahu cache efektif | Hit/miss/error metrics |
| Local cache tanpa TTL | Stale antar pod | TTL pendek + signal |

---

## 37. Senior Engineer Checklist

Sebelum approve cache invalidation design:

```text
[ ] Source of truth jelas
[ ] Cache key include tenant/env/policy context
[ ] TTL jelas dan justified
[ ] Invalidation terjadi setelah DB commit
[ ] Critical invalidation punya outbox/retry
[ ] Search/list cache punya stale strategy
[ ] Negative cache hanya untuk confirmed not found
[ ] Negative cache TTL pendek
[ ] Create flow menghapus negative cache
[ ] Stampede protection untuk hot expensive key
[ ] Lock release verify ownership token
[ ] Jitter dipakai untuk high-volume TTL
[ ] Stale-while-revalidate hanya untuk data low-risk
[ ] Redis failure behavior jelas
[ ] SCAN cleanup tidak di request path
[ ] Metrics/logging cukup untuk incident
```

---

## 38. Ringkasan Praktis

```text
Cache invalidation:
  - TTL only untuk low-risk stale data
  - delete exact key untuk entity by ID
  - versioned key untuk policy/config/aggregate version
  - dependency index untuk selective invalidation
  - outbox untuk critical retryable invalidation

Stampede protection:
  - TTL jitter
  - lock-on-miss
  - double-check after lock
  - stale-while-revalidate
  - early refresh
  - bounded fallback

Negative cache:
  - hanya confirmed not found
  - TTL pendek
  - delete saat create
  - jangan untuk DB timeout/permission denied
```

Kalimat kunci:

> Cache yang baik bukan cache yang selalu hit. Cache yang baik adalah cache yang tetap benar, bounded stale, observable, dan tidak menjatuhkan database saat miss massal.

## Response:
7/13/2026, 6:56:21 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 8 — Keyspace Notification Pattern**. Fokusnya: kapan event expired/delete Redis berguna, kenapa tidak boleh dianggap reliable event bus, dan bagaimana membuat fallback reconciliation agar sistem tetap benar.

# Seri 8 - Keyspace Notification Pattern

Status: **Seri 8 dari 22 seri lanjutan**.  
Setelah seri ini selesai, tersisa **14 seri**.

---

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

**Redis Keyspace Notification** memungkinkan aplikasi subscribe ke event perubahan key Redis, misalnya:

```text
- key expired
- key deleted
- key evicted
- key overwritten
- key created
- command tertentu seperti SET, DEL, EXPIRE, HSET, LPUSH
```

Di microservice Java Jersey/JAX-RS, pattern ini berguna untuk:

```text
- local cache invalidation antar pod
- reaction terhadap expired temporary key
- lightweight timeout signal
- cleanup best-effort
- observability/debug event Redis
- WebSocket notification trigger
- worker wake-up signal
```

Tapi batasnya sangat penting: **keyspace notification bukan durable event bus**. Redis mengimplementasikan keyspace notification lewat Pub/Sub; jika subscriber disconnect, event yang terjadi selama disconnect hilang. Redis docs menyatakan ini secara eksplisit. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/pubsub/keyspace-notifications/))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text
Redis Keyspace Notification
= event signal dari perubahan Redis key
= berbasis Pub/Sub
= realtime-ish
= best-effort
= at-most-once
= tidak durable
= tidak replayable
= tidak cocok untuk audit/event bisnis final
```

Gunakan keyspace notification sebagai **signal**, bukan sebagai **source of truth**.

```text
Benar:
  "Ada key expired, coba lakukan cleanup / refresh / notify."

Salah:
  "Key expired berarti workflow bisnis harus final berubah status."
```

Redis Pub/Sub punya delivery semantic **at-most-once**: message dikirim sekali jika berhasil dikirim; kalau subscriber error atau disconnect, message hilang permanen. Untuk guarantee lebih kuat, Redis docs menyarankan melihat Redis Streams karena Streams persisted dan mendukung delivery semantics yang lebih kuat. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/pubsub/))

---

## 3. Diagram

```text
Redis
│
├── Key changed
│   ├── SET
│   ├── DEL
│   ├── EXPIRE
│   ├── expired
│   ├── evicted
│   └── overwritten
│
├── Keyspace Notification Engine
│   └── publish event to Pub/Sub channel
│
├── Channels
│   ├── __keyspace@0__:<key>
│   │     └── message = event name
│   │
│   └── __keyevent@0__:<event>
│         └── message = key name
│
└── Java Microservice Subscriber
    ├── dedicated Redis Pub/Sub connection
    ├── event parser
    ├── pattern filter
    ├── handler dispatch
    ├── idempotent reaction
    └── fallback reconciliation
```

---

## 4. Keyspace vs Keyevent

Redis menghasilkan dua jenis channel untuk operasi yang memengaruhi keyspace.

Contoh operasi:

```text
DEL mykey
```

Redis dapat menghasilkan dua message konseptual:

```text
PUBLISH __keyspace@0__:mykey del
PUBLISH __keyevent@0__:del mykey
```

Pada channel **keyspace**, nama channel berisi key, dan message berisi nama event. Pada channel **keyevent**, nama channel berisi event, dan message berisi nama key. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/pubsub/keyspace-notifications/))

| Jenis | Channel | Message | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|---|
| Keyspace | `__keyspace@0__:<key>` | event name, misalnya `expired` | Monitor event untuk key tertentu |
| Keyevent | `__keyevent@0__:<event>` | key name | Monitor semua key yang mengalami event tertentu |

Untuk aplikasi biasanya lebih praktis subscribe ke **keyevent**:

```text
__keyevent@0__:expired
__keyevent@0__:del
__keyevent@0__:evicted
```

Karena handler biasanya ingin tahu “key apa yang expired/deleted”.

---

## 5. Configuration: `notify-keyspace-events`

Secara default, Redis menonaktifkan keyspace notifications karena fitur ini memakai CPU tambahan. Redis mengaktifkannya lewat parameter `notify-keyspace-events` di `redis.conf` atau `CONFIG SET`. Minimal harus ada `K` atau `E`, kalau tidak event tidak dikirim walaupun flag lain diset. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/pubsub/keyspace-notifications/))

### Flag penting

| Flag | Arti |
|---|---|
| `K` | Keyspace events, channel `__keyspace@<db>__:<key>` |
| `E` | Keyevent events, channel `__keyevent@<db>__:<event>` |
| `g` | Generic commands seperti `DEL`, `EXPIRE`, `RENAME` |
| `$` | String commands |
| `l` | List commands |
| `s` | Set commands |
| `h` | Hash commands |
| `z` | Sorted set commands |
| `t` | Stream commands |
| `x` | Expired events |
| `e` | Evicted events |
| `m` | Key miss events |
| `n` | New key events |
| `o` | Overwritten events |
| `c` | Type-changed events |
| `A` | Alias untuk sebagian besar event class, kecuali beberapa event seperti miss/new/overwrite/type-change |

### Contoh konfigurasi

Expired events saja, memakai keyevent channel:

```text
notify-keyspace-events Ex
```

Expired + deleted/generic events:

```text
notify-keyspace-events Exg
```

Banyak event umum untuk observability/dev:

```text
notify-keyspace-events KEA
```

Untuk production, jangan asal pakai `KEA` kalau hanya butuh expired event. Aktifkan event sesedikit mungkin.

---

## 6. Event Expired Tidak Selalu Tepat Saat TTL = 0

Ini detail penting. Redis tidak menjamin `expired` event muncul tepat di detik TTL mencapai nol. Redis menghapus key expired ketika key diakses dan ditemukan expired, atau lewat background system yang mencari key expired secara incremental. Jadi bisa ada delay antara TTL menjadi nol dan event `expired` benar-benar dikirim. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/pubsub/keyspace-notifications/))

Artinya:

```text
TTL reaches zero at 10:00:00
expired event may be generated at 10:00:00+
```

Jangan gunakan keyspace notification untuk scheduler yang butuh presisi waktu tinggi atau guarantee kuat.

---

## 7. Redis Cluster Caveat

Dalam Redis Cluster, keyspace event bersifat **node-specific**. Redis docs menjelaskan setiap node menghasilkan event untuk subset keyspace miliknya sendiri, dan event keyspace tidak dibroadcast ke semua node. Untuk menerima semua keyspace events di cluster, client perlu subscribe ke setiap node. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/pubsub/keyspace-notifications/))

Implikasinya:

```text
Single-node Redis:
  satu subscriber cukup

Redis Cluster:
  perlu subscriber per node/master shard
  atau gunakan Redis client/library yang memang mendukung subscription per node
```

Jangan deploy keyspace notification di cluster lalu hanya subscribe ke satu node dan menganggap semua event tertangkap.

---

## 8. Use Case yang Cocok

| Use case | Cocok? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Local cache invalidation | Ya | Best-effort, TTL local tetap pendek |
| Expired session cleanup signal | Ya | Jangan jadi satu-satunya cleanup |
| WebSocket notification trigger | Ya | Kalau message loss acceptable |
| Presence offline signal | Ya, hati-hati | Harus ada heartbeat/reconciliation |
| Temporary UI reminder | Ya | Untuk best-effort notification |
| Cache debug/observability | Ya | Jangan aktifkan event berlebihan |
| Worker wake-up signal | Ya | Worker tetap harus scan source |
| Audit event | Tidak | Gunakan DB/Kafka |
| Payment timeout finalization | Tidak | Gunakan DB scheduler/Camunda/Kafka |
| Workflow transition final | Tidak | Gunakan Camunda/job/outbox |
| Durable delayed job | Tidak sebagai satu-satunya mekanisme | Gunakan ZSet scheduler/Streams/DB job |

---

## 9. Use Case 1 - Local Cache Invalidation

Scenario:

```text
Pod A update reference data
  ↓
Pod A delete Redis cache
  ↓
Redis emits del event
  ↓
Pod B/C/D receive event
  ↓
Pod B/C/D delete local in-memory cache
```

Flow:

```text
PostgreSQL commit
  ↓
UNLINK config-svc:prod:tenant:t1:cache:feature:v1:feature:f1
  ↓
Keyevent del notification
  ↓
Subscriber in every pod receives key
  ↓
localCache.invalidate(key)
```

Caveat:

```text
Kalau Pod B disconnect saat event terjadi,
Pod B tidak menerima invalidation.
```

Maka local cache tetap wajib punya TTL pendek.

Recommended design:

```text
Redis shared cache TTL: 5-30 minutes
Local in-memory cache TTL: 5-60 seconds
Keyspace notification: best-effort immediate invalidation
```

---

## 10. Use Case 2 - Expired Temporary Key Reaction

Scenario:

```text
Set temporary key:
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:timeout:review-reminder:v1:case:c991
TTL 15 minutes

When expired:
  send reminder notification / enqueue lightweight work
```

Key:

```text
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:timeout:review-reminder:v1:case:c991
```

Subscribe:

```text
PSUBSCRIBE __keyevent@0__:expired
```

Handler:

```text
if key matches case-svc:*:timeout:review-reminder:*
  parse tenant/caseId
  check PostgreSQL current state
  if still due:
    send reminder / enqueue job
```

Critical guardrail:

```text
Expired event only triggers a check.
It does not directly mutate final business state.
```

---

## 11. Use Case 3 - Presence Offline Signal

Pattern:

```text
realtime-svc:prod:tenant:t1:presence:v1:user:u123:device:d456 = online
TTL 60s
```

Client heartbeat refreshes key every 20 seconds. If heartbeat stops, key expires, and keyspace notification can trigger “device maybe offline”.

Flow:

```text
Heartbeat SET presence key EX 60
  ↓
Client disappears
  ↓
Key expires
  ↓
expired event
  ↓
subscriber marks local websocket state as maybe offline
  ↓
checks current presence keys before final offline event
```

Guardrail:

```text
Before announcing offline, check whether another device/session/presence key exists.
```

Reason:

```text
- expired event may be delayed
- user may have reconnected with new device key
- event may arrive after state already changed
```

---

## 12. Use Case 4 - Worker Wake-Up Signal

Keyspace notification can wake a worker, but should not be the worker’s only source of truth.

Example:

```text
ZSET scheduler contains due jobs
Keyspace event wakes worker
Worker scans ZSET for due jobs
```

Better flow:

```text
Producer writes due job into ZSET/DB/Stream
  ↓
Producer optionally writes short TTL wake-up key
  ↓
expired event wakes worker
  ↓
worker scans durable-ish source
```

Do not do this:

```text
expired event itself contains the only job
```

Because if the event is missed, the job is lost.

---

## 13. Pattern: Event as Trigger, DB/Redis Source as Truth

Safe architecture:

```text
Keyspace notification
  ↓
Trigger handler
  ↓
Re-read source of truth
  ├── PostgreSQL
  ├── Redis ZSET
  ├── Redis Stream
  ├── Camunda
  └── durable outbox
  ↓
Decide action
```

Unsafe architecture:

```text
Keyspace notification
  ↓
Directly perform irreversible business action
```

Rule:

```text
Keyspace event may start a workflow.
It should not be the only proof that workflow must happen.
```

---

## 14. Java/Jersey Architecture

Keyspace notification listener bukan bagian dari request path.

```text
Jersey Application
│
├── JAX-RS Resource
│   └── handles HTTP
│
├── Application Service
│   └── business use cases
│
├── Redis Adapter
│   └── command operations
│
└── Redis Keyspace Listener
    ├── dedicated Pub/Sub connection
    ├── pattern subscription
    ├── event parser
    ├── handler registry
    ├── bounded executor
    ├── reconciliation job
    └── metrics
```

Jangan subscribe dari `Resource` class. Listener harus dikelola sebagai lifecycle component saat aplikasi start/stop.

---

## 15. Dedicated Connection Rule

Redis Pub/Sub subscription connection tidak boleh dicampur dengan command biasa di request path. Redis Pub/Sub docs menjelaskan client dalam subscribed mode memiliki batasan command, dan subscriber menerima pushed messages dari Redis. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/pubsub/))

Gunakan:

```text
Redis command connection:
  GET, SET, HSET, EVAL, DEL

Redis Pub/Sub connection:
  PSUBSCRIBE __keyevent@0__:expired
```

Dalam Lettuce/Jedis/Redisson, buat listener connection terpisah dari pool request command.

---

## 16. Listener Lifecycle

```text
Application startup
  ↓
Create Redis command client
  ↓
Create dedicated Pub/Sub client/connection
  ↓
Subscribe to configured channels/patterns
  ↓
Mark listener healthy
  ↓
Process events
  ↓
On disconnect:
    mark listener degraded
    reconnect with backoff
    run reconciliation
  ↓
Application shutdown:
    unsubscribe
    stop executor
    close Pub/Sub connection
    close Redis client
```

State machine:

```text
NOT_STARTED
  ↓
CONNECTING
  ↓
SUBSCRIBED
  ↓
RUNNING
  ↕
DEGRADED / RECONNECTING
  ↓
STOPPING
  ↓
STOPPED
```

---

## 17. Event Handler Design

Jangan satu handler melakukan semua hal dengan `if` panjang.

Lebih baik:

```text
KeyspaceNotificationDispatcher
├── CacheInvalidationHandler
├── PresenceExpiredHandler
├── ReminderTimeoutHandler
├── DebugLoggingHandler
└── MetricsHandler
```

Interface:

```java
public interface KeyspaceEventHandler {
    boolean supports(KeyspaceEvent event);
    void handle(KeyspaceEvent event);
}
```

Event object:

```java
public record KeyspaceEvent(
    String redisDatabase,
    String eventName,
    String key,
    Instant receivedAt
) {}
```

---

## 18. Parsing Key dengan Key Factory

Karena keyspace notification hanya memberi event/key string, key naming harus disiplin.

Contoh key:

```text
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:timeout:review-reminder:v1:case:c991
```

Parser:

```java
public Optional<ReviewReminderTimeoutKey> parseReviewReminderTimeout(String key) {
    String[] p = key.split(":");

    if (p.length != 11) {
        return Optional.empty();
    }

    if (!p[0].equals("case-svc")) return Optional.empty();
    if (!p[3].equals("tenant")) return Optional.empty();
    if (!p[5].equals("timeout")) return Optional.empty();
    if (!p[6].equals("review-reminder")) return Optional.empty();
    if (!p[7].equals("v1")) return Optional.empty();
    if (!p[8].equals("case")) return Optional.empty();

    return Optional.of(new ReviewReminderTimeoutKey(
        p[1], // env
        p[4], // tenant
        p[9]  // caseId, adjust based on actual segment shape
    ));
}
```

Lebih baik lagi: gunakan `RedisKeyFactory` dan parser formal agar format key tidak tersebar di handler.

---

## 19. Example: Lettuce Listener Shape

Pseudocode:

```java
public final class RedisKeyspaceNotificationListener implements ManagedLifecycle {

    private final RedisClient redisClient;
    private final KeyspaceNotificationDispatcher dispatcher;
    private final ExecutorService handlerExecutor;
    private StatefulRedisPubSubConnection<String, String> pubSubConnection;

    public void start() {
        this.pubSubConnection = redisClient.connectPubSub();

        pubSubConnection.addListener(new RedisPubSubAdapter<>() {
            @Override
            public void message(String pattern, String channel, String message) {
                KeyspaceEvent event = KeyspaceEventParser.parse(channel, message);

                handlerExecutor.submit(() -> dispatcher.dispatch(event));
            }
        });

        pubSubConnection.sync().psubscribe(
            "__keyevent@0__:expired",
            "__keyevent@0__:del",
            "__keyevent@0__:evicted"
        );
    }

    public void stop() {
        if (pubSubConnection != null) {
            pubSubConnection.sync().punsubscribe();
            pubSubConnection.close();
        }

        handlerExecutor.shutdown();
    }
}
```

Catatan:

```text
- pakai dedicated Pub/Sub connection
- handler dieksekusi di bounded executor
- jangan blocking Pub/Sub event loop
- semua handler harus idempotent
```

---

## 20. Handler Example: Review Reminder Timeout

```java
public final class ReviewReminderExpiredHandler implements KeyspaceEventHandler {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final NotificationService notificationService;

    @Override
    public boolean supports(KeyspaceEvent event) {
        return event.eventName().equals("expired")
            && event.key().contains(":timeout:review-reminder:");
    }

    @Override
    public void handle(KeyspaceEvent event) {
        ReviewReminderTimeoutKey key = parse(event.key())
            .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid key"));

        CaseReviewState state = caseRepository.findReviewState(
            key.tenantId(),
            key.caseId()
        );

        if (!state.isStillWaitingForReview()) {
            return;
        }

        notificationService.sendReviewReminder(
            key.tenantId(),
            key.caseId()
        );
    }
}
```

Important:

```text
Handler re-check PostgreSQL.
Expired event hanya trigger, bukan source of truth.
```

---

## 21. Fallback Reconciliation

Karena event bisa hilang, setiap important-ish keyspace notification pattern harus punya reconciliation.

### Example: review reminder

Selain TTL key:

```text
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:timeout:review-reminder:v1:case:c991
```

Simpan juga due timestamp di PostgreSQL:

```sql
case_review_reminders (
  tenant_id,
  case_id,
  due_at,
  status,
  last_checked_at
)
```

Reconciliation worker:

```text
Every N minutes:
  SELECT reminders WHERE due_at <= now AND status = 'PENDING'
  for each:
    check current case state
    send reminder if still needed
    mark status
```

Dengan begitu, kalau keyspace event hilang, job tetap tertangani.

---

## 22. Alternative: ZSET Scheduler Lebih Aman untuk Delayed Work

Daripada mengandalkan expired key, pakai Sorted Set:

```text
ZADD case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:zschedule:review-reminder:v1 dueAtMillis case:c991
```

Worker:

```text
ZRANGEBYSCORE schedule -inf now LIMIT 0 100
  ↓
process due item
  ↓
ZREM after success
```

Keyspace notification bisa tetap dipakai sebagai wake-up signal, tapi worker tetap scan ZSET.

```text
Expired event:
  wake worker

ZSET:
  source of due work
```

Ini jauh lebih aman daripada:

```text
Expired key event:
  only source of due work
```

---

## 23. Keyspace Notification vs ZSET vs Stream vs Kafka vs Camunda Timer

| Need | Keyspace Notification | ZSET Scheduler | Redis Stream | Kafka | Camunda Timer |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Lightweight signal | Sangat cocok | Bisa | Bisa | Bisa | Tidak utama |
| Guaranteed delivery | Tidak | Worker polling bisa recover | Lebih baik | Kuat | Engine-managed |
| Replay | Tidak | Manual | Ada | Kuat | Process history |
| Ack/retry | Tidak | Manual | Native-ish | Consumer-managed | Native engine behavior |
| Precise timing | Tidak kuat | Cukup baik | Tidak timer-native | Perlu scheduler | Baik untuk BPMN |
| Workflow semantics | Tidak | Tidak | Tidak | Tidak | Ya |
| Auditability | Lemah | Sedang jika backed DB | Sedang | Kuat | Kuat |
| Operational complexity | Rendah | Sedang | Sedang | Tinggi | Sedang |

Rule untuk stack Anda:

```text
UI/cache/local invalidation:
  Keyspace notification OK

Delayed background job:
  ZSET or Redis Stream

Cross-service durable event:
  Kafka

Workflow timer / human-task lifecycle:
  Camunda timer

Compliance/audit-sensitive timeout:
  PostgreSQL/Camunda/Kafka, not keyspace notification only
```

---

## 24. Configuration in Docker/Kubernetes

### Redis config

```conf
notify-keyspace-events Exg
```

Atau runtime:

```text
CONFIG SET notify-keyspace-events Exg
```

Untuk Kubernetes/managed Redis, cara set config tergantung deployment:

```text
On-prem Redis:
  redis.conf

Docker:
  command args or mounted config

Kubernetes:
  ConfigMap / Helm values / operator CR

AWS/Azure managed Redis:
  parameter/configuration setting if supported
```

Jangan asumsi `CONFIG SET` selalu diizinkan di managed Redis. Banyak managed service membatasi command/admin config tertentu.

---

## 25. Channel Subscription Examples

Expired event semua key DB 0:

```text
PSUBSCRIBE __keyevent@0__:expired
```

Delete event semua key DB 0:

```text
PSUBSCRIBE __keyevent@0__:del
```

Semua keyevent:

```text
PSUBSCRIBE __keyevent@0__:*
```

Semua keyspace/keyevent:

```text
PSUBSCRIBE __key*__:* 
```

Untuk production, lebih baik subscribe sempit:

```text
__keyevent@0__:expired
```

Lalu filter key prefix di aplikasi:

```text
case-svc:prod:tenant:*:timeout:review-reminder:*
```

---

## 26. Filtering Strategy

Redis Pub/Sub pattern tidak ideal untuk filter kompleks berdasarkan key prefix karena channel keyevent hanya berisi event name, sedangkan key ada di message body.

Example:

```text
channel = __keyevent@0__:expired
message = case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:timeout:review-reminder:v1:case:c991
```

Jadi filtering prefix biasanya dilakukan di aplikasi:

```java
if (!event.key().startsWith("case-svc:prod:")) {
    return;
}
```

Untuk mengurangi overhead, jangan enable event terlalu luas.

---

## 27. Idempotency Handler

Karena event bisa duplicate secara praktis dari reconnect/multiple subscriber deployment atau handler retry internal, handler harus idempotent.

Example:

```text
expired event for review reminder
  ↓
handler sends reminder
```

Guard:

```sql
UPDATE case_review_reminders
SET status = 'SENT', sent_at = now()
WHERE tenant_id = ?
  AND case_id = ?
  AND status = 'PENDING';
```

Jika update count = 1, kirim. Jika 0, sudah diproses.

Lebih baik:

```text
DB state transition protects side effect.
```

---

## 28. Multi-Pod Subscriber Problem

Jika setiap pod subscribe ke keyspace event yang sama, semua pod bisa menerima event yang sama.

```text
Redis expired event
  ↓
Pod A receives
Pod B receives
Pod C receives
```

Untuk local cache invalidation, ini bagus. Untuk side effect seperti kirim email/reminder, ini berbahaya.

Pilihan:

### A. All pods handle local-only event

Cocok untuk local cache invalidation.

### B. Handler acquire distributed lock

```text
lock:handler:review-reminder:case:c991
```

Hanya satu pod lanjut.

### C. Push to durable queue

Event handler hanya enqueue ke Stream/Kafka/DB outbox, worker group memproses.

### D. Single leader subscriber

Gunakan leader election/lease, tapi tetap perlu idempotency karena leader bisa split/lease expired.

---

## 29. Security / Data Leakage

Keyspace notification mengirim nama key ke subscriber. Jika key berisi PII, event juga membawa PII.

Hindari key seperti:

```text
auth-svc:prod:otp:phone:+628123456789
auth-svc:prod:session:email:john.doe@example.com
```

Gunakan hash:

```text
auth-svc:prod:otp:phone-hash:sha256-abc
auth-svc:prod:session:user-hash:sha256-def
```

Jangan log raw key penuh dari keyspace event jika key mengandung identifier sensitif.

---

## 30. Observability

Metrics:

```text
redis.keyspace.event.received.count
redis.keyspace.event.filtered.count
redis.keyspace.event.handled.count
redis.keyspace.event.handler.error.count
redis.keyspace.event.handler.latency
redis.keyspace.listener.connected
redis.keyspace.listener.reconnect.count
redis.keyspace.listener.disconnected.count
redis.keyspace.reconciliation.scanned.count
redis.keyspace.reconciliation.recovered.count
```

Useful labels:

```text
service
env
event_name
handler_name
result
exception_class
```

Avoid:

```text
key=<raw redis key>
```

Use hashed or family-level labels:

```text
key_family=review-reminder-timeout
```

---

## 31. Logging

Good log:

```json
{
  "event": "redis_keyspace_event_handled",
  "eventName": "expired",
  "keyFamily": "review-reminder-timeout",
  "tenantHash": "sha256-...",
  "handler": "ReviewReminderExpiredHandler",
  "result": "ignored_not_due",
  "correlation": "redis-keyspace-listener"
}
```

Bad log:

```json
{
  "key": "auth-svc:prod:otp:phone:+628123456789"
}
```

---

## 32. Failure Modes

| Failure | Dampak | Guardrail |
|---|---|---|
| Subscriber disconnect | Event hilang | Reconciliation |
| Redis failover | Listener reconnect; event gap | Reconciliation + metrics |
| Multiple pods process same event | Duplicate side effect | Idempotency/lock |
| Expired event delayed | Late action | Re-check DB/current state |
| Key evicted, not expired | Different event | Subscribe `evicted` only if needed |
| Config disabled | No events | Startup health/config check |
| Managed Redis disallows config | Feature not available | Use ZSET/Streams/DB scheduler |
| Cluster subscription only one node | Missing shard events | Subscribe to every node |
| Handler slow | Backlog/thread exhaustion | Bounded executor |
| Handler throws | Event lost after failure | Idempotent retry via durable source |
| Key format changed | Parser failure | Versioned key parser |

---

## 33. Testing Matrix

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| `notify-keyspace-events` enabled | Config benar |
| Expired key emits event | Basic behavior |
| Delete key emits event | Generic delete behavior |
| Listener reconnects | Resilience |
| Handler ignores unrelated key | Prefix filter benar |
| Handler parses valid key | Key format benar |
| Handler rejects malformed key | Safety |
| Multiple pods receive event | Duplicate safe |
| Event missed while listener down | Reconciliation catches |
| Expired event delayed | Handler re-checks state |
| Redis cluster all-node subscription | No shard gap |
| Handler slow/error | Executor bounded, metrics error |
| Sensitive key not logged | Security |

---

## 34. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Kenapa buruk | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Keyspace notification untuk payment timeout final | Event bisa hilang/delay | DB scheduler/Camunda/Kafka |
| Handler langsung mutate business state | Event bukan source of truth | Re-check DB/source |
| Tidak ada reconciliation | Missed event jadi lost work | Periodic scanner |
| Semua pod kirim email dari event yang sama | Duplicate side effect | Lock/idempotency/queue |
| Subscribe `KEA` di production tanpa perlu | Overhead/noise | Enable minimal flags |
| Listener pakai Redis command connection biasa | Connection behavior kacau | Dedicated Pub/Sub connection |
| Key mengandung PII | Event/log bocor | Hash identifier |
| Tidak test Redis cluster behavior | Event shard hilang | Subscribe tiap node |
| Menganggap expired event presisi | Timeout terlambat | ZSET/Camunda timer |
| No metrics | Event loss tak terlihat | Listener/handler metrics |

---

## 35. Practical Design Template

Gunakan template ini sebelum memakai keyspace notification:

```text
Keyspace Notification Use Case:
  Review reminder timeout signal

Event:
  expired

Redis Config:
  notify-keyspace-events Ex

Subscribed Channel:
  __keyevent@0__:expired

Key Pattern:
  case-svc:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:timeout:review-reminder:v1:case:{caseId}

Source of Truth:
  PostgreSQL case_review_reminders + cases

Handler:
  ReviewReminderExpiredHandler

Handler Action:
  Re-check DB state, then enqueue/send reminder if still pending

Idempotency:
  DB status transition PENDING -> SENT

Duplicate Handling:
  update count = 1 proceeds; update count = 0 ignored

Missed Event Recovery:
  reconciliation worker scans due reminders every N minutes

Failure Mode:
  missed event delays reminder, does not lose it

Security:
  no raw PII in key; case ID classification reviewed

Metrics:
  event received, handled, ignored, recovered, handler error

Cluster Behavior:
  subscribe to all Redis master nodes if cluster
```

---

## 36. Senior Engineer Checklist

Sebelum approve keyspace notification design:

```text
[ ] Event dipakai sebagai signal, bukan source of truth
[ ] Redis config minimal: Ex / Exg / sesuai kebutuhan
[ ] Dedicated Pub/Sub connection
[ ] Listener lifecycle jelas
[ ] Reconnect behavior jelas
[ ] Handler idempotent
[ ] Multiple pod behavior aman
[ ] Ada fallback reconciliation
[ ] Handler re-check PostgreSQL/Redis source/current state
[ ] Tidak ada irreversible side effect tanpa idempotency
[ ] Key tidak mengandung PII/secret
[ ] Raw key tidak jadi metric label
[ ] Expired event delay sudah diterima desain
[ ] Redis Cluster node-specific behavior dipahami
[ ] Managed Redis support sudah diverifikasi
[ ] Tests mencakup missed event dan duplicate event
```

---

## 37. Ringkasan Praktis

```text
Keyspace notification bagus untuk:
  - best-effort signal
  - local cache invalidation
  - expired-key reaction ringan
  - presence maybe-offline
  - worker wake-up
  - observability/debug

Keyspace notification buruk untuk:
  - durable event
  - audit trail
  - final business transition
  - guaranteed delayed job
  - payment/regulatory/workflow correctness

Pattern aman:
  event received
    ↓
  parse key
    ↓
  re-check source of truth
    ↓
  perform idempotent action
    ↓
  reconciliation covers missed events
```

Kalimat kunci:

> Redis keyspace notification adalah alarm, bukan ledger. Alarm boleh membangunkan worker, tetapi keputusan final harus dicek ulang ke source of truth.
