# Seri 9 - Rate Limiting in JAX-RS Filter

Status: **Seri 9 dari 22 seri lanjutan**.  
Setelah seri ini selesai, tersisa **13 seri**.

---

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Rate limiting adalah pattern untuk membatasi jumlah request/action dalam periode tertentu. Di aplikasi **Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice**, rate limiter biasanya dipasang di:

```text id="2cgpd7"
ContainerRequestFilter
  ↓
RateLimiter service
  ↓
Redis
  ↓
allow / reject request
```

Redis cocok untuk rate limiting karena punya command atomic seperti `INCR`, TTL via `EXPIRE`/`SET EX`, Sorted Set untuk sliding window, dan Lua scripting untuk menggabungkan beberapa operasi menjadi satu eksekusi atomic di server. Redis docs secara eksplisit memakai `INCR` sebagai contoh pattern rate limiter untuk membatasi operasi per interval waktu. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/incr/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

Rate limiter bukan sekadar counter. Ia adalah **traffic control policy**.

```text id="5ibfjv"
Rate limiter =
  subject + action + window + limit + algorithm + failure policy
```

Contoh:

```text id="9e15a7"
Subject:
  user / API key / tenant / IP / client app

Action:
  POST /orders
  GET /cases/{id}
  login attempt
  export report
  workflow command

Window:
  per second
  per minute
  per hour
  per day

Limit:
  100 requests/minute
  5 login attempts/15 minutes
  10 exports/hour

Algorithm:
  fixed window
  sliding window
  token bucket
  leaky bucket

Failure policy:
  fail-open
  fail-closed
  fallback
```

Untuk microservice production, bagian paling penting sering bukan algoritmanya, tetapi **apa yang terjadi saat Redis timeout/down**.

---

## 3. Where Rate Limiting Lives in Jersey/JAX-RS

```text id="zuzgbj"
HTTP Request
  ↓
ContainerRequestFilter
  ├── extract subject
  ├── resolve route/action
  ├── resolve tenant
  ├── load policy
  ├── call RateLimiter
  └── allow or abort
  ↓
Resource Method
  ↓
Application Service
```

JAX-RS menyediakan `ContainerRequestFilter` untuk memfilter request sebelum masuk ke resource method. Filter ini cocok untuk policy seperti authentication, authorization pre-check, request validation, correlation ID, dan rate limiting. ([MOST 1058 FM](https://most1058fm.com/2026/07/south-jakartas-ppkd-commences-skills-development-program-for-first-and-second-cohorts/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4. Rate Limiter Placement

| Lokasi | Cocok untuk | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| API Gateway / Ingress | Global public API limit | Bagus sebagai lapisan pertama |
| JAX-RS `ContainerRequestFilter` | Service-specific limit | Bisa paham tenant/user/route/domain action |
| Application Service | Business action quota | Cocok untuk command tertentu |
| Database constraint | Final hard limit | Untuk quota yang harus audit/correctness kuat |
| Redis | Shared fast counter antar pod | Cocok untuk distributed low-latency enforcement |

Rekomendasi praktis:

```text id="c5rwen"
API Gateway:
  coarse external protection

JAX-RS Filter:
  service/API route protection

Application Service:
  domain-aware quota

PostgreSQL:
  final auditable quota/invariant
```

Redis bagus untuk low-latency shared limiter, tapi jangan jadikan Redis satu-satunya enforcement untuk quota finansial, legal, atau compliance-critical.

---

## 5. Diagram

```text id="1mwjp9"
Client
  ↓
JAX-RS ContainerRequestFilter
  ↓
RateLimitSubjectResolver
  ├── tenant
  ├── user
  ├── API key
  ├── IP
  └── route/action
  ↓
RateLimitPolicyResolver
  ├── limit
  ├── window
  ├── algorithm
  └── fail policy
  ↓
RedisRateLimiter
  ├── fixed window counter
  ├── sliding window ZSET
  ├── token bucket Lua
  └── metrics
  ↓
Decision
  ├── ALLOW → continue request
  └── DENY  → HTTP 429 Too Many Requests
```

---

## 6. Core Domain Model

```java id="hf89hd"
public record RateLimitSubject(
    String tenantId,
    String userIdHash,
    String apiKeyHash,
    String ipHash,
    String routeId,
    String action
) {}
```

```java id="sju43x"
public record RateLimitPolicy(
    String policyName,
    RateLimitAlgorithm algorithm,
    int limit,
    Duration window,
    boolean failOpen,
    String scope
) {}
```

```java id="b2sxwl"
public enum RateLimitAlgorithm {
    FIXED_WINDOW,
    SLIDING_WINDOW,
    TOKEN_BUCKET
}
```

```java id="xrmhzj"
public record RateLimitDecision(
    boolean allowed,
    int limit,
    long remaining,
    Duration retryAfter,
    String policyName
) {
    public static RateLimitDecision allow(
        int limit,
        long remaining,
        String policyName
    ) {
        return new RateLimitDecision(
            true,
            limit,
            remaining,
            Duration.ZERO,
            policyName
        );
    }

    public static RateLimitDecision deny(
        int limit,
        Duration retryAfter,
        String policyName
    ) {
        return new RateLimitDecision(
            false,
            limit,
            0,
            retryAfter,
            policyName
        );
    }
}
```

---

## 7. HTTP Behavior

Jika request ditolak, response umum adalah:

```text id="pzti8w"
HTTP 429 Too Many Requests
```

Header yang umum diberikan:

```text id="p3gj37"
Retry-After: <seconds>
X-RateLimit-Limit: <limit>
X-RateLimit-Remaining: <remaining>
X-RateLimit-Reset: <epoch seconds>
```

Jangan mengembalikan `500` untuk limit exceeded. Itu bukan server error; itu policy rejection.

---

## 8. Key Design

Key harus memasukkan semua dimensi policy:

```text id="i8e50w"
{service}:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:rate:{algorithm}:v1:{subjectType}:{subjectHash}:route:{routeHash}:window:{windowId}
```

Contoh fixed window per API key per route per minute:

```text id="l3y2dj"
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:rate:fixed:v1:api-key:akHash:route:rHash:window:202607131430
```

Contoh login attempt per user:

```text id="wp81o0"
auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:rate:login:v1:user:uHash:window:2026071314
```

Contoh sliding window:

```text id="t9lq9p"
api-svc:prod:tenant:t1:rate:sliding:v1:user:uHash:route:rHash
```

Key tidak boleh mengandung raw email, phone, token, API key, atau IP jika itu sensitif. Hash subject sebelum masuk key.

---

## 9. Subject Selection

| Subject | Cocok untuk | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| IP | Public unauthenticated endpoint | NAT/shared IP bisa kena false positive |
| API key | Partner/client API | API key harus di-hash di key Redis |
| User ID | Authenticated user action | Perlu tenant boundary |
| Tenant ID | Protect tenant-level quota | Tenant besar bisa butuh sub-limit |
| Route | Protect expensive endpoint | Route harus canonical, bukan raw path |
| Action/domain command | Business quota | Lebih akurat daripada route |
| Composite | Real production | Contoh: tenant + user + route |

Rekomendasi:

```text id="141wgk"
Public unauthenticated:
  IP + route

Partner API:
  API key + route + tenant

Authenticated internal:
  tenant + user + action

Expensive export/report:
  tenant + user + action + daily/hourly window

Login:
  user/email hash + IP hash + tenant
```

---

## 10. Route Canonicalization

Jangan pakai raw path:

```text id="g579jk"
GET /cases/123
GET /cases/456
GET /cases/789
```

Kalau raw path dipakai, setiap entity ID membuat rate limit key berbeda.

Gunakan canonical route:

```text id="7uuvn0"
GET /cases/{caseId}
POST /cases/{caseId}/actions/assign
POST /orders/{orderId}/submit
```

Lalu hash:

```text id="vjyxb3"
route:sha256-canonical-route
```

---

# Bagian A - Fixed Window Rate Limiter

## 11. Fixed Window Mental Model

Fixed window menghitung request di window diskrit:

```text id="6czlva"
14:30:00 - 14:30:59 → counter A
14:31:00 - 14:31:59 → counter B
```

Redis tutorial terbaru mendeskripsikan fixed window sebagai algoritma paling sederhana: request dihitung dalam interval non-overlapping, counter dinaikkan setiap request, dan request ditolak ketika counter melewati limit. ([Redis](https://redis.io/tutorials/howtos/ratelimiting/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 12. Fixed Window Flow

```text id="v9wuy8"
Request arrives
  ↓
Compute window id
  ↓
INCR key
  ↓
If count == 1:
  EXPIRE key window + buffer
  ↓
If count <= limit:
  allow
else:
  deny
```

Redis docs menunjukkan pattern rate limiter berbasis `INCR` dan `EXPIRE`, termasuk pendekatan counter per IP/per second. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/incr/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 13. Fixed Window Example

Limit:

```text id="812fkr"
100 requests per minute per API key per route
```

Key:

```text id="4n138x"
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:rate:fixed:v1:api-key:akHash:route:rHash:window:202607131430
```

Commands:

```text id="b7wxan"
INCR key
EXPIRE key 70
```

TTL dibuat `window + buffer` agar key tidak langsung hilang persis di boundary dan masih bisa diobservasi sebentar.

---

## 14. Race Condition: `INCR` + `EXPIRE`

Masalah klasik:

```text id="pedzm5"
count = INCR key
if count == 1:
  EXPIRE key 60
```

Jika aplikasi crash setelah `INCR` tetapi sebelum `EXPIRE`, key bisa hidup tanpa TTL. Redis docs juga membahas pattern `INCR` untuk rate limiting dan penggunaan `EXPIRE`; secara production, operasi multi-command seperti ini perlu dibuat aman, misalnya dengan Lua, agar increment dan expiry menjadi atomic dari sisi server. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/incr/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Solusi: Lua fixed window

```lua id="2z4ba6"
local key = KEYS[1]
local limit = tonumber(ARGV[1])
local ttl = tonumber(ARGV[2])

local current = redis.call("INCR", key)

if current == 1 then
  redis.call("EXPIRE", key, ttl)
end

if current > limit then
  local remainingTtl = redis.call("TTL", key)
  return {0, current, remainingTtl}
end

local remaining = limit - current
local remainingTtl = redis.call("TTL", key)
return {1, remaining, remainingTtl}
```

Return:

```text id="hzacii"
[allowed, remainingOrCurrent, retryAfterSeconds]
```

---

## 15. Fixed Window Pros / Cons

| Aspek | Nilai |
|---|---|
| Complexity | Rendah |
| Memory | Rendah |
| Accuracy | Sedang |
| Burst behavior | Lemah di boundary |
| Redis commands | Simple |
| Cocok untuk | Basic API limit, abuse protection ringan |

Problem boundary:

```text id="5keipf"
User bisa melakukan 100 request di akhir window
dan 100 request lagi di awal window berikutnya.
Efektif burst = 200 request dalam waktu sangat singkat.
```

Kalau burst boundary tidak bisa diterima, gunakan sliding window atau token bucket.

---

# Bagian B - Sliding Window Rate Limiter

## 16. Sliding Window Mental Model

Sliding window menghitung request dalam rentang waktu bergerak:

```text id="r486y5"
now = 14:30:30
window = 60 seconds

Count requests from:
14:29:30 to 14:30:30
```

Biasanya memakai Redis Sorted Set:

```text id="84bjam"
score = timestamp millis
member = unique request id
```

---

## 17. Sliding Window Flow

```text id="rqo9en"
Request arrives
  ↓
ZREMRANGEBYSCORE key -inf now-window
  ↓
ZCARD key
  ↓
if count >= limit:
    deny
else:
    ZADD key now requestId
    EXPIRE key window+buffer
    allow
```

Karena ini beberapa command, gunakan Lua agar cleanup, count, add, dan expiry menjadi atomic. Redis Sorted Set cocok untuk sliding window karena member bisa diberi score timestamp dan query range berdasarkan waktu.

---

## 18. Sliding Window Lua

```lua id="qvdi0x"
local key = KEYS[1]
local now = tonumber(ARGV[1])
local windowMillis = tonumber(ARGV[2])
local limit = tonumber(ARGV[3])
local requestId = ARGV[4]
local ttlSeconds = tonumber(ARGV[5])

local min = now - windowMillis

redis.call("ZREMRANGEBYSCORE", key, "-inf", min)

local current = redis.call("ZCARD", key)

if current >= limit then
  local oldest = redis.call("ZRANGE", key, 0, 0, "WITHSCORES")
  local retryAfterMillis = windowMillis

  if oldest[2] ~= nil then
    retryAfterMillis = (tonumber(oldest[2]) + windowMillis) - now
    if retryAfterMillis < 0 then
      retryAfterMillis = 0
    end
  end

  return {0, current, retryAfterMillis}
end

redis.call("ZADD", key, now, requestId)
redis.call("EXPIRE", key, ttlSeconds)

local remaining = limit - current - 1
return {1, remaining, 0}
```

---

## 19. Sliding Window Pros / Cons

| Aspek | Nilai |
|---|---|
| Complexity | Sedang |
| Memory | Lebih tinggi |
| Accuracy | Tinggi |
| Burst boundary | Lebih baik |
| Redis structure | Sorted Set |
| Cocok untuk | Login, expensive endpoint, public API yang butuh fairness |

Risiko:

```text id="qyccsa"
- Setiap request menambah member ke ZSET
- Hot subject bisa membuat ZSET besar
- Cleanup harus selalu dilakukan
- Clock source harus konsisten
```

Gunakan timestamp dari aplikasi dengan hati-hati. Untuk distributed pods, NTP/time sync penting. Alternatif: gunakan Redis `TIME` di Lua, tapi ini membuat script sedikit berbeda.

---

# Bagian C - Token Bucket Rate Limiter

## 20. Token Bucket Mental Model

Token bucket mengizinkan burst terbatas.

```text id="fdu3dn"
Bucket capacity = 100 tokens
Refill rate = 10 tokens/sec

Each request consumes 1 token.
If bucket has token -> allow.
If no token -> deny.
```

Cocok ketika Anda ingin:

```text id="9d6m27"
- rata-rata throughput terkendali
- burst kecil tetap diizinkan
- pengalaman client lebih smooth daripada fixed window
```

---

## 21. Token Bucket State

Redis key bisa Hash/String JSON berisi:

```text id="a7wsxt"
tokens
lastRefillAtMillis
```

Key:

```text id="0bhbtb"
api-svc:prod:tenant:t1:rate:bucket:v1:api-key:akHash:route:rHash
```

---

## 22. Token Bucket Lua

```lua id="8fvpwn"
local key = KEYS[1]

local now = tonumber(ARGV[1])
local capacity = tonumber(ARGV[2])
local refillRatePerSecond = tonumber(ARGV[3])
local requested = tonumber(ARGV[4])
local ttlSeconds = tonumber(ARGV[5])

local data = redis.call("HMGET", key, "tokens", "lastRefillAt")
local tokens = tonumber(data[1])
local lastRefillAt = tonumber(data[2])

if tokens == nil then
  tokens = capacity
  lastRefillAt = now
end

local elapsedMillis = math.max(0, now - lastRefillAt)
local refill = (elapsedMillis / 1000.0) * refillRatePerSecond

tokens = math.min(capacity, tokens + refill)
lastRefillAt = now

if tokens < requested then
  redis.call("HMSET", key, "tokens", tokens, "lastRefillAt", lastRefillAt)
  redis.call("EXPIRE", key, ttlSeconds)

  local missing = requested - tokens
  local retryAfterMillis = math.ceil((missing / refillRatePerSecond) * 1000)

  return {0, math.floor(tokens), retryAfterMillis}
end

tokens = tokens - requested

redis.call("HMSET", key, "tokens", tokens, "lastRefillAt", lastRefillAt)
redis.call("EXPIRE", key, ttlSeconds)

return {1, math.floor(tokens), 0}
```

---

## 23. Token Bucket Pros / Cons

| Aspek | Nilai |
|---|---|
| Complexity | Sedang-tinggi |
| Memory | Rendah |
| Accuracy | Baik |
| Burst support | Baik |
| Cocok untuk | API quota dengan burst allowance |
| Risiko | Perhitungan waktu/refill harus benar |

Untuk API eksternal/partner, token bucket sering lebih natural dibanding fixed window.

---

# Bagian D - JAX-RS Integration

## 24. Filter Shape

```java id="tmv4u6"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION)
public final class RateLimitFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private final RateLimiter rateLimiter;
    private final RateLimitSubjectResolver subjectResolver;
    private final RateLimitPolicyResolver policyResolver;

    @Inject
    public RateLimitFilter(
        RateLimiter rateLimiter,
        RateLimitSubjectResolver subjectResolver,
        RateLimitPolicyResolver policyResolver
    ) {
        this.rateLimiter = rateLimiter;
        this.subjectResolver = subjectResolver;
        this.policyResolver = policyResolver;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) {
        RateLimitSubject subject = subjectResolver.resolve(requestContext);
        RateLimitPolicy policy = policyResolver.resolve(requestContext, subject);

        RateLimitDecision decision = rateLimiter.check(subject, policy);

        if (!decision.allowed()) {
            requestContext.abortWith(toTooManyRequests(decision));
        }
    }

    private Response toTooManyRequests(RateLimitDecision decision) {
        return Response.status(429)
            .header("Retry-After", decision.retryAfter().toSeconds())
            .header("X-RateLimit-Limit", decision.limit())
            .header("X-RateLimit-Remaining", decision.remaining())
            .entity(new ErrorResponse(
                "RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED",
                "Too many requests. Please retry later."
            ))
            .build();
    }
}
```

---

## 25. Subject Resolver

```java id="xm5xng"
public final class RateLimitSubjectResolver {

    public RateLimitSubject resolve(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        String tenantId = resolveTenant(ctx);
        String routeId = resolveCanonicalRoute(ctx);
        String userHash = resolveUserHash(ctx).orElse("anonymous");
        String apiKeyHash = resolveApiKeyHash(ctx).orElse("none");
        String ipHash = hash(resolveClientIp(ctx));

        return new RateLimitSubject(
            tenantId,
            userHash,
            apiKeyHash,
            ipHash,
            routeId,
            resolveAction(ctx)
        );
    }
}
```

Important:

```text id="yqkcum"
- Jangan percaya X-Forwarded-For kecuali dari trusted proxy.
- Normalize route sebelum hash.
- Hash API key / IP / user external identifier.
- Include tenant.
```

---

## 26. Policy Resolver

Policy bisa berasal dari config static, database, feature flag, atau central policy service.

```java id="x9tpwt"
public final class RateLimitPolicyResolver {

    public RateLimitPolicy resolve(
        ContainerRequestContext ctx,
        RateLimitSubject subject
    ) {
        String method = ctx.getMethod();
        String route = subject.routeId();

        if (method.equals("POST") && route.equals("POST /cases/{caseId}/submit")) {
            return new RateLimitPolicy(
                "submit-case-per-user",
                RateLimitAlgorithm.SLIDING_WINDOW,
                10,
                Duration.ofMinutes(1),
                false,
                "tenant-user-route"
            );
        }

        if (route.equals("POST /auth/login")) {
            return new RateLimitPolicy(
                "login-attempt",
                RateLimitAlgorithm.SLIDING_WINDOW,
                5,
                Duration.ofMinutes(15),
                false,
                "tenant-user-ip"
            );
        }

        return new RateLimitPolicy(
            "default-api-key",
            RateLimitAlgorithm.FIXED_WINDOW,
            1000,
            Duration.ofMinutes(1),
            true,
            "tenant-api-key-route"
        );
    }
}
```

---

## 27. RateLimiter Interface

```java id="eehpk8"
public interface RateLimiter {
    RateLimitDecision check(
        RateLimitSubject subject,
        RateLimitPolicy policy
    );
}
```

Implementation memilih algoritma:

```java id="8hrvxa"
public final class RedisRateLimiter implements RateLimiter {

    private final FixedWindowRateLimiter fixedWindow;
    private final SlidingWindowRateLimiter slidingWindow;
    private final TokenBucketRateLimiter tokenBucket;

    @Override
    public RateLimitDecision check(
        RateLimitSubject subject,
        RateLimitPolicy policy
    ) {
        return switch (policy.algorithm()) {
            case FIXED_WINDOW -> fixedWindow.check(subject, policy);
            case SLIDING_WINDOW -> slidingWindow.check(subject, policy);
            case TOKEN_BUCKET -> tokenBucket.check(subject, policy);
        };
    }
}
```

---

## 28. Failure Policy: Fail-Open vs Fail-Closed

Ini keputusan paling penting.

| Endpoint | Redis failure policy | Alasan |
|---|---|---|
| Public read API | Fail-open terbatas | Availability lebih penting |
| Expensive export/report | Fail-closed atau degraded | Lindungi resource |
| Login attempt | Fail-closed atau fallback | Security |
| Payment/order submit | Fail-closed/fallback DB | Duplicate/abuse risk |
| Internal health/readiness | Usually bypass | Jangan rate-limit health |
| Admin dangerous action | Fail-closed | Safety |
| Low-risk cacheable GET | Fail-open | User experience |

### Fail-open

```text id="8zpqtp"
Redis timeout
  ↓
allow request
  ↓
record metric
```

Cocok untuk availability-sensitive read traffic.

### Fail-closed

```text id="l9s7og"
Redis timeout
  ↓
reject request with 503 or 429-like dependency error
```

Cocok untuk security-sensitive actions.

### Fallback

```text id="tjr7rl"
Redis timeout
  ↓
check local emergency limiter or PostgreSQL-backed quota
```

Cocok untuk critical domains.

---

## 29. Redis Timeout Budget

Rate limiter ada di request path, jadi timeout harus sangat pendek.

```text id="wg67nb"
HTTP timeout: 2000 ms
Service budget: 1500 ms
Redis rate limit budget: 20-100 ms
```

Rule:

```text id="ofbtjy"
Rate limiter Redis call tidak boleh menggantung request thread.
```

Jika Redis lambat, sistem bisa lebih parah daripada tanpa limiter karena semua request tertahan di filter.

---

## 30. Avoiding Hot Keys

Hot key terjadi jika terlalu banyak request menghantam key yang sama.

Contoh:

```text id="7ah40g"
api-svc:prod:global:rate:all-requests:window:202607131430
```

Ini bisa menjadi bottleneck.

Mitigasi:

```text id="dk15ax"
- shard limit by tenant/apiKey/user/route
- gunakan hierarchical limit hanya bila perlu
- jangan semua traffic masuk satu global key
- monitor top keys/command latency
```

Jika butuh global limit sangat tinggi, pertimbangkan rate limiting di API gateway atau dedicated limiter service.

---

## 31. Hierarchical Rate Limit

Kadang perlu lebih dari satu limit:

```text id="crrxu8"
- per user: 100/min
- per tenant: 10.000/min
- per API key: 1.000/min
- per IP: 60/min
- per route: 500/min
```

Flow:

```text id="l9w211"
Check most specific / cheapest first
  ↓
Check user limit
  ↓
Check API key limit
  ↓
Check tenant limit
  ↓
Allow only if all allow
```

Risk:

```text id="mq2e8p"
Kalau setiap request melakukan 5 Redis scripts,
latency dan Redis load naik.
```

Optimisasi:

```text id="rfrsg5"
- hanya aktifkan hierarchical checks untuk endpoint penting
- combine beberapa counters dalam satu Lua script jika same hash slot
- cache policy config lokal
- gunakan API gateway untuk coarse limit
```

---

## 32. Redis Cluster Consideration

Redis Cluster membutuhkan multi-key operation berada pada hash slot yang sama jika script memakai lebih dari satu key. Jika rate limiter memakai satu key per check, aman. Jika hierarchical limiter memakai beberapa key dalam satu Lua script, gunakan hash tag yang hati-hati.

Contoh:

```text id="hwc4qs"
api-svc:prod:{tenant:t1:user:u1}:rate:user:v1:route:r1
api-svc:prod:{tenant:t1:user:u1}:rate:route:v1:route:r1
```

Tapi jangan pakai hash tag terlalu luas seperti `{tenant:t1}` untuk semua rate key jika tenant besar, karena bisa membuat hot slot.

---

## 33. Headers and Response Body

Response 429:

```json id="yjhyzm"
{
  "code": "RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED",
  "message": "Too many requests. Please retry later.",
  "policy": "submit-case-per-user",
  "retryAfterSeconds": 42
}
```

Headers:

```text id="p7dpae"
Retry-After: 42
X-RateLimit-Limit: 10
X-RateLimit-Remaining: 0
X-RateLimit-Policy: submit-case-per-user
```

Jangan expose internal Redis key ke client.

---

## 34. Bypass Rules

Endpoint yang biasanya di-bypass:

```text id="9k86tj"
- /health
- /readiness
- /metrics
- static internal liveness route
```

Tapi jangan bypass:

```text id="qzqlku"
- login
- password reset
- OTP request
- export/report
- expensive search
- workflow command
```

---

## 35. Login Rate Limiting

Login butuh multi-dimensional limiter:

```text id="lqdnhm"
- per account/email hash
- per IP hash
- per tenant
- per device/browser fingerprint if available
```

Policy:

```text id="vwz59f"
5 attempts / 15 minutes per account
20 attempts / 15 minutes per IP
100 attempts / 15 minutes per tenant
```

Use sliding window or fixed window with careful lockout policy.

Guardrail:

```text id="uy5m90"
- Jangan leak apakah account exists.
- Negative result response harus sama.
- Hash email/phone.
- Redis failure sebaiknya fail-closed atau fallback security store.
```

---

## 36. Expensive Search Rate Limiting

Search endpoint bisa membebani PostgreSQL.

Policy:

```text id="f1jr44"
GET /cases/search
  tenant + user + route
  60/minute
  sliding window
```

Tambahan:

```text id="4rcwsp"
- limit page size
- require indexed filters
- cache popular query
- reject unbounded query
```

Rate limiter hanya satu lapisan; query validation tetap wajib.

---

## 37. Export / Report Rate Limiting

Export/report biasanya mahal dan harus dibatasi lebih keras.

Policy:

```text id="pzn9zq"
POST /reports/export
  5/hour per user
  100/day per tenant
```

Untuk report yang benar-benar mahal, Redis limiter saja tidak cukup. Simpan job record di PostgreSQL dan gunakan unique/constraint/status untuk mencegah duplicate export.

---

## 38. Workflow / Camunda Command Rate Limiting

Untuk command seperti:

```text id="f9ftqi"
POST /cases/{caseId}/submit
POST /cases/{caseId}/approve
POST /cases/{caseId}/assign
```

Rate limit bisa dipakai untuk:

```text id="c46cxq"
- mencegah double-click storm
- mencegah automation abuse
- melindungi Camunda engine
- melindungi downstream service
```

Tapi correctness tetap harus dijaga oleh:

```text id="7yqfdp"
- idempotency key
- domain state transition validation
- optimistic locking / DB constraint
- Camunda process state
```

Rate limiter bukan pengganti idempotency.

---

## 39. Rate Limiting vs Idempotency

| Pattern | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Rate limiting | Membatasi frekuensi request |
| Idempotency | Membuat retry command aman |
| Deduplication | Mencegah duplicate processing |
| Lock | Mengontrol critical section |
| Authorization | Menentukan boleh/tidaknya action |

Contoh:

```text id="t8dyxm"
User submit order 10x karena retry network.
Rate limiter mungkin menolak request ke-6.
Tapi idempotency tetap wajib agar request 1-5 tidak membuat order ganda.
```

---

## 40. Observability

Metrics wajib:

```text id="e18q41"
rate_limit.check.count
rate_limit.allowed.count
rate_limit.denied.count
rate_limit.redis.timeout.count
rate_limit.redis.error.count
rate_limit.fail_open.count
rate_limit.fail_closed.count
rate_limit.lua.latency
rate_limit.remaining.distribution
```

Labels:

```text id="hhjkf4"
service
env
policy
algorithm
route_family
subject_type
decision
exception_class
```

Jangan label dengan:

```text id="vzfdpm"
userId
apiKey
ip
rawRoute
redisKey
```

Karena PII dan cardinality risk.

---

## 41. Logging

Good log:

```json id="xhiuq0"
{
  "event": "rate_limit_denied",
  "policy": "login-attempt",
  "algorithm": "SLIDING_WINDOW",
  "tenantHash": "sha256-...",
  "subjectType": "user-ip",
  "routeFamily": "POST /auth/login",
  "retryAfterSeconds": 120,
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

Bad log:

```json id="21pdzr"
{
  "apiKey": "raw-api-key",
  "ip": "192.0.2.10",
  "redisKey": "auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:rate:login:user:john@example.com"
}
```

---

## 42. Testing Matrix

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Under limit allowed | Basic path |
| Over limit denied with 429 | Policy enforcement |
| Retry-After correct | Client usability |
| Fixed window boundary behavior | Understand burst |
| Sliding window cleanup | ZSET does not grow forever |
| Token bucket refill | Correct burst/refill |
| Concurrent requests | Atomicity |
| Redis timeout fail-open | Availability policy |
| Redis timeout fail-closed | Security policy |
| Route canonicalization | Avoid per-ID key explosion |
| Tenant isolation | No cross-tenant collision |
| Header spoofing | Trusted proxy logic |
| Lua script return mapping | Correct decision |
| TTL exists on counter key | No permanent limiter key |
| Metrics emitted | Operability |

---

## 43. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Rate limit key pakai raw path | Key explosion | Canonical route |
| Raw API key di Redis key | Secret leak | Hash |
| `INCR` tanpa TTL atomicity | Permanent counter key | Lua |
| Redis timeout menggantung request | Thread exhaustion | Short timeout |
| Semua endpoint fail-closed | Redis outage jadi total outage | Policy per endpoint |
| Login fail-open | Security bypass | Fail-closed/fallback |
| Global single hot key | Redis bottleneck | Shard by subject |
| Sliding window tanpa cleanup | ZSET memory growth | `ZREMRANGEBYSCORE` |
| Pub/Sub untuk rate limit sync | Tidak perlu/tidak reliable | Direct Redis command/Lua |
| Rate limiter dianggap idempotency | Duplicate command tetap mungkin | Idempotency store |
| Metric label user/apiKey | Cardinality/PII | Aggregate labels |
| No integration test concurrency | Race condition | Test parallel requests |

---

## 44. Senior Engineer Checklist

```text id="92zilt"
[ ] Endpoint policy jelas
[ ] Subject jelas: tenant/user/apiKey/IP/route/action
[ ] Route canonicalized
[ ] Sensitive identifiers hashed
[ ] Algorithm dipilih sesuai kebutuhan
[ ] Fixed window boundary burst diterima
[ ] Sliding window memory cleanup jelas
[ ] Token bucket refill logic dites
[ ] Lua dipakai untuk multi-command atomicity
[ ] TTL selalu ada untuk limiter key
[ ] Redis timeout budget sangat pendek
[ ] Fail-open/fail-closed policy per endpoint
[ ] Login/security endpoint tidak fail-open sembarangan
[ ] 429 response punya Retry-After
[ ] Metrics/logging aman dari PII
[ ] Redis Cluster hash slot dipahami jika multi-key script
[ ] Rate limiter tidak menggantikan idempotency/authorization
```

---

## 45. Ringkasan Praktis

```text id="74x0ml"
Fixed window:
  sederhana, murah, tapi boundary burst

Sliding window:
  lebih adil, lebih mahal, cocok untuk login/expensive endpoint

Token bucket:
  cocok untuk API quota dengan burst allowance

JAX-RS integration:
  ContainerRequestFilter -> RateLimiter -> Redis -> allow/deny

Redis safety:
  Lua untuk atomic multi-command
  TTL wajib
  timeout pendek
  failure policy jelas

HTTP behavior:
  allow atau 429 Too Many Requests
  jangan expose Redis detail
```

Kalimat kunci:

> Rate limiter yang baik bukan hanya menghitung request. Ia menjaga fairness, melindungi resource, punya failure policy saat Redis bermasalah, dan tidak menggantikan idempotency atau authorization.

# Seri 10 - Idempotency Key, Request Deduplication & Replay Protection

Status: **Seri 10 dari 22 seri lanjutan**.  
Setelah seri ini selesai, tersisa **12 seri**.

---

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas cara membuat request **POST/PUT/PATCH command** aman terhadap:

```text id="3clurq"
- client retry karena timeout
- user double-click submit
- mobile network retry
- API gateway retry
- message/worker duplicate delivery
- pod crash di tengah proses
- response hilang padahal transaksi sukses
- concurrent duplicate request
```

Di stack **Java Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL + Redis + Camunda**, idempotency harus dipahami sebagai **business correctness pattern**, bukan sekadar Redis key.

Redis cocok sebagai **fast gate** karena `SET` mendukung conditional write seperti `NX` dan expiry seperti `EX/PX`, sehingga bisa membuat “hanya request pertama yang menang” dengan TTL. Tetapi invariant final tetap harus dijaga PostgreSQL lewat unique constraint, transaction, dan business state validation. Redis `SET` sendiri mendokumentasikan opsi `NX` untuk set hanya jika key belum ada, serta opsi expiry seperti `EX`/`PX`; Redis juga mencontohkan `SET resource-name value NX EX max-lock-time` sebagai pola atomic conditional set dengan expiry. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/set/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="9l41zj"
Idempotency =
  request yang sama boleh dikirim berkali-kali,
  tetapi efek bisnisnya terjadi satu kali,
  dan client mendapatkan hasil yang konsisten.
```

Contoh:

```text id="2ywu78"
POST /orders
Idempotency-Key: abc-123

Request pertama:
  create order O-9001
  return 201 Created

Request retry dengan key dan payload sama:
  return result yang sama / equivalent
  tidak membuat order baru

Request dengan key sama tapi payload beda:
  reject sebagai conflict
```

Stripe mendokumentasikan model serupa: client mengirim idempotency key unik, server menggunakannya untuk mengenali retry request, dan key yang sama sebaiknya tidak dipakai untuk request yang berbeda. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/idempotent_requests?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 3. Idempotency vs Deduplication vs Replay Protection

| Pattern | Fokus | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| **Idempotency** | Efek bisnis sama walau request diulang | `POST /orders` tidak membuat order ganda |
| **Deduplication** | Mendeteksi item/event/request sudah pernah diproses | Worker mengabaikan event ID yang sama |
| **Replay protection** | Mencegah request lama dipakai ulang secara berbahaya | Signed request dengan nonce + TTL |
| **Rate limiting** | Membatasi frekuensi | Maksimal 10 submit/menit |
| **Distributed lock** | Mengontrol critical section | Satu worker rebuild cache |
| **Optimistic locking** | Mencegah lost update | `version = expectedVersion` di PostgreSQL |

Kalimat penting:

```text id="o1tx9i"
Rate limiter menahan terlalu banyak request.
Idempotency membuat retry request aman.
Lock mengatur siapa yang bekerja sekarang.
Unique constraint memastikan invariant final tidak bocor.
```

Jangan mengganti idempotency dengan rate limiter. User bisa mengirim 2 request saja dan tetap menghasilkan duplicate business effect jika idempotency tidak ada.

---

## 4. Kapan Idempotency Wajib?

| Endpoint / action | Butuh idempotency? | Alasan |
|---|---:|---|
| `GET /cases/{id}` | Tidak | Secara HTTP seharusnya safe/read-only |
| `POST /orders` | Ya | Create bisa duplicate |
| `POST /payments` | Wajib | Efek finansial |
| `POST /cases/{id}/submit` | Ya | State transition tidak boleh double |
| `POST /cases/{id}/approve` | Ya | Decision/action penting |
| `POST /reports/export` | Ya | Job mahal bisa duplicate |
| `POST /notifications/send` | Ya | Bisa kirim dobel |
| Worker process external event | Ya | Event bisa redelivered |
| Camunda external task completion | Ya | Worker retry bisa duplicate |
| Login attempt | Biasanya bukan idempotency | Lebih cocok rate limit/replay nonce |
| Upload file chunk | Ya, untuk chunk/part | Retry upload harus aman |

Untuk domain enforcement/case management, idempotency wajib di command yang mengubah lifecycle:

```text id="ubflgs"
submit case
assign case
claim task
approve/reject decision
start workflow
complete external task
send notice
create enforcement action
generate official document
```

---

## 5. Diagram Besar

```text id="4mgbvd"
Client
  ↓
POST /orders
Idempotency-Key: k123
  ↓
JAX-RS Resource
  ↓
Command Service
  ↓
IdempotencyStore.checkOrStart(k123, requestHash)
  │
  ├── NEW / STARTED
  │     ↓
  │   execute business transaction
  │     ↓
  │   PostgreSQL commit
  │     ↓
  │   mark idempotency COMPLETED(resultRef)
  │     ↓
  │   return result
  │
  ├── COMPLETED same requestHash
  │     ↓
  │   replay result / return existing result
  │
  ├── PROCESSING same requestHash
  │     ↓
  │   return 202 / 409 / wait briefly
  │
  └── SAME KEY different requestHash
        ↓
      return 409 Conflict
```

---

## 6. Idempotency State Machine

```text id="7f0rcn"
[ABSENT]
   ↓ first valid request
[PROCESSING]
   ├── business success
   │     ↓
   │   [COMPLETED]
   │
   ├── retryable failure
   │     ↓
   │   [FAILED_RETRYABLE]
   │
   ├── final failure
   │     ↓
   │   [FAILED_FINAL]
   │
   └── timeout / crash
         ↓
       [UNKNOWN / STALE_PROCESSING]
```

### State Meaning

| State | Arti | Retry behavior |
|---|---|---|
| `ABSENT` | Belum pernah diproses | Request boleh start |
| `PROCESSING` | Request sedang diproses | Jangan eksekusi ulang langsung |
| `COMPLETED` | Efek bisnis sudah sukses | Replay result |
| `FAILED_RETRYABLE` | Gagal sementara | Retry boleh start lagi atau return retryable |
| `FAILED_FINAL` | Gagal validasi/domain | Return failure yang sama |
| `STALE_PROCESSING` | Redis bilang processing, tapi sudah terlalu lama | Reconcile ke PostgreSQL |

State `UNKNOWN` adalah bagian tersulit. Bisa terjadi ketika:

```text id="smnkjr"
- DB commit sukses, app crash sebelum mark COMPLETED
- external side effect sukses, response hilang
- Redis timeout setelah script mungkin sudah execute
- Redis key expired terlalu cepat
```

Karena itu PostgreSQL unique constraint/business key tetap wajib.

---

## 7. Key Design

Header dari client:

```text id="r85d6b"
Idempotency-Key: 3e9f4b52-....
```

Redis key:

```text id="yyluao"
{service}:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:idem:{operation}:v1:key:{idempotencyKeyHash}
```

Contoh:

```text id="bb3hin"
order-svc:prod:tenant:t1:idem:create-order:v1:key:sha256-k123
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:idem:submit-case:v1:key:sha256-k456
payment-svc:prod:tenant:t1:idem:create-payment:v1:key:sha256-k789
```

Jangan simpan raw idempotency key kalau key tersebut berasal dari client dan bisa dianggap secret/correlation-sensitive. Hash dulu.

### Key harus include operation

Buruk:

```text id="qiz57r"
idem:{key}
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="0osde0"
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:idem:submit-case:v1:key:{keyHash}
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:idem:approve-case:v1:key:{keyHash}
```

Kenapa?

```text id="wojz9a"
Idempotency-Key yang sama untuk operasi berbeda tidak boleh saling bertabrakan.
```

---

## 8. Request Hash

Idempotency key saja tidak cukup. Server harus memastikan key yang sama tidak dipakai untuk payload berbeda.

Canonical request hash:

```text id="j5sda4"
requestHash = SHA-256(
  method + "\n" +
  canonicalRoute + "\n" +
  tenantId + "\n" +
  canonicalJsonBody + "\n" +
  relevantQueryParams
)
```

Contoh:

```json id="kq5hie"
{
  "method": "POST",
  "route": "/orders",
  "tenantId": "t1",
  "body": {
    "customerId": "c1",
    "items": [
      {"sku": "A", "qty": 2}
    ]
  }
}
```

Rules:

```text id="t55e8q"
- canonicalize JSON: stable field order
- exclude volatile headers seperti Date, User-Agent, correlation ID
- include tenant
- include operation/route
- include fields yang memengaruhi business effect
- include authenticated principal jika result/effect user-specific
```

Jika same idempotency key + different request hash:

```text id="dsy5ny"
HTTP 409 Conflict
```

---

## 9. Redis Value Shape

Pakai Hash atau String JSON.

### Hash

```text id="t9pclx"
HSET idemKey
  requestHash sha256-request
  status PROCESSING
  operation create-order
  resultRef ""
  httpStatus ""
  responseBodyHash ""
  startedAt 2026-07-13T14:30:00+07:00
  updatedAt 2026-07-13T14:30:00+07:00
EXPIRE idemKey 172800
```

### JSON

```json id="m4ui73"
{
  "schemaVersion": 1,
  "requestHash": "sha256-request",
  "status": "COMPLETED",
  "operation": "create-order",
  "resultRef": "order:o-9001",
  "httpStatus": 201,
  "responseBody": {
    "orderId": "o-9001",
    "status": "SUBMITTED"
  },
  "startedAt": "2026-07-13T14:30:00+07:00",
  "completedAt": "2026-07-13T14:30:01+07:00"
}
```

### Pilihan

| Format | Cocok untuk | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| Hash | Update field status/resultRef | Enak untuk state machine |
| String JSON | Replay full response | Simpler, tapi rewrite full value |
| PostgreSQL table | Critical/auditable idempotency | Lebih kuat, lebih lambat |
| Redis + PostgreSQL | Fast gate + final invariant | Rekomendasi untuk command penting |

---

## 10. TTL Policy

| Use case | TTL rekomendasi |
|---|---:|
| Create order/case | 24-72 jam |
| Payment/financial command | Sesuai retry/compliance policy, sering lebih lama dan DB-backed |
| Submit/approve case | 24-72 jam |
| Export/report job | 12-48 jam |
| External event dedup | Sesuai retention event, bisa hari-minggu |
| Upload chunk | Sampai upload session selesai + buffer |
| Login nonce/replay protection | Menit |

Rule:

```text id="i1n3rj"
TTL harus lebih panjang dari periode retry client/gateway/worker.
```

Stripe menyebut idempotency key bisa dibersihkan setelah minimal 24 jam, dan reuse setelah pruning diperlakukan seperti request baru; itu cocok sebagai referensi API publik, tetapi domain Anda bisa butuh retention lebih lama untuk command kritikal. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/idempotent_requests?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 11. Basic Redis `SET NX EX` Pattern

Flow sederhana:

```text id="c661vw"
SET idemKey PROCESSING NX EX 172800
```

Jika return `OK`:

```text id="2h94ka"
request pertama menang
lanjut execute business command
```

Jika return nil:

```text id="bslkb6"
key sudah ada
baca existing state
putuskan replay / conflict / processing
```

Redis `SET` dengan `NX` dan `EX/PX` membuat conditional creation dan expiry menjadi satu command, menghindari race klasik “set berhasil tapi expire gagal”. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/set/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Namun untuk idempotency production, value biasanya perlu menyimpan `requestHash`, `status`, dan metadata. Karena itu sering lebih baik memakai Lua.

---

## 12. Atomic `checkOrStart` dengan Lua

Kita butuh operasi atomic:

```text id="kqgk5b"
If key absent:
  create PROCESSING with requestHash + TTL
  return STARTED

If key exists and requestHash same:
  return current status

If key exists and requestHash different:
  return CONFLICT
```

Lua cocok karena Redis menjamin script dieksekusi secara atomic di server; selama script berjalan, aktivitas server lain tidak berjalan bersamaan. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/programmability/eval-intro/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Lua Script

```lua id="b86mca"
local key = KEYS[1]

local requestHash = ARGV[1]
local operation = ARGV[2]
local now = ARGV[3]
local ttlSeconds = tonumber(ARGV[4])

local existingHash = redis.call("HGET", key, "requestHash")

if not existingHash then
  redis.call("HSET", key,
    "requestHash", requestHash,
    "operation", operation,
    "status", "PROCESSING",
    "resultRef", "",
    "httpStatus", "",
    "startedAt", now,
    "updatedAt", now
  )
  redis.call("EXPIRE", key, ttlSeconds)
  return {"STARTED", "PROCESSING", ""}
end

if existingHash ~= requestHash then
  return {"CONFLICT", redis.call("HGET", key, "status"), ""}
end

local status = redis.call("HGET", key, "status")
local resultRef = redis.call("HGET", key, "resultRef")

return {"EXISTS", status or "", resultRef or ""}
```

Return mapping:

| Result | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `STARTED` | Caller boleh execute business command |
| `EXISTS + COMPLETED` | Replay result |
| `EXISTS + PROCESSING` | Sedang diproses |
| `EXISTS + FAILED_*` | Return/retry sesuai policy |
| `CONFLICT` | Same key, different payload |

---

## 13. `markCompleted` dengan Lua

Setelah business transaction berhasil:

```lua id="k5g84x"
local key = KEYS[1]

local requestHash = ARGV[1]
local resultRef = ARGV[2]
local httpStatus = ARGV[3]
local responseBody = ARGV[4]
local now = ARGV[5]
local ttlSeconds = tonumber(ARGV[6])

local existingHash = redis.call("HGET", key, "requestHash")

if not existingHash then
  return {"MISSING"}
end

if existingHash ~= requestHash then
  return {"CONFLICT"}
end

redis.call("HSET", key,
  "status", "COMPLETED",
  "resultRef", resultRef,
  "httpStatus", httpStatus,
  "responseBody", responseBody,
  "completedAt", now,
  "updatedAt", now
)

redis.call("EXPIRE", key, ttlSeconds)

return {"COMPLETED"}
```

Catatan:

```text id="w8qsgw"
Untuk response besar, jangan simpan responseBody penuh di Redis.
Simpan resultRef lalu rebuild response dari PostgreSQL.
```

---

## 14. PostgreSQL Final Guard

Redis fast gate bisa gagal, expire, atau unavailable. PostgreSQL tetap harus menjaga final invariant.

Contoh tabel orders:

```sql id="vp901h"
CREATE TABLE orders (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    client_request_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    customer_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT uq_orders_client_request
        UNIQUE (tenant_id, client_request_id)
);
```

PostgreSQL unique index/constraint mencegah beberapa row memiliki nilai key yang sama; dokumentasi PostgreSQL menyatakan unique index tidak mengizinkan beberapa row dengan nilai indexed yang sama. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/indexes-unique.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Kenapa unique constraint wajib?

Sequence buruk tanpa unique constraint:

```text id="bcfrc2"
Redis timeout
  ↓
App menganggap belum ada key
  ↓
2 request concurrent masuk DB
  ↓
2 order tercipta
```

Dengan unique constraint:

```text id="cywubj"
2 request concurrent masuk DB
  ↓
hanya satu INSERT menang
  ↓
yang lain kena unique violation
  ↓
load existing result
```

---

## 15. Recommended Architecture: Redis + PostgreSQL

```text id="so4qfb"
Redis idempotency store
= fast gate
= short/medium-term replay cache
= reduce duplicate work

PostgreSQL unique/business table
= final correctness
= audit/recovery
= source of truth
```

Flow:

```text id="8uq2n5"
1. checkOrStart in Redis
2. if STARTED, execute DB transaction
3. DB insert/update guarded by unique constraint/version/state
4. commit
5. markCompleted in Redis
6. return response
```

Recovery if Redis incomplete:

```text id="quopoq"
Redis says PROCESSING too long
  ↓
check PostgreSQL by client_request_id
  ├── found -> mark Redis COMPLETED, replay
  └── not found -> allow retry / mark FAILED_RETRYABLE
```

---

## 16. JAX-RS Integration

Idempotency can be enforced in:

| Layer | Cocok untuk | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `ContainerRequestFilter` | Header validation, early rejection | Jangan execute business logic di filter |
| Resource | Extract request context | Thin only |
| Command Service | Main idempotency orchestration | Rekomendasi |
| Repository/DB | Unique constraint | Final guard |

### Filter untuk validasi header

Jakarta REST `ContainerRequestFilter` bisa menghentikan chain dengan `ContainerRequestContext.abortWith(Response)`, sehingga cocok untuk menolak request yang wajib punya idempotency key sebelum masuk resource method. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/3.1/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/container/containerrequestfilter?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="8scmli"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION)
public final class IdempotencyHeaderFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private static final Set<String> IDEMPOTENT_REQUIRED_ROUTES = Set.of(
        "POST /orders",
        "POST /cases/{caseId}/submit",
        "POST /reports/export"
    );

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        String route = canonicalRoute(ctx);

        if (!IDEMPOTENT_REQUIRED_ROUTES.contains(route)) {
            return;
        }

        String key = ctx.getHeaderString("Idempotency-Key");

        if (key == null || key.isBlank()) {
            ctx.abortWith(Response.status(400)
                .entity(new ErrorResponse(
                    "IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED",
                    "Idempotency-Key header is required for this operation."
                ))
                .build());
        }
    }
}
```

Tetapi orchestration `checkOrStart → execute → markCompleted` sebaiknya tetap di command service.

---

## 17. Command Service Shape

```java id="xaivju"
public final class OrderCommandService {

    private final IdempotencyStore idempotencyStore;
    private final OrderRepository orderRepository;
    private final TransactionRunner transactionRunner;
    private final Clock clock;

    public OrderResponse createOrder(
        TenantId tenantId,
        UserContext user,
        CreateOrderRequest request,
        String idempotencyKey
    ) {
        String requestHash = RequestHasher.hash(
            "POST",
            "/orders",
            tenantId,
            user.userId(),
            request
        );

        IdempotencyDecision decision = idempotencyStore.checkOrStart(
            tenantId,
            "create-order",
            idempotencyKey,
            requestHash,
            Duration.ofHours(48)
        );

        return switch (decision.type()) {
            case STARTED -> executeCreateOrder(
                tenantId,
                user,
                request,
                idempotencyKey,
                requestHash
            );

            case COMPLETED -> replayCreateOrderResult(
                tenantId,
                decision.resultRef()
            );

            case PROCESSING -> throw new RequestAlreadyProcessingException();

            case CONFLICT -> throw new IdempotencyConflictException();

            case FAILED_FINAL -> replayFailure(decision);

            case FAILED_RETRYABLE -> retryOrReturnRetryable(decision);
        };
    }
}
```

---

## 18. Execute + Mark Completed

```java id="ucd00r"
private OrderResponse executeCreateOrder(
    TenantId tenantId,
    UserContext user,
    CreateOrderRequest request,
    String idempotencyKey,
    String requestHash
) {
    OrderId orderId = transactionRunner.run(() -> {
        return orderRepository.insertOrder(
            tenantId,
            request,
            hash(idempotencyKey) // client_request_id
        );
    });

    OrderResponse response = orderRepository.findResponseById(tenantId, orderId);

    idempotencyStore.markCompleted(
        tenantId,
        "create-order",
        idempotencyKey,
        requestHash,
        "order:" + orderId.value(),
        201,
        response,
        Duration.ofHours(48)
    );

    return response;
}
```

### Important

`markCompleted` terjadi setelah DB commit. Kalau app crash sebelum `markCompleted`, retry akan melihat `PROCESSING`, lalu perlu reconciliation ke PostgreSQL.

---

## 19. Handling `PROCESSING`

Jika duplicate request datang saat request pertama masih diproses:

Pilihan response:

| Strategy | HTTP | Cocok untuk |
|---|---:|---|
| Return `409 Conflict` | 409 | Client harus retry later |
| Return `202 Accepted` | 202 | Async command/job |
| Wait briefly then replay | 200/201 if complete | Low latency command |
| Return operation status URL | 202 | Long-running process |
| Poll by idempotency key | 200/202 | API yang support status endpoint |

Untuk command cepat:

```text id="5x9gsi"
wait 50-200 ms
check Redis again
if completed -> replay
else -> 409/202
```

Untuk command panjang seperti export/report:

```text id="3q42x9"
return 202 Accepted
Location: /operations/{operationId}
```

---

## 20. Handling Conflict

Same idempotency key, different payload:

```text id="7ccj9p"
HTTP 409 Conflict
```

Response:

```json id="bnpq50"
{
  "code": "IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_CONFLICT",
  "message": "The provided Idempotency-Key was already used with a different request payload."
}
```

Jangan execute business command.

Jangan return existing result, karena client mungkin salah reuse key.

---

## 21. Replay Response Strategy

Ada dua pendekatan.

### A. Store full response in Redis

```json id="p6a2ky"
{
  "status": "COMPLETED",
  "httpStatus": 201,
  "responseBody": {
    "orderId": "o-9001",
    "status": "SUBMITTED"
  }
}
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="h88vk8"
- replay cepat
- tidak perlu DB read
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="tx68vz"
- memory lebih besar
- response schema migration sulit
- sensitive data risk
```

### B. Store result reference

```json id="jzh2sf"
{
  "status": "COMPLETED",
  "resultRef": "order:o-9001"
}
```

Replay:

```text id="4slj15"
read resultRef
  ↓
load current/preserved response from PostgreSQL
  ↓
return response
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="l854dy"
- Redis value kecil
- source tetap PostgreSQL
- lebih aman untuk PII
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="yy6xo4"
- replay perlu DB read
- response mungkin berubah jika bukan snapshot
```

Rekomendasi untuk domain Anda:

```text id="h2ip1k"
Simpan resultRef di Redis.
Simpan source/audit/result di PostgreSQL.
Simpan full response hanya untuk payload kecil dan non-sensitive.
```

---

## 22. Handling Failure

| Failure type | State | Retry behavior |
|---|---|---|
| Validation error | `FAILED_FINAL` | Replay same failure |
| Authorization denied | Biasanya jangan cache terlalu lama | Bisa berubah jika permission berubah |
| DB transient error | `FAILED_RETRYABLE` atau delete key | Client boleh retry |
| Redis markCompleted fail after DB commit | `PROCESSING` stale | Reconcile DB |
| App crash before DB commit | `PROCESSING` stale | Reconcile, then retry |
| External side effect unknown | `UNKNOWN` | Reconcile external system |
| Request hash conflict | `CONFLICT` | Reject 409 |

### Validation failure

Boleh simpan `FAILED_FINAL` jika request sama akan selalu gagal:

```text id="vw1s5a"
invalid field format
missing required business field
invalid enum
```

Tapi hati-hati untuk failure yang bisa berubah:

```text id="x15n6o"
permission denied
insufficient quota
case state temporarily locked
```

---

## 23. Stale `PROCESSING` Recovery

Problem:

```text id="w2nh2a"
Redis status = PROCESSING
startedAt = 30 minutes ago
```

Kemungkinan:

```text id="1lp0zg"
- request masih berjalan sangat lama
- app crash
- DB commit sukses tapi Redis markCompleted gagal
- DB commit gagal tapi Redis masih processing
```

Recovery flow:

```text id="nwd8us"
If PROCESSING age > processingTimeout:
  check PostgreSQL by client_request_id / business id
    ├── found success result
    │     mark Redis COMPLETED
    │     replay result
    │
    ├── found failed final state
    │     mark Redis FAILED_FINAL
    │     replay failure
    │
    └── not found
          mark Redis FAILED_RETRYABLE or delete key
          allow retry
```

Do not blindly delete stale `PROCESSING` for critical command without checking DB.

---

## 24. PostgreSQL Idempotency Table

Untuk command yang benar-benar penting, buat tabel idempotency di PostgreSQL juga.

```sql id="o2w7yl"
CREATE TABLE idempotency_records (
    tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    operation TEXT NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
    request_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL,
    result_type TEXT NULL,
    result_id TEXT NULL,
    http_status INT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, operation, idempotency_key_hash)
);
```

Redis tetap dipakai untuk fast path, PostgreSQL untuk recovery/audit.

### Kapan perlu DB table?

```text id="dijnj3"
- payment/order critical
- regulatory action
- official document generation
- external side effect expensive
- compliance audit perlu
- retry window lebih lama dari TTL Redis
```

---

## 25. Insert Idempotency Record Pattern

```sql id="geppgd"
INSERT INTO idempotency_records (
    tenant_id,
    operation,
    idempotency_key_hash,
    request_hash,
    status
)
VALUES (
    #{tenantId},
    #{operation},
    #{idempotencyKeyHash},
    #{requestHash},
    'PROCESSING'
)
ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, operation, idempotency_key_hash)
DO NOTHING;
```

Jika insert count = 1:

```text id="17e156"
request pertama boleh proses
```

Jika insert count = 0:

```text id="h3pah8"
load existing record
compare requestHash
return replay/conflict/processing
```

PostgreSQL unique constraint memberi final guard walau Redis gagal.

---

## 26. MyBatis Mapper Example

```java id="f7wfrb"
public interface IdempotencyMapper {

    int insertProcessing(IdempotencyRecord record);

    IdempotencyRecord find(
        @Param("tenantId") String tenantId,
        @Param("operation") String operation,
        @Param("keyHash") String keyHash
    );

    int markCompleted(
        @Param("tenantId") String tenantId,
        @Param("operation") String operation,
        @Param("keyHash") String keyHash,
        @Param("resultType") String resultType,
        @Param("resultId") String resultId,
        @Param("httpStatus") int httpStatus
    );

    int markFailed(
        @Param("tenantId") String tenantId,
        @Param("operation") String operation,
        @Param("keyHash") String keyHash,
        @Param("status") String status
    );
}
```

---

## 27. Redis-Only vs DB-Backed Idempotency

| Model | Cocok untuk | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| Redis-only | Low-risk command, short retry window | Redis loss/expiry bisa allow duplicate |
| DB unique only | Simpler correctness, less replay speed | Lebih banyak DB load |
| Redis + DB unique | Production balanced | Kompleksitas lebih tinggi |
| Redis + DB idempotency table | Critical/auditable | Paling defensible |

Rekomendasi:

```text id="fmgla4"
Low-risk:
  Redis-only + natural business uniqueness

Medium-risk:
  Redis + PostgreSQL unique constraint

High-risk:
  Redis + PostgreSQL idempotency table + business unique constraint
```

---

## 28. Idempotency for Camunda / Workflow Commands

Contoh:

```text id="7hwdvo"
POST /cases/{caseId}/submit
```

Risiko:

```text id="xgqz07"
- start process instance dua kali
- complete task dua kali
- send signal dua kali
- external task worker retry
```

Pattern:

```text id="y8hhzl"
Idempotency key
  ↓
Redis checkOrStart
  ↓
PostgreSQL transition with expected state/version
  ↓
Camunda command with business key
  ↓
markCompleted
```

Camunda boundary:

```text id="nni7tm"
Redis tidak menggantikan process state.
PostgreSQL/Camunda tetap memvalidasi transition.
```

Gunakan business key/unique process start guard jika workflow tidak boleh double-start.

---

## 29. Idempotency for External Side Effects

Contoh side effect:

```text id="y8xlip"
- call payment provider
- send email/SMS
- submit document to external regulator
- create ticket in external system
```

Pattern wajib:

```text id="ptpfv5"
1. Generate stable external request id
2. Store outbound request record in PostgreSQL
3. Use provider idempotency mechanism if available
4. Retry with same external idempotency key
5. Store provider response
6. Mark internal idempotency completed
```

Jangan:

```text id="qa5gam"
Redis says STARTED
  ↓
call external provider with random new id each retry
```

Itu bisa membuat side effect ganda walau internal Redis punya idempotency key.

---

## 30. Idempotency for Worker/Event Dedup

Untuk worker:

```text id="03ut9v"
eventId / jobId / externalMessageId = dedup key
```

Redis key:

```text id="c2b9o8"
worker-svc:prod:tenant:t1:dedup:event:v1:id:sha256-event
```

Pattern:

```text id="zxrzof"
SET dedupKey PROCESSING NX EX retention
  ├── OK -> process event
  └── nil -> already seen; skip or inspect state
```

But final correctness still:

```text id="0xaq1n"
- DB unique event processing table
- idempotent side effect
- event offset/ack after durable processing
```

---

## 31. Replay Protection for Signed Requests

Replay protection berbeda dari idempotency.

Use case:

```text id="czvqhq"
partner sends signed webhook/request
attacker replays same signed request later
```

Pattern:

```text id="fmxtnt"
signature valid?
timestamp within allowed skew?
nonce not seen?
  SET nonceKey seen NX EX 300
```

Redis key:

```text id="p12wo5"
api-svc:prod:tenant:t1:replay-nonce:v1:partner:pHash:nonce:nHash
```

If `SET NX EX` fails:

```text id="xs69y1"
reject replay
```

TTL biasanya pendek:

```text id="o7nr7b"
5-15 minutes
```

Jangan gunakan TTL panjang untuk nonce tanpa alasan, karena memory bisa tumbuh besar.

---

## 32. Response Semantics

| Situation | Recommended HTTP |
|---|---:|
| Missing idempotency key for required command | 400 |
| Invalid key format | 400 |
| First request accepted and completed sync | 201/200 |
| Duplicate completed same request | 200/201 equivalent replay |
| Same key, different payload | 409 |
| Same request still processing | 202 or 409 |
| Stale processing, recovery pending | 202/503 retryable |
| Redis unavailable for critical command | 503 retryable |
| DB unique violation but existing result found | 200/201 replay |
| Final validation failure same request | Same 4xx replay or re-evaluate |

Note:

```text id="a6lerd"
Jika original response 201, replay bisa tetap 201 atau 200 tergantung API contract.
Yang penting: tidak menciptakan efek bisnis baru dan response konsisten.
```

Stripe, misalnya, menyimpan status code dan body dari request pertama untuk idempotency key tertentu, termasuk failure tertentu; desain internal Anda bisa memilih replay resultRef dari DB, tetapi harus konsisten dalam kontrak API. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/idempotent_requests?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 33. Idempotency Key Validation

Rules:

```text id="x72elu"
- required untuk command endpoint tertentu
- max length, misalnya 128/255 char
- allowed characters jelas
- reject blank
- normalize exactly? usually do not lowercase arbitrary keys
- hash before Redis key
```

Example:

```java id="mq7dz5"
public final class IdempotencyKeyValidator {

    private static final Pattern ALLOWED =
        Pattern.compile("^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{8,128}$");

    public void validate(String key) {
        if (key == null || key.isBlank()) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Idempotency-Key is required");
        }

        if (!ALLOWED.matcher(key).matches()) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Invalid Idempotency-Key format");
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 34. Request Hash Canonicalization Pitfalls

| Pitfall | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| JSON field order beda | Hash beda untuk payload sama | Canonical JSON |
| Include timestamp volatile | Retry dianggap conflict | Exclude volatile fields |
| Exclude tenant | Cross-tenant collision risk | Include tenant |
| Exclude user/scope | Replay lintas user risk | Include auth context if relevant |
| Include raw body whitespace | Hash beda | Parse + canonicalize |
| Different default values | Hash beda | Normalize defaults |
| Multipart upload | Hash sulit | Use upload session/chunk checksum |
| Large file | Hash expensive | Use content checksum/metadata |

---

## 35. Concurrency Scenarios

### Scenario A - Two identical requests arrive together

```text id="2zv8j2"
R1 checkOrStart -> STARTED
R2 checkOrStart -> EXISTS PROCESSING
R1 commits DB
R1 markCompleted
R2 retry/poll -> replay
```

### Scenario B - Redis allows one, DB unique catches duplicate

```text id="n4klod"
R1 Redis STARTED
Redis temporarily unavailable for R2
R2 goes DB fallback
DB unique constraint allows only one
```

### Scenario C - App crash after DB commit

```text id="z5dtcn"
R1 Redis PROCESSING
R1 DB commit success
R1 crashes before markCompleted
R2 sees PROCESSING stale
R2 checks DB by client_request_id
R2 marks COMPLETED
R2 replays result
```

### Scenario D - Same key different payload

```text id="hv5fu2"
R1 key k1 requestHash A -> STARTED/COMPLETED
R2 key k1 requestHash B -> CONFLICT
```

---

## 36. Redis Timeout Semantics

Careful:

```text id="s13ucd"
Client times out waiting for Redis Lua response.
```

The script may have:

```text id="qxwla6"
- not executed
- executed and response lost
```

Therefore:

```text id="6atc0b"
For critical command, do not blindly retry as new.
Retry checkOrStart with same idempotency key and same requestHash.
```

Because Redis Lua script atomicity does not mean client knows result if network timeout occurs.

---

## 37. Observability

Metrics:

```text id="iy560k"
idempotency.check.count
idempotency.started.count
idempotency.completed.count
idempotency.replayed.count
idempotency.conflict.count
idempotency.processing.count
idempotency.failed_retryable.count
idempotency.failed_final.count
idempotency.stale_processing.count
idempotency.recovered_from_db.count
idempotency.redis.timeout.count
idempotency.redis.error.count
idempotency.db_unique_violation.count
```

Labels:

```text id="waoj90"
service
env
operation
result
exception_class
```

Do not label with:

```text id="6q1l3t"
raw idempotency key
userId
requestHash
Redis key
```

---

## 38. Logging

Good log:

```json id="dkoszt"
{
  "event": "idempotency_replayed",
  "operation": "create-order",
  "tenantHash": "sha256-tenant",
  "keyHash": "sha256-key",
  "resultRef": "order:o-9001",
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

Conflict log:

```json id="qq9554"
{
  "event": "idempotency_conflict",
  "operation": "submit-case",
  "tenantHash": "sha256-tenant",
  "keyHash": "sha256-key",
  "reason": "same_key_different_request_hash",
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

Jangan log:

```text id="h68wty"
- raw idempotency key
- full request body sensitif
- token/API key
- raw Redis key jika mengandung identifier sensitif
```

---

## 39. Testing Matrix

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Missing key rejected | Header enforcement |
| Invalid key rejected | Input validation |
| First request starts | Basic path |
| Same key + same payload replays | Idempotency |
| Same key + different payload conflicts | Safety |
| Concurrent same request only one executes | Atomicity |
| DB unique violation loads existing result | Final guard |
| Crash after DB commit before Redis completed | Recovery |
| Redis timeout during check | Retry behavior |
| Redis timeout during markCompleted | Stale processing recovery |
| TTL expiry after retry window | Behavior documented |
| Validation failure replay | Consistent errors |
| External side effect retry uses same external key | No duplicate side effect |
| Worker duplicate event skipped | Dedup |
| Sensitive key not logged | Security |
| Metrics emitted | Operability |

---

## 40. Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Redis-only untuk payment/order final | Duplicate risk saat Redis loss | DB unique/idempotency table |
| Idempotency key tanpa request hash | Payload berbeda bisa replay salah | Store/compare requestHash |
| Key tidak include operation | Collision antar endpoint | Include operation |
| Key tidak include tenant | Cross-tenant collision | Include tenant |
| TTL terlalu pendek | Retry lama jadi duplicate | Align with retry window |
| Simpan full response sensitif di Redis | PII/security risk | Store resultRef |
| PROCESSING stale tidak direconcile | Client stuck | DB recovery flow |
| markCompleted sebelum DB commit | Replay success palsu | Commit first |
| External call tanpa external idempotency key | Side effect duplicate | Stable external request ID |
| Unique constraint tidak ada | Redis failure bisa duplicate | PostgreSQL constraint |
| Same key different payload diterima | Data corruption | 409 conflict |
| Raw key di log/metric | Secret/PII leak | Hash/mask |

---

## 41. Senior Engineer Checklist

```text id="3h2zxs"
[ ] Endpoint command wajib idempotency sudah diidentifikasi
[ ] Header Idempotency-Key required untuk endpoint tersebut
[ ] Key validation ada
[ ] Redis key include service/env/tenant/operation/keyHash
[ ] Request hash canonical dan include relevant business inputs
[ ] Same key different payload -> 409
[ ] State machine PROCESSING/COMPLETED/FAILED jelas
[ ] Redis checkOrStart atomic via SET NX EX atau Lua
[ ] TTL sesuai retry window
[ ] PostgreSQL unique constraint / idempotency table ada untuk command penting
[ ] DB commit dilakukan sebelum markCompleted
[ ] Stale PROCESSING recovery mengecek PostgreSQL/source of truth
[ ] External side effect memakai stable external idempotency key
[ ] Replay response strategy jelas: full response vs resultRef
[ ] Redis failure policy jelas
[ ] Metrics/logging aman dari PII
[ ] Concurrency tests ada
```

---

## 42. Ringkasan Praktis

```text id="m6d2ge"
Idempotency key:
  mengenali retry request yang sama

Request hash:
  mencegah key sama dipakai untuk payload berbeda

Redis:
  fast gate, PROCESSING/COMPLETED state, TTL, replay cache

PostgreSQL:
  final invariant, unique constraint, recovery, audit

Command service:
  checkOrStart
  execute transaction
  markCompleted
  replay or conflict

Failure recovery:
  stale PROCESSING harus dicek ke PostgreSQL
```

Kalimat kunci:

> Idempotency yang baik bukan sekadar “simpan key di Redis”. Idempotency yang baik memastikan efek bisnis terjadi satu kali, response retry tetap konsisten, dan correctness tetap bertahan walau Redis timeout, app crash, atau request dikirim bersamaan.
