# Seri 11 - Redis-backed Job Queue

Status: **Seri 11 dari 22 seri lanjutan**.  
Setelah seri ini selesai, tersisa **11 seri**.

---

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas penggunaan Redis sebagai **job queue** untuk background processing dalam aplikasi:

```text id="8e1kyc"
Java 17+
Jersey/JAX-RS
MyBatis
PostgreSQL
Camunda
Redis
Docker/Kubernetes
```

Redis job queue cocok ketika request HTTP tidak perlu menunggu pekerjaan berat selesai, misalnya:

```text id="10kdtz"
- kirim email/notifikasi
- generate report
- call external API
- proses file upload
- webhook delivery
- image/document processing
- async cache rebuild
- lightweight workflow helper
```

Redis sendiri mendokumentasikan job queue sebagai pola untuk memindahkan background work dari user-facing application ke worker, dengan contoh seperti email, payment processing, image processing, ML inference, dan webhook. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/use-cases/job-queue/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="tuc0e3"
HTTP request path
= cepat, bounded latency, user-facing

Job queue
= async work, retryable, worker-driven

PostgreSQL
= source of truth job/business state

Redis
= fast queue / stream / scheduling layer

Kafka
= durable event backbone lintas service

Camunda
= workflow state dan BPMN orchestration
```

Kalimat penting:

```text id="42u610"
Redis queue boleh mempercepat dan mempermudah async processing,
tetapi jangan menjadikannya satu-satunya bukti bisnis untuk pekerjaan critical.
```

Untuk job yang berdampak bisnis besar, job record tetap sebaiknya ada di PostgreSQL. Redis bisa menjadi queue signal/execution layer.

---

## 3. Redis Queue Primitive: List vs Sorted Set vs Stream

| Primitive | Cocok untuk | Kelebihan | Kelemahan |
|---|---|---|---|
| **List** | Simple FIFO queue | Sangat sederhana, blocking pop tersedia | Ack/retry/recovery manual |
| **Sorted Set** | Delayed job/scheduler | Score bisa menjadi `executeAt` | Bukan queue ack-based |
| **Stream** | Worker queue dengan consumer group | Ack, pending entries, replay terbatas | Lebih kompleks |
| **Pub/Sub** | Wake-up signal | Realtime ringan | Bukan queue, message bisa hilang |
| **Kafka** | Durable cross-service event/job | Retention, replay, partitioning kuat | Operational lebih berat |
| **PostgreSQL job table** | Critical/auditable job | Durable, transactional | Polling/locking perlu didesain |

Redis Lists punya blocking pop seperti `BLPOP`, yang memblokir connection sampai ada elemen tersedia. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/blpop/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Redis Streams adalah append-only log yang mendukung strategi konsumsi kompleks seperti consumer groups. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/data-types/streams/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4. Diagram Besar

```text id="sin1wq"
HTTP Request
  ↓
JAX-RS Resource
  ↓
Application Service
  ↓
PostgreSQL transaction
  ├── update business state
  └── insert job record / outbox record
  ↓
Commit
  ↓
Enqueue job to Redis
  ↓
Return 202/201/200 to client

Worker Pod
  ↓
Read job from Redis
  ↓
Load job/business state from PostgreSQL
  ↓
Process job
  ↓
Update PostgreSQL status
  ↓
Ack/remove job from Redis
  ↓
Retry or DLQ on failure
```

Rule utama:

```text id="s5i0eq"
Queue message should identify work.
PostgreSQL should describe the durable work state.
```

Jangan menyimpan semua business payload penting hanya di Redis queue jika job tidak boleh hilang.

---

## 5. Kapan Redis Job Queue Cocok?

| Use case | Redis queue cocok? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Kirim email non-critical | Ya | Idempotency tetap perlu |
| Generate report async | Ya | Job record di DB |
| Webhook delivery retry | Ya | Store delivery state di DB |
| Cache rebuild | Ya | Redis-only sering cukup |
| File post-processing | Ya | File/object metadata di DB |
| External API call worker | Ya | Job state di DB |
| Payment settlement | Hati-hati | DB/provider idempotency wajib |
| Regulatory decision finalization | Tidak sebagai primary | Camunda/PostgreSQL/Kafka |
| Cross-service domain event | Lebih cocok Kafka | Redis Stream bisa internal only |
| Long-running workflow state | Camunda | Redis hanya helper |

---

## 6. Basic Queue Lifecycle

```text id="w44z08"
[CREATED]
   ↓ enqueue
[QUEUED]
   ↓ worker claim
[PROCESSING]
   ├── success
   │     ↓
   │   [COMPLETED]
   │
   ├── retryable failure
   │     ↓
   │   [RETRY_SCHEDULED]
   │     ↓ due
   │   [QUEUED]
   │
   ├── max retry exceeded
   │     ↓
   │   [DEAD_LETTERED]
   │
   └── worker crash
         ↓ visibility timeout / pending recovery
       [QUEUED or CLAIMED_BY_OTHER]
```

Job state harus eksplisit. Kalau status hanya ada sebagai item di queue, Anda akan sulit menjawab:

```text id="b5pzrt"
- job ini sudah diproses belum?
- siapa worker terakhir?
- kenapa gagal?
- sudah retry berapa kali?
- boleh replay tidak?
- user harus melihat status apa?
```

---

## 7. Job Record di PostgreSQL

Untuk job penting, buat tabel:

```sql id="pwikvf"
CREATE TABLE async_jobs (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    job_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    result JSONB NULL,
    attempt_count INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    max_attempts INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 3,
    next_run_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
    locked_by TEXT NULL,
    locked_until TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
    error_code TEXT NULL,
    error_message TEXT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

Redis queue message cukup berisi:

```json id="2yqzop"
{
  "jobId": "uuid",
  "tenantId": "t1",
  "jobType": "SEND_EMAIL",
  "schemaVersion": 1
}
```

Bukan:

```json id="kwdvzj"
{
  "allBusinessData": "... huge critical payload only in Redis ..."
}
```

---

## 8. Option A - Redis List Queue

### 8.1 Mental Model

Redis List queue paling sederhana:

```text id="im628h"
Producer:
  RPUSH queue job

Consumer:
  BLPOP queue timeout
```

`BLPOP` adalah blocking list pop primitive; ia memblokir connection ketika list kosong sampai ada elemen yang bisa di-pop atau timeout. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/blpop/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 8.2 Key Design

```text id="obcxja"
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:list:email-task:v1
```

### 8.3 Producer

```text id="qtkw27"
RPUSH job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:list:email-task:v1 '{"jobId":"..."}'
```

### 8.4 Consumer

```text id="9n0bhr"
BLPOP job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:list:email-task:v1 5
```

### 8.5 Pros / Cons

| Aspek | Nilai |
|---|---|
| Simplicity | Sangat tinggi |
| Throughput | Baik |
| Consumer group | Tidak ada |
| Ack | Tidak native |
| Retry | Manual |
| Recovery worker crash | Manual |
| Cocok untuk | Non-critical/simple jobs |

Problem besar List sederhana:

```text id="78y1n7"
Worker BLPOP job
  ↓
Job hilang dari list
  ↓
Worker crash sebelum selesai
  ↓
Job hilang kecuali ada recovery design
```

---

## 9. Reliable Queue dengan List + Processing List

Redis `LMOVE` docs menjelaskan pola reliable queue: item dipindahkan dari queue utama ke processing queue, lalu setelah berhasil diproses, item dihapus dari processing queue; jika consumer crash, item masih ada di processing queue dan bisa dipulihkan. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/lmove/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Flow

```text id="88n78a"
Producer:
  LPUSH mainQueue job

Worker:
  BLMOVE mainQueue processingQueue RIGHT LEFT timeout
  process job
  LREM processingQueue 1 job
```

### Diagram

```text id="8lpo9x"
mainQueue
  ↓ BLMOVE
processingQueue
  ↓ process success
LREM processingQueue

worker crash:
processingQueue still contains job
  ↓ recovery worker
move back to mainQueue
```

### Caveat

Processing list recovery butuh:

```text id="y4sn3m"
- timestamp/lease metadata
- scanner untuk stuck processing item
- idempotent job processing
- max retry
- DLQ
```

Karena List item sendiri tidak punya structured pending metadata seperti Stream consumer group.

---

## 10. Option B - Sorted Set Delayed Queue

### 10.1 Mental Model

Sorted Set cocok untuk delayed job dan retry scheduling:

```text id="x7wbky"
score = executeAt epoch millis
member = jobId
```

Key:

```text id="uk0hhg"
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:zschedule:email-retry:v1
```

### 10.2 Producer

```text id="etglz6"
ZADD retryQueue executeAtMillis jobId
```

### 10.3 Worker

```text id="7ncqe7"
ZRANGEBYSCORE retryQueue -inf now LIMIT 0 100
  ↓
for each jobId:
  acquire lock / claim in DB
  ZREM retryQueue jobId
  enqueue to Stream/List or process
```

### 10.4 Cocok untuk

```text id="2acwqw"
- retry delay
- scheduled reminder
- timeout scanning
- delayed webhook delivery
- job backoff
```

### 10.5 Tidak cukup untuk

```text id="pkn8ml"
- ack-based processing
- consumer group
- pending recovery
```

Sorted Set biasanya dipasangkan dengan Stream/List atau DB job table.

---

## 11. Option C - Redis Streams Job Queue

### 11.1 Mental Model

Redis Stream adalah append-only log dengan entry ID. Dengan consumer group, beberapa worker dapat memproses stream yang sama secara paralel, dan Redis melacak message yang sudah dikirim ke consumer tetapi belum di-ack melalui pending entries. Redis docs menjelaskan Stream mendukung consumer groups dan cocok untuk strategi konsumsi lebih kompleks. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/data-types/streams/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 11.2 Key Design

```text id="fo0zmb"
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task:v1
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task-dlq:v1
```

Consumer group:

```text id="12p7cj"
email-worker-v1
```

Consumer name:

```text id="s89iyq"
pod-name + ":" + worker-thread
```

---

## 12. Stream Producer

```text id="twsnso"
XADD job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task:v1 * 
  jobId 8c44...
  tenantId t1
  jobType SEND_EMAIL
  schemaVersion 1
```

`XADD` menambahkan entry ke Stream. Redis Streams entry berisi field-value pairs, sehingga cocok untuk membawa job metadata kecil. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/data-types/streams/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Recommended payload:

```text id="4tmpff"
jobId only + minimal metadata
```

Avoid:

```text id="qwpvd2"
huge payload
PII-heavy payload
irrecoverable business data only in Redis
```

---

## 13. Consumer Group Setup

```text id="zivv6e"
XGROUP CREATE streamKey groupName $ MKSTREAM
```

Mode start:

| Start ID | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `$` | Group mulai dari message baru setelah group dibuat |
| `0` | Group mulai dari awal stream |

Untuk job queue baru biasanya `$` cukup jika group dibuat sebelum producer aktif. Untuk migration/replay, bisa mulai dari `0`.

---

## 14. Worker Read Loop

```text id="0cva4q"
XREADGROUP GROUP email-worker-v1 pod-a
  COUNT 10
  BLOCK 5000
  STREAMS streamKey >
```

`XREADGROUP` adalah varian `XREAD` yang mendukung consumer groups. Ia membaca message baru untuk consumer dalam group, dan bisa digunakan dengan blocking read. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/xreadgroup/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Flow:

```text id="vt4qfo"
Read batch
  ↓
For each entry:
  load job from PostgreSQL
  claim/update job status
  process
  update DB result
  XACK
```

---

## 15. Acknowledgement

Setelah job selesai:

```text id="3zf4c7"
XACK streamKey groupName entryId
```

`XACK` memberi tahu Redis bahwa message sudah berhasil diproses dan dapat dihapus dari pending list consumer group. Redis docs menjelaskan XACK dipakai setelah consumer selesai memproses entry. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/use-cases/streaming/nodejs/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Important:

```text id="ah2sbq"
Ack hanya setelah side effect/job state durable selesai.
```

Jangan:

```text id="2ps7hi"
XACK sebelum update PostgreSQL
```

Karena kalau worker crash setelah ack tetapi sebelum DB update, job dianggap selesai di Redis padahal belum selesai.

---

## 16. Pending Entries dan Recovery

Jika worker membaca message tetapi crash sebelum `XACK`, message masuk **pending entries list** untuk consumer group.

Recovery flow:

```text id="u2k56l"
XPENDING stream group
  ↓
find entries idle > visibilityTimeout
  ↓
XAUTOCLAIM to another consumer
  ↓
retry processing
```

`XAUTOCLAIM` mengubah/mengambil ownership message pending dalam consumer group berdasarkan minimum idle time. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/xautoclaim/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Conceptually:

```text id="ey704g"
Message delivered to worker A
  ↓
Worker A crash
  ↓
Message remains pending
  ↓
Worker B claims after idle timeout
  ↓
Worker B processes and XACKs
```

---

## 17. Visibility Timeout Equivalent

Redis Streams tidak memakai istilah “visibility timeout” seperti SQS, tetapi behavior-nya mirip dengan:

```text id="f66hd9"
pending entry idle time > threshold
  ↓
another consumer may claim
```

Design parameter:

```text id="4fp6xf"
minIdleTime = expectedMaxProcessingTime + safety buffer
```

Contoh:

```text id="iqvv8d"
email job expected 5s
minIdleTime 60s

report job expected 5min
minIdleTime 10min
```

Kalau terlalu pendek:

```text id="5s6suv"
Job yang masih diproses bisa di-claim worker lain
  → duplicate processing
```

Kalau terlalu panjang:

```text id="d19ipf"
Crash recovery lambat
```

---

## 18. Retry Strategy

Retry harus dikontrol, bukan infinite loop.

### Retry state di PostgreSQL

```text id="xv2eby"
attempt_count
max_attempts
next_run_at
last_error
status
```

### Flow

```text id="qq7x7s"
Worker fails job
  ↓
attempt_count += 1
  ↓
if attempt_count < max_attempts:
    schedule retry
else:
    move to DLQ / DEAD_LETTERED
```

Backoff:

```text id="ix1i64"
retryDelay = min(baseDelay * 2^attempt, maxDelay)
plus jitter
```

Contoh:

```text id="qjkqo6"
attempt 1: 10s
attempt 2: 30s
attempt 3: 2m
attempt 4: 10m
attempt 5: DLQ
```

---

## 19. Retry with Stream + ZSET

Pattern yang rapi:

```text id="r50x7b"
Main Stream:
  immediate work

Retry ZSET:
  delayed retries

DLQ Stream:
  exhausted failures
```

Flow failure:

```text id="sv4019"
Worker reads Stream entry
  ↓
processing fails retryable
  ↓
update DB job status RETRY_SCHEDULED
  ↓
ZADD retryZset nextRunAt jobId
  ↓
XACK original Stream entry
```

Retry scheduler:

```text id="242n6x"
ZRANGEBYSCORE retryZset -inf now
  ↓
XADD mainStream jobId ...
  ↓
ZREM retryZset jobId
```

Kenapa `XACK` setelah schedule retry?

```text id="kbq9dg"
Original message sudah ditangani.
Retry direpresentasikan oleh retry schedule baru.
```

---

## 20. Dead Letter Queue

DLQ adalah tempat untuk job yang gagal setelah retry maksimal.

Redis tutorial job queue berbasis Streams secara eksplisit membahas retry failed jobs, memindahkan exhausted work ke dead-letter stream, dan inspeksi job state. ([Redis](https://redis.io/tutorials/redis-backed-job-queue-for-background-workers/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

DLQ stream key:

```text id="wgpvmm"
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task-dlq:v1
```

DLQ entry:

```text id="uklij5"
XADD dlqStream *
  jobId ...
  originalStream ...
  originalEntryId ...
  errorCode SMTP_TIMEOUT
  attempts 5
  failedAt ...
```

DLQ bukan tempat sampah tanpa owner. Harus ada:

```text id="jxhsxy"
- dashboard
- alert
- replay policy
- manual resolution
- retention/trim
```

---

## 21. Idempotent Worker

Worker harus aman terhadap duplicate processing karena:

```text id="3ric0k"
- message pending bisa di-claim ulang
- worker bisa crash setelah side effect tapi sebelum ack
- producer bisa enqueue duplicate
- retry bisa berjalan bersamaan jika lock/claim buruk
```

Pattern:

```text id="5a43e1"
Worker receives jobId
  ↓
DB claim:
  UPDATE async_jobs
  SET status='PROCESSING', locked_by=?, locked_until=?
  WHERE id=?
    AND status IN ('QUEUED','RETRY_SCHEDULED')
    AND (locked_until IS NULL OR locked_until < now())
  RETURNING *
```

Jika update count = 0:

```text id="quzzu7"
job already processed/claimed
  ↓
XACK stream entry if safe
```

Final guard:

```text id="keercb"
External side effect must use stable idempotency key.
```

---

## 22. Worker Claim di PostgreSQL

```sql id="ksffsl"
UPDATE async_jobs
SET status = 'PROCESSING',
    locked_by = #{workerId},
    locked_until = now() + interval '5 minutes',
    attempt_count = attempt_count + 1,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE id = #{jobId}
  AND status IN ('QUEUED', 'RETRY_SCHEDULED')
  AND (locked_until IS NULL OR locked_until < now())
RETURNING *;
```

Ini membuat Redis message duplicate tidak otomatis menghasilkan duplicate business work.

---

## 23. Producer Pattern: Transaction + Enqueue

Problem:

```text id="ov0xly"
DB insert job success
Redis enqueue fails
```

Atau:

```text id="d6w6a0"
Redis enqueue success
DB transaction rollback
```

Solusi paling defensible: **transactional outbox**.

### Pattern

```text id="t87aab"
DB transaction:
  insert business state
  insert async_jobs
  insert outbox event JOB_CREATED
commit

Outbox dispatcher:
  reads JOB_CREATED
  XADD Redis Stream
  marks outbox dispatched
```

Dengan begitu, jika Redis down, job tetap ada di DB dan akan dikirim saat Redis pulih.

---

## 24. Redis-only Enqueue vs Outbox-backed Enqueue

| Model | Cocok untuk | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| Redis-only queue | Low-risk ephemeral work | Job hilang jika Redis loss/producer failure |
| DB job + direct Redis enqueue | Medium | Perlu reconciliation jika enqueue fail |
| DB job + outbox dispatcher | Production-safe | Lebih kompleks |
| Kafka outbox | Cross-service event | Governance lebih kuat |

Rekomendasi:

```text id="o7tbl7"
Non-critical cache rebuild:
  Redis-only boleh

User-visible async job:
  DB job + Redis queue

Critical business job:
  DB job + outbox + Redis/Kafka dispatcher
```

---

## 25. HTTP API Pattern untuk Async Job

### Request

```text id="qg947b"
POST /reports/export
Idempotency-Key: abc123
```

### Response

```text id="7hop0t"
202 Accepted
Location: /jobs/{jobId}
```

Body:

```json id="m4t543"
{
  "jobId": "8c44...",
  "status": "QUEUED"
}
```

### Status endpoint

```text id="bq4f82"
GET /jobs/{jobId}
```

Response:

```json id="25ga2l"
{
  "jobId": "8c44...",
  "status": "PROCESSING",
  "attemptCount": 1,
  "createdAt": "...",
  "updatedAt": "..."
}
```

---

## 26. JAX-RS Resource Shape

```java id="zf1yt2"
@Path("/reports")
public final class ReportResource {

    private final ReportCommandService commandService;

    @POST
    @Path("/export")
    public Response exportReport(
        ExportReportRequest request,
        @HeaderParam("Idempotency-Key") String idempotencyKey,
        @Context SecurityContext securityContext
    ) {
        JobCreatedResponse created = commandService.createExportJob(
            UserContext.from(securityContext),
            request,
            idempotencyKey
        );

        return Response.accepted(created)
            .location(URI.create("/jobs/" + created.jobId()))
            .build();
    }
}
```

Command service:

```text id="frm11a"
validate idempotency
create DB job
commit
enqueue via outbox/direct Redis
return jobId
```

---

## 27. Worker Lifecycle in Kubernetes

```text id="msfsjx"
Pod starts
  ↓
Create Redis Stream consumer
  ↓
Create worker ID from pod name
  ↓
Start read loop
  ↓
Process jobs
  ↓
On SIGTERM:
    stop reading new jobs
    finish in-flight jobs within grace period
    extend/release DB lock properly
    ack only completed jobs
    shutdown Redis connection
```

Important Kubernetes behavior:

```text id="ojdrrt"
Worker should stop reading before app shutdown closes Redis client.
```

Jika worker shutdown saat memproses job:

```text id="asyzcd"
- jangan XACK
- pending entry akan di-claim ulang setelah idle timeout
- DB lock expires
- job retry by another worker
```

---

## 28. Backpressure

Tanpa backpressure, worker bisa membaca terlalu banyak job dan overload DB/external service.

Control knobs:

```text id="8g7mil"
- XREADGROUP COUNT
- number of worker threads
- max in-flight jobs
- DB connection pool size
- external API concurrency
- semaphore per tenant/provider
- rate limiter for external calls
```

Example:

```text id="zdm033"
COUNT 10
workerThreads 4
maxInFlight 20
externalProviderSemaphore 5
```

Rule:

```text id="ru57ne"
Queue throughput must be bounded by slowest downstream dependency.
```

---

## 29. Priority Queue

Option A: multiple streams/lists:

```text id="ygoztp"
stream:email-high
stream:email-normal
stream:email-low
```

Worker reads high first.

Option B: Sorted Set priority:

```text id="s7eciw"
score = priority + time
```

Option C: DB job table priority:

```sql id="n9zn5s"
ORDER BY priority DESC, created_at ASC
```

For production, priority often belongs in DB job record, with Redis as execution signal.

---

## 30. Multi-Tenant Fairness

Problem:

```text id="fbdh1r"
Tenant A enqueues 1 million jobs.
Tenant B enqueues 10 jobs.
B starves behind A.
```

Mitigation:

```text id="rfzy99"
- queue per tenant or tenant group
- worker round-robin tenants
- per-tenant concurrency semaphore
- per-tenant rate limit
- DB query ORDER BY tenant fairness
```

Redis key example:

```text id="9v0s5v"
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task:v1
job-svc:prod:tenant:t2:stream:email-task:v1
```

Caveat: too many streams can be operationally heavy. Use tenant grouping if many tenants.

---

## 31. Poison Message

Poison message = job yang selalu gagal.

Signs:

```text id="8vpxpj"
- same job retried repeatedly
- worker logs same error
- queue lag grows
- DLQ fills
```

Guardrail:

```text id="31d9xn"
- maxAttempts
- classify retryable vs non-retryable
- DLQ after max attempts
- alert on DLQ
- manual replay with patch/fix
```

Do not infinite-retry without delay. It can starve all other work.

---

## 32. Error Classification

| Error | Retry? | Example |
|---|---:|---|
| Validation error | No | Missing required payload field |
| Not found permanent | No | Referenced entity deleted |
| Authorization/policy final | No | Operation forbidden |
| DB timeout | Yes | Transient infra |
| Redis timeout ack failure | Yes/recover | Check pending/DB state |
| External 429 | Yes with backoff | Rate limited |
| External 5xx | Yes | Provider down |
| External 4xx final | No | Bad request to provider |
| Unknown after side effect | Reconcile | Query external status |

---

## 33. Queue Lag Metrics

Metrics wajib:

```text id="fg0g6p"
job.enqueued.count
job.started.count
job.completed.count
job.failed.count
job.retry_scheduled.count
job.dead_lettered.count
job.processing.duration
job.queue.lag.seconds
job.queue.depth
job.inflight.count
job.oldest_pending.age
job.pending.count
job.claimed.count
job.ack.count
job.ack.error.count
job.dlq.depth
worker.reconnect.count
worker.shutdown.inflight.count
```

Redis-specific:

```text id="vi74wv"
stream.length
stream.group.pending.count
stream.consumer.pending.count
zset.retry.due.count
list.length
```

For Streams, `XPENDING` is used to inspect pending messages in a consumer group; Redis Streams docs cover pending entries as part of consumer group processing. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/data-types/streams/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 34. Logging

Good log:

```json id="lalrus"
{
  "event": "job_processing_failed",
  "jobId": "8c44...",
  "jobType": "SEND_EMAIL",
  "tenantHash": "sha256-...",
  "attempt": 3,
  "maxAttempts": 5,
  "retryable": true,
  "nextRunAt": "2026-07-13T15:20:00+07:00",
  "errorCode": "SMTP_TIMEOUT",
  "workerId": "pod-a:worker-1",
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

Do not log:

```text id="7q03ib"
- full email body
- tokens
- raw PII
- huge payload
- Redis stream entry with sensitive fields
```

---

## 35. Stream Retention / Trimming

Streams can grow forever if not trimmed.

Use:

```text id="d49rjk"
XTRIM stream MAXLEN ~ 100000
```

Or `XADD ... MAXLEN ~ N`.

But caution:

```text id="rl60fx"
Do not trim messages still needed for pending/recovery.
```

Practical pattern:

```text id="lk6jnq"
- Keep durable job state in PostgreSQL
- Trim Stream for operational boundedness
- Use DLQ retention separately
```

---

## 36. Redis Persistence / Eviction Warning

If Redis queue stores important jobs, Redis persistence, replication, and eviction policy matter.

Critical warning:

```text id="jzdjjg"
Do not run important queues in same Redis instance configured as volatile cache with aggressive eviction.
```

Better:

```text id="c9ldyu"
- separate Redis instance/logical cluster for queue/control-plane
- noeviction or carefully selected policy
- persistence configured if jobs are not DB-backed
- DB/outbox backing for important work
```

---

## 37. Streams vs Kafka vs Camunda vs PostgreSQL Job Table

| Need | Redis Stream | Kafka | Camunda | PostgreSQL job table |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Internal background worker | Good | OK but heavier | Sometimes | Good |
| Cross-service durable event | Limited | Best | No | Limited |
| Long retention/replay | Limited | Best | History oriented | Queryable |
| BPMN/human workflow | No | No | Best | No |
| Transaction with business data | No | With outbox | Engine-specific | Best |
| DLQ/retry | Manual/good | Mature | Engine-level | Manual |
| Operational simplicity | Medium | Higher complexity | Medium | Medium |
| Strict audit | Needs DB | Good with governance | Good | Good |

Rule:

```text id="pifk1w"
Redis Stream:
  internal job queue

Kafka:
  durable cross-service event backbone

Camunda:
  workflow lifecycle and timers

PostgreSQL job table:
  durable auditable job state

Redis List:
  simple ephemeral work
```

---

## 38. Jersey + Redis Queue Package Design

```text id="v6771s"
com.company.report
├── api
│   ├── ReportResource.java
│   └── JobResource.java
├── application
│   ├── ReportCommandService.java
│   └── JobQueryService.java
├── job
│   ├── JobQueue.java
│   ├── JobDispatcher.java
│   ├── JobWorker.java
│   ├── JobHandler.java
│   ├── JobRetryPolicy.java
│   └── RedisStreamJobQueue.java
├── persistence
│   ├── AsyncJobMapper.java
│   └── AsyncJobRepository.java
└── redis
    ├── RedisLifecycle.java
    └── RedisKeys.java
```

Interface:

```java id="12qqiq"
public interface JobQueue {
    void enqueue(JobMessage message);
    List<JobMessageEnvelope> readBatch(String workerId, int count, Duration block);
    void ack(JobMessageEnvelope envelope);
    void sendToDlq(JobMessageEnvelope envelope, JobFailure failure);
}
```

---

## 39. Worker Handler Pattern

```java id="7n7676"
public interface JobHandler {
    String jobType();
    JobResult handle(AsyncJob job);
}
```

Dispatcher:

```java id="ex8a3r"
public final class JobDispatcher {

    private final Map<String, JobHandler> handlers;

    public JobResult dispatch(AsyncJob job) {
        JobHandler handler = handlers.get(job.jobType());

        if (handler == null) {
            return JobResult.failedFinal("UNKNOWN_JOB_TYPE");
        }

        return handler.handle(job);
    }
}
```

Worker loop:

```java id="69ub5r"
while (running.get()) {
    List<JobMessageEnvelope> batch = queue.readBatch(workerId, 10, Duration.ofSeconds(5));

    for (JobMessageEnvelope envelope : batch) {
        processOne(envelope);
    }

    recoverPendingIfNeeded();
}
```

---

## 40. Process One Job Flow

```text id="5hogph"
Read message from stream
  ↓
Load job row from PostgreSQL
  ↓
Try claim job row
  ├── failed claim
  │     ↓
  │   XACK duplicate/stale message if safe
  │
  └── claimed
        ↓
      Execute handler
        ├── success
        │     ↓
        │   mark DB COMPLETED
        │     ↓
        │   XACK
        │
        ├── retryable failure
        │     ↓
        │   mark DB RETRY_SCHEDULED
        │     ↓
        │   ZADD retry schedule
        │     ↓
        │   XACK
        │
        └── final failure / max attempts
              ↓
            mark DB DEAD_LETTERED
              ↓
            XADD DLQ
              ↓
            XACK
```

---

## 41. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Redis List simple queue untuk critical job | Job hilang saat worker crash | Stream/DB-backed job |
| Ack sebelum DB update | Job lost on crash | Ack last |
| Full business payload hanya di Redis | Data loss risk | Store job in PostgreSQL |
| Infinite retry tanpa backoff | Queue starvation | Retry policy + DLQ |
| No DLQ owner | Failed jobs menumpuk | Dashboard/alert/replay |
| No pending recovery | Stuck messages | XPENDING/XAUTOCLAIM |
| Worker tidak idempotent | Duplicate side effects | DB claim + external idempotency |
| Redis queue di cache instance eviction | Job evicted | Dedicated instance/noeviction/DB backing |
| Too many in-flight jobs | DB/external overload | Backpressure |
| Tenant besar menguasai queue | Starvation | Fairness/concurrency cap |
| Stream tanpa trim | Memory growth | Retention policy |
| Job creation DB commit + Redis enqueue tidak atomic | Lost enqueue | Outbox dispatcher |

---

## 42. Senior Engineer Checklist

```text id="ybi32i"
[ ] Apakah job critical atau ephemeral?
[ ] Apakah job state durable di PostgreSQL?
[ ] Apakah Redis hanya membawa jobId/minimal metadata?
[ ] Apakah enqueue terjadi via outbox untuk job penting?
[ ] Apakah worker idempotent?
[ ] Apakah ack dilakukan setelah durable success/failure state?
[ ] Apakah retry policy jelas?
[ ] Apakah DLQ ada dan punya owner?
[ ] Apakah pending recovery ada untuk Streams?
[ ] Apakah stuck processing bisa direcover?
[ ] Apakah external side effect memakai idempotency key?
[ ] Apakah queue punya backpressure?
[ ] Apakah tenant fairness dipertimbangkan?
[ ] Apakah stream/list/zset retention jelas?
[ ] Apakah Redis eviction/persistence sesuai?
[ ] Apakah shutdown worker graceful?
[ ] Apakah metrics queue lag/pending/DLQ ada?
```

---

## 43. Ringkasan Praktis

```text id="wowugp"
Redis List:
  queue sederhana, cocok untuk non-critical work

List + processing list:
  lebih reliable, tapi recovery manual

Sorted Set:
  delayed job, retry schedule, timeout scanner

Redis Stream:
  job queue lebih matang dengan consumer group, pending entries, ack

PostgreSQL job table:
  durable source of job state

Outbox:
  membuat enqueue ke Redis recoverable setelah DB commit

DLQ:
  tempat job gagal permanen setelah retry maksimal

Worker:
  harus idempotent, bounded, observable, graceful shutdown
```

Kalimat kunci:

> Redis job queue yang aman bukan sekadar `LPUSH` dan `BLPOP`. Desain production harus menjawab: job state disimpan di mana, kapan ack dilakukan, bagaimana retry, bagaimana worker crash direcover, dan apa yang terjadi saat Redis atau downstream gagal.

# Seri 12 - Redis Streams Consumer Group Pattern

Status: **Seri 12 dari 22 seri lanjutan**.  
Setelah seri ini selesai, tersisa **10 seri**.

---

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini memperdalam penggunaan **Redis Streams + Consumer Groups** sebagai pattern job queue / event-processing internal microservice.

Fokusnya:

```text
- XADD
- XGROUP CREATE
- XREADGROUP
- XACK
- XPENDING
- XAUTOCLAIM
- Pending Entries List / PEL
- retry
- dead-letter queue
- stream trimming
- worker lifecycle
- recovery setelah worker crash
- boundary dengan Kafka, Camunda, dan PostgreSQL
```

Redis Streams adalah struktur data seperti **append-only log** yang mendukung access pattern lebih kompleks, termasuk **consumer groups**. Redis docs menyebut Streams dapat digunakan untuk mencatat dan mendistribusikan event secara real time, dengan kemampuan konsumsi seperti consumer group. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/data-types/streams/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Redis Streams

```text
Redis Stream
= append-only log
= ordered entries
= each entry has stream ID
= each entry has field-value pairs

Consumer Group
= group of consumers sharing work
= each entry delivered to one consumer in the group
= pending entries tracked until acknowledged

Pending Entries List / PEL
= entries delivered but not yet acknowledged
= basis for retry/recovery
```

Kalimat penting:

```text
Redis Stream bukan sekadar queue.
Redis Stream adalah log ringan dengan consumer group dan acknowledgement.
```

Tetapi:

```text
Redis Stream bukan pengganti Kafka untuk enterprise event backbone.
Redis Stream bukan pengganti Camunda untuk workflow lifecycle.
Redis Stream bukan pengganti PostgreSQL untuk durable business state.
```

---

## 3. Diagram Besar

```text
Producer Service
  ↓
XADD stream jobId=...
  ↓
Redis Stream
  ↓
Consumer Group
  ├── Consumer A
  ├── Consumer B
  └── Consumer C
        ↓
      XREADGROUP
        ↓
      Process job
        ↓
      Durable success/failure state
        ↓
      XACK
```

Recovery path:

```text
Consumer A reads entry
  ↓
Entry enters PEL
  ↓
Consumer A crashes before XACK
  ↓
Entry remains pending
  ↓
Consumer B uses XPENDING / XAUTOCLAIM
  ↓
Consumer B reprocesses
  ↓
Consumer B XACKs
```

`XACK` menghapus message dari **Pending Entries List** milik consumer group setelah message selesai diproses. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/xack/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4. Core Commands

| Command | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `XADD` | Menambahkan entry ke stream |
| `XGROUP CREATE` | Membuat consumer group |
| `XREADGROUP` | Membaca entry sebagai member consumer group |
| `XACK` | Mengakui entry sudah selesai diproses |
| `XPENDING` | Melihat pending entries |
| `XCLAIM` | Memindahkan ownership pending entry ke consumer lain |
| `XAUTOCLAIM` | Claim pending entries secara incremental berdasarkan idle time |
| `XDEL` | Menghapus entry tertentu dari stream |
| `XTRIM` | Membatasi panjang stream |
| `XINFO` | Melihat metadata stream/group/consumer |

`XREADGROUP` adalah versi khusus `XREAD` yang mendukung consumer groups. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/xreadgroup/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) `XAUTOCLAIM` digunakan untuk mengambil/memindahkan ownership message pending ke consumer lain berdasarkan minimum idle time. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/xautoclaim/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5. Stream Key Design

Gunakan key yang jelas dan versioned.

```text
{service}:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:stream:{jobType}:v1
```

Contoh:

```text
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task:v1
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:report-export:v1
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:case-notification:v1
workflow-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:external-task-helper:v1
```

DLQ stream:

```text
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task-dlq:v1
```

Retry ZSET:

```text
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:zschedule:email-task-retry:v1
```

Consumer group:

```text
email-worker-v1
report-export-worker-v1
case-notification-worker-v1
```

Consumer name:

```text
{podName}:{workerThread}
```

Contoh:

```text
report-svc-7df9d8b4c9-x92aa:worker-3
```

---

## 6. Message Payload Design

Redis Stream entry terdiri dari field-value pairs. Untuk job queue production, entry sebaiknya membawa **minimal metadata**, bukan payload bisnis besar.

### Baik

```text
XADD stream * 
  jobId 8c44...
  tenantId t1
  jobType SEND_EMAIL
  schemaVersion 1
  traceId abc...
```

### Buruk

```text
XADD stream *
  fullEmailBody ...
  customerFullName ...
  documentContent ...
  entireBusinessPayload ...
```

Rule:

```text
Stream message identifies the work.
PostgreSQL stores durable job/business state.
```

Untuk job penting, simpan payload di PostgreSQL:

```sql
async_jobs(
  id,
  tenant_id,
  job_type,
  status,
  payload jsonb,
  attempt_count,
  max_attempts,
  next_run_at,
  created_at,
  updated_at
)
```

---

## 7. XADD Producer Pattern

### Basic

```text
XADD job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task:v1 *
  jobId 8c44
  tenantId t1
  jobType SEND_EMAIL
  schemaVersion 1
```

### With approximate max length

```text
XADD job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task:v1
  MAXLEN ~ 100000
  *
  jobId 8c44
  tenantId t1
  jobType SEND_EMAIL
  schemaVersion 1
```

`MAXLEN ~` melakukan trimming approximate agar lebih efisien.

Namun jangan trimming sembarangan kalau masih ada pending entries yang belum selesai. Stream retention harus disesuaikan dengan recovery policy.

---

## 8. Producer Transaction Boundary

Problem klasik:

```text
DB insert job success
Redis XADD fails
```

Atau:

```text
Redis XADD success
DB transaction rollback
```

Untuk job penting, jangan langsung `XADD` di tengah transaction DB.

Pattern aman:

```text
PostgreSQL transaction
  ├── insert business data
  ├── insert async_jobs
  └── insert outbox event JOB_CREATED
commit

Outbox dispatcher
  ↓
XADD Redis Stream
  ↓
mark outbox dispatched
```

Dengan ini, jika Redis down, job tetap tersimpan di PostgreSQL dan dispatcher bisa retry.

---

## 9. XGROUP CREATE

Membuat group:

```text
XGROUP CREATE streamKey groupName $ MKSTREAM
```

Contoh:

```text
XGROUP CREATE job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task:v1 email-worker-v1 $ MKSTREAM
```

Makna ID:

| ID | Makna |
|---|---|
| `$` | Group mulai dari entry baru setelah group dibuat |
| `0` | Group mulai dari entry paling awal |
| specific ID | Mulai dari posisi tertentu |

Untuk production job queue baru, biasanya:

```text
XGROUP CREATE stream group $ MKSTREAM
```

Untuk recovery/replay historical entries:

```text
XGROUP CREATE stream group 0
```

Jika group sudah ada, Redis akan error `BUSYGROUP`. Application startup harus memperlakukan itu sebagai kondisi normal.

---

## 10. Startup Consumer Group Safely

Pseudo-flow:

```text
on worker startup:
  try XGROUP CREATE stream group $ MKSTREAM
  if BUSYGROUP:
    ignore
  else if other error:
    fail startup or mark degraded
```

Jangan membuat group secara manual ad-hoc tanpa automation, karena scaling pod baru harus reproducible.

---

## 11. XREADGROUP: Read New Messages

Command:

```text
XREADGROUP GROUP email-worker-v1 pod-a:worker-1
  COUNT 10
  BLOCK 5000
  STREAMS job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task:v1 >
```

Makna `>`:

```text
Baca message baru yang belum pernah dikirim ke consumer group.
```

`XREADGROUP` mendukung consumer group dan dipakai untuk membaca message sebagai anggota group. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/xreadgroup/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Worker loop:

```text
while running:
  XREADGROUP ... BLOCK 5000 COUNT 10 STREAMS stream >
  for each entry:
    process
    XACK after durable success/failure handling
```

---

## 12. Jangan Ack Terlalu Awal

Salah:

```text
XREADGROUP
  ↓
XACK
  ↓
process job
  ↓
worker crash
```

Akibat:

```text
Redis menganggap job selesai,
padahal side effect / DB update belum selesai.
```

Benar:

```text
XREADGROUP
  ↓
load DB job
  ↓
claim/update DB status
  ↓
process side effect
  ↓
update DB completed/retry/DLQ
  ↓
XACK
```

Rule:

```text
Ack last.
Ack only after durable state is correct.
```

---

## 13. Pending Entries List / PEL

Saat consumer group mengirim entry ke consumer, entry masuk ke **Pending Entries List** sampai `XACK`.

```text
XREADGROUP delivered entry
  ↓
entry is pending
  ↓
XACK removes it from PEL
```

Redis docs menjelaskan bahwa data yang dibaca via consumer group dan tidak di-ack akan menjadi pending entries; `XACK` menghapus entry dari PEL karena setelah message sukses diproses, group tidak perlu lagi melacak owner message itu. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/xpending/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

PEL menyimpan informasi seperti:

```text
- entry ID
- consumer owner
- idle time
- delivery count
```

Informasi ini adalah dasar untuk retry dan crash recovery.

---

## 14. XPENDING

Basic:

```text
XPENDING stream group
```

Extended:

```text
XPENDING stream group - + 10
```

By consumer:

```text
XPENDING stream group - + 10 consumerName
```

Gunanya:

```text
- melihat jumlah pending
- menemukan entry yang stuck
- melihat idle time
- melihat delivery count
- menentukan apakah entry perlu di-claim ulang
```

Operational metrics dari `XPENDING`:

```text
stream.group.pending.count
stream.consumer.pending.count
stream.oldest_pending.idle_ms
stream.delivery_count.max
```

---

## 15. XAUTOCLAIM

`XAUTOCLAIM` mengambil pending entries yang idle lebih lama dari threshold dan memindahkan ownership ke consumer baru. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/xautoclaim/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```text
XAUTOCLAIM stream group pod-b:worker-1 60000 0 COUNT 10
```

Makna:

```text
Claim entries yang idle >= 60000 ms
mulai scan dari ID 0
maksimal 10 entry
owner baru = pod-b:worker-1
```

Flow:

```text
Worker A reads job
  ↓
Worker A crashes
  ↓
Entry pending idle > 60s
  ↓
Worker B XAUTOCLAIM
  ↓
Worker B processes
  ↓
Worker B XACK
```

---

## 16. Visibility Timeout Equivalent

Redis Streams tidak memakai istilah “visibility timeout” secara resmi seperti queue lain, tetapi konsepnya mirip:

```text
entry pending idle time > minIdleTime
  ↓
eligible to be claimed by another consumer
```

Parameter:

```text
minIdleTime = expected max processing time + buffer
```

Contoh:

| Job type | Expected processing | minIdleTime |
|---|---:|---:|
| Send email | 1-5 sec | 60 sec |
| Webhook delivery | 5-20 sec | 2 min |
| Generate report | 2-10 min | 15 min |
| External API sync | 30-120 sec | 5 min |

Kalau terlalu pendek:

```text
same job can be processed twice while first worker still running
```

Kalau terlalu panjang:

```text
crash recovery lambat
```

---

## 17. Delivery Count dan Poison Message

Pending entry punya delivery count. Jika satu entry berkali-kali di-claim dan gagal, itu mungkin poison message.

Rule:

```text
if deliveryCount >= maxAttempts:
  send to DLQ
  XACK original entry
else:
  retry processing
```

Pseudo-flow:

```text
XAUTOCLAIM pending entry
  ↓
check delivery count / DB attempt count
  ├── attempts < maxAttempts
  │     ↓
  │   process again
  │
  └── attempts >= maxAttempts
        ↓
      write DLQ
        ↓
      mark DB DEAD_LETTERED
        ↓
      XACK original
```

Untuk production, attempt count sebaiknya disimpan di PostgreSQL job table, bukan hanya mengandalkan Redis delivery count.

---

## 18. Worker Processing Flow

```text
read stream entry
  ↓
parse message
  ↓
load async_jobs row by jobId
  ↓
try claim DB job
  ├── claim failed
  │     ├── job already completed?
  │     │     ↓
  │     │   XACK as duplicate/stale
  │     └── job locked by active worker?
  │           ↓
  │         leave pending or retry later
  │
  └── claim success
        ↓
      execute handler
        ├── success
        │     ↓
        │   mark DB COMPLETED
        │     ↓
        │   XACK
        │
        ├── retryable failure
        │     ↓
        │   mark DB RETRY_SCHEDULED
        │     ↓
        │   ZADD retry schedule
        │     ↓
        │   XACK original
        │
        └── final failure / max attempts
              ↓
            mark DB DEAD_LETTERED
              ↓
            XADD DLQ
              ↓
            XACK original
```

---

## 19. DB Claim Pattern

PostgreSQL claim protects against duplicate processing.

```sql
UPDATE async_jobs
SET status = 'PROCESSING',
    locked_by = #{workerId},
    locked_until = now() + interval '5 minutes',
    attempt_count = attempt_count + 1,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE id = #{jobId}
  AND status IN ('QUEUED', 'RETRY_SCHEDULED')
  AND (locked_until IS NULL OR locked_until < now())
RETURNING *;
```

If no row returned:

```text
job not claimable
```

Then check current state:

```text
COMPLETED       -> XACK duplicate stream entry
PROCESSING      -> leave/retry later
DEAD_LETTERED   -> XACK
NOT_FOUND       -> XACK or DLQ depending policy
```

---

## 20. Idempotent Handler

Every handler must tolerate duplicate execution.

Why duplicates happen:

```text
- worker crashed after side effect before XACK
- minIdleTime too short
- message claimed by another worker
- producer sent duplicate jobId
- retry scheduler enqueued duplicate
```

Handler guardrails:

```text
- DB job claim before side effect
- stable external idempotency key
- unique constraint for final records
- check current job/business state before acting
- XACK stale duplicate only after DB confirms safe
```

Example external call:

```text
providerRequestId = jobId or deterministic business key
```

Do not generate random provider request ID per retry.

---

## 21. Retry Scheduling Pattern

Redis Stream sendiri bukan delayed queue. Untuk retry delay, pasangkan dengan Sorted Set.

```text
Main Stream:
  job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task:v1

Retry ZSET:
  job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:zschedule:email-task-retry:v1
```

On retryable failure:

```text
update async_jobs:
  status = RETRY_SCHEDULED
  next_run_at = now + delay
  last_error = ...
  attempt_count already incremented

ZADD retryZset nextRunAtMillis jobId

XACK original stream entry
```

Retry scheduler:

```text
ZRANGEBYSCORE retryZset -inf now LIMIT 0 100
  ↓
for each jobId:
  check DB job status = RETRY_SCHEDULED
  XADD mainStream jobId ...
  ZREM retryZset jobId
  update DB status = QUEUED
```

---

## 22. Backoff Strategy

```text
baseDelay = 10 seconds
maxDelay = 15 minutes

delay = min(baseDelay * 2^(attempt-1), maxDelay)
delay = delay + random jitter
```

Example:

| Attempt | Delay |
|---:|---:|
| 1 | 10 sec |
| 2 | 30 sec |
| 3 | 1-2 min |
| 4 | 5 min |
| 5 | 15 min |
| > max | DLQ |

Gunakan jitter untuk mencegah retry storm.

---

## 23. Dead Letter Queue Pattern

DLQ stream:

```text
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task-dlq:v1
```

DLQ entry:

```text
XADD dlqStream *
  jobId 8c44
  originalStream job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:email-task:v1
  originalEntryId 169...
  jobType SEND_EMAIL
  errorCode SMTP_550
  attempts 5
  failedAt 2026-07-13T15:30:00+07:00
```

DB job row:

```text
status = DEAD_LETTERED
error_code = ...
error_message = ...
updated_at = now
```

DLQ harus punya:

```text
- dashboard
- alert
- manual replay command
- retention policy
- owner team
```

Tanpa owner, DLQ hanya menjadi tempat menumpuknya masalah.

---

## 24. Manual Replay from DLQ

Replay flow:

```text
Operator fixes root cause
  ↓
Select DLQ job
  ↓
Validate safe to replay
  ↓
Update DB job status = RETRY_SCHEDULED / QUEUED
  ↓
XADD main stream
  ↓
Optionally annotate DLQ entry / audit table
```

Jangan replay DLQ hanya dengan copy message ke stream tanpa cek DB/job state. Bisa menghasilkan duplicate side effect.

---

## 25. Stream Trimming

Streams bisa tumbuh terus. Gunakan retention.

Options:

```text
XADD stream MAXLEN ~ 100000 * field value
XTRIM stream MAXLEN ~ 100000
```

Namun trimming berbahaya jika terlalu agresif:

```text
- pending entries mungkin masih perlu recovery
- operator mungkin perlu inspect historical failures
- consumer group lag bisa kehilangan entry jika stream dipangkas
```

Rule:

```text
Stream retention must be longer than max expected processing + recovery window.
```

Untuk job penting:

```text
PostgreSQL = durable job history
Redis Stream = execution queue
```

Maka stream bisa di-trim lebih agresif karena sumber audit tetap di DB.

---

## 26. Stream Deletion vs Ack

`XACK` tidak menghapus entry dari stream; ia hanya menghapus entry dari PEL. `XACK` menghapus pending entry dari PEL, bukan otomatis menghapus stream entry. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/xack/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Artinya:

```text
XACK:
  consumer group no longer tracks it as pending

XTRIM:
  reduces stream length

XDEL:
  deletes specific entry from stream
```

Production biasanya:

```text
XACK after processing
XTRIM by retention policy
```

Jangan `XDEL` setiap message kalau tidak perlu; itu menambah overhead dan tidak selalu menyelesaikan retention secara rapi.

---

## 27. Consumer Lifecycle in Kubernetes

Startup:

```text
1. Generate workerId from pod name + thread index
2. Ensure XGROUP exists
3. Start read loop
4. Start pending recovery loop
5. Start retry scheduler loop if owned by same service
```

Runtime:

```text
- read batch with COUNT
- block with timeout
- process bounded concurrency
- ack after durable result
- periodically recover pending
```

Shutdown:

```text
1. Stop accepting new HTTP traffic if same app serves HTTP
2. Stop reading new stream messages
3. Finish in-flight jobs within grace period
4. Do not XACK unfinished jobs
5. Let DB lock expire / mark retryable if safe
6. Close Redis connections
```

Kubernetes SIGTERM behavior means worker harus punya graceful shutdown. Kalau pod mati di tengah job tanpa XACK, PEL recovery akan mengambil alih.

---

## 28. Separate Connections

Pisahkan:

```text
Redis command connection:
  XADD, XACK, XGROUP, XINFO

Blocking stream read connection:
  XREADGROUP BLOCK

Recovery connection:
  XPENDING / XAUTOCLAIM

Normal request path connection:
  GET/SET/cache/rate-limit
```

Jangan biarkan `XREADGROUP BLOCK` menggunakan connection request path. Blocking operation bisa mengganggu latency REST.

---

## 29. Backpressure

Backpressure mencegah worker membaca terlalu banyak job.

Controls:

```text
XREADGROUP COUNT
BLOCK timeout
worker thread pool size
max in-flight jobs
DB pool size
external API semaphore
per-tenant concurrency
global worker count
```

Example config:

```text
countPerRead = 10
workerThreads = 4
maxInFlight = 20
streamBlockTimeout = 5s
externalProviderSemaphore = 5
```

Rule:

```text
Do not read more work than you can safely process.
```

---

## 30. Multi-Tenant Fairness

Masalah:

```text
Tenant A produces 1,000,000 jobs
Tenant B produces 10 jobs
Single stream FIFO causes B starvation
```

Options:

### A. Stream per tenant

```text
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:report:v1
job-svc:prod:tenant:t2:stream:report:v1
```

Kelebihan: fairness lebih mudah.  
Kekurangan: terlalu banyak stream jika tenant banyak.

### B. Stream per tenant group

```text
job-svc:prod:tenant-group:large:stream:report:v1
job-svc:prod:tenant-group:standard:stream:report:v1
```

### C. DB-driven fairness

Stream hanya wake-up signal, worker memilih job dari DB:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM async_jobs
WHERE status = 'QUEUED'
ORDER BY tenant_priority, created_at
FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
LIMIT 10;
```

Untuk platform multi-tenant besar, opsi C sering lebih defensible.

---

## 31. Ordering Semantics

Redis Stream menjaga order entry di stream, tetapi dalam consumer group dengan banyak worker:

```text
processing completion order can differ from stream order
```

Contoh:

```text
Entry 1 -> Worker A -> slow
Entry 2 -> Worker B -> fast
Entry 2 completes first
```

Kalau ordering per aggregate penting:

```text
- gunakan stream/key per aggregate partition
- gunakan DB state transition validation
- gunakan Kafka partition by aggregateId
- gunakan Camunda workflow for ordered lifecycle
```

Jangan mengandalkan multi-worker consumer group untuk strict per-case ordering.

---

## 32. Redis Cluster Consideration

Untuk Redis Cluster, stream key berada di hash slot tertentu. Commands terhadap satu stream key biasanya diarahkan ke node pemilik slot tersebut. Jika Anda melakukan operasi multi-key dalam script/transaction, key harus berada di hash slot yang sama.

Pattern:

```text
job-svc:prod:{tenant:t1:email}:stream:main:v1
job-svc:prod:{tenant:t1:email}:stream:dlq:v1
job-svc:prod:{tenant:t1:email}:zschedule:retry:v1
```

Caveat:

```text
- hash tag terlalu luas bisa membuat hot slot
- hash tag terlalu sempit bisa menyulitkan batch
```

Gunakan hash tag hanya bila perlu multi-key atomicity atau co-location.

---

## 33. Stream Lag / Health Monitoring

Monitor:

```text
- stream length
- group pending count
- oldest pending idle time
- consumer count
- consumer idle time
- DLQ depth
- retry ZSET due count
- processing duration
- job age
- DB job status counts
```

Redis `XPENDING` membantu melihat pending entries dan kondisi message yang sudah delivered tapi belum acknowledged. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/xpending/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Possible alerts:

```text
DLQ depth > 0 for critical job
oldest pending idle > threshold
retry due count growing
stream length growing faster than completion
consumer count = 0
job age p95 > SLA
```

---

## 34. XINFO for Operations

Useful commands:

```text
XINFO STREAM streamKey
XINFO GROUPS streamKey
XINFO CONSUMERS streamKey groupName
```

Gunanya:

```text
- melihat length stream
- melihat consumer group
- melihat lag
- melihat consumer idle time
- melihat pending per consumer
```

Gunakan untuk dashboard/runbook.

---

## 35. Error Classification

| Error | Action |
|---|---|
| Payload malformed | DLQ / failed final |
| Job not found in DB | XACK + alert or DLQ |
| DB timeout | retry |
| External 429 | retry with backoff |
| External 5xx | retry with backoff |
| External 4xx final | failed final / DLQ |
| Serialization error | DLQ |
| Unknown after side effect | reconcile external system |
| Redis XACK timeout | check pending later; do not assume ack succeeded blindly |

---

## 36. Redis Failure Scenarios

| Failure | Impact | Guardrail |
|---|---|---|
| Redis down before XADD | Job not enqueued | Outbox retry |
| Redis down during XREADGROUP | Worker waits/reconnects | Reconnect loop |
| Worker crash before XACK | Entry pending | XAUTOCLAIM |
| Redis failover after delivery | Possible reconnect/gap | DB idempotency + pending scan |
| XACK timeout | Unknown ack state | XPENDING check |
| Stream trimmed too early | Message lost for slow consumer | Retention > recovery window |
| Redis eviction | Queue data can disappear | noeviction/dedicated Redis/DB backing |
| Duplicate delivery | Duplicate side effect risk | idempotent worker |

---

## 37. Java Interface Design

```java
public interface StreamJobQueue {
    void enqueue(JobMessage message);

    List<StreamJobEnvelope> readNew(
        String group,
        String consumer,
        int count,
        Duration block
    );

    List<StreamJobEnvelope> claimStalePending(
        String group,
        String consumer,
        Duration minIdleTime,
        int count
    );

    void ack(String group, String entryId);

    void sendToDlq(StreamJobEnvelope envelope, JobFailure failure);
}
```

Envelope:

```java
public record StreamJobEnvelope(
    String streamKey,
    String entryId,
    Map<String, String> fields
) {}
```

Message:

```java
public record JobMessage(
    String jobId,
    String tenantId,
    String jobType,
    int schemaVersion,
    String traceId
) {}
```

---

## 38. Worker Loop Pseudocode

```java
public final class StreamJobWorker implements Runnable {

    private final AtomicBoolean running = new AtomicBoolean(true);
    private final StreamJobQueue queue;
    private final JobProcessor processor;
    private final String group;
    private final String consumer;

    @Override
    public void run() {
        while (running.get()) {
            recoverPending();

            List<StreamJobEnvelope> batch = queue.readNew(
                group,
                consumer,
                10,
                Duration.ofSeconds(5)
            );

            for (StreamJobEnvelope envelope : batch) {
                processOne(envelope);
            }
        }
    }

    private void processOne(StreamJobEnvelope envelope) {
        try {
            JobProcessingResult result = processor.process(envelope);

            if (result.acknowledge()) {
                queue.ack(group, envelope.entryId());
            }

        } catch (Exception ex) {
            // Do not ack unknown failures unless DB state says retry/DLQ recorded.
            logFailure(envelope, ex);
        }
    }

    private void recoverPending() {
        List<StreamJobEnvelope> stale = queue.claimStalePending(
            group,
            consumer,
            Duration.ofMinutes(2),
            10
        );

        for (StreamJobEnvelope envelope : stale) {
            processOne(envelope);
        }
    }

    public void stop() {
        running.set(false);
    }
}
```

---

## 39. Processor Pseudocode

```java
public JobProcessingResult process(StreamJobEnvelope envelope) {
    JobMessage message = JobMessage.from(envelope.fields());

    Optional<AsyncJob> claimed = jobRepository.tryClaim(
        message.jobId(),
        workerId,
        Duration.ofMinutes(5)
    );

    if (claimed.isEmpty()) {
        AsyncJob existing = jobRepository.findById(message.jobId());

        if (existing.isTerminal()) {
            return JobProcessingResult.ack();
        }

        return JobProcessingResult.doNotAckYet();
    }

    try {
        JobResult result = dispatcher.dispatch(claimed.get());

        if (result.success()) {
            jobRepository.markCompleted(message.jobId(), result.payload());
            return JobProcessingResult.ack();
        }

        if (result.retryable() && claimed.get().attemptCount() < claimed.get().maxAttempts()) {
            jobRepository.markRetryScheduled(message.jobId(), result.error());
            retryScheduler.schedule(message.jobId(), nextRetryTime(claimed.get()));
            return JobProcessingResult.ack();
        }

        jobRepository.markDeadLettered(message.jobId(), result.error());
        queue.sendToDlq(envelope, result.toFailure());
        return JobProcessingResult.ack();

    } catch (Exception ex) {
        jobRepository.markRetryScheduled(message.jobId(), JobFailure.from(ex));
        retryScheduler.schedule(message.jobId(), nextRetryTime(claimed.get()));
        return JobProcessingResult.ack();
    }
}
```

Key idea:

```text
Stream ack follows durable DB state.
```

---

## 40. Testing Matrix

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| `XGROUP CREATE` idempotent startup | Worker bisa restart |
| `XADD` writes minimal job message | Producer correctness |
| `XREADGROUP` reads new messages | Happy path |
| `XACK` after DB completed | Ack order |
| Worker crash before ack | Entry remains pending |
| `XAUTOCLAIM` recovers stale pending | Crash recovery |
| Duplicate stream entry same jobId | DB claim prevents duplicate |
| Retryable failure schedules ZSET retry | Backoff |
| Max attempts sends DLQ | Poison handling |
| DLQ replay validates DB state | Safe replay |
| Stream trimming does not lose pending | Retention |
| Redis down before enqueue | Outbox retry |
| Redis down during worker read | Reconnect |
| XACK timeout | Pending check recovery |
| Kubernetes shutdown | No unfinished ack |
| Multi-tenant fairness | No starvation |
| External side effect duplicate | Idempotency guard |

---

## 41. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Store full critical payload only in Stream | Data loss risk | Store job in PostgreSQL |
| Ack before processing | Lost job | Ack last |
| No pending recovery | Stuck jobs | XPENDING/XAUTOCLAIM |
| No DLQ | Poison message loops forever | DLQ + maxAttempts |
| Infinite retry no backoff | Queue starvation | Exponential backoff + jitter |
| Stream no trimming | Memory growth | XTRIM/MAXLEN |
| Trim too aggressively | Lose slow/pending work | Retention > recovery window |
| Assume strict processing order | Out-of-order completion | Partition/DB validation |
| No DB claim | Duplicate side effects | DB claim lock |
| No external idempotency | Provider duplicate | Stable provider request ID |
| Blocking stream read on HTTP Redis connection | REST latency issues | Dedicated connection |
| Single tenant floods stream | Starvation | Fairness controls |
| No outbox producer | Lost enqueue | Transactional outbox |

---

## 42. Senior Engineer Checklist

```text
[ ] Stream key follows namespace/version convention
[ ] Message contains jobId/minimal metadata only
[ ] Durable job state exists in PostgreSQL for important jobs
[ ] Producer uses outbox if job must not be lost
[ ] Consumer group creation is idempotent
[ ] Worker name unique per pod/thread
[ ] XACK happens only after durable state update
[ ] Pending recovery via XPENDING/XAUTOCLAIM exists
[ ] minIdleTime is based on job duration
[ ] Retry policy has maxAttempts and backoff
[ ] DLQ exists and has owner/runbook
[ ] Worker handler is idempotent
[ ] DB claim prevents duplicate processing
[ ] External side effects use stable idempotency key
[ ] Stream trimming/retention is configured
[ ] Redis eviction/persistence is compatible with queue use
[ ] Worker shutdown is graceful
[ ] Metrics for pending/lag/DLQ/retry exist
[ ] Redis Cluster hash-slot behavior is understood
```

---

## 43. Ringkasan Praktis

```text
Redis Streams:
  append-only log ringan untuk internal job/event processing

Consumer Group:
  membagi work ke beberapa consumer

XREADGROUP:
  baca message sebagai consumer group

PEL:
  message delivered tapi belum ack

XACK:
  hapus message dari PEL setelah sukses diproses

XPENDING/XAUTOCLAIM:
  recovery message yang ditinggal worker crash

ZSET Retry:
  delayed retry/backoff

DLQ:
  job gagal permanen setelah max retry

PostgreSQL:
  durable job state dan final correctness

Kafka:
  lebih cocok untuk durable cross-service event backbone

Camunda:
  lebih cocok untuk workflow lifecycle dan BPMN timer
```

Kalimat kunci:

> Redis Streams Consumer Group memberi fondasi queue yang jauh lebih production-ready daripada List sederhana, tetapi reliability-nya tetap bergantung pada ack discipline, pending recovery, idempotent worker, retry/DLQ, dan durable state di PostgreSQL untuk job yang penting.
