## Response:
7/13/2026, 7:20:07 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 13 — Pub/Sub vs Keyspace Notification vs Streams vs Kafka Boundary**. Fokusnya: memilih primitive event/messaging yang tepat berdasarkan durability, replay, fanout, consumer group, dan risiko kehilangan message.

# Seri 13 - Pub/Sub vs Keyspace Notification vs Streams vs Kafka Boundary

Status: **Seri 13 dari 22 seri lanjutan**.  
Setelah seri ini selesai, tersisa **9 seri**.

---

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas batas pemakaian beberapa primitive event/messaging:

```text id="a6szsp"
Redis Pub/Sub
Redis Keyspace Notification
Redis Streams
Kafka
PostgreSQL Outbox
Camunda Timer / Workflow
```

Fokusnya adalah menjawab:

```text id="qnkg8m"
- Kapan boleh pakai Pub/Sub?
- Kapan keyspace notification cukup?
- Kapan harus pakai Redis Streams?
- Kapan harus naik ke Kafka?
- Kapan sebaiknya pakai Camunda atau PostgreSQL?
- Apa risiko jika salah memilih primitive?
```

Redis Pub/Sub bersifat **at-most-once**: setelah message dikirim oleh Redis server, message tidak akan dikirim ulang; jika subscriber tidak bisa menerima karena error atau network disconnect, message hilang permanen. Ini membuat Pub/Sub cocok untuk signal realtime, tetapi tidak cocok untuk durable business event. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/pubsub/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Besar

```text id="4rjqg1"
Pub/Sub
= live signal
= fire-and-forget
= no replay
= no persistence
= at-most-once

Keyspace Notification
= Pub/Sub event dari perubahan Redis key
= best-effort signal
= no replay
= not business truth

Redis Streams
= Redis-side append-only log
= consumer group
= ack / pending entries
= internal queue/event-lite

Kafka
= distributed durable event log
= retention
= replay
= offsets
= cross-service event backbone

PostgreSQL Outbox
= transactional event record with business data
= reliable bridge from DB transaction to messaging

Camunda
= workflow state machine
= timers
= BPMN lifecycle
= long-running orchestration
```

Kalimat kunci:

```text id="9lwab5"
Signal boleh hilang.
Job penting harus bisa direcover.
Event bisnis harus durable.
Workflow state harus dimiliki workflow engine.
```

---

## 3. Diagram Boundary

```text id="eoh9ot"
Need to notify live subscribers only?
  └── Redis Pub/Sub

Need to react to Redis key expired/deleted?
  └── Keyspace Notification
      + reconciliation if important

Need internal worker queue with ack/retry?
  └── Redis Streams

Need delayed retry/scheduler?
  └── Redis ZSET + worker
      or Camunda Timer / PostgreSQL job table

Need cross-service durable event?
  └── Kafka + outbox

Need business transaction + event atomicity?
  └── PostgreSQL Outbox
      then Kafka/Redis dispatcher

Need long-running BPMN workflow?
  └── Camunda
```

---

## 4. Comparison Table

| Primitive | Durable? | Replay? | Consumer Group? | Ack? | Best For | Avoid For |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|---|
| Redis Pub/Sub | Tidak | Tidak | Tidak | Tidak | realtime signal, WebSocket fanout | durable job/event |
| Keyspace Notification | Tidak | Tidak | Tidak | Tidak | expired/delete signal | guaranteed timeout/job |
| Redis Streams | Sedang, tergantung Redis config/retention | Terbatas | Ya | Ya | internal job queue | enterprise event backbone |
| Redis ZSET | Data structure, bukan event bus | Manual | Tidak | Manual | delayed job/scheduler | ack-based processing sendirian |
| Kafka | Ya, dengan topic retention | Ya selama retention | Ya | Offset commit | cross-service durable events | tiny local signal |
| PostgreSQL Outbox | Ya | Queryable | Manual | Manual | transaction-safe event record | high-throughput pub/sub langsung |
| Camunda Timer | Ya, engine-managed | Workflow history | Worker model berbeda | Engine-managed | BPMN timer/workflow | generic event bus |

Redis Streams adalah data structure seperti append-only log dan mendukung consumption strategy seperti consumer groups; ini membuatnya lebih kuat daripada Pub/Sub untuk job queue internal. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/data-types/streams/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Kafka, di sisi lain, mempertahankan published messages untuk periode retention yang dikonfigurasi, terlepas dari apakah message sudah dikonsumsi atau belum, sehingga lebih cocok sebagai durable log untuk replay lintas service. ([Apache Kafka](https://kafka.apache.org/082/getting-started/introduction/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5. Delivery Semantics

### Redis Pub/Sub

```text id="e0nk4f"
Publisher sends message
  ↓
Redis pushes to currently connected subscribers
  ↓
If subscriber disconnected:
    message is gone
```

Use case aman:

```text id="8ofxav"
- broadcast websocket notification
- invalidate local cache best-effort
- wake-up signal
- live dashboard update
```

Use case tidak aman:

```text id="ccq54k"
- create order event
- payment completed event
- workflow transition
- audit event
- job that must run
```

Redis docs menyebut Pub/Sub sebagai at-most-once delivery; jika subscriber tidak menerima message karena error atau network disconnect, message hilang selamanya. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/pubsub/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

### Keyspace Notification

```text id="knr9x7"
Redis key changed / expired / deleted
  ↓
Redis emits Pub/Sub event
  ↓
subscriber receives event if connected
```

Karena keyspace notification dibangun di atas Pub/Sub, ia mewarisi sifat best-effort dan tidak replayable. Redis docs menjelaskan keyspace notification memungkinkan client subscribe ke Pub/Sub channel untuk menerima event yang memengaruhi dataset Redis. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/pubsub/keyspace-notifications/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Use case aman:

```text id="gj1yvp"
- expired-key signal
- local cache invalidation
- presence maybe-offline signal
- cleanup trigger
```

Use case tidak aman:

```text id="qn09iy"
- guaranteed delayed job
- final payment timeout
- regulatory deadline transition
- Camunda workflow state transition final
```

---

### Redis Streams

```text id="e4uuev"
Producer XADD
  ↓
Stream entry stored
  ↓
Consumer group XREADGROUP
  ↓
Entry pending until XACK
  ↓
Pending entry can be claimed/retried
```

Redis Streams consumer group membantu membagi entry ke consumer dalam group, dan consumer harus acknowledge entry setelah diproses. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/tools/insight/insight-stream-consumer/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Use case aman:

```text id="jffh92"
- internal worker queue
- async email/report job
- webhook delivery worker
- retryable internal processing
- short/medium retention event-lite
```

Use case perlu hati-hati:

```text id="f6jisp"
- event sourcing utama
- regulatory audit log
- cross-service long replay
- multi-domain enterprise backbone
```

---

### Kafka

```text id="xmipzm"
Producer sends record to topic partition
  ↓
Kafka stores record according to retention policy
  ↓
Consumer group reads and commits offsets
  ↓
Consumer can resume from committed offset
  ↓
Replay possible while data retained
```

Kafka consumers track offsets per partition and can commit offsets so they can resume from those offsets after restart. ([Apache Kafka](https://kafka.apache.org/30/implementation/distribution/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Kafka’s retention model is also fundamentally different from Redis Pub/Sub because records remain available for the configured retention period, independent of whether they have been consumed. ([Apache Kafka](https://kafka.apache.org/082/getting-started/introduction/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Use case aman:

```text id="z3748l"
- cross-service domain event
- durable integration event
- CDC/outbox event stream
- analytics/event pipeline
- replayable event processing
- multi-consumer ecosystem
```

Use case overkill:

```text id="vxljg2"
- local cache invalidation dalam satu service
- simple wake-up signal
- one-off internal worker kecil
```

---

## 6. Pub/Sub Pattern

### Use Case

```text id="gwj8pb"
Order service updates order
  ↓
publish "order-cache-invalidated"
  ↓
live pods clear local cache
```

Channel:

```text id="9ay68g"
order-svc:prod:pubsub:cache-invalidation:v1
```

Message:

```json id="eqifnr"
{
  "eventType": "CACHE_INVALIDATED",
  "cacheFamily": "order-summary",
  "tenantIdHash": "sha256-...",
  "entityIdHash": "sha256-...",
  "version": 1
}
```

### Safe Usage

```text id="1r5zyx"
- message loss acceptable
- subscriber can recover via TTL/reconciliation
- no irreversible side effect
- not used as source of truth
```

### Unsafe Usage

```text id="v36owo"
Pub/Sub message = only record that order was created
```

Karena kalau consumer offline, event hilang.

---

## 7. Pub/Sub + Durable State Pattern

Safe version:

```text id="b20tua"
PostgreSQL commit
  ↓
durable state changed
  ↓
Redis PUBLISH notification
  ↓
live subscribers react
  ↓
offline subscribers reconcile later from PostgreSQL
```

Unsafe version:

```text id="w4j8sg"
Redis PUBLISH event
  ↓
consumer expected to persist business state
```

Rule:

```text id="ou5a4q"
Publish notification after durable write.
Do not make Pub/Sub the durable write.
```

---

## 8. Keyspace Notification Pattern

### Use Case

```text id="vihj0k"
presence key expires
  ↓
keyspace notification emitted
  ↓
subscriber checks if user has other active sessions
  ↓
if none, mark maybe-offline / notify websocket
```

Key:

```text id="f6j5mc"
realtime-svc:prod:tenant:t1:presence:v1:user:uHash:device:dHash
```

Subscribe:

```text id="swtb56"
__keyevent@0__:expired
```

### Safe Handler

```text id="r3ps9h"
expired event received
  ↓
parse key
  ↓
re-check Redis/PostgreSQL current state
  ↓
perform idempotent action
```

### Unsafe Handler

```text id="w1uy4h"
expired event received
  ↓
directly mark user permanently offline
```

Keyspace notification is enabled via Redis configuration and can publish keyspace/keyevent messages to Pub/Sub channels; because it uses Pub/Sub, designs must tolerate missed events. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/pubsub/keyspace-notifications/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9. Redis Streams Pattern

### Use Case

```text id="0o7gls"
POST /reports/export
  ↓
create job in PostgreSQL
  ↓
XADD report-export stream jobId
  ↓
worker XREADGROUP
  ↓
generate report
  ↓
update DB job status
  ↓
XACK
```

Stream key:

```text id="5dswa4"
report-svc:prod:tenant:t1:stream:report-export:v1
```

Group:

```text id="hp1u9w"
report-worker-v1
```

Message:

```json id="2m9q8e"
{
  "jobId": "job-123",
  "tenantId": "t1",
  "jobType": "REPORT_EXPORT",
  "schemaVersion": 1
}
```

### Why Stream, not Pub/Sub?

```text id="zn3m6c"
Pub/Sub:
  if worker offline, job lost

Stream:
  entry can remain in stream
  consumer group can track pending
  worker can XACK after processing
```

Redis Streams supports consumer groups and acknowledgement, which is the key distinction from Pub/Sub for worker queue patterns. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/tools/insight/insight-stream-consumer/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10. Kafka Pattern

### Use Case

```text id="a3cl3d"
Order committed in PostgreSQL
  ↓
Outbox row inserted in same transaction
  ↓
Outbox dispatcher publishes OrderCreated to Kafka
  ↓
Billing service consumes
  ↓
Notification service consumes
  ↓
Analytics consumes
  ↓
Fraud service consumes
```

Topic:

```text id="llf01r"
order.events.v1
```

Event:

```json id="dk6m6s"
{
  "eventId": "uuid",
  "eventType": "OrderCreated",
  "aggregateId": "order-123",
  "aggregateVersion": 1,
  "tenantId": "t1",
  "occurredAt": "2026-07-13T15:00:00+07:00",
  "payload": {
    "orderId": "order-123",
    "status": "CREATED"
  }
}
```

### Why Kafka, not Redis Streams?

```text id="bq7tkt"
Need multiple independent service consumers
Need retention/replay
Need partitioning by aggregate ID
Need operational event backbone
Need integration with analytics/CDC ecosystem
```

Kafka is designed around persisted records with configurable retention, and consumers resume using committed offsets. ([Apache Kafka](https://kafka.apache.org/082/getting-started/introduction/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 11. PostgreSQL Outbox Boundary

Outbox solves this problem:

```text id="xqmda7"
DB transaction succeeds
but message publish fails
```

Pattern:

```text id="aoo6e2"
BEGIN
  update business table
  insert outbox event
COMMIT

Outbox dispatcher:
  read undispatched event
  publish to Kafka or Redis Stream
  mark dispatched
```

### When to use outbox

```text id="0fdc9k"
- business state change must emit event
- enqueue must not be lost
- Redis/Kafka may be temporarily unavailable
- audit/recovery matters
```

### Outbox Table

```sql id="js5fzl"
CREATE TABLE outbox_events (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    aggregate_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    dispatched_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
    attempt_count INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    last_error TEXT NULL
);
```

Outbox bukan pengganti Kafka atau Redis Stream. Outbox adalah bridge agar event publish recoverable setelah transaksi PostgreSQL commit.

---

## 12. Camunda Boundary

Gunakan Camunda ketika concern-nya adalah:

```text id="f7t3d1"
- BPMN process lifecycle
- human task
- timer boundary event
- escalation
- long-running workflow
- compensation
- external task orchestration
```

Jangan mengganti Camunda timer dengan Redis key expiration untuk workflow penting.

Buruk:

```text id="ravzzi"
Redis key expired
  ↓
case auto-approved
```

Lebih aman:

```text id="r4zns4"
Camunda timer fires
  ↓
worker checks PostgreSQL state
  ↓
business transition
  ↓
publish event/outbox
```

Redis keyspace notification boleh menjadi helper signal, tapi bukan source of workflow truth.

---

## 13. Decision Tree

```text id="x93ktz"
Apakah message boleh hilang?
├── Ya
│   ├── Apakah event berasal dari Redis key change?
│   │   └── Keyspace Notification
│   └── Apakah hanya live signal?
│       └── Redis Pub/Sub
│
└── Tidak
    ↓
Apakah message adalah internal job dalam satu bounded service/domain?
├── Ya
│   ├── Butuh ack/retry?
│   │   └── Redis Streams
│   └── Butuh delay?
│       └── ZSET + Stream/worker
│
└── Cross-service domain event?
    ├── Ya
    │   └── Kafka + Outbox
    │
    └── Workflow state/timer?
        └── Camunda / PostgreSQL job table
```

---

## 14. Practical Use Case Matrix

| Use Case | Recommended Primitive | Kenapa |
|---|---|---|
| WebSocket fanout antar pod | Redis Pub/Sub | Live signal cukup |
| Local cache invalidation | Pub/Sub / Keyspace Notification | Best-effort + TTL |
| Presence expired maybe-offline | Keyspace Notification + re-check | Expired signal cukup |
| Guaranteed reminder | Camunda Timer / DB job / ZSET scheduler | Perlu recovery |
| Async email internal | Redis Streams + DB job | Ack/retry cukup |
| Report export job | DB job + Redis Streams | User-visible job |
| Webhook delivery retry | DB job + Stream + ZSET retry | Retry/DLQ perlu |
| Cross-service `OrderCreated` | Kafka + Outbox | Durable multi-consumer |
| Audit event | PostgreSQL/Kafka | Retention/replay penting |
| Payment completed event | Kafka + outbox + idempotency | Tidak boleh hilang |
| Cache rebuild wake-up | Pub/Sub or Stream | Tergantung importance |
| Camunda external task helper | Redis Stream optional | Camunda remains workflow owner |
| Feature flag change notification | Pub/Sub + config version | Best-effort ok |
| Authorization policy changed | Kafka/outbox or version key | Stale auth risk tinggi |

---

## 15. Pub/Sub vs Keyspace Notification

| Aspek | Redis Pub/Sub | Keyspace Notification |
|---|---|---|
| Event source | Aplikasi publish sendiri | Redis emits due to key change |
| Channel design | Bebas | `__keyevent@db__:*` / `__keyspace@db__:*` |
| Message content | Bebas | Event/key depending channel |
| Reliability | At-most-once | At-most-once |
| Replay | Tidak | Tidak |
| Use case utama | live signal | Redis-key lifecycle reaction |
| Good for | websocket, cache signal | expired/delete signal |
| Bad for | durable events | guaranteed delayed job |

Rule:

```text id="7v52xo"
Gunakan Pub/Sub jika aplikasi tahu kapan harus publish.
Gunakan keyspace notification jika event yang dicari adalah perubahan Redis key.
```

---

## 16. Pub/Sub vs Streams

| Aspek | Pub/Sub | Redis Streams |
|---|---:|---:|
| Message persists? | Tidak | Ya, sebagai stream entry sampai trimmed/deleted |
| Offline consumer receives later? | Tidak | Bisa, selama entry masih ada dan group/offset sesuai |
| Consumer group | Tidak | Ya |
| Ack | Tidak | Ya |
| Pending recovery | Tidak | Ya |
| Latency | Sangat rendah | Rendah |
| Complexity | Rendah | Sedang |
| Use case | live notification | internal job/event-lite |

Redis Pub/Sub has at-most-once semantics and message loss on disconnect; Redis Streams adds stored entries and consumer-group processing. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/pubsub/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 17. Streams vs Kafka

| Aspek | Redis Streams | Kafka |
|---|---:|---:|
| Scope ideal | Internal service/domain job | Cross-service event backbone |
| Retention | Bounded Redis memory/persistence config | Topic retention designed for log storage |
| Replay | Terbatas | Kuat selama retention |
| Consumer group | Ya | Ya |
| Pending/ack | Stream PEL/XACK | Offset commit model |
| Partitioning | Key-slot/sharding Redis | Topic partitions |
| Ecosystem | Redis-centric | Event streaming ecosystem luas |
| Operational cost | Lebih ringan | Lebih berat |
| Best for | internal queue | durable domain events |

Kafka retains messages for a configured retention period and consumers can resume from committed offsets; those two properties make it better suited for replayable cross-service event processing than Redis Pub/Sub and often stronger than Redis Streams for enterprise event backbone use cases. ([Apache Kafka](https://kafka.apache.org/082/getting-started/introduction/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 18. Streams vs PostgreSQL Job Table

| Aspek | Redis Streams | PostgreSQL Job Table |
|---|---:|---:|
| Queue throughput | Tinggi | Sedang |
| Transaction with business data | Tidak langsung | Ya |
| Query/debug | Terbatas | Kuat SQL |
| Audit | Terbatas | Kuat |
| Retry metadata | Manual/Redis+DB | Natural |
| Worker wakeup | Bagus | Polling/notify |
| Best design | execution queue | durable job state |

Recommended:

```text id="htncbd"
PostgreSQL job table:
  durable state

Redis Stream:
  execution signal / queue

Worker:
  reads stream, updates DB, XACK last
```

---

## 19. Streams vs Camunda

| Aspek | Redis Streams | Camunda |
|---|---:|---:|
| Job queue | Ya | External task/job worker model |
| BPMN | Tidak | Ya |
| Human task | Tidak | Ya |
| Timer event | Manual | Native workflow timer |
| Compensation | Manual | BPMN-supported |
| Process history | Manual | Engine-managed |
| Best for | technical async work | business workflow orchestration |

Gunakan Redis Streams untuk technical worker queue. Gunakan Camunda untuk long-running process, human workflow, timer, escalation, compensation, dan state transition yang merupakan bagian dari BPMN.

---

## 20. Message Loss Tolerance Classification

| Class | Kehilangan message boleh? | Primitive |
|---|---:|---|
| UI signal | Ya | Pub/Sub |
| Local cache invalidation | Ya, jika TTL pendek | Pub/Sub/Keyspace |
| Presence maybe-offline | Ya, jika re-check | Keyspace |
| Cache rebuild | Biasanya ya | Pub/Sub/Stream |
| Internal job non-critical | Sebaiknya tidak | Stream/List with recovery |
| User-visible async job | Tidak | DB job + Stream |
| Cross-service business event | Tidak | Outbox + Kafka |
| Audit/compliance event | Tidak | DB/Kafka |
| Workflow timer | Tidak | Camunda/DB scheduler |

Rule:

```text id="mogz9h"
Jika message loss menyebabkan data salah,
jangan pakai Pub/Sub/keyspace notification sebagai mekanisme utama.
```

---

## 21. Replay Requirement Classification

| Need Replay? | Example | Primitive |
|---|---|---|
| Tidak | websocket online notification | Pub/Sub |
| Reconcile only | local cache invalidation | Pub/Sub + TTL/source read |
| Short internal retry | email job | Redis Stream |
| Operational replay | webhook delivery | DB job + Stream |
| Cross-service replay | order event to billing/analytics | Kafka |
| Legal/audit replay | enforcement decision event | DB/Kafka with governance |

Kalau consumer baru harus bisa memproses event lama, Pub/Sub dan keyspace notification langsung gugur.

---

## 22. Fanout Pattern

### Pub/Sub Fanout

```text id="ex8azn"
1 publish
  ↓
all currently connected subscribers receive
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="vej62a"
- websocket broadcast
- local cache invalidation
```

Tidak cocok untuk:

```text id="skvgsz"
- setiap subscriber harus pasti menerima
```

### Kafka Fanout

```text id="m783q7"
1 topic
  ↓
many independent consumer groups
  ↓
each group has its own offset
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="75n5xt"
- billing
- notification
- analytics
- fraud
- audit projection
```

### Redis Streams Consumer Group

```text id="q7di6p"
1 stream + 1 group
  ↓
message distributed among consumers in group
```

Jika butuh multiple independent consumers di Redis Streams, biasanya buat multiple groups. Tapi untuk broad enterprise fanout, Kafka lebih natural.

---

## 23. Ordering Requirement

| Ordering Need | Recommended |
|---|---|
| No ordering needed | Pub/Sub/Stream/Kafka |
| Per worker queue order approximate | Redis Stream/List |
| Per aggregate order | Kafka partition by aggregateId or DB/Camunda validation |
| Global order | Avoid if possible; expensive |
| Workflow order | Camunda / DB state machine |

Redis Streams preserve stream ID order, tetapi multi-consumer processing bisa selesai out of order. Kafka preserves order within a partition, not globally across partitions. Untuk business correctness, jangan hanya mengandalkan message order; validasi state transition di DB/domain tetap wajib.

---

## 24. Example Architecture for Stack Anda

```text id="wtw271"
Jersey Resource
  ↓
Command Service
  ↓
PostgreSQL transaction via MyBatis
  ├── update business table
  ├── insert outbox event
  └── insert async job if needed
  ↓
Commit
  ↓
Outbox Dispatcher
  ├── publish domain event to Kafka
  └── enqueue internal job to Redis Stream
  ↓
Redis Pub/Sub
  └── optional live notification / local cache invalidation
  ↓
Camunda
  └── workflow state/timer if BPMN lifecycle needed
```

Boundary:

```text id="92ou0m"
PostgreSQL:
  truth

Kafka:
  durable external/domain event

Redis Stream:
  internal execution queue

Redis Pub/Sub:
  live signal

Keyspace Notification:
  Redis key lifecycle signal

Camunda:
  workflow lifecycle
```

---

## 25. Scenario: Case Submitted

### Requirement

```text id="n0zlyb"
User submits case.
Need:
- update case status
- start/continue workflow
- notify UI
- publish domain event
- maybe enqueue document generation
```

Recommended:

```text id="gl8p3n"
PostgreSQL transaction:
  update cases status = SUBMITTED
  insert outbox CaseSubmitted
  insert async job GenerateSubmissionDocument

After commit:
  outbox -> Kafka topic case.events.v1
  job dispatcher -> Redis Stream document-job
  Pub/Sub -> UI notification best-effort
  Camunda -> BPMN process continuation
```

Do not:

```text id="oqv0kg"
Use Pub/Sub as the only CaseSubmitted event.
Use key expiration to drive official workflow transition.
```

---

## 26. Scenario: Local Cache Invalidation

Requirement:

```text id="lpc3we"
Policy/config changed.
All pods should clear local cache quickly.
If a pod misses signal, TTL should eventually fix it.
```

Recommended:

```text id="5qh2cc"
PostgreSQL/config source version updated
  ↓
Redis shared cache deleted/version bumped
  ↓
Redis Pub/Sub publish local invalidation signal
  ↓
Pods clear local cache
  ↓
Local cache TTL short as fallback
```

Primitive:

```text id="g4kz5x"
Pub/Sub is enough
```

Because message loss only delays invalidation until TTL/version refresh.

---

## 27. Scenario: Expired Reminder

Requirement:

```text id="o62n4c"
Send reminder when case review is overdue.
Reminder should not be lost.
```

Bad:

```text id="5zz7vi"
Set Redis key with TTL
Use keyspace expired event as only trigger
```

Good:

```text id="64hqgk"
PostgreSQL reminder table or Camunda timer stores due work
  ↓
Redis keyspace notification can wake worker
  ↓
worker checks DB/Camunda state
  ↓
sends idempotent reminder
  ↓
reconciliation scans due reminders periodically
```

Primitive:

```text id="xxwx2u"
Camunda Timer or DB/ZSET scheduler = source
Keyspace notification = optional signal
```

---

## 28. Scenario: Async Report Export

Requirement:

```text id="eabnq1"
User requests report export.
Job must be visible in status API.
Retries needed.
DLQ needed.
```

Recommended:

```text id="2guwpa"
POST /reports/export
  ↓
idempotency check
  ↓
insert async_jobs row
  ↓
outbox or direct enqueue
  ↓
Redis Stream report-export
  ↓
worker processes
  ↓
DB job status updated
  ↓
XACK
```

Primitive:

```text id="oym0rh"
DB job table + Redis Stream
```

Kafka is often overkill unless report request is cross-domain event consumed by many services.

---

## 29. Scenario: Order Created Domain Event

Requirement:

```text id="2vpytu"
Order creation must be consumed by billing, notification, analytics, and audit.
Consumers may be offline and catch up later.
```

Recommended:

```text id="o5z8jy"
PostgreSQL order transaction
  ↓
Outbox OrderCreated
  ↓
Kafka order.events.v1
  ↓
Independent consumer groups
```

Primitive:

```text id="do70gs"
Kafka + Outbox
```

Redis Pub/Sub is unsafe because offline consumers miss events. Redis Streams can work for internal processing, but Kafka is more appropriate as durable cross-service event backbone.

---

## 30. Failure Mode Comparison

| Failure | Pub/Sub | Keyspace Notification | Redis Stream | Kafka |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Subscriber offline | Message lost | Event lost | Can read later if retained/group setup | Can read later if retained |
| Consumer crashes mid-processing | No recovery | No recovery | Pending entry can be claimed | Offset not committed, can reprocess |
| Need replay | No | No | Limited | Yes within retention |
| Producer publish failed | Message absent | N/A | XADD failure, use outbox | Produce failure, use outbox/retry |
| Slow consumer | Misses nothing? No, Pub/Sub can buffer only connection-level and fail | Same | Pending/lag grows | Lag grows |
| Message storage growth | None | None | Stream grows until trim | Topic grows until retention |
| Duplicate processing | Possible via app retry | Possible via duplicate reactions | Possible; must be idempotent | Possible; must be idempotent |

---

## 31. Misuse Patterns

| Misuse | Kenapa bahaya | Ganti dengan |
|---|---|---|
| Pub/Sub untuk payment event | Consumer offline kehilangan event | Kafka + outbox |
| Keyspace expired untuk guaranteed job | Event bisa hilang/delay | DB job/Camunda/ZSET+reconcile |
| Stream untuk enterprise audit log tanpa DB/Kafka | Retention/ops kurang cocok | Kafka/DB |
| Kafka untuk local cache invalidation kecil | Overkill | Pub/Sub |
| Camunda untuk simple email queue | Overkill workflow | Redis Stream |
| PostgreSQL polling untuk high-volume ephemeral notification | DB berat | Pub/Sub/Stream |
| Redis-only queue untuk critical job | Redis loss/eviction risk | DB job + Stream/outbox |
| Pub/Sub event langsung mutate DB final | At-most-once unsafe | Durable event/source check |

---

## 32. Selection Heuristics

### Use Redis Pub/Sub when:

```text id="hefz2w"
[ ] consumer must be online now
[ ] message loss acceptable
[ ] no replay needed
[ ] no ack needed
[ ] low latency signal
```

Examples:

```text id="opxtoa"
- websocket fanout
- local cache invalidation
- wake-up signal
```

---

### Use Keyspace Notification when:

```text id="sd3wge"
[ ] event is about Redis key lifecycle
[ ] expired/delete/set signal is useful
[ ] missed event can be recovered or ignored
[ ] handler re-checks source of truth
```

Examples:

```text id="i9n8zm"
- presence expired maybe-offline
- debug expired keys
- cleanup signal
```

---

### Use Redis Streams when:

```text id="gird2b"
[ ] internal async job
[ ] ack/retry needed
[ ] worker crash recovery needed
[ ] retention bounded
[ ] consumers are within same bounded domain/service
```

Examples:

```text id="tt3svs"
- email worker
- report export
- webhook delivery
- file processing
```

---

### Use Kafka when:

```text id="cz9pit"
[ ] cross-service event
[ ] multiple independent consumers
[ ] replay needed
[ ] retention governance needed
[ ] event schema/versioning matters
[ ] consumer groups need independent offsets
```

Examples:

```text id="zhj8dt"
- OrderCreated
- PaymentCaptured
- CaseSubmitted
- CustomerUpdated
- audit/event pipeline
```

---

### Use PostgreSQL Outbox when:

```text id="8di2lo"
[ ] DB state change and event publish must not diverge
[ ] event/job must survive Redis/Kafka outage
[ ] recovery/audit matters
```

---

### Use Camunda when:

```text id="1q34kk"
[ ] it is a workflow, not just a queue
[ ] timer is part of BPMN
[ ] human task/escalation/compensation exists
[ ] process state must be visible and managed
```

---

## 33. Recommended Standard for Your Stack

Untuk stack **Jersey + MyBatis + PostgreSQL + Camunda + Redis + Kafka**, saya sarankan standard ini:

```text id="xkfmv3"
1. Pub/Sub
   Only for best-effort live signal.

2. Keyspace Notification
   Only for Redis key lifecycle signal, always with fallback if important.

3. Redis Streams
   Internal technical job queue, with DB job state for important jobs.

4. Kafka
   Cross-service durable domain/integration events.

5. PostgreSQL Outbox
   Required bridge for business transaction → event/job publish.

6. Camunda
   Owner of workflow/timer/human-task lifecycle.
```

Policy singkat:

```text id="2q0ag7"
If it changes business truth:
  PostgreSQL first.

If it informs other services:
  Outbox + Kafka.

If it runs background work:
  DB job + Redis Stream.

If it only notifies live pods:
  Pub/Sub.

If it is workflow:
  Camunda.
```

---

## 34. Java Boundary Interfaces

### Live Signal

```java id="b7lbb5"
public interface LiveSignalPublisher {
    void publish(CacheInvalidationSignal signal);
}
```

Implementation:

```text id="dey8jd"
Redis Pub/Sub
```

---

### Internal Job Queue

```java id="sneuf6"
public interface InternalJobQueue {
    void enqueue(JobMessage message);
}
```

Implementation:

```text id="byv13n"
Redis Streams
```

---

### Domain Event Publisher

```java id="rtgzt8"
public interface DomainEventPublisher {
    void publish(DomainEvent event);
}
```

Implementation:

```text id="p612u3"
Outbox dispatcher -> Kafka
```

---

### Workflow Gateway

```java id="vpghyy"
public interface WorkflowGateway {
    void startOrCorrelateCaseProcess(CaseSubmitted event);
}
```

Implementation:

```text id="1zn0np"
Camunda client/service
```

Rule:

```text id="r2me6d"
Application service depends on intent interface,
not directly on Redis Pub/Sub, Redis Stream, Kafka, or Camunda client.
```

## 37. Testing Matrix

| Test | Primitive |
|---|---|
| Pub/Sub subscriber offline misses message and system tolerates it | Pub/Sub |
| Local cache eventually refreshes despite missed signal | Pub/Sub |
| Keyspace expired event handler re-checks DB | Keyspace |
| Missed keyspace event recovered by reconciliation | Keyspace |
| Stream worker crash before XACK leads to pending recovery | Stream |
| Stream duplicate message does not duplicate side effect | Stream |
| Kafka consumer restart resumes from committed offset | Kafka |
| Kafka event can be replayed within retention | Kafka |
| Outbox event not lost when Kafka/Redis down | Outbox |
| Camunda timer fires and validates DB state | Camunda |
| Wrong primitive blocked in architecture review | Governance |

---

## 38. Architecture Review Checklist

```text id="4sqsx5"
[ ] Is message loss acceptable?
[ ] Is replay required?
[ ] Are there multiple independent consumers?
[ ] Is this event internal to one service or cross-service?
[ ] Does this event represent business truth?
[ ] Is there a DB transaction involved?
[ ] Does publish need to be atomic with DB commit?
[ ] Does consumer need ack/retry/DLQ?
[ ] Is ordering required? Global or per aggregate?
[ ] Is this actually a workflow/timer?
[ ] What happens if consumer is offline?
[ ] What happens if Redis/Kafka is down?
[ ] What is the retention policy?
[ ] What is the source of truth?
[ ] How is duplicate processing prevented?
```

---

## 39. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Pub/Sub as domain event bus | Event loss | Kafka + outbox |
| Keyspace notification as scheduler | Missed/delayed jobs | ZSET/DB/Camunda |
| Redis Stream with no DB state for critical job | Hard recovery/audit | DB job table |
| Kafka for every tiny local signal | Complexity overhead | Pub/Sub |
| Camunda for simple technical queue | Workflow overhead | Redis Stream |
| Outbox skipped because publish “usually works” | Lost events on failure | Outbox |
| No idempotency in consumers | Duplicate side effects | DB constraints/idempotency |
| No retention strategy | Memory/disk growth | Retention policy |
| No DLQ | Poison loops | DLQ + owner |
| No reconciliation for best-effort signal | Silent stale state | Periodic reconciliation |

---

## 40. Ringkasan Praktis

```text id="kd2pzf"
Redis Pub/Sub:
  live signal, boleh hilang, no replay

Keyspace Notification:
  Redis key lifecycle signal, Pub/Sub-based, best-effort

Redis Streams:
  internal queue/event-lite, consumer group, ack, pending recovery

Kafka:
  durable cross-service event log, retention, replay, offsets

PostgreSQL Outbox:
  transaction-safe bridge from DB state to messaging

Camunda:
  workflow lifecycle, timers, human task, BPMN orchestration
```

Kalimat kunci:

> Pilih primitive berdasarkan failure semantics, bukan kenyamanan API. Kalau message boleh hilang, Pub/Sub cukup. Kalau job harus selesai, pakai Stream + durable state. Kalau event harus dikonsumsi banyak service dan bisa direplay, pakai Kafka + outbox. Kalau itu workflow, pakai Camunda.

## Response:
7/13/2026, 7:25:31 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 14 — Distributed Coordination Pattern**. Fokusnya: memakai Redis untuk koordinasi antar pod/worker secara aman, termasuk lease, heartbeat, semaphore, ownership, dan fencing token—serta batas kapan Redis tidak cukup.

# Seri 14 - Distributed Coordination Pattern

Status: **Seri 14 dari 22 seri lanjutan**.  
Setelah seri ini selesai, tersisa **8 seri**.

---

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

**Distributed coordination** adalah teknik agar beberapa instance service/pod/worker bisa bekerja bersama tanpa saling merusak state.

Di stack Anda:

```text id="gx13mt"
Java 17+
Jersey/JAX-RS
MyBatis
PostgreSQL
Redis
Camunda
Docker/Kubernetes
On-Prem / AWS / Azure
```

Redis sering dipakai untuk coordination karena cepat, shared, mendukung TTL, atomic command, Lua scripting, dan struktur seperti String, Hash, Set, Sorted Set, Stream. Tetapi Redis **bukan consensus system** seperti ZooKeeper/etcd, dan Redis lock/lease tidak boleh dianggap sebagai jaminan absolut untuk correctness bisnis yang kritikal.

Redis `SET` mendukung pola conditional set dengan `NX` dan expiry seperti `EX`/`PX`, sehingga bisa dipakai sebagai primitive lock/lease sederhana; Redis docs juga mendokumentasikan Redlock sebagai algoritma distributed lock yang lebih kuat dibanding single-instance lock sederhana. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/set/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="wdjqlw"
Distributed coordination =
  cara beberapa proses/pod/worker berbagi keputusan sementara:
    - siapa yang boleh menjalankan job?
    - siapa leader saat ini?
    - berapa worker boleh jalan bersamaan?
    - apakah worker masih hidup?
    - siapa owner resource sementara?
    - apakah lease masih valid?
```

Tetapi coordination harus dibedakan dari **business correctness**.

```text id="xegddp"
Redis coordination:
  temporary control plane

PostgreSQL:
  final business invariant

Camunda:
  workflow lifecycle

Kubernetes Lease:
  cluster-native leader election / coordination

Kafka:
  durable event coordination across services
```

Kalimat kunci:

```text id="pt3m5r"
Redis boleh membantu koordinasi.
Redis tidak boleh menjadi satu-satunya penjaga kebenaran bisnis final.
```

---

## 3. Diagram Besar

```text id="u7nnlf"
Multiple Pods / Workers
│
├── Pod A
├── Pod B
├── Pod C
└── Pod D
      ↓
Redis Coordination Layer
│
├── lock key
├── lease key
├── heartbeat key
├── semaphore key
├── worker ownership key
├── sorted-set lease index
├── stream consumer group
└── fencing token counter
      ↓
Business Safety Layer
│
├── PostgreSQL unique constraint
├── PostgreSQL optimistic lock
├── PostgreSQL job claim
├── Camunda process state
├── external provider idempotency key
└── domain state transition validation
```

Interpretasi:

```text id="hcnplh"
Redis decides who should try.
PostgreSQL/Camunda decides whether the action is still valid.
```

---

## 4. Coordination Pattern Map

| Pattern | Redis primitive | Cocok untuk | Jangan untuk |
|---|---|---|---|
| Lease / lock | String `SET NX PX` | cache rebuild, singleton job ringan | final payment/business correctness sendirian |
| Heartbeat | String TTL / Hash | worker presence, pod liveness app-level | Kubernetes health replacement |
| Semaphore | ZSet/Lua or Redisson | limit concurrent workers/API calls | quota final/auditable |
| Worker ownership | Hash/String + TTL | claim temporary task | permanent ownership |
| Leader guard | String lease / Kubernetes Lease | one active scheduler | strong consensus |
| Fencing token | Redis counter + DB check | reject stale owner writes | tanpa downstream enforcement |
| Barrier | Set/Counter + TTL | wait N workers ready | long-lived workflow |
| Rate/concurrency cap | Counter/ZSet/Lua | protect downstream | legal quota final |
| Stream group coordination | Redis Streams PEL | worker distribution/retry | workflow engine replacement |
| Delayed schedule | ZSet score=time | retry/reminder helper | guaranteed BPMN timer tanpa reconciliation |

---

## 5. Important Boundary: Coordination vs Correctness

### Coordination

```text id="s3lg1g"
"Pod A boleh mencoba menjalankan job ini."
```

### Correctness

```text id="qsmgpg"
"Job ini memang masih boleh dijalankan,
belum pernah selesai,
dan efek bisnisnya tidak akan double."
```

Redis bisa membantu jawaban pertama. Jawaban kedua harus tetap dijaga oleh:

```text id="m7gmf7"
- PostgreSQL transaction
- unique constraint
- optimistic locking
- status transition check
- Camunda process/task state
- external idempotency key
```

Contoh:

```text id="mdod9k"
Redis lock acquired
  ↓
worker tries to approve case
  ↓
PostgreSQL checks:
  status = WAITING_APPROVAL
  version = expected
  approver is allowed
  not already approved
  ↓
only then update status
```

---

## 6. Pattern 1 - Lease

Lease adalah hak sementara untuk melakukan sesuatu.

```text id="wxsro6"
SET leaseKey ownerToken NX PX 30000
```

Jika berhasil:

```text id="zb4tfe"
ownerToken holds lease for max 30 seconds
```

Jika worker crash:

```text id="xgldeq"
lease expires automatically
```

Redis `SET resource-name anystring NX EX max-lock-time` didokumentasikan sebagai cara sederhana untuk locking, meski Redis docs menganjurkan Redlock untuk guarantee yang lebih baik pada skenario distributed lock yang lebih kompleks. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/set/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Key

```text id="ux7k4n"
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:lease:case-rebuild:v1:case:c991
```

### Value

```json id="lwmkme"
{
  "owner": "case-svc-pod-a",
  "token": "uuid",
  "startedAt": "2026-07-13T16:00:00+07:00",
  "leaseMs": 30000
}
```

### Cocok untuk

```text id="c9syxw"
- cache rebuild
- singleton lightweight scheduler
- temporary worker ownership
- polling guard
- dedup expensive computation
```

### Tidak cocok sendirian untuk

```text id="hquydi"
- final payment capture
- legal/regulatory final transition
- irreversible external side effect
- multi-resource transaction
```

---

## 7. Safe Lease Release

Jangan release dengan:

```text id="c282ac"
DEL leaseKey
```

Karena lease lama bisa expire, owner baru acquire, lalu owner lama menjalankan `DEL` dan menghapus lease owner baru.

Gunakan token comparison via Lua:

```lua id="br8qpq"
if redis.call("GET", KEYS[1]) == ARGV[1] then
  return redis.call("DEL", KEYS[1])
else
  return 0
end
```

Rule:

```text id="5gwwa2"
Release hanya boleh dilakukan oleh owner yang memegang token yang sama.
```

---

## 8. Pattern 2 - Renewable Lease

Lease bisa diperpanjang jika job valid dan owner masih aktif.

```text id="50mct3"
Acquire lease 30s
  ↓
Every 10s:
  if token still matches:
    extend lease to 30s
```

Lua renew:

```lua id="sxyubw"
if redis.call("GET", KEYS[1]) == ARGV[1] then
  return redis.call("PEXPIRE", KEYS[1], ARGV[2])
else
  return 0
end
```

### Guardrail

```text id="ebjlfm"
- renew interval < lease TTL
- stop renew before shutdown
- max total lease duration optional
- handler must tolerate lease loss
- DB state must still validate action
```

### Anti-pattern

```text id="xiv1a7"
Lease renew forever without max duration
```

Ini bisa menyembunyikan stuck worker.

---

## 9. Pattern 3 - Heartbeat / Presence

Heartbeat menunjukkan worker/pod masih hidup di level aplikasi.

### Key

```text id="f0mj1r"
worker-svc:prod:tenant:t1:heartbeat:v1:worker:pod-a
```

### Command

```text id="tzcf1j"
SET heartbeatKey "alive" EX 30
```

Worker refresh setiap 10 detik.

### Use case

```text id="6we3tp"
- track active workers
- show worker status dashboard
- detect maybe-dead worker
- cleanup temporary ownership
```

### Caveat

Heartbeat Redis bukan pengganti Kubernetes liveness/readiness probe. Kubernetes sendiri punya mekanisme Lease untuk use case seperti node heartbeats dan leader election, dan custom workload juga bisa memakai Lease API untuk leader election melalui Kubernetes API. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/architecture/leases/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10. Pattern 4 - Worker Ownership

Worker ownership digunakan agar satu worker mengklaim task tertentu untuk waktu terbatas.

```text id="mfwu0u"
SET taskOwnerKey ownerToken NX PX 60000
```

Key:

```text id="0fm9ch"
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:owner:report-job:v1:job:j123
```

Flow:

```text id="smmqkg"
worker sees job j123
  ↓
try SET owner key NX PX
  ├── success -> process
  └── fail -> another worker owns it
```

Namun untuk job penting, ownership Redis harus dipadukan dengan DB claim:

```sql id="qg6he1"
UPDATE async_jobs
SET status = 'PROCESSING',
    locked_by = #{workerId},
    locked_until = now() + interval '5 minutes'
WHERE id = #{jobId}
  AND status IN ('QUEUED', 'RETRY_SCHEDULED')
  AND (locked_until IS NULL OR locked_until < now())
RETURNING *;
```

Rule:

```text id="duw4vm"
Redis ownership reduces contention.
PostgreSQL claim prevents duplicate business work.
```

---

## 11. Pattern 5 - Distributed Semaphore

Semaphore membatasi jumlah worker/action concurrent.

Contoh:

```text id="2x3l8y"
Maksimal 5 worker boleh call external provider X bersamaan.
```

Redis structure:

```text id="rsoi3g"
ZSET semaphoreKey
  member = ownerToken
  score = leaseExpiryMillis
```

Key:

```text id="5aee8w"
worker-svc:prod:global:semaphore:external-provider-x:v1
```

Flow atomic via Lua:

```text id="u622e2"
1. remove expired permits
2. count active permits
3. if active < max:
      add owner token with expiry
      allow
   else:
      deny
```

Lua shape:

```lua id="jz396u"
local key = KEYS[1]
local now = tonumber(ARGV[1])
local leaseUntil = tonumber(ARGV[2])
local maxPermits = tonumber(ARGV[3])
local owner = ARGV[4]

redis.call("ZREMRANGEBYSCORE", key, "-inf", now)

local count = redis.call("ZCARD", key)

if count >= maxPermits then
  return {0, count}
end

redis.call("ZADD", key, leaseUntil, owner)
redis.call("PEXPIRE", key, ARGV[5])

return {1, count + 1}
```

Release:

```text id="zdir4k"
ZREM semaphoreKey ownerToken
```

### Cocok untuk

```text id="94ysw8"
- limit concurrent external API calls
- limit report generation workers
- limit tenant-level background jobs
- protect downstream PostgreSQL/service
```

### Tidak cocok untuk

```text id="lbvc5g"
- billing quota final
- compliance quota
- irreversible guarantee
```

---

## 12. Pattern 6 - Leader Guard

Leader guard memastikan hanya satu pod menjalankan tugas tertentu.

Use case:

```text id="u6h2l3"
- one scheduler instance scans retry ZSET
- one worker runs cleanup
- one pod refreshes reference cache
- one reconciliation loop per service
```

Redis approach:

```text id="6hth20"
SET leaderKey ownerToken NX PX 30000
renew periodically
```

Key:

```text id="kc7pyd"
job-svc:prod:global:leader:retry-scheduler:v1
```

Flow:

```text id="fnrdtk"
pod tries acquire leader lease
  ├── success -> run scheduler loop
  └── fail -> standby
```

But for Kubernetes-native workloads, **Kubernetes Lease** is often a better choice for leader election because it is supported by the Kubernetes coordination API and stored through the API server. Kubernetes docs describe Leases as usable by workloads for leader election, and Kubernetes control-plane components also use Lease-based leader election in HA setups. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/architecture/leases/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Redis Leader Guard vs Kubernetes Lease

| Aspect | Redis Leader Guard | Kubernetes Lease |
|---|---|---|
| Works outside K8s | Ya | Tidak |
| Native to K8s | Tidak | Ya |
| Requires Redis | Ya | Tidak |
| Good for app-level coordination | Ya | Ya |
| Better for controller-style leader election | Bisa | Biasanya lebih cocok |
| Needs fencing for correctness | Ya | Ya |

Important caveat: Kubernetes client-go leader election documentation notes that leader election does **not guarantee** only one client is acting as leader, also described as lack of fencing. Jadi tetap desain leader task idempotent dan gunakan downstream guard. ([Go Packages](https://pkg.go.dev/k8s.io/client-go/tools/leaderelection?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 13. Pattern 7 - Fencing Token

Fencing token adalah angka monotonik yang diberikan setiap kali lease/lock didapat.

Problem:

```text id="56dm1g"
Worker A gets lock
Worker A pauses / GC pause / network freeze
Lock expires
Worker B gets lock
Worker B writes new data
Worker A resumes and writes stale data
```

Lease saja tidak cukup untuk mencegah stale owner menulis.

Solusi: fencing token.

```text id="s7j8gk"
Acquire lease
  ↓
INCR fencing counter
  ↓
owner gets token 42

Next owner gets token 43
```

Downstream storage harus menolak token lama:

```sql id="q0hj9j"
UPDATE resource_table
SET value = #{value},
    fencing_token = #{token}
WHERE id = #{id}
  AND #{token} > fencing_token;
```

Jika token 42 mencoba menulis setelah token 43:

```text id="ovckim"
UPDATE count = 0
stale owner rejected
```

### Key

```text id="r3r706"
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:fence:case-rebuild:v1:case:c991
```

### Rule

```text id="dihzw7"
Fencing token only works if downstream system checks it.
```

Kalau downstream tidak memvalidasi token, token hanya angka dekoratif.

---

## 14. Pattern 8 - Barrier

Barrier menunggu beberapa participant mencapai titik tertentu.

Contoh:

```text id="x69r4j"
Need 3 shard workers ready before starting batch
```

Redis Set:

```text id="jw9e5j"
SADD barrierKey workerId
SCARD barrierKey
EXPIRE barrierKey 300
```

Flow:

```text id="2uoplp"
worker ready
  ↓
SADD barrierKey workerId
  ↓
if SCARD >= required:
    proceed
```

Use case:

```text id="ayiiam"
- lightweight startup coordination
- batch phase transition internal
- test/integration coordination
```

Avoid for:

```text id="pwp3ez"
- long-running workflow
- critical business approval
- human process synchronization
```

Gunakan Camunda atau PostgreSQL state machine untuk workflow yang harus auditable.

---

## 15. Pattern 9 - Work Deduplication

Dedup mencegah work yang sama diproses berkali-kali.

```text id="p67fu4"
SET dedupKey PROCESSING NX EX 3600
```

Key:

```text id="4djk7a"
worker-svc:prod:tenant:t1:dedup:event:v1:event:eHash
```

Flow:

```text id="o1ykkf"
event arrives
  ↓
SET dedup NX EX
  ├── success -> process
  └── fail -> already processing/processed
```

Untuk critical event, kombinasikan dengan PostgreSQL unique table:

```sql id="54rts3"
CREATE TABLE processed_events (
  tenant_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  event_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  processed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, event_id)
);
```

Redis dedup mempercepat. PostgreSQL dedup memastikan final.

---

## 16. Pattern 10 - Stream Consumer Group Coordination

Redis Streams sudah menyediakan coordination untuk worker queue:

```text id="q4z3oa"
XREADGROUP
  ↓
one entry delivered to one consumer in group
  ↓
entry remains pending until XACK
  ↓
XAUTOCLAIM can transfer stale pending entry
```

`XAUTOCLAIM` mentransfer ownership message pending yang idle lebih lama dari `min-idle-time` ke consumer lain. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/xautoclaim/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Use case:

```text id="ohz3g3"
- worker distribution
- crash recovery
- retry pending job
```

But still:

```text id="jmci7f"
Stream coordination does not replace DB idempotency.
```

Worker tetap harus:

```text id="r7qw26"
- claim DB job
- be idempotent
- ack after durable processing
- handle duplicate delivery
```

---

## 17. Redis Coordination and Time

Redis coordination banyak bergantung pada waktu:

```text id="24nkvi"
- TTL
- lease expiry
- heartbeat expiry
- semaphore lease
- pending idle time
- retry schedule
```

Risiko:

```text id="2eb2w2"
- clock skew antar pod
- GC pause
- network pause
- Redis latency spike
- failover delay
- process freeze
```

Mitigasi:

```text id="ei6w97"
- use Redis server TTL for lock expiration
- choose lease duration with safety margin
- keep critical section short
- renew lease before half TTL
- use fencing token for stale owner protection
- downstream DB validates state/version/token
```

---

## 18. Coordination Failure Modes

| Failure | Contoh | Guardrail |
|---|---|---|
| Owner pauses beyond lease | GC pause | Fencing token + DB check |
| Owner crashes | Pod killed | TTL expiry |
| Owner resumes after losing lease | stale write | Fencing token |
| Redis timeout on acquire | unknown state | retry same operation carefully |
| Redis failover | lease may be lost/delayed | idempotent work |
| Lock key evicted | false unlock | noeviction / dedicated Redis |
| Release deletes another owner | stale DEL | token compare Lua |
| Semaphore permit leaked | worker crash | score expiry |
| Leader split-brain | two schedulers | idempotent scheduler + DB claim |
| Clock skew | wrong expiry score | Redis time / generous buffers |
| Network partition | false ownership | downstream correctness check |

---

## 19. Redis Instance Separation

Jangan campur semua workload dalam satu Redis instance tanpa policy.

```text id="n3j03x"
Cache workload:
  data boleh di-evict

Coordination workload:
  lock/lease/semaphore tidak boleh hilang sembarangan

Queue workload:
  stream/list/zset tidak boleh di-evict jika job penting
```

Rekomendasi:

```text id="5380p2"
- cache Redis instance
- coordination/control-plane Redis instance
- queue/stream Redis instance
```

Atau minimal logical separation + eviction policy yang aman.

Critical:

```text id="5l85dt"
Do not run locks/semaphores/queues on Redis configured as aggressive cache eviction
unless losing them is acceptable.
```

---

## 20. Eviction Policy Warning

Jika Redis memory penuh dan eviction menghapus lock/lease/semaphore key, coordination bisa salah.

Untuk coordination penting:

```text id="97he54"
prefer noeviction
monitor memory
set maxmemory alerts
separate from volatile cache
```

Jika Redis dipakai hanya untuk cache, eviction bisa normal. Jika dipakai untuk coordination, eviction adalah correctness risk.

---

## 21. Java Interface Design

Jangan sebarkan command Redis langsung.

```java id="5j73wc"
public interface LeaseService {
    Optional<LeaseToken> tryAcquire(
        String resource,
        Duration leaseTime
    );

    boolean renew(
        String resource,
        LeaseToken token,
        Duration leaseTime
    );

    boolean release(
        String resource,
        LeaseToken token
    );
}
```

```java id="jga8wa"
public record LeaseToken(
    String ownerId,
    String token,
    long fencingToken,
    Instant acquiredAt,
    Duration leaseTime
) {}
```

Semaphore:

```java id="sryzfe"
public interface DistributedSemaphore {
    Optional<PermitToken> tryAcquire(
        String resource,
        int maxPermits,
        Duration leaseTime
    );

    boolean release(
        String resource,
        PermitToken token
    );
}
```

Leader:

```java id="jlo97u"
public interface LeaderGuard {
    boolean isLeader();
    void start();
    void stop();
}
```

---

## 22. Redis Key Design

Lease:

```text id="rq81fn"
{service}:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:lease:{purpose}:v1:{entity}:{id}
```

Semaphore:

```text id="7wixxc"
{service}:{env}:global:semaphore:{resource}:v1
```

Heartbeat:

```text id="lix8i9"
{service}:{env}:heartbeat:v1:worker:{workerId}
```

Fencing counter:

```text id="zfg3ki"
{service}:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:fence:{purpose}:v1:{entity}:{id}
```

Leader:

```text id="hv2xvi"
{service}:{env}:global:leader:{component}:v1
```

Use hashed IDs for sensitive identifiers.

---

## 23. Coordination in Jersey/JAX-RS Application

Do not coordinate inside Resource directly.

Bad:

```java id="ny7ihk"
@Path("/cases")
public class CaseResource {
    @POST
    public Response submit(...) {
        redis.set(...); // bad
    }
}
```

Better:

```text id="1100r2"
Resource
  ↓
Command Service
  ↓
Coordination Port
  ↓
Redis Coordination Adapter
  ↓
PostgreSQL final state check
```

Example:

```java id="pmms2s"
public void rebuildCaseCache(TenantId tenant, CaseId caseId) {
    String resource = coordinationKeys.caseCacheRebuild(tenant, caseId);

    Optional<LeaseToken> lease = leaseService.tryAcquire(
        resource,
        Duration.ofSeconds(15)
    );

    if (lease.isEmpty()) {
        return;
    }

    try {
        CaseAggregate aggregate = caseRepository.loadCurrent(tenant, caseId);
        cacheStore.setCaseAggregate(tenant, caseId, aggregate);
    } finally {
        leaseService.release(resource, lease.get());
    }
}
```

---

## 24. Coordination for Scheduled Workers

Example retry scheduler:

```text id="2rpyv3"
Multiple pods run same app.
Only one should scan retry ZSET at a time.
```

Flow:

```text id="e7i1sr"
try acquire leader lease
  ├── fail -> standby
  └── success -> scan due retry jobs
        ↓
      for each job:
        DB claim job
        XADD stream
```

Even if two pods become leaders due to lease edge case:

```text id="jg8mfk"
DB claim prevents duplicate enqueue/process.
```

Rule:

```text id="h6xzip"
Leader lease optimizes.
DB claim protects.
```

---

## 25. Coordination for External API Concurrency

Requirement:

```text id="r55vef"
Provider X allows only 10 concurrent calls per tenant.
```

Use distributed semaphore:

```text id="f6vd3k"
provider-svc:prod:tenant:t1:semaphore:provider-x:v1
maxPermits = 10
lease = 2 minutes
```

Flow:

```text id="h6ivvd"
worker wants external call
  ↓
try acquire permit
  ├── denied -> retry later
  └── allowed -> call provider
        ↓
      release permit
```

Guardrails:

```text id="9f1qyy"
- permit lease auto-expires
- external call timeout < permit lease
- release token after call
- provider idempotency key for retries
```

---

## 26. Coordination for Cache Rebuild

Hot key expired:

```text id="9cir18"
100 pods see cache miss
```

Use rebuild lease:

```text id="e9ju5j"
cache-rebuild lease
  ↓
only one pod loads DB and sets cache
others wait briefly or return stale/fallback
```

This is a good Redis coordination use case because:

```text id="18ysvd"
- correctness impact low
- duplicate rebuild is efficiency problem
- DB remains source of truth
```

---

## 27. Coordination for Camunda Worker

Camunda worker may need concurrency cap:

```text id="jacw7j"
Only 5 external tasks of type X processed concurrently per tenant.
```

Use Redis semaphore:

```text id="oafly8"
workflow-svc:prod:tenant:t1:semaphore:camunda-task-x:v1
```

But process/task state remains Camunda-owned:

```text id="gf78ko"
Redis controls worker concurrency.
Camunda controls task lifecycle.
PostgreSQL controls business state.
```

---

## 28. Coordination for PostgreSQL Migrations / Maintenance

For app-level maintenance job:

```text id="k0gqwt"
only one pod should run data backfill step
```

Options:

```text id="kpntzz"
- Kubernetes Job/CronJob with concurrencyPolicy
- PostgreSQL advisory lock
- Redis leader lease
- Kubernetes Lease
```

For DB migration, prefer:

```text id="t6ie3p"
- migration tool lock table
- PostgreSQL advisory lock
- Flyway/Liquibase lock mechanism
```

Not Redis as the only migration guard.

---

## 29. Redis vs PostgreSQL Advisory Lock

| Aspect | Redis Lease | PostgreSQL Advisory Lock |
|---|---:|---:|
| Fast | Ya | Cukup |
| TTL auto-expire | Ya | Session/transaction based |
| Close to DB transaction | Tidak | Ya |
| Good for DB-only coordination | Not ideal | Better |
| Good for app-wide non-DB coordination | Ya | Bisa, tapi DB load |
| Survives process crash | TTL/session behavior | DB releases session lock |
| Needs separate infra | Redis | PostgreSQL already there |

Rule:

```text id="zoewj9"
If coordination protects PostgreSQL-only operation,
consider PostgreSQL lock/transaction first.

If coordination protects distributed app/external work,
Redis lease/semaphore can be appropriate.
```

---

## 30. Redis vs Kubernetes Lease

| Aspect | Redis | Kubernetes Lease |
|---|---:|---:|
| Works outside K8s | Ya | Tidak |
| Best for app-level low-latency control | Ya | Sedang |
| Native leader election in K8s | Tidak | Ya |
| Requires Redis availability | Ya | Tidak |
| Good for Kubernetes controllers | Bisa | Ya |
| Strong fencing | Tidak otomatis | Tidak otomatis |

Kubernetes Lease is useful for leader election and coordination through the Kubernetes API, but even common leader election implementations still require downstream safety if duplicate leaders would be harmful. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/architecture/leases/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 31. Redis vs Camunda

| Need | Redis Coordination | Camunda |
|---|---:|---:|
| Limit worker concurrency | Ya | Bisa via worker config/model |
| Temporary lock | Ya | Tidak utama |
| Human workflow | Tidak | Ya |
| Timer boundary | Tidak utama | Ya |
| Process state | Tidak | Ya |
| Compensation | Manual | BPMN |
| Audit process lifecycle | Lemah | Kuat |

Rule:

```text id="lz1ocd"
If it is workflow semantics, use Camunda.
If it is temporary technical coordination, Redis is fine.
```

---

## 32. Redis vs Kafka

Redis coordination is for **current ownership/control**. Kafka is for **durable event ordering and replay**.

```text id="thohzw"
Redis:
  who should work now?

Kafka:
  what happened and who should consume it?
```

Do not use Redis locks to replace event ordering/replay guarantees. Use Kafka/outbox if events must be durable and replayable.

---

## 33. Redlock Positioning

Redis docs describe Redlock as a distributed lock algorithm intended to provide better guarantees than a simple single-instance lock. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/clients/patterns/distributed-locks/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical recommendation:

```text id="7qf67g"
Use simple SET NX PX lease:
  for efficiency locks and low-risk coordination

Use Redlock / library support:
  if you really need Redis-based distributed lock across independent Redis masters

Use PostgreSQL/Kubernetes/Camunda/etcd-style coordination:
  if correctness impact is high

Use fencing token:
  whenever stale owner write would be harmful
```

Most application bugs are not solved only by choosing Redlock. They are solved by:

```text id="yjps9f"
- short critical section
- TTL
- safe release
- idempotency
- fencing token
- DB state validation
- operational monitoring
```

---

## 34. Failure Policy

| Coordination operation | Failure behavior |
|---|---|
| Cache rebuild lock unavailable | Skip/retry/fallback stale |
| Leader lease unavailable | Standby, do not run scheduler |
| Semaphore unavailable | Fail closed for expensive downstream |
| Heartbeat write failed | Mark worker degraded |
| Fencing token unavailable | Do not perform critical write |
| Lock release failed | Let TTL expire, log warning |
| Redis down | Disable coordination-dependent work or fallback DB/K8s |
| Redis slow | Short timeout, no request thread pile-up |

Rule:

```text id="gfuhi4"
Coordination failure should not silently produce duplicate business effects.
```

---

## 35. Observability

Metrics:

```text id="800mg0"
coordination.lease.acquire.success
coordination.lease.acquire.failure
coordination.lease.renew.success
coordination.lease.renew.failure
coordination.lease.release.success
coordination.lease.release.failure
coordination.lease.lost.count

coordination.semaphore.acquire.success
coordination.semaphore.acquire.denied
coordination.semaphore.release.count
coordination.semaphore.active_permits

coordination.leader.is_leader
coordination.leader.change.count

coordination.fencing.token.issued
coordination.fencing.stale_write_rejected

coordination.redis.timeout
coordination.redis.error
```

Labels:

```text id="3duke7"
service
env
coordination_type
resource_family
result
exception_class
```

Avoid labels:

```text id="1z3ehh"
raw resource id
raw tenant id
raw Redis key
owner token
```

---

## 36. Logging

Good log:

```json id="9dy4ig"
{
  "event": "coordination_lease_lost",
  "coordinationType": "leader",
  "resourceFamily": "retry-scheduler",
  "ownerId": "job-svc-pod-a",
  "leaseMs": 30000,
  "action": "stop_scheduler_loop",
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

Semaphore denied:

```json id="s97xq6"
{
  "event": "semaphore_acquire_denied",
  "resourceFamily": "external-provider-x",
  "maxPermits": 10,
  "action": "retry_later"
}
```

Do not log:

```text id="kuz2zw"
- raw lock token
- full Redis key with PII
- API keys
- sensitive resource identifiers
```

---

## 37. Testing Matrix

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| acquire lease success | Basic |
| acquire lease conflict | Mutual exclusion |
| lease expires after crash | Recovery |
| release with wrong token fails | Safe release |
| renew with wrong token fails | Ownership |
| owner pause beyond TTL | Stale owner scenario |
| fencing token rejects stale write | Correctness |
| semaphore enforces max permits | Concurrency cap |
| semaphore permit expires | Leak recovery |
| leader failover | Scheduler continuity |
| two leaders briefly active | DB claim/idempotency protects |
| Redis timeout acquire | Failure policy |
| Redis down | Work disabled/fallback |
| Redis eviction scenario | Coordination safety |
| Kubernetes shutdown | Lease release/expiry |
| metric/log emitted | Operability |

---

## 38. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| `SETNX` then `EXPIRE` separate | Lock tanpa TTL jika crash | `SET NX PX` |
| `DEL lockKey` tanpa token check | Hapus lock owner lain | Lua compare-delete |
| Lease terlalu panjang | Recovery lambat | Short lease + renew |
| Lease terlalu pendek | Duplicate work karena expiry | Duration based on work + buffer |
| Lock dianggap final correctness | Duplicate side effect | DB constraint/idempotency |
| No fencing token for critical write | Stale owner can overwrite | Fencing token + DB check |
| Semaphore tanpa expiry | Permit leak | ZSet score expiry |
| Leader election tanpa idempotency | Split-brain causes duplicate work | DB claim/idempotent scheduler |
| Coordination key di cache Redis eviction | Key can disappear | Separate/noeviction |
| Blocking coordination call in request path | Latency spike | Short timeout/fallback |
| Raw resource ID in key/log | PII risk | Hash/mask |
| Redis lock for DB migration | Wrong tool | Migration lock/DB advisory lock |

---

## 39. Senior Engineer Checklist

```text id="qdmuy5"
[ ] Apa resource yang dikoordinasikan?
[ ] Apakah coordination untuk efficiency atau correctness?
[ ] Apa yang terjadi jika dua worker tetap jalan?
[ ] Apakah downstream punya DB constraint/state check?
[ ] Apakah lease punya TTL?
[ ] Apakah release mengecek owner token?
[ ] Apakah lease perlu renew?
[ ] Apakah stale owner bisa menulis? Jika ya, ada fencing token?
[ ] Apakah semaphore permit bisa leak? Jika ya, ada expiry?
[ ] Apakah Redis instance eviction-safe untuk coordination?
[ ] Apakah failure policy jelas saat Redis timeout/down?
[ ] Apakah Kubernetes Lease lebih cocok?
[ ] Apakah PostgreSQL lock lebih cocok?
[ ] Apakah Camunda seharusnya menjadi owner workflow?
[ ] Apakah metrics/logging cukup?
[ ] Apakah test pause/crash/failover ada?
```

---

## 40. Ringkasan Praktis

```text id="2z6u7q"
Redis coordination cocok untuk:
  - lease
  - lightweight lock
  - worker ownership
  - semaphore
  - leader guard
  - heartbeat
  - cache rebuild guard
  - external API concurrency cap

Redis coordination harus dilindungi oleh:
  - TTL
  - owner token
  - safe release Lua
  - renew policy
  - idempotent work
  - DB state validation
  - fencing token untuk critical writes

Gunakan alternatif saat lebih tepat:
  - PostgreSQL lock/constraint untuk DB correctness
  - Kubernetes Lease untuk K8s-native leader election
  - Camunda untuk workflow/timer
  - Kafka/outbox untuk durable event coordination
```

Kalimat kunci:

> Redis coordination adalah traffic light, bukan tembok beton. Ia mengatur siapa yang boleh maju, tetapi keselamatan akhir tetap harus dijaga oleh invariant di PostgreSQL, Camunda, atau sistem downstream yang memvalidasi ownership/fencing.
