Saya lanjut ke **Seri 15 — Distributed Lock, Redlock, Lease & Fencing Token**. Fokusnya lebih tajam dari seri sebelumnya: correctness lock, stale owner, safe unlock, renewal, dan kapan Redis lock tidak cukup.

# Seri 15 - Distributed Lock, Redlock, Lease & Fencing Token

Status: **Seri 15 dari 22 seri lanjutan**.  
Setelah seri ini selesai, tersisa **7 seri**.

---

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini memperdalam distributed lock di Redis:

```text id="map01"
- SET NX PX
- lock token
- safe unlock dengan Lua
- lease vs lock
- lease renewal
- stale owner problem
- fencing token
- Redlock
- kapan Redis lock cukup
- kapan Redis lock tidak cukup
- integrasi dengan PostgreSQL/MyBatis/Camunda
```

Redis `SET` mendukung opsi `NX` untuk set hanya jika key belum ada, dan opsi expiry seperti `EX`/`PX`. Redis documentation juga mencatat pola `SET resource-name anystring NX EX max-lock-time` sebagai cara sederhana membuat lock, walaupun dokumentasi Redis menyarankan Redlock untuk skenario distributed lock yang membutuhkan fault tolerance lebih baik. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/set/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

Distributed lock biasanya dipakai untuk menjawab:

```text id="mental01"
"Siapa yang boleh mencoba melakukan pekerjaan ini sekarang?"
```

Tapi lock **tidak otomatis menjawab**:

```text id="mental02"
"Apakah efek bisnisnya pasti tidak akan double?"
"Apakah stale worker tidak bisa menulis data lama?"
"Apakah external provider tidak akan menerima duplicate call?"
"Apakah workflow state masih valid?"
```

Untuk stack Anda, boundary yang sehat:

```text id="mental03"
Redis lock / lease:
  temporary coordination

PostgreSQL:
  final invariant, unique constraint, optimistic locking

Camunda:
  workflow/process/task lifecycle

Kafka/outbox:
  durable event publication

External provider:
  provider-side idempotency key jika tersedia
```

Kalimat kunci:

```text id="mental04"
Redis lock boleh mengurangi concurrency.
Redis lock tidak boleh menjadi satu-satunya penjaga correctness bisnis final.
```

---

## 3. Diagram Besar

```text id="diagram01"
Worker A / Pod A
Worker B / Pod B
Worker C / Pod C
      ↓
Redis Lock / Lease
      ↓
Only one worker should proceed
      ↓
Business action
      ↓
PostgreSQL / Camunda / External system validates:
  - state masih valid
  - version masih benar
  - fencing token lebih baru
  - idempotency key belum diproses
  - unique constraint tidak dilanggar
```

---

## 4. Lock vs Lease

Dalam distributed system, istilah “lock” sering sebenarnya berarti **lease**.

| Istilah | Makna |
|---|---|
| Lock | Hak eksklusif untuk mengakses resource |
| Lease | Lock yang otomatis expire setelah waktu tertentu |
| Lock token | Nilai unik yang membuktikan ownership |
| Lease time | Durasi lock dianggap valid |
| Renewal | Memperpanjang lease sebelum expire |
| Fencing token | Angka monotonik untuk menolak stale owner |

Redis lock harus selalu punya TTL. Tanpa TTL, worker crash bisa meninggalkan lock selamanya.

```text id="lease01"
Bad:
  SETNX lockKey token
  // no TTL

Better:
  SET lockKey token NX PX 30000
```

---

## 5. Basic Redis Lock dengan `SET NX PX`

### Acquire

```text id="acquire01"
SET lockKey token NX PX 30000
```

Makna:

```text id="acquire02"
NX:
  set only if key does not exist

PX 30000:
  expire after 30,000 ms

token:
  random unique value owned by caller
```

Jika Redis return `OK`, caller mendapatkan lock.

Jika Redis return nil, lock sedang dimiliki caller lain.

---

## 6. Key Design

Pattern:

```text id="key01"
{service}:{env}:tenant:{tenant}:lock:{purpose}:v1:{entity}:{id}
```

Contoh:

```text id="key02"
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:lock:case-submit:v1:case:c991
job-svc:prod:tenant:t1:lock:report-generate:v1:job:j123
cache-svc:prod:tenant:t1:lock:cache-rebuild:v1:case:c991
workflow-svc:prod:tenant:t1:lock:external-task:v1:task:t123
```

Value minimal:

```text id="value01"
uuid-random-token
```

Value lebih operasional:

```json id="value02"
{
  "ownerId": "case-svc-pod-a",
  "token": "uuid",
  "purpose": "case-submit",
  "acquiredAt": "2026-07-13T16:30:00+07:00",
  "leaseMs": 30000
}
```

Untuk safe unlock, value paling penting adalah **token unik**.

---

## 7. Safe Unlock

Jangan release lock dengan `DEL lockKey` langsung.

Problem:

```text id="unlock01"
Worker A acquires lock tokenA, TTL 30s
Worker A pause 40s
Lock expires
Worker B acquires lock tokenB
Worker A resumes
Worker A executes DEL lockKey
Worker A accidentally deletes Worker B's lock
```

Safe unlock harus compare token:

```lua id="unlock02"
if redis.call("GET", KEYS[1]) == ARGV[1] then
  return redis.call("DEL", KEYS[1])
else
  return 0
end
```

Rule:

```text id="unlock03"
Only the owner that holds the same token may release the lock.
```

Redis distributed lock documentation juga menggunakan pola token random dan Lua script untuk melepas lock hanya jika value masih sama dengan token pemilik lock. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/clients/patterns/distributed-locks/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8. Java Interface

Jangan expose Redis command langsung ke business code.

```java id="java01"
public interface DistributedLockService {

    Optional<LockToken> tryAcquire(
        LockResource resource,
        Duration leaseTime
    );

    boolean release(
        LockResource resource,
        LockToken token
    );

    boolean renew(
        LockResource resource,
        LockToken token,
        Duration leaseTime
    );
}
```

```java id="java02"
public record LockToken(
    String ownerId,
    String token,
    Instant acquiredAt,
    Duration leaseTime,
    Optional<Long> fencingToken
) {}
```

```java id="java03"
public record LockResource(
    String key,
    String purpose
) {}
```

---

## 9. Acquire Implementation Shape

```java id="java04"
public Optional<LockToken> tryAcquire(
    LockResource resource,
    Duration leaseTime
) {
    String token = UUID.randomUUID().toString();

    boolean acquired = redis.set(
        resource.key(),
        token,
        SetArgs.Builder.nx().px(leaseTime.toMillis())
    );

    if (!acquired) {
        return Optional.empty();
    }

    return Optional.of(new LockToken(
        ownerId,
        token,
        Instant.now(clock),
        leaseTime,
        Optional.empty()
    ));
}
```

Catatan:

```text id="java05"
Actual API berbeda antara Jedis, Lettuce, dan Redisson.
Tapi abstraction-nya sama:
  SET key token NX PX leaseMs
```

---

## 10. Release Implementation Shape

```java id="java06"
public boolean release(
    LockResource resource,
    LockToken token
) {
    Long result = redis.eval(
        SAFE_UNLOCK_LUA,
        List.of(resource.key()),
        List.of(token.token())
    );

    return result != null && result == 1L;
}
```

Safe unlock failure **tidak selalu fatal**:

```text id="release01"
release returns 0
  berarti:
    - lock sudah expire
    - lock sudah dimiliki owner lain
    - key hilang
```

Response yang tepat:

```text id="release02"
- log debug/warn sesuai konteks
- jangan DEL paksa
- jangan menganggap masih owner
```

---

## 11. Lease Time Selection

Lease time harus lebih panjang dari expected critical section, tapi tidak terlalu panjang.

```text id="lease02"
leaseTime > p99 critical section duration + network/GC buffer
```

Contoh:

| Use case | Typical lease |
|---|---:|
| Cache rebuild ringan | 5-30 sec |
| Report generation guard | 1-10 min, dengan renewal |
| External API call | timeout eksternal + buffer |
| Scheduler leader guard | 15-60 sec, renewable |
| Case submit lock | pendek, idealnya < 10 sec |
| DB migration guard | Jangan Redis; gunakan DB/migration lock |

Risiko lease terlalu pendek:

```text id="lease03"
Worker masih bekerja
Lease expire
Worker lain acquire
Duplicate work
```

Risiko lease terlalu panjang:

```text id="lease04"
Worker crash
Resource terkunci terlalu lama
Recovery lambat
```

---

## 12. Lock untuk Efficiency vs Correctness

Martin Kleppmann membedakan dua alasan memakai lock: **efficiency** dan **correctness**. Untuk efficiency, lock menghindari kerja dobel; kalau lock gagal, biaya ekstra tetapi data tidak rusak. Untuk correctness, lock failure bisa menyebabkan data corrupt atau efek bisnis salah. ([Martin Kleppmann](https://martin.kleppmann.com/2016/02/08/how-to-do-distributed-locking.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Efficiency Lock

Contoh:

```text id="efficiency01"
- cache rebuild
- duplicate report precomputation
- refresh reference data
- background cleanup
```

Jika dua worker jalan bersamaan:

```text id="efficiency02"
cost naik, tapi correctness tetap aman
```

Redis lock cocok.

### Correctness Lock

Contoh:

```text id="correctness01"
- payment capture
- approve regulatory decision
- finalize order
- generate official document number
- external irreversible side effect
```

Jika dua worker jalan bersamaan:

```text id="correctness02"
data bisa salah, uang bisa double, decision bisa corrupt
```

Redis lock saja tidak cukup. Butuh DB constraint, idempotency, fencing token, transaction, atau workflow state validation.

---

## 13. Stale Owner Problem

Ini problem utama distributed lock.

```text id="stale01"
Worker A acquires lock
Worker A pauses due to GC / network / CPU freeze
Lock expires
Worker B acquires lock
Worker B performs valid write
Worker A resumes
Worker A still thinks it owns lock
Worker A performs stale write
```

Lease expiration tidak otomatis menghentikan proses Worker A.

Lock hanya hidup di Redis. Worker A tidak “dibunuh” ketika lease expire.

Maka, sebelum menulis ke state penting, worker harus melakukan salah satu:

```text id="stale02"
- cek token/lease masih valid
- cek DB state/version
- cek fencing token
- gunakan idempotency key
- gunakan optimistic locking
```

Untuk write penting, **fencing token** adalah guard yang lebih kuat.

---

## 14. Fencing Token

Fencing token adalah angka monotonik yang diberikan saat lock/lease diperoleh.

```text id="fence01"
Worker A gets token 41
Worker A pauses
Worker B gets token 42
Worker B writes with token 42
Worker A resumes and tries write with token 41
Storage rejects token 41
```

Martin Kleppmann menekankan fencing token sebagai cara membuat stale lock holder tidak bisa menulis ke shared resource setelah lease-nya expired. ([Martin Kleppmann](https://martin.kleppmann.com/2016/02/08/how-to-do-distributed-locking.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Redis counter

```text id="fence02"
INCR case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:fence:case-submit:v1:case:c991
```

### Lock acquire with token

Idealnya acquire lock dan issue fencing token dibuat atomic dalam Lua:

```lua id="fence03"
local lockKey = KEYS[1]
local fenceKey = KEYS[2]

local ownerToken = ARGV[1]
local leaseMs = tonumber(ARGV[2])

local acquired = redis.call("SET", lockKey, ownerToken, "NX", "PX", leaseMs)

if not acquired then
  return {0, 0}
end

local fencingToken = redis.call("INCR", fenceKey)

return {1, fencingToken}
```

Return:

```text id="fence04"
{1, 42} = acquired with fencing token 42
{0, 0}  = not acquired
```

---

## 15. Fencing Token di PostgreSQL

Downstream harus menolak token lama.

```sql id="fence05"
UPDATE case_processing_guard
SET fencing_token = #{newToken},
    updated_at = now()
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND case_id = #{caseId}
  AND #{newToken} > fencing_token;
```

Atau langsung pada business table:

```sql id="fence06"
UPDATE cases
SET status = #{newStatus},
    fencing_token = #{fencingToken},
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND case_id = #{caseId}
  AND #{fencingToken} > fencing_token
  AND status = #{expectedStatus};
```

Jika update count = 0:

```text id="fence07"
- token stale
- state sudah berubah
- worker tidak boleh lanjut
```

Rule paling penting:

```text id="fence08"
Fencing token tidak berguna jika downstream tidak memvalidasinya.
```

---

## 16. Fencing Token vs Lock Token

| Token | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| Lock token | Membuktikan pemilik lock untuk release/renew |
| Fencing token | Mencegah stale owner menulis ke downstream |
| Idempotency key | Mencegah duplicate business effect |
| DB version | Mencegah lost update |
| External request id | Mencegah duplicate provider side effect |

Jangan tertukar:

```text id="token01"
UUID lock token:
  random, untuk ownership Redis key

Fencing token:
  monotonically increasing, untuk downstream ordering
```

---

## 17. Lease Renewal

Untuk pekerjaan panjang, lease perlu diperpanjang.

Renew harus mengecek token:

```lua id="renew01"
if redis.call("GET", KEYS[1]) == ARGV[1] then
  return redis.call("PEXPIRE", KEYS[1], ARGV[2])
else
  return 0
end
```

Flow:

```text id="renew02"
Acquire lease 30s
  ↓
Every 10s renew if still owner
  ↓
If renew fails:
    stop work or switch to safe mode
```

### Guardrail

```text id="renew03"
- renew interval < leaseTime / 2
- renewal failure means ownership uncertain
- do not continue critical write after renewal failure
- max total duration optional
- stop renewal before shutdown
```

### Renewal failure handling

```text id="renew04"
If renew failed:
  - for cache rebuild: stop or finish without writing if safe
  - for DB write: verify fencing token/state before write
  - for external call: avoid starting new side effect
```

---

## 18. Lock + DB Transaction Pattern

Untuk command penting:

```text id="pattern01"
1. acquire Redis lock/lease
2. begin PostgreSQL transaction
3. load row FOR UPDATE or check version/state
4. perform domain transition
5. commit
6. release Redis lock
```

Example:

```java id="pattern02"
public void submitCase(TenantId tenantId, CaseId caseId, UserContext user) {
    LockResource resource = lockKeys.caseSubmit(tenantId, caseId);

    Optional<LockToken> lock = lockService.tryAcquire(
        resource,
        Duration.ofSeconds(10)
    );

    if (lock.isEmpty()) {
        throw new ConflictException("Case is being submitted");
    }

    try {
        transaction.run(() -> {
            CaseRow row = caseRepository.findForUpdate(tenantId, caseId)
                .orElseThrow(NotFoundException::new);

            domainPolicy.assertCanSubmit(row, user);

            caseRepository.updateStatus(
                tenantId,
                caseId,
                CaseStatus.SUBMITTED,
                row.version()
            );

            outboxRepository.insert(CaseSubmittedEvent.from(row));
        });
    } finally {
        lockService.release(resource, lock.get());
    }
}
```

Redis lock mengurangi concurrent submit. PostgreSQL transaction tetap menjaga correctness.

---

## 19. Lock + Optimistic Locking

Untuk write path yang tidak ingin menahan DB row lock lama:

```sql id="optimistic01"
UPDATE cases
SET status = #{newStatus},
    version = version + 1
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND case_id = #{caseId}
  AND version = #{expectedVersion}
  AND status = #{expectedStatus};
```

Jika update count = 0:

```text id="optimistic02"
state changed, reject/retry
```

Pattern:

```text id="optimistic03"
Redis lock:
  reduce duplicate concurrent work

PostgreSQL optimistic lock:
  enforce final state transition correctness
```

---

## 20. Lock + Idempotency

Distributed lock dan idempotency menyelesaikan problem berbeda.

```text id="idem01"
Lock:
  "only one worker should process this resource now"

Idempotency:
  "same request retried should not create duplicate effect"
```

Untuk command `POST /orders`:

```text id="idem02"
Idempotency key:
  required

Redis lock:
  optional, if protecting same aggregate/action

PostgreSQL unique constraint:
  required for final correctness
```

Jangan pakai lock sebagai pengganti idempotency.

---

## 21. Lock + External API Call

Problem:

```text id="external01"
Worker acquires lock
Calls payment provider
Network timeout
Worker retries
Another worker also runs
Provider receives duplicate calls
```

Guardrail:

```text id="external02"
- stable provider idempotency key
- DB outbound request record
- Redis lock only controls local concurrency
- reconcile provider result if unknown
```

Pattern:

```text id="external03"
PostgreSQL:
  insert outbound_request(provider_request_id, status=PENDING)

Redis lock:
  only one worker attempts send now

External provider:
  receives provider_request_id as idempotency key

After response:
  update outbound_request status
```

---

## 22. Redlock Overview

Redlock adalah algoritma distributed lock Redis yang mencoba memperoleh lock pada beberapa Redis master independen dan menganggap lock berhasil jika majority berhasil dalam waktu tertentu. Redis docs mempresentasikan Redlock sebagai algoritma distributed lock manager yang lebih aman daripada single-instance vanilla approach dan sebagai starting point untuk desain yang lebih kompleks. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/clients/patterns/distributed-locks/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Simplified idea:

```text id="redlock01"
Have N independent Redis masters, commonly 5
Client tries SET NX PX on all
If lock acquired on majority and time elapsed < validity:
  lock acquired
Else:
  release acquired partial locks
```

Tujuan Redlock:

```text id="redlock02"
- tolerate failure of some Redis nodes
- reduce single Redis instance SPOF
- improve lock availability/safety versus one Redis master
```

---

## 23. Redlock Debate

Ada perdebatan terkenal tentang Redlock.

Kleppmann mengkritik Redlock untuk use case correctness-critical dan menekankan bahwa lock berbasis waktu tetap rentan terhadap pause/network delay; ia merekomendasikan fencing token untuk mencegah stale client menulis ke shared resource. ([Martin Kleppmann](https://martin.kleppmann.com/2016/02/08/how-to-do-distributed-locking.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Antirez, pembuat Redis, menanggapi kritik tersebut dan berargumen bahwa dalam banyak kasus, jika sistem downstream sudah bisa menolak race via token/fencing, maka lock kuat mungkin tidak lagi menjadi satu-satunya mekanisme correctness; ia juga membingkai Redlock sebagai improvement dibanding lock Redis sederhana. ([antirez.com](https://antirez.com/news/101?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical takeaway untuk senior engineer:

```text id="redlock03"
- Redlock bukan silver bullet.
- Redlock lebih kuat dari single Redis lock dalam skenario tertentu.
- Untuk correctness-critical writes, tetap gunakan fencing token / DB invariant.
- Untuk efficiency locks, single Redis SET NX PX sering cukup.
```

---

## 24. Kapan Pakai Simple Redis Lock?

Gunakan simple `SET NX PX` lock untuk:

```text id="simple01"
- cache rebuild
- duplicate expensive calculation prevention
- lightweight scheduler guard
- worker dedup low-risk
- avoid thundering herd
- non-critical background task
```

Syarat:

```text id="simple02"
- duplicate work tidak merusak data
- lock punya TTL
- unlock safe dengan token
- operation idempotent
- Redis failure punya fallback
```

---

## 25. Kapan Pakai Redlock?

Pertimbangkan Redlock jika:

```text id="redlock04"
- Anda punya beberapa Redis master independen
- lock availability/fault tolerance lebih penting
- single Redis instance SPOF tidak diterima
- workload masih bisa tolerate timing-based lease semantics
- library/client implementation matang tersedia
```

Tetap jangan lupa:

```text id="redlock05"
Redlock does not remove need for idempotency, fencing, and downstream validation.
```

---

## 26. Kapan Jangan Pakai Redis Lock sebagai Mekanisme Utama?

Hindari Redis lock sebagai satu-satunya guard untuk:

```text id="avoid01"
- payment capture
- final settlement
- regulatory/legal decision
- official numbering
- inventory final decrement jika oversell fatal
- multi-row relational invariant
- workflow transition yang harus auditable
- DB migration lock
```

Gunakan:

```text id="avoid02"
- PostgreSQL transaction / unique constraint / row lock
- PostgreSQL advisory lock
- idempotency table
- Camunda process state/timer
- external provider idempotency
- Kafka/outbox for event durability
- fencing token if Redis lease remains involved
```

---

## 27. PostgreSQL Row Lock vs Redis Lock

| Aspek | Redis Lock | PostgreSQL Row Lock |
|---|---:|---:|
| Scope | Cross-process app coordination | DB row/transaction |
| TTL | Ya | Transaction/session lifecycle |
| Cocok untuk external work | Ya, hati-hati | Tidak ideal untuk long external call |
| Cocok untuk DB invariant | Bukan utama | Ya |
| Failure recovery | TTL expiry | Transaction rollback/session close |
| Risk stale owner | Ya | Dalam transaction lebih terkendali |
| Observability | Custom | DB-level |

Rule:

```text id="pg01"
Jika resource utama adalah row PostgreSQL,
pakai PostgreSQL locking/transaction sebagai guard final.
Redis boleh menjadi pre-guard untuk mengurangi contention.
```

---

## 28. PostgreSQL Advisory Lock vs Redis Lock

PostgreSQL advisory lock cocok untuk coordination yang sangat dekat dengan DB.

```text id="pg02"
Use PostgreSQL advisory lock:
  - migration/backfill DB-only
  - prevent duplicate DB maintenance
  - protect DB operation

Use Redis lock:
  - app-level distributed coordination
  - external API concurrency
  - cache rebuild
  - worker ownership outside DB transaction
```

Untuk database migration, Flyway/Liquibase biasanya sudah punya lock mechanism sendiri. Jangan mengganti migration lock dengan Redis lock kecuali ada alasan sangat kuat.

---

## 29. Kubernetes Lease vs Redis Lock

Kubernetes Lease cocok untuk leader election di dalam cluster. Kubernetes docs menjelaskan Lease API dipakai untuk node heartbeats dan juga bisa dipakai workload untuk leader election. 

Namun common Kubernetes leader election implementation juga tidak memberi fencing otomatis. `client-go` leader election documentation menyatakan implementasi leader election tidak menjamin hanya satu client bertindak sebagai leader; ia tidak melakukan fencing. 

Karena citation ID untuk Kubernetes belum ada di pencarian sesi ini, ringkasnya:

```text id="k8s01"
Kubernetes Lease:
  good for K8s-native leader election

Redis Lock:
  good for app-level cross-runtime coordination

Both:
  still need idempotent work and downstream guard
```

Catatan: untuk detail Kubernetes Lease, lihat kembali prinsip dari Seri 14. Di seri ini fokus utama tetap Redis lock/fencing.

---

## 30. Lock Timeout dan Request Path

Jangan biarkan request HTTP menunggu lock terlalu lama.

Bad:

```text id="timeout01"
User submits request
  ↓
wait 30 seconds for Redis lock
  ↓
request thread exhausted
```

Better:

```text id="timeout02"
try lock with short wait
  ├── success -> proceed
  └── fail -> 409 Conflict / 423 Locked / 202 Accepted
```

Response options:

| Situation | HTTP |
|---|---:|
| Same command already processing | `409 Conflict` or `202 Accepted` |
| Resource temporarily locked | `423 Locked` if API style allows |
| Retry later due to contention | `409` / `429` / `503` depending cause |
| Async job accepted | `202 Accepted` |

For REST API:

```text id="timeout03"
Lock wait budget usually 0-200 ms.
```

---

## 31. Lock Granularity

Lock terlalu luas:

```text id="gran01"
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:lock:case-all
```

Dampak:

```text id="gran02"
Semua case tenant t1 serial, throughput buruk.
```

Lock terlalu sempit:

```text id="gran03"
lock per tiny subfield
```

Dampak:

```text id="gran04"
Sulit reason, rawan deadlock/logical race.
```

Recommended:

```text id="gran05"
Lock by aggregate/action:
  case:c991:submit
  order:o123:finalize
  report:j123:generate
  cache:case:c991:rebuild
```

---

## 32. Multiple Locks and Deadlock

Jika perlu acquire lebih dari satu lock:

```text id="multi01"
lock case A
lock case B
```

Deadlock risk:

```text id="multi02"
Worker 1 locks A then waits B
Worker 2 locks B then waits A
```

Mitigation:

```text id="multi03"
- avoid multi-lock when possible
- acquire locks in deterministic order
- short timeout
- release all on failure
- prefer DB transaction/constraint if invariant spans multiple entities
```

For multi-row business invariant, PostgreSQL transaction sering lebih tepat.

---

## 33. Lock Renewal Risk

Renewal membuat lock bisa bertahan lama, tapi juga bisa menyembunyikan stuck process.

Guardrail:

```text id="renew05"
- track total lock age
- max renewal count or max duration
- metrics for long-held locks
- heartbeat separate from lock
- watchdog only if critical section safe
```

Alert:

```text id="renew06"
lock held > expected p99 * 2
renew failures > threshold
lock contention high
```

---

## 34. Redisson Watchdog Note

Redisson menyediakan high-level lock abstraction dan watchdog behavior untuk memperpanjang lock selama client masih hidup. Ini bisa memudahkan, tetapi tidak menghapus problem stale owner/business correctness.

Guideline:

```text id="redisson01"
Redisson lock:
  useful for developer ergonomics

Still required:
  - understand lease time
  - know watchdog behavior
  - avoid long blocking critical sections
  - use DB constraint/fencing for critical writes
```

Jangan treat Redisson `RLock` seperti Java `synchronized` lokal. Ini tetap distributed system.

---

## 35. Lock Observability

Metrics:

```text id="metrics01"
redis_lock.acquire.success.count
redis_lock.acquire.failure.count
redis_lock.acquire.latency
redis_lock.release.success.count
redis_lock.release.failure.count
redis_lock.renew.success.count
redis_lock.renew.failure.count
redis_lock.contention.count
redis_lock.held.duration
redis_lock.expired_while_working.count
redis_lock.fencing_token.issued.count
redis_lock.stale_write_rejected.count
```

Labels aman:

```text id="metrics02"
service
env
lock_family
purpose
result
exception_class
```

Jangan label:

```text id="metrics03"
raw lock key
caseId
userId
tenantId raw
token
```

---

## 36. Lock Logging

Good:

```json id="log01"
{
  "event": "redis_lock_acquire_failed",
  "lockFamily": "case-submit",
  "resourceHash": "sha256-...",
  "leaseMs": 10000,
  "result": "already_locked",
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

Lease lost:

```json id="log02"
{
  "event": "redis_lock_renew_failed",
  "lockFamily": "report-generate",
  "ownerId": "report-svc-pod-a",
  "action": "stop_processing_or_reconcile",
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

Bad:

```json id="log03"
{
  "lockKey": "case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:lock:case-submit:v1:case:c991",
  "token": "raw-uuid-token"
}
```

Token jangan dilog raw kecuali benar-benar aman dan dibatasi.

---

## 37. Failure Mode Table

| Failure | Risiko | Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|
| Worker crash | Lock tertahan | TTL |
| Worker pause > TTL | Stale owner | Fencing token + DB check |
| Release without token | Delete owner lain | Lua compare-delete |
| Redis timeout on acquire | Unknown acquisition | Retry carefully, idempotent path |
| Redis timeout on release | Lock may remain until TTL | Log, let TTL expire |
| Redis failover | Lock may be lost/delayed | Idempotency + DB correctness |
| Lease too short | Duplicate work | Proper lease duration / renew |
| Lease too long | Slow recovery | Short lease + renew |
| Multi-lock | Deadlock | Deterministic order / avoid |
| Lock key evicted | False unlock | noeviction/dedicated Redis |
| Clock issue | Bad lease assumptions | Use Redis TTL, buffers, fencing |
| Long critical section | Lease unreliable | Break work, renew, DB claim |

---

## 38. Testing Matrix

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Acquire lock success | Basic |
| Acquire same lock conflict | Mutual exclusion |
| Lock expires after TTL | Crash recovery |
| Safe release with correct token | Ownership |
| Release with wrong token fails | Safety |
| Renew with correct token | Long job |
| Renew after expiry fails | Stale owner detection |
| Worker pause beyond TTL | Stale owner test |
| Fencing token increases monotonically | Token correctness |
| Stale fencing token rejected by DB | Critical safety |
| Two concurrent submit requests | DB invariant protected |
| Redis timeout during acquire | Failure policy |
| Redis timeout during release | TTL fallback |
| Redis failover simulation | Resilience |
| Multi-lock ordering | Deadlock prevention |
| Lock metrics emitted | Operability |

---

## 39. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| `SETNX` lalu `EXPIRE` terpisah | Lock bisa tanpa TTL | `SET NX PX` |
| `DEL` tanpa token check | Hapus lock owner lain | Lua safe unlock |
| Lock tanpa TTL | Deadlock permanen | Lease always |
| Lease terlalu pendek | Duplicate worker | Duration + renewal |
| Lease terlalu panjang | Recovery lambat | Short lease + renew |
| Lock sebagai satu-satunya guard payment | Duplicate payment risk | Idempotency + DB/provider guard |
| Tidak ada fencing token untuk stale write | Stale owner overwrite | Fencing + DB check |
| Lock key terlalu global | Throughput buruk | Per aggregate/action |
| Multi-lock tanpa order | Deadlock | Deterministic order |
| Lock di Redis cache eviction instance | Lock hilang | Dedicated/noeviction |
| Long external call inside lock tanpa idempotency | Duplicate side effect | External idempotency key |
| No metrics | Lock contention invisible | Metrics/logs |

---

## 40. Practical Design Recipes

### A. Cache Rebuild Lock

```text id="recipe01"
Use:
  SET NX PX 10s

No fencing needed usually.

If lock unavailable:
  wait briefly / return stale / fallback DB

Correctness:
  DB remains source of truth
```

---

### B. Report Generation Lock

```text id="recipe02"
Use:
  lock by jobId
  lease 1-5 min
  renew if long-running
  DB job status claim

Correctness:
  async_jobs row prevents duplicate final result
```

---

### C. Submit Case Command

```text id="recipe03"
Use:
  idempotency key required
  optional Redis lock by caseId/action
  PostgreSQL optimistic/row lock
  outbox event
  Camunda state validation if workflow involved

Correctness:
  DB/Camunda, not Redis lock alone
```

---

### D. External Provider Call

```text id="recipe04"
Use:
  Redis semaphore/lock for concurrency
  PostgreSQL outbound_request record
  provider idempotency key
  retry/reconcile

Correctness:
  provider idempotency + DB status
```

---

### E. Leader Scheduler

```text id="recipe05"
Use:
  Redis leader lease or Kubernetes Lease
  scheduler loop only if leader
  DB claim for each job before enqueue/process

Correctness:
  DB claim, not leader lease alone
```

---

## 41. Senior Engineer Checklist

```text id="check01"
[ ] Apakah lock untuk efficiency atau correctness?
[ ] Apa yang terjadi jika dua worker tetap jalan?
[ ] Apakah key granularity tepat?
[ ] Apakah acquire memakai SET NX PX?
[ ] Apakah value lock adalah random token?
[ ] Apakah release memakai compare-token Lua?
[ ] Apakah TTL/lease time justified?
[ ] Apakah renewal diperlukan?
[ ] Apa yang terjadi jika renewal gagal?
[ ] Apakah stale owner bisa menulis?
[ ] Jika ya, apakah ada fencing token?
[ ] Apakah downstream memvalidasi fencing token?
[ ] Apakah PostgreSQL unique/optimistic/row lock tetap ada?
[ ] Apakah idempotency key tetap ada untuk command retry?
[ ] Apakah external side effect punya provider idempotency key?
[ ] Apakah Redis instance eviction-safe?
[ ] Apakah timeout lock di request path pendek?
[ ] Apakah metrics/logging contention ada?
[ ] Apakah failure/pause/concurrency tests ada?
```

---

## 42. Ringkasan Praktis

```text id="summary01"
SET NX PX:
  primitive dasar Redis lock

Lock token:
  bukti ownership untuk release/renew

Safe unlock:
  Lua compare GET == token then DEL

Lease:
  lock dengan expiry

Renewal:
  memperpanjang lease jika masih owner

Stale owner:
  worker lama bisa resume setelah lease expire

Fencing token:
  angka monotonik untuk menolak stale writes

Redlock:
  algoritma Redis distributed lock multi-master,
  lebih kuat dari single Redis lock untuk skenario tertentu,
  tapi bukan pengganti fencing/DB correctness

PostgreSQL/Camunda:
  final correctness boundary

Redis lock:
  coordination helper, bukan sumber kebenaran bisnis
```

Kalimat kunci:

> Distributed lock yang aman bukan cuma “berhasil acquire lock”. Desain yang aman harus menjawab apa yang terjadi saat owner pause, lease expire, Redis failover, unlock terlambat, request retry, dan stale worker mencoba menulis setelah lock-nya hilang.

7/13/2026, 7:42:54 AM

# Seri 16 - Distributed Concurrency Control

Status: **Seri 16 dari 22 seri lanjutan**.  
Setelah seri ini selesai, tersisa **6 seri**.

---

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas cara mengontrol concurrency di aplikasi:

```text id="o6g453"
Java 17+
Jersey/JAX-RS
MyBatis
PostgreSQL
Redis
Camunda
Kafka
Docker/Kubernetes
```

Fokusnya:

```text id="vfxo8r"
- optimistic concurrency
- pessimistic concurrency
- Redis CAS dengan Lua
- Redis WATCH / MULTI / EXEC
- PostgreSQL version column
- SELECT FOR UPDATE
- unique constraint
- per-aggregate serialization
- idempotency
- Redis lock vs DB lock
- cara memilih primitive yang tepat
```

Redis mendukung optimistic locking via `WATCH`, yang membuat `EXEC` gagal jika key yang diawasi berubah sebelum transaksi dieksekusi. Redis juga menjamin Lua script dieksekusi secara atomic di server: selama script berjalan, aktivitas server lain diblokir sampai script selesai. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/using-commands/transactions/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

Distributed concurrency control menjawab:

```text id="v6b07l"
Apa yang terjadi jika dua request/worker/pod
mencoba membaca, memutuskan, dan menulis state yang sama
pada waktu bersamaan?
```

Contoh problem:

```text id="h2dvi8"
- dua user submit case yang sama
- dua worker memproses job yang sama
- dua request update status order bersamaan
- cache rebuild menulis value lama
- quota counter di-update bersamaan
- external API dipanggil dua kali
- workflow task completed dua kali
```

Rule utama:

```text id="aaw2ix"
Concurrency control untuk data bisnis final harus berada di PostgreSQL/Camunda/domain state.
Redis boleh membantu sebagai fast coordination/control plane.
```

---

## 3. Jenis Race Condition yang Sering Muncul

| Race | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| Lost update | Dua request update field sama, update pertama tertimpa | Data hilang |
| Double submit | User klik submit dua kali | State transition ganda |
| Duplicate worker | Dua worker proses job sama | Side effect dobel |
| Stale write | Worker lama resume lalu menulis data lama | Data rollback/logical corruption |
| Write skew | Dua transaksi melihat kondisi valid, lalu menulis state yang bersama-sama invalid | Invariant bocor |
| ABA problem | State A → B → A, caller mengira tidak berubah | Validasi palsu |
| Cache stale overwrite | Cache lama ditulis setelah invalidation | User lihat data lama |
| External side effect duplicate | Retry call provider dengan request ID berbeda | Pembayaran/email/tiket dobel |

---

## 4. Tool Map

| Problem | Redis Tool | PostgreSQL Tool | Catatan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Atomic counter | `INCR`, Lua | `UPDATE counter = counter + 1` | Redis cocok untuk fast ephemeral counter |
| CAS Redis state | Lua / `WATCH` | N/A | Untuk Redis-owned temporary state |
| Update business row | Optional lock | Version column / row lock | DB harus final |
| Prevent duplicate create | Idempotency key | Unique constraint | Redis fast gate, DB final guard |
| Worker claim | Stream PEL / lock | `UPDATE ... WHERE status... RETURNING` | DB claim lebih defensible |
| Limit concurrent external call | Semaphore Lua | DB quota if auditable | Redis bagus untuk concurrency cap |
| Prevent stale owner write | Fencing token | `WHERE token > fencing_token` | Token harus divalidasi downstream |
| Workflow task state | Optional lock | Camunda/process state | Redis bukan workflow engine |
| Cross-service event ordering | Tidak ideal | Outbox/Kafka | Kafka partition/domain version |

---

## 5. Redis vs PostgreSQL Responsibility

```text id="umayou"
Redis:
  - fast shared state
  - temporary coordination
  - ephemeral counters
  - request-path guard
  - cache/control plane
  - queue coordination

PostgreSQL:
  - source of truth
  - transaction
  - relational invariant
  - unique constraint
  - row/version locking
  - audit/recovery

Camunda:
  - workflow/process lifecycle
  - timers
  - external task lifecycle
  - human task state
```

Redis transaction dan Lua berguna, tetapi Redis transaction bukan transaksi relasional seperti PostgreSQL. Redis transaction mengeksekusi command secara serialized, namun Redis tidak melakukan rollback seperti database relasional jika command tertentu gagal setelah `EXEC`; karena itu, untuk invariant bisnis multi-row/multi-entity, PostgreSQL tetap boundary utama. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/using-commands/transactions/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# Bagian A - Redis Concurrency Control

## 6. Redis Atomic Single Commands

Banyak command Redis sudah atomic per command.

Contoh:

```text id="6w9d3n"
INCR key
HINCRBY key field 1
SADD key member
SET key value NX PX 30000
ZADD key score member
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="7747ju"
- counter
- rate limit
- simple dedup
- lock acquire
- unique membership
- lightweight quota
```

Tapi begitu logic butuh beberapa langkah:

```text id="bdfzms"
GET current
if current == expected
  SET new value
```

Maka perlu CAS via Lua atau `WATCH/MULTI/EXEC`.

---

## 7. Redis CAS dengan Lua

CAS = compare-and-set.

Use case:

```text id="2qfj4x"
Update Redis state hanya jika status/version masih sesuai ekspektasi.
```

Contoh key:

```text id="2bor7p"
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:tmp:submission-state:v1:case:c991
```

Value Hash:

```text id="ux7q2r"
status = PROCESSING
version = 7
owner = pod-a
```

Lua CAS:

```lua id="a6z1ik"
local key = KEYS[1]

local expectedStatus = ARGV[1]
local expectedVersion = ARGV[2]
local newStatus = ARGV[3]
local newVersion = ARGV[4]
local ttlSeconds = tonumber(ARGV[5])

local currentStatus = redis.call("HGET", key, "status")
local currentVersion = redis.call("HGET", key, "version")

if currentStatus ~= expectedStatus then
  return {"STATUS_MISMATCH", currentStatus or "", currentVersion or ""}
end

if currentVersion ~= expectedVersion then
  return {"VERSION_MISMATCH", currentStatus or "", currentVersion or ""}
end

redis.call("HSET", key,
  "status", newStatus,
  "version", newVersion,
  "updatedAt", ARGV[6]
)

redis.call("EXPIRE", key, ttlSeconds)

return {"UPDATED", newStatus, newVersion}
```

Karena Redis menjamin Lua script berjalan atomic, tidak ada command lain yang menyela di tengah script. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/programmability/eval-intro/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8. Kapan Lua CAS Cocok?

Gunakan Lua CAS untuk Redis-owned state seperti:

```text id="0x736f"
- idempotency state
- rate limiter state
- token bucket
- semaphore
- temporary ownership
- job retry marker
- presence state
- cache rebuild metadata
```

Jangan gunakan Lua CAS sebagai pengganti DB transaction untuk:

```text id="8xfr2w"
- order final status
- payment state
- regulatory decision
- entitlement final
- workflow process state
- multi-row relational invariant
```

Rule:

```text id="u7yhr4"
Jika state final ada di PostgreSQL, concurrency final juga harus di PostgreSQL.
```

---

## 9. Redis `WATCH / MULTI / EXEC`

Redis `WATCH` menyediakan optimistic locking. Jika key yang di-watch berubah sebelum `EXEC`, transaction dibatalkan dan client harus retry. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/using-commands/transactions/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Flow:

```text id="ly20f3"
WATCH key
GET key
compute new value
MULTI
SET key newValue
EXEC
  ├── success -> update applied
  └── null/abort -> watched key changed, retry
```

Pseudo-code:

```java id="di7wqb"
for (int attempt = 0; attempt < maxRetries; attempt++) {
    redis.watch(key);

    String current = redis.get(key);
    String next = computeNext(current);

    redis.multi();
    redis.set(key, next);
    List<Object> result = redis.exec();

    if (result != null) {
        return success();
    }

    backoff(attempt);
}

throw new ConcurrentModificationException();
```

---

## 10. Kapan `WATCH` Cocok?

Cocok untuk:

```text id="9t6sfx"
- low contention CAS
- simple compare/update
- value kecil
- logic ringan di client
- retry acceptable
```

Tidak cocok untuk:

```text id="sji4iu"
- high contention hot key
- long computation antara WATCH dan EXEC
- external API call di tengah transaction
- multi-key di Redis Cluster tanpa hash-slot planning
- business transaction final
```

Anti-pattern:

```text id="hlga48"
WATCH key
call external API
MULTI
SET key
EXEC
```

Karena selama call eksternal, state bisa berubah berkali-kali, dan external side effect sudah terjadi walau `EXEC` gagal.

---

## 11. Lua vs WATCH

| Aspek | Lua CAS | WATCH/MULTI/EXEC |
|---|---:|---:|
| Atomic server-side | Ya | `EXEC` atomic, tetapi logic compute di client |
| Retry client | Biasanya tidak perlu untuk simple CAS | Perlu jika watched key berubah |
| Network roundtrip | Lebih sedikit | Lebih banyak |
| Logic kompleks | Bisa, tapi harus pendek | Bisa di client |
| External call di tengah | Jangan | Jangan |
| High contention | Lebih baik | Bisa banyak abort |
| Debuggability | Script perlu governance | Lebih eksplisit |
| Recommendation | Default untuk Redis state transition kecil | Cocok untuk simple low-contention CAS |

Rule praktis:

```text id="vkkh69"
Untuk state transition Redis yang kecil dan sering:
  Lua lebih baik.

Untuk update sederhana, jarang konflik, dan ingin code lebih eksplisit:
  WATCH boleh.
```

---

## 12. Redis Transaction Limitation

Redis transaction dengan `MULTI/EXEC` membuat command dieksekusi sebagai satu isolated operation, tetapi Redis tidak melakukan rollback seperti RDBMS jika command yang sudah di-queue dieksekusi dan salah satu command menghasilkan error runtime. Redis docs membedakan transaction Redis dari model rollback relasional. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/using-commands/transactions/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Implikasi:

```text id="jqbd6f"
Jangan menyamakan Redis MULTI/EXEC dengan PostgreSQL transaction.
```

Gunakan Redis transaction untuk Redis state kecil, bukan untuk menggantikan relational consistency.

---

## 13. Redis Counter Concurrency

Counter paling sederhana:

```text id="nnipfu"
INCR quotaKey
```

Use case:

```text id="594jyb"
- request count
- login attempt count
- job attempt count
- temporary quota
- sequence non-business-critical
```

Jika perlu counter dengan TTL atomic:

```lua id="0j90f6"
local key = KEYS[1]
local ttl = tonumber(ARGV[1])

local current = redis.call("INCR", key)

if current == 1 then
  redis.call("EXPIRE", key, ttl)
end

return current
```

Cocok untuk rate limit fixed window. Untuk quota legal/auditable, pakai PostgreSQL.

---

## 14. Redis Semaphore Concurrency Control

Distributed semaphore membatasi concurrency, bukan total count.

```text id="y1u52b"
max 5 report jobs per tenant
max 10 external API calls globally
```

ZSET semaphore:

```text id="k0ju1a"
member = ownerToken
score = leaseUntilMillis
```

Lua:

```lua id="rhozmo"
local key = KEYS[1]
local now = tonumber(ARGV[1])
local leaseUntil = tonumber(ARGV[2])
local maxPermits = tonumber(ARGV[3])
local owner = ARGV[4]
local ttlMs = tonumber(ARGV[5])

redis.call("ZREMRANGEBYSCORE", key, "-inf", now)

local count = redis.call("ZCARD", key)

if count >= maxPermits then
  return {0, count}
end

redis.call("ZADD", key, leaseUntil, owner)
redis.call("PEXPIRE", key, ttlMs)

return {1, count + 1}
```

Rule:

```text id="rd3u3l"
Semaphore membatasi jumlah worker yang boleh jalan.
Semaphore tidak menjamin efek bisnis tidak duplicate.
```

---

# Bagian B - PostgreSQL Concurrency Control

## 15. Optimistic Concurrency Control

Optimistic concurrency cocok jika konflik relatif jarang.

Pattern:

```text id="vwk4bn"
Read row with version
  ↓
Client/service computes update
  ↓
UPDATE ... WHERE id = ? AND version = ?
  ↓
if row count = 1:
    success
else:
    conflict/retry/reload
```

Table:

```sql id="uaoqze"
ALTER TABLE cases
ADD COLUMN version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;
```

Update:

```sql id="i2b4sr"
UPDATE cases
SET status = #{newStatus},
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND case_id = #{caseId}
  AND version = #{expectedVersion}
  AND status = #{expectedStatus};
```

Jika update count = 0:

```text id="m6dk98"
- row tidak ada
- version berubah
- status tidak sesuai
- caller stale
```

Response umum:

```text id="x3y27x"
HTTP 409 Conflict
```

---

## 16. Optimistic Locking di MyBatis

Mapper:

```java id="l5ksby"
public interface CaseMapper {

    int updateStatusOptimistic(
        @Param("tenantId") String tenantId,
        @Param("caseId") String caseId,
        @Param("expectedVersion") long expectedVersion,
        @Param("expectedStatus") String expectedStatus,
        @Param("newStatus") String newStatus
    );
}
```

Service:

```java id="72g9ex"
public void submitCase(
    TenantId tenantId,
    CaseId caseId,
    long expectedVersion
) {
    int updated = caseMapper.updateStatusOptimistic(
        tenantId.value(),
        caseId.value(),
        expectedVersion,
        "DRAFT",
        "SUBMITTED"
    );

    if (updated != 1) {
        throw new OptimisticConflictException(
            "Case was modified by another request"
        );
    }
}
```

Optimistic locking bagus untuk REST API karena client bisa membawa version/ETag.

---

## 17. Pessimistic Concurrency: `SELECT FOR UPDATE`

Pessimistic locking cocok ketika konflik sering atau critical section harus serial dalam DB transaction.

Flow:

```text id="4u46xf"
BEGIN
SELECT ... FOR UPDATE
validate current state
UPDATE ...
COMMIT
```

PostgreSQL row-level locks memblokir writer/locker lain pada row yang sama, tetapi tidak memblokir query biasa; lock row dilepas saat transaction selesai. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/explicit-locking.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

SQL:

```sql id="z7i2sb"
SELECT *
FROM cases
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND case_id = #{caseId}
FOR UPDATE;
```

Kemudian:

```sql id="zae9iw"
UPDATE cases
SET status = 'SUBMITTED',
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND case_id = #{caseId};
```

---

## 18. Kapan `SELECT FOR UPDATE` Cocok?

Gunakan untuk:

```text id="p0mojs"
- update state machine critical
- claim job di PostgreSQL
- ensure one transition per aggregate
- avoid concurrent modification saat transaction pendek
```

Jangan gunakan untuk:

```text id="v3ol9j"
- menahan lock selama external API call lama
- long-running workflow
- user think time
- report generation panjang
```

Anti-pattern:

```text id="hfyx3g"
BEGIN
SELECT row FOR UPDATE
call external provider for 30 seconds
UPDATE row
COMMIT
```

Ini menahan DB lock terlalu lama. Lebih baik:

```text id="rx6sbi"
1. DB transaction creates outbound request
2. commit
3. worker calls external provider with idempotency key
4. DB transaction updates result
```

---

## 19. PostgreSQL Isolation Levels

PostgreSQL mendukung standard isolation levels, tetapi secara internal hanya tiga level berbeda yang diimplementasikan; dokumentasi PostgreSQL menjelaskan behavior masing-masing isolation level seperti Read Committed, Repeatable Read, dan Serializable. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical guidance:

| Isolation | Use |
|---|---|
| Read Committed | Default umum, banyak aplikasi cukup |
| Repeatable Read | Butuh snapshot konsisten dalam transaction |
| Serializable | Invariant kompleks, siap retry serialization failure |
| Explicit row lock | Saat perlu serialisasi row tertentu |
| Unique constraint | Saat perlu invariant uniqueness final |

Rule:

```text id="tfx2h5"
Jangan naik isolation level tanpa memahami retry dan dampak performa.
Sering kali version column, unique constraint, dan SELECT FOR UPDATE lebih jelas.
```

---

## 20. Unique Constraint sebagai Concurrency Control

Untuk mencegah duplicate create, unique constraint adalah guard paling kuat.

Contoh idempotent order create:

```sql id="g4lmrw"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_orders_client_request
ON orders (tenant_id, client_request_id);
```

PostgreSQL unique index menjamin tidak ada lebih dari satu row dengan kombinasi nilai indexed yang sama. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-insert.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Use case:

```text id="sdc0kk"
- idempotency key
- external event ID processed once
- one active assignment per case
- unique order number
- unique workflow business key
```

Redis dedup bagus untuk fast path, tetapi unique constraint adalah final guard.

---

## 21. Job Claim Pattern

Untuk worker concurrency, gunakan atomic DB claim.

```sql id="w5yfch"
UPDATE async_jobs
SET status = 'PROCESSING',
    locked_by = #{workerId},
    locked_until = now() + interval '5 minutes',
    attempt_count = attempt_count + 1,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE id = #{jobId}
  AND status IN ('QUEUED', 'RETRY_SCHEDULED')
  AND (locked_until IS NULL OR locked_until < now())
RETURNING *;
```

Jika tidak ada row returned:

```text id="eb6ptk"
worker tidak berhasil claim
job mungkin sudah diproses atau sedang dimiliki worker lain
```

Ini lebih defensible daripada hanya Redis lock, karena job state final ada di PostgreSQL.

---

## 22. `SKIP LOCKED` untuk Worker Polling

Jika worker polling dari PostgreSQL, pattern umum:

```sql id="q1tx5u"
SELECT id
FROM async_jobs
WHERE status = 'QUEUED'
ORDER BY created_at
FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
LIMIT 10;
```

Lalu update rows di transaction yang sama. `FOR UPDATE` adalah locking clause pada `SELECT`, dan PostgreSQL dokumentasi menjelaskan locking clause seperti `FOR UPDATE`, `FOR NO KEY UPDATE`, `FOR SHARE`, dan `FOR KEY SHARE`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-select.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Cocok untuk:

```text id="elcf87"
- DB-backed job queue
- critical/auditable jobs
- fair polling
```

Tapi untuk high throughput, Redis Streams/Kafka bisa lebih scalable.

---

# Bagian C - Choosing the Right Concurrency Control

## 23. Decision Table

| Scenario | Recommended |
|---|---|
| Update single business aggregate | Optimistic version or `SELECT FOR UPDATE` |
| Create unique entity | Unique constraint + idempotency |
| Claim background job | DB claim + Redis Stream ack |
| Avoid cache stampede | Redis lock/lease |
| Limit external concurrent calls | Redis semaphore |
| Increment ephemeral counter | Redis atomic counter/Lua |
| Modify Redis state machine | Lua CAS |
| Modify PostgreSQL state machine | DB transaction/version |
| Complete Camunda task | Camunda state + idempotency |
| Publish cross-service event | Outbox + Kafka |
| Prevent stale worker write | Fencing token + DB check |
| Low contention temporary Redis state | WATCH/MULTI/EXEC |
| High contention hot Redis state | Lua or redesign/shard |

---

## 24. Per-Aggregate Serialization

Kadang cara paling mudah menjaga correctness adalah memastikan command untuk aggregate yang sama diproses serial.

Aggregate:

```text id="w03e9j"
case:c991
order:o123
customer:c456
```

Options:

```text id="ftocbx"
1. PostgreSQL row lock per aggregate
2. Optimistic version per aggregate
3. Kafka partition by aggregate ID
4. Redis lock per aggregate as pre-guard
5. Camunda process instance per aggregate
```

Rule:

```text id="y879ij"
Per-aggregate ordering harus divalidasi oleh state/version,
bukan hanya oleh queue order.
```

---

## 25. State Machine Transition Guard

Untuk business state, jangan hanya:

```sql id="qiy9qc"
UPDATE cases
SET status = 'APPROVED'
WHERE case_id = #{caseId};
```

Lebih aman:

```sql id="2ctn0g"
UPDATE cases
SET status = 'APPROVED',
    version = version + 1,
    approved_at = now()
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND case_id = #{caseId}
  AND status = 'WAITING_APPROVAL'
  AND version = #{expectedVersion};
```

Jika update count = 0:

```text id="5z0ps5"
transition tidak valid karena state berubah
```

Ini mencegah double approve, approve after reject, stale update, dan lost update.

---

## 26. ABA Problem

ABA:

```text id="hkglqd"
Request reads status = OPEN
Another request changes OPEN -> CLOSED -> OPEN
First request checks status = OPEN and thinks nothing changed
```

Solusi:

```text id="4sg9eo"
Use version, not only status.
```

Bad:

```sql id="76nbf8"
WHERE status = 'OPEN'
```

Better:

```sql id="dqvpk1"
WHERE status = 'OPEN'
  AND version = #{expectedVersion}
```

Version naik setiap perubahan, walau status kembali ke nilai awal.

---

## 27. Fencing Token for Stale Worker

Redis lease bisa expire saat worker pause. Fencing token menolak stale worker.

Redis:

```text id="ng51tn"
INCR fenceKey -> token 42
```

PostgreSQL:

```sql id="6aavm8"
UPDATE resources
SET value = #{value},
    fencing_token = #{token}
WHERE id = #{id}
  AND #{token} > fencing_token;
```

Jika worker lama token 41 mencoba menulis setelah token 42:

```text id="7yj3xr"
update count = 0
stale write rejected
```

Rule:

```text id="gz0hdm"
Fencing token hanya berguna jika downstream mengecek token.
```

---

## 28. Concurrency Control for Idempotency

Redis idempotency state:

```text id="r3es1s"
PROCESSING -> COMPLETED
```

Concurrency rule:

```text id="1l57ev"
Same key + same request hash:
  replay or processing

Same key + different hash:
  conflict
```

Redis Lua `checkOrStart` cocok untuk fast path. PostgreSQL unique constraint tetap final guard.

```sql id="amg29j"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_idem
ON idempotency_records (
  tenant_id,
  operation,
  idempotency_key_hash
);
```

---

## 29. Concurrency Control for Cache

Cache concurrency problem:

```text id="bux5og"
T1 reads DB old value
T2 updates DB and invalidates cache
T1 writes old value to cache
```

Mitigation:

```text id="6ic236"
- short TTL
- cache rebuild lock
- DB version in cache value
- versioned key
- delayed double delete if acceptable
- outbox invalidation
```

Cache correctness should not exceed source-of-truth correctness.

---

## 30. Concurrency Control for Redis Streams Worker

Concurrency tools:

```text id="0mj5xh"
Redis Stream consumer group:
  distribute entries

PEL:
  track unacked messages

XAUTOCLAIM:
  recover stale pending

PostgreSQL job claim:
  final duplicate prevention

Idempotent handler:
  side effect safety
```

Rule:

```text id="98l8jd"
XREADGROUP coordination is not enough.
Worker still needs DB claim or idempotency.
```

---

## 31. Concurrency Control for Camunda

Camunda owns workflow state. Redis can only help around it.

Use:

```text id="pfs3ci"
- idempotency key for REST command
- DB state transition validation
- Camunda business key/process correlation
- external task lock semantics from Camunda
- Redis semaphore for worker concurrency cap
```

Do not:

```text id="cav9c4"
Redis lock says okay
  therefore complete Camunda task blindly
```

Always check process/task state and handle duplicate completion safely.

---

# Bagian D - Implementation Patterns in Jersey/MyBatis

## 32. Resource Layer

Resource should not implement concurrency control directly.

Bad:

```java id="5s3c6q"
@POST
@Path("/{caseId}/submit")
public Response submit(@PathParam String caseId) {
    redis.set(...);
    mapper.update(...);
    return ok();
}
```

Better:

```java id="42u12l"
@POST
@Path("/{caseId}/submit")
public Response submit(
    @PathParam("caseId") String caseId,
    @HeaderParam("Idempotency-Key") String idempotencyKey,
    SubmitCaseRequest request
) {
    SubmitCaseResult result = commandService.submitCase(
        CaseId.of(caseId),
        request,
        idempotencyKey
    );

    return Response.ok(result).build();
}
```

Concurrency belongs in command service + repository.

---

## 33. Command Service Pattern

```java id="v9l41f"
public SubmitCaseResult submitCase(
    CaseId caseId,
    SubmitCaseRequest request,
    String idempotencyKey
) {
    IdempotencyDecision idem = idempotencyStore.checkOrStart(
        "submit-case",
        idempotencyKey,
        requestHasher.hash(request)
    );

    if (idem.completed()) {
        return replay(idem);
    }

    if (idem.conflict()) {
        throw new IdempotencyConflictException();
    }

    try {
        SubmitCaseResult result = transaction.run(() -> {
            CaseRow row = caseRepository.findForUpdate(caseId)
                .orElseThrow(NotFoundException::new);

            domainPolicy.assertCanSubmit(row);

            caseRepository.submit(
                row.tenantId(),
                row.caseId(),
                row.version()
            );

            outboxRepository.insert(CaseSubmittedEvent.from(row));

            return SubmitCaseResult.from(row);
        });

        idempotencyStore.markCompleted(idem, result.reference());
        return result;

    } catch (Exception ex) {
        idempotencyStore.markFailedIfAppropriate(idem, ex);
        throw ex;
    }
}
```

---

## 34. Repository Pattern: Optimistic Update

```java id="ew6dr7"
public void submit(String tenantId, String caseId, long expectedVersion) {
    int updated = mapper.submitOptimistic(
        tenantId,
        caseId,
        expectedVersion,
        "DRAFT",
        "SUBMITTED"
    );

    if (updated != 1) {
        throw new OptimisticConflictException();
    }
}
```

Mapper SQL:

```sql id="a6jmto"
UPDATE cases
SET status = #{newStatus},
    version = version + 1,
    submitted_at = now(),
    updated_at = now()
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND case_id = #{caseId}
  AND status = #{expectedStatus}
  AND version = #{expectedVersion};
```

---

## 35. Repository Pattern: Pessimistic Lock

```java id="9hvc2p"
public CaseRow findForUpdate(TenantId tenantId, CaseId caseId) {
    return mapper.findForUpdate(tenantId.value(), caseId.value());
}
```

SQL:

```sql id="t6hz2t"
SELECT *
FROM cases
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND case_id = #{caseId}
FOR UPDATE;
```

Keep transaction short.

---

## 36. Handling Conflict in API

Conflict response:

```json id="9ko4yw"
{
  "code": "CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION",
  "message": "The resource was modified by another request. Please reload and retry.",
  "resource": "case",
  "caseId": "c991"
}
```

HTTP status:

```text id="cwpljf"
409 Conflict
```

For async job already processing:

```json id="8zj9pm"
{
  "code": "ALREADY_PROCESSING",
  "message": "The request is already being processed.",
  "operationId": "op-123"
}
```

HTTP status:

```text id="v6m07m"
202 Accepted or 409 Conflict
```

---

# Bagian E - Choosing Strategy by Conflict Profile

## 37. Low Conflict

Use optimistic concurrency.

```text id="083q11"
- user profile update
- case metadata edit
- non-hot aggregate command
```

Pattern:

```text id="s3a9ek"
version column
UPDATE ... WHERE version = ?
409 on conflict
```

---

## 38. High Conflict

Use pessimistic lock or queue/serialization.

```text id="q15ryp"
- hot case assignment queue
- high-frequency counter
- shared quota
- worker claim
```

Options:

```text id="hbqtyn"
- SELECT FOR UPDATE
- DB claim update
- Redis Lua counter/semaphore
- per-aggregate queue
```

---

## 39. Critical External Side Effect

Use idempotency + durable outbound state.

```text id="jw8m3y"
- payment
- email official notice
- external regulator submission
```

Pattern:

```text id="bs7m4q"
PostgreSQL outbound_request
provider idempotency key
Redis lock/semaphore optional
reconciliation worker
```

---

## 40. Workflow Lifecycle

Use Camunda + DB validation.

```text id="729zcp"
- submit case
- approve/reject
- escalate
- SLA timeout
```

Pattern:

```text id="22mdhf"
REST idempotency
DB state transition
Camunda process/task command
outbox event
```

---

## 41. Ephemeral Control Plane

Use Redis Lua/lock/semaphore.

```text id="dw5gpa"
- rate limiter
- cache stampede
- temporary session state
- presence
- worker concurrency cap
```

Pattern:

```text id="sn0knk"
Redis atomic command or Lua
TTL
short timeout
fallback policy
```

---

# Bagian F - Anti-Patterns

## 42. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Read-modify-write Redis tanpa Lua/WATCH | Lost update | Lua CAS / WATCH |
| Redis transaction dianggap seperti DB transaction | Salah assumption rollback | Pakai PostgreSQL untuk invariant |
| Lock Redis sebagai satu-satunya guard payment | Duplicate payment risk | Idempotency + DB/provider guard |
| Update DB tanpa `WHERE version/status` | Lost update/state corruption | Optimistic update |
| `SELECT FOR UPDATE` sambil call external API lama | DB lock contention | Outbox/outbound job |
| Worker hanya mengandalkan Redis Stream PEL | Duplicate side effect | DB job claim |
| No unique constraint untuk idempotency | Duplicate create | Unique index |
| Status check tanpa version | ABA problem | Version column |
| Retry conflict tanpa backoff | Retry storm | Backoff/jitter |
| Lua script terlalu panjang/berat | Redis blocked | Keep script short |
| Hot key concurrency semua ke satu Redis key | Bottleneck | Shard/design ulang |
| No metrics conflict | Race invisible | Observability |

---

## 43. Observability

Metrics:

```text id="4wgx98"
concurrency.optimistic.conflict.count
concurrency.pessimistic.lock.wait_ms
concurrency.db.deadlock.count
concurrency.db.serialization_failure.count
concurrency.redis.cas.success.count
concurrency.redis.cas.conflict.count
concurrency.redis.watch.abort.count
concurrency.redis.lua.latency_ms
concurrency.worker.claim.success.count
concurrency.worker.claim.conflict.count
concurrency.idempotency.replay.count
concurrency.idempotency.conflict.count
concurrency.fencing.stale_rejected.count
```

Labels aman:

```text id="0gl3og"
service
env
operation
aggregate_type
strategy
result
exception_class
```

Jangan label dengan:

```text id="z2pg3a"
raw caseId
raw userId
raw tenantId
raw Redis key
idempotency key
```

---

## 44. Logging

Good:

```json id="f28rqh"
{
  "event": "optimistic_conflict",
  "operation": "submit-case",
  "aggregateType": "case",
  "aggregateIdHash": "sha256-...",
  "expectedVersion": 17,
  "action": "return_409",
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

Worker claim conflict:

```json id="r5ip5r"
{
  "event": "job_claim_conflict",
  "jobType": "REPORT_EXPORT",
  "jobIdHash": "sha256-...",
  "workerId": "report-pod-a:worker-1",
  "action": "ack_if_terminal_or_retry_later"
}
```

---

## 45. Testing Matrix

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Two concurrent optimistic updates | Only one succeeds |
| Stale version update returns 409 | API correctness |
| `SELECT FOR UPDATE` serializes update | Pessimistic correctness |
| Unique constraint catches duplicate create | Final guard |
| Redis Lua CAS conflict | Atomic Redis update |
| WATCH abort on concurrent change | Retry behavior |
| Worker duplicate stream message | DB claim prevents duplicate |
| Stale fencing token | DB rejects stale write |
| External side effect retry | Same provider idempotency key |
| Cache rebuild stampede | Only one rebuilds |
| Camunda task duplicate complete | Safe conflict handling |
| Serialization failure retry | Serializable handling |
| Deadlock handling | Retry/backoff |
| Redis timeout during CAS | Failure policy |
| High contention hot key | Latency and abort metrics |

---

## 46. Senior Engineer Checklist

```text id="d7xpv1"
[ ] Apa shared resource/aggregate yang diperebutkan?
[ ] Apakah state final ada di Redis, PostgreSQL, Camunda, atau external system?
[ ] Apakah konflik jarang atau sering?
[ ] Apakah optimistic version cukup?
[ ] Apakah perlu SELECT FOR UPDATE?
[ ] Apakah unique constraint sudah ada untuk duplicate create?
[ ] Apakah Redis CAS memakai Lua/WATCH, bukan GET lalu SET biasa?
[ ] Apakah external side effect punya idempotency key?
[ ] Apakah worker claim final ada di PostgreSQL?
[ ] Apakah Redis lock hanya pre-guard, bukan final correctness?
[ ] Apakah stale owner bisa menulis? Jika ya, ada fencing token?
[ ] Apakah operation bisa retry dengan aman?
[ ] Apakah conflict response/API contract jelas?
[ ] Apakah metrics conflict/lock wait/CAS abort ada?
[ ] Apakah test concurrency paralel ada?
```

---

## 47. Ringkasan Praktis

```text id="hzpvlh"
Redis Lua:
  atomic update untuk Redis-owned temporary state

Redis WATCH/MULTI/EXEC:
  optimistic CAS untuk low-contention Redis state

Redis lock/semaphore:
  coordination dan concurrency cap

PostgreSQL optimistic version:
  default untuk business aggregate update

PostgreSQL SELECT FOR UPDATE:
  pessimistic lock untuk transaction pendek

PostgreSQL unique constraint:
  final guard terhadap duplicate create/idempotency

Fencing token:
  menolak stale owner write

Camunda:
  owner workflow/process state

Kafka/outbox:
  durable event ordering/publication
```

Kalimat kunci:

> Distributed concurrency control yang baik dimulai dari pertanyaan “state final hidup di mana?”. Jika state final hidup di PostgreSQL, maka version, constraint, transaction, dan lock PostgreSQL adalah penjaga utama. Redis membantu mempercepat dan mengoordinasikan, tetapi tidak menggantikan invariant final.
