Saya lanjut ke **Seri 17 — Lua Scripting, Pipeline & Transaction Pattern**. Fokusnya: kapan pakai Lua untuk atomicity, kapan pipeline untuk throughput, kapan `MULTI/EXEC`, dan jebakan Redis Cluster/script governance di aplikasi Java.

# Seri 17 - Lua Scripting, Pipeline & Transaction Pattern

Status: **Seri 17 dari 22 seri lanjutan**.  
Setelah seri ini selesai, tersisa **5 seri**.

---

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas tiga mekanisme Redis yang sering tertukar:

```text
1. Lua scripting
   Untuk atomic multi-command logic di sisi Redis server.

2. Pipeline
   Untuk mengurangi network round-trip saat mengirim banyak command.

3. MULTI / EXEC transaction
   Untuk mengeksekusi beberapa command Redis sebagai satu batch transactional Redis.

4. WATCH / MULTI / EXEC
   Untuk optimistic concurrency control pada key Redis.
```

Di aplikasi **Java Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL + Redis**, pemilihan mekanisme ini penting karena salah pilih bisa menyebabkan:

```text
- race condition
- lost update
- counter tanpa TTL
- partial write yang sulit dipulihkan
- Redis blocking karena script terlalu berat
- error di Redis Cluster karena multi-key beda hash slot
- salah asumsi bahwa Redis transaction sama seperti PostgreSQL transaction
```

Redis mendukung Lua scripting untuk menjalankan logic di server, pipeline untuk mengirim banyak command tanpa menunggu response satu per satu, dan transaction dengan `MULTI`, `EXEC`, `DISCARD`, serta `WATCH`. Redis juga menjelaskan bahwa script Lua dieksekusi secara atomic di server. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/programmability/eval-intro/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Singkat

```text
Lua:
  "Saya butuh beberapa Redis command + conditional logic
   berjalan atomic tanpa disela command lain."

Pipeline:
  "Saya butuh mengirim banyak command lebih cepat,
   tapi tidak butuh atomicity."

MULTI/EXEC:
  "Saya butuh beberapa command Redis dieksekusi sebagai satu unit,
   tetapi logic-nya sederhana."

WATCH/MULTI/EXEC:
  "Saya butuh optimistic locking:
   jalankan transaction hanya jika key tidak berubah."
```

Kalimat kunci:

> Lua untuk **atomic logic**, pipeline untuk **throughput**, transaction untuk **grouped execution**, dan PostgreSQL tetap untuk **business transaction final**.

---

## 3. Diagram Besar

```text
Java Service
  ↓
Redis Adapter
  ├── Simple Command
  │     └── GET / SET / INCR / HSET
  │
  ├── Pipeline
  │     └── Batch many independent commands
  │
  ├── MULTI / EXEC
  │     └── Execute queued Redis commands as transaction
  │
  ├── WATCH / MULTI / EXEC
  │     └── Optimistic CAS on Redis keys
  │
  └── Lua Script
        └── Atomic conditional multi-command logic
```

---

## 4. Decision Table

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Single atomic increment | `INCR` |
| Counter + TTL atomic | Lua |
| Rate limiter fixed/sliding/token bucket | Lua |
| Safe unlock lock token | Lua |
| Semaphore acquire/release | Lua |
| Idempotency `checkOrStart` | Lua |
| Bulk cache `GET` / `SET` many keys | Pipeline |
| Bulk delete many keys after SCAN | Pipeline + `UNLINK` chunks |
| Execute several simple Redis commands together | `MULTI/EXEC` |
| Optimistic update if key unchanged | `WATCH/MULTI/EXEC` |
| Business transaction involving orders/payments/cases | PostgreSQL transaction |
| Workflow transition/timer | Camunda |
| Durable cross-service event | Outbox + Kafka |

---

# Bagian A - Lua Scripting

## 5. Apa Itu Redis Lua Script?

Redis Lua script memungkinkan client mengirim script ke Redis untuk dieksekusi di server. Karena script berjalan di server Redis, script bisa membaca/menulis data Redis tanpa bolak-balik network round-trip antar command. Redis documentation menyatakan scripting engine memastikan script berjalan atomic. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/programmability/eval-intro/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh sederhana:

```lua
local value = redis.call("GET", KEYS[1])

if value == ARGV[1] then
  return redis.call("SET", KEYS[1], ARGV[2])
else
  return "NOOP"
end
```

---

## 6. Kenapa Lua Penting?

Tanpa Lua:

```text
GET current
if current == expected:
  SET newValue
```

Race:

```text
Client A GET current = 1
Client B GET current = 1
Client A SET 2
Client B SET 2
```

Dengan Lua:

```text
Redis executes compare + set atomically
No other command interleaves inside script
```

Redis Lua cocok untuk operasi **read-check-write** pada Redis-owned state.

---

## 7. Pattern: Counter + TTL Atomic

Problem klasik:

```text
INCR key
if value == 1:
  EXPIRE key 60
```

Jika aplikasi crash setelah `INCR` tetapi sebelum `EXPIRE`, counter bisa hidup tanpa TTL.

Lua:

```lua
local key = KEYS[1]
local ttl = tonumber(ARGV[1])

local current = redis.call("INCR", key)

if current == 1 then
  redis.call("EXPIRE", key, ttl)
end

return current
```

Use case:

```text
- fixed-window rate limiter
- login attempt counter
- temporary quota
- retry attempt counter
```

---

## 8. Pattern: Safe Lock Release

Jangan release lock dengan `DEL lockKey` langsung.

Lua safe unlock:

```lua
if redis.call("GET", KEYS[1]) == ARGV[1] then
  return redis.call("DEL", KEYS[1])
else
  return 0
end
```

Use case:

```text
SET lockKey token NX PX 30000
...
release only if token still matches
```

Ini mencegah worker lama menghapus lock milik worker baru.

---

## 9. Pattern: Idempotency `checkOrStart`

```lua
local key = KEYS[1]

local requestHash = ARGV[1]
local operation = ARGV[2]
local now = ARGV[3]
local ttlSeconds = tonumber(ARGV[4])

local existingHash = redis.call("HGET", key, "requestHash")

if not existingHash then
  redis.call("HSET", key,
    "requestHash", requestHash,
    "operation", operation,
    "status", "PROCESSING",
    "resultRef", "",
    "startedAt", now,
    "updatedAt", now
  )

  redis.call("EXPIRE", key, ttlSeconds)

  return {"STARTED", "PROCESSING", ""}
end

if existingHash ~= requestHash then
  return {"CONFLICT", redis.call("HGET", key, "status") or "", ""}
end

local status = redis.call("HGET", key, "status") or ""
local resultRef = redis.call("HGET", key, "resultRef") or ""

return {"EXISTS", status, resultRef}
```

Use case:

```text
POST /orders
POST /cases/{caseId}/submit
POST /reports/export
worker event deduplication
```

---

## 10. Pattern: Sliding Window Rate Limiter

```lua
local key = KEYS[1]

local now = tonumber(ARGV[1])
local windowMillis = tonumber(ARGV[2])
local limit = tonumber(ARGV[3])
local requestId = ARGV[4]
local ttlSeconds = tonumber(ARGV[5])

local minScore = now - windowMillis

redis.call("ZREMRANGEBYSCORE", key, "-inf", minScore)

local current = redis.call("ZCARD", key)

if current >= limit then
  local oldest = redis.call("ZRANGE", key, 0, 0, "WITHSCORES")
  local retryAfterMillis = windowMillis

  if oldest[2] ~= nil then
    retryAfterMillis = tonumber(oldest[2]) + windowMillis - now
    if retryAfterMillis < 0 then
      retryAfterMillis = 0
    end
  end

  return {0, current, retryAfterMillis}
end

redis.call("ZADD", key, now, requestId)
redis.call("EXPIRE", key, ttlSeconds)

return {1, limit - current - 1, 0}
```

Kenapa Lua?

```text
ZREMRANGEBYSCORE
ZCARD
ZADD
EXPIRE
```

Harus dilihat sebagai satu operasi atomic.

---

## 11. Pattern: Distributed Semaphore Acquire

```lua
local key = KEYS[1]

local now = tonumber(ARGV[1])
local leaseUntil = tonumber(ARGV[2])
local maxPermits = tonumber(ARGV[3])
local owner = ARGV[4]
local ttlMs = tonumber(ARGV[5])

redis.call("ZREMRANGEBYSCORE", key, "-inf", now)

local count = redis.call("ZCARD", key)

if count >= maxPermits then
  return {0, count}
end

redis.call("ZADD", key, leaseUntil, owner)
redis.call("PEXPIRE", key, ttlMs)

return {1, count + 1}
```

Use case:

```text
- max 5 report jobs per tenant
- max 10 external API calls globally
- max N Camunda external task workers per tenant
```

---

## 12. Lua Script Rules

| Rule | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Script harus pendek | Redis event loop bisa terblokir |
| Jangan lakukan heavy computation | Redis bukan compute engine |
| Jangan loop besar tanpa batas | Bisa mengganggu semua client |
| Semua key harus lewat `KEYS[]` | Penting untuk Redis Cluster dan correctness |
| Data input lewat `ARGV[]` | Pisahkan key dan argument |
| Jangan dynamic key tersembunyi dari ARGV | Redis Cluster/key routing bisa salah |
| Return value harus stabil | Memudahkan Java mapping |
| Script harus versioned | Deployment aman |
| Script harus dites sebagai unit | Banyak bug sulit terlihat di runtime |

Redis Cluster menggunakan hash slot; multi-key operation perlu key berada pada hash slot yang sama, dan Redis mendukung hash tags `{...}` untuk memaksa key terkait berada di slot yang sama. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/operate/oss_and_stack/reference/cluster-spec/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 13. Redis Cluster dan Lua

Jika script menyentuh banyak key, semua key harus berada di hash slot yang sama dalam Redis Cluster.

Bad:

```text
KEYS[1] = case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:lock:case:c991
KEYS[2] = case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:fence:case:c991
```

Bisa beda slot.

Better dengan hash tag:

```text
case-svc:prod:{tenant:t1:case:c991}:lock:v1
case-svc:prod:{tenant:t1:case:c991}:fence:v1
```

Caveat:

```text
Jangan hash tag terlalu luas seperti {tenant:t1}
jika semua key tenant besar jadi hot slot.
```

---

## 14. EVAL vs SCRIPT LOAD / EVALSHA

Ada dua pola umum:

### A. `EVAL`

Client mengirim script setiap kali.

```text
EVAL "<lua script>" 1 key arg
```

Kelebihan:

```text
simple
```

Kekurangan:

```text
script dikirim terus
lebih boros network untuk script panjang
```

### B. `SCRIPT LOAD` + `EVALSHA`

Client load script, Redis mengembalikan SHA, lalu client menjalankan SHA tersebut.

```text
SCRIPT LOAD "<lua script>"
EVALSHA sha 1 key arg
```

Kelebihan:

```text
lebih efisien untuk script tetap
script governance lebih rapi
```

Kekurangan:

```text
harus handle NOSCRIPT
script cache bisa hilang setelah restart/failover/eviction
```

Redis `EVAL` docs menyebut bahwa mulai Redis 7.4, script yang dimuat dengan `EVAL` atau `EVAL_RO` dapat dieviction dari script cache secara LRU saat cache mencapai ukuran tertentu; metrik `evicted_scripts` tersedia di `INFO`. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/eval/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 15. Script Cache Handling

Production adapter harus siap terhadap:

```text
NOSCRIPT
Redis restart
Redis failover
Redis 7.4 script cache eviction
client reconnect
```

Pattern:

```text
try EVALSHA
  ├── success -> return
  └── NOSCRIPT -> SCRIPT LOAD, retry EVALSHA
```

Pseudo-code:

```java
public Object evalScript(RedisScript script, List<String> keys, List<String> args) {
    try {
        return redis.evalsha(script.sha(), keys, args);
    } catch (NoScriptException ex) {
        String sha = redis.scriptLoad(script.source());
        scriptRegistry.updateSha(script.name(), sha);
        return redis.evalsha(sha, keys, args);
    }
}
```

---

## 16. Redis Functions Note

Redis 7 memperkenalkan Redis Functions sebagai cara mengelola server-side logic dalam Redis, sedangkan Redis 6.2 dan sebelumnya menggunakan Lua scripting via `EVAL`. Redis documentation menyebut Redis Functions sebagai bagian dari Redis programmability di Redis 7+. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/programmability/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical stance:

```text
Untuk aplikasi baru di Redis 7+:
  Redis Functions bisa dipertimbangkan untuk script yang stabil dan shared.

Untuk compatibility sederhana:
  Lua EVALSHA masih umum dan portable.

Untuk tim onboarding:
  kuasai Lua scripting dulu, lalu evaluasi Redis Functions jika ops maturity cukup.
```

---

# Bagian B - Pipeline

## 17. Apa Itu Redis Pipeline?

Redis pipelining adalah teknik mengirim banyak command sekaligus tanpa menunggu response satu per satu, sehingga mengurangi round-trip time antara client dan Redis. Pipelining bukan atomicity mechanism. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/using-commands/pipelining/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Tanpa pipeline:

```text
SET k1 v1 -> wait response
SET k2 v2 -> wait response
SET k3 v3 -> wait response
```

Dengan pipeline:

```text
send SET k1 v1
send SET k2 v2
send SET k3 v3
then read responses
```

---

## 18. Pipeline Mental Model

```text
Pipeline =
  network batching

Not:
  transaction
  lock
  rollback
  all-or-nothing
  conditional atomic operation
```

Gunakan pipeline untuk **banyak command independent**.

---

## 19. Kapan Pipeline Cocok?

```text
- bulk cache warmup
- batch GET banyak key
- batch SET banyak cache value
- batch UNLINK setelah SCAN
- batch HGET untuk banyak hash
- refresh reference data
- preload permission snapshots
- bulk metric-ish counter updates jika independent
```

Contoh use case:

```text
GET 100 case summary cache keys
```

Pipeline mengurangi latency karena tidak perlu 100 round-trip.

---

## 20. Kapan Pipeline Tidak Cocok?

Jangan pakai pipeline jika:

```text
- command kedua bergantung pada hasil command pertama
- perlu atomic read-check-write
- perlu rollback/all-or-nothing
- perlu conditional update
- tiap command harus berhenti jika command sebelumnya gagal
```

Bad:

```text
pipeline:
  GET key
  SET key computedFromGetResult
```

Masalah:

```text
computedFromGetResult belum tersedia saat command dikirim
```

Untuk conditional logic, gunakan Lua atau WATCH.

---

## 21. Pipeline for Bulk Cache Read

```java
public Map<String, Optional<String>> mgetPipeline(List<String> keys) {
    RedisPipeline pipeline = redis.pipeline();

    List<Response<String>> responses = new ArrayList<>();

    for (String key : keys) {
        responses.add(pipeline.get(key));
    }

    pipeline.sync();

    Map<String, Optional<String>> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();

    for (int i = 0; i < keys.size(); i++) {
        result.put(keys.get(i), Optional.ofNullable(responses.get(i).get()));
    }

    return result;
}
```

Use case:

```text
GET cache for 50 case IDs in one endpoint
```

Guardrail:

```text
- limit batch size
- avoid raw huge response
- timeout still bounded
- handle partial missing values
```

---

## 22. Pipeline for Bulk Cache Write

```java
public void setManyCacheValues(Map<String, String> values, Duration ttl) {
    RedisPipeline pipeline = redis.pipeline();

    for (Map.Entry<String, String> e : values.entrySet()) {
        pipeline.setex(e.getKey(), ttl.toSeconds(), e.getValue());
    }

    pipeline.sync();
}
```

Use case:

```text
- warmup reference cache
- write query result fragments
- refresh authz snapshots
```

---

## 23. Pipeline for SCAN + UNLINK

Untuk delete banyak key:

```text
SCAN MATCH pattern COUNT 1000
  ↓
pipeline UNLINK key1 key2 key3 ...
```

Jangan:

```text
KEYS pattern
DEL thousands of keys in request path
```

Pattern aman:

```java
public long unlinkByScan(String pattern, int scanCount, int deleteBatchSize) {
    long deleted = 0;

    ScanCursor cursor = ScanCursor.initial();

    do {
        ScanResult<String> page = redis.scan(cursor, pattern, scanCount);

        List<List<String>> batches = partition(page.keys(), deleteBatchSize);

        for (List<String> batch : batches) {
            RedisPipeline pipeline = redis.pipeline();

            for (String key : batch) {
                pipeline.unlink(key);
            }

            pipeline.sync();
            deleted += batch.size();
        }

        cursor = page.nextCursor();

    } while (!cursor.isFinished());

    return deleted;
}
```

Guardrail:

```text
- jangan jalankan di request path
- rate limit cleanup
- dry-run count/sample
- log key family, bukan raw key
```

---

## 24. Pipeline Batch Size

Batch terlalu kecil:

```text
round-trip terlalu banyak
```

Batch terlalu besar:

```text
client memory naik
Redis output buffer naik
latency spike
timeout risk
```

Practical starting point:

```text
GET/SET small values:
  50-500 commands per pipeline

Large values:
  smaller batch

Delete/unlink:
  100-1000 keys per batch, tergantung size dan Redis latency
```

Ukuran harus ditentukan lewat load test, bukan angka mutlak.

---

## 25. Pipeline Error Handling

Dalam pipeline, tiap command punya response/error sendiri.

Jangan menganggap:

```text
pipeline.sync() success = semua command semantic success
```

Harus inspect response jika command penting.

Pattern:

```text
execute pipeline
  ↓
for each response:
  if error:
    record key family + error
    decide retry/ignore/fail
```

Untuk cache write:

```text
SET failure:
  log metric, request bisa tetap sukses jika DB result sudah ada
```

Untuk critical coordination:

```text
Jangan gunakan pipeline sebagai pengganti atomic operation.
```

---

# Bagian C - Redis Transactions

## 26. MULTI / EXEC

Redis transaction menggunakan command:

```text
MULTI
  command 1
  command 2
  command 3
EXEC
```

Redis transaction menjalankan sekelompok command dalam satu langkah; Redis docs menyebut transaction berpusat pada `MULTI`, `EXEC`, `DISCARD`, dan `WATCH`. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/using-commands/transactions/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```text
MULTI
INCR counter
EXPIRE counter 60
EXEC
```

---

## 27. Apa yang Dijamin Redis Transaction?

Redis transaction memberi guarantee bahwa command yang sudah diqueue akan dieksekusi secara berurutan sebagai satu isolated operation; request client lain tidak disisipkan di tengah eksekusi transaction. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/using-commands/transactions/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Namun:

```text
Redis transaction bukan PostgreSQL transaction.
```

Redis tidak melakukan rollback seperti RDBMS jika ada command runtime error setelah `EXEC`. Redis docs menjelaskan bahwa Redis tidak mendukung rollback seperti relational database. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/using-commands/transactions/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 28. MULTI/EXEC vs Lua

| Aspek | MULTI/EXEC | Lua |
|---|---:|---:|
| Group command | Ya | Ya |
| Conditional branching | Terbatas | Ya |
| Read result lalu decide write | Sulit tanpa WATCH/client logic | Ya |
| Atomic server-side logic | Tidak sefleksibel Lua | Ya |
| Debuggability | Lebih command-like | Script perlu governance |
| Complex rate limiter | Kurang cocok | Cocok |
| Safe unlock | Tidak ideal | Cocok |
| Counter + TTL | Bisa, tapi Lua lebih rapi | Cocok |

Rule:

```text
Jika butuh conditional logic:
  pakai Lua.

Jika hanya ingin beberapa command dieksekusi bersama:
  MULTI/EXEC bisa cukup.
```

---

## 29. WATCH / MULTI / EXEC

`WATCH` membuat Redis transaction menjadi optimistic. Jika key yang di-watch berubah sebelum `EXEC`, transaction dibatalkan. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/using-commands/transactions/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Flow:

```text
WATCH key
GET key
compute newValue
MULTI
SET key newValue
EXEC
  ├── success
  └── null/abort because watched key changed
```

Use case:

```text
- low-contention CAS
- temporary Redis state update
- simple compare/update
```

Avoid:

```text
- high contention hot key
- long computation between WATCH and EXEC
- external API call between WATCH and EXEC
```

---

## 30. WATCH Example

```java
public boolean compareAndSet(String key, String expected, String next) {
    for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++) {
        redis.watch(key);

        String current = redis.get(key);

        if (!Objects.equals(current, expected)) {
            redis.unwatch();
            return false;
        }

        redis.multi();
        redis.set(key, next);

        List<Object> result = redis.exec();

        if (result != null) {
            return true;
        }

        sleepBackoff(attempt);
    }

    throw new ConcurrentModificationException("Redis CAS failed after retries");
}
```

Untuk high contention, lebih baik Lua CAS atau redesign key sharding.

---

# Bagian D - Memilih Lua vs Pipeline vs Transaction

## 31. Comparison Table

| Need | Lua | Pipeline | MULTI/EXEC | WATCH |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Reduce RTT | Ya, kadang | Ya, utama | Sedikit | Tidak utama |
| Atomic multi-command | Ya | Tidak | Ya | Ya jika EXEC success |
| Conditional logic | Ya | Tidak | Tidak praktis | Ya, di client |
| Rollback RDBMS-style | Tidak | Tidak | Tidak | Tidak |
| Bulk independent commands | Tidak ideal | Ya | Kadang | Tidak |
| High contention CAS | Ya | Tidak | Tidak | Kurang ideal |
| Simple grouped write | Bisa | Tidak atomic | Ya | Bisa |
| Redis Cluster multi-key | Same slot required | Client-dependent | Same slot for multi-key | Same slot if multi-key |

---

## 32. Practical Rule

```text
Use Lua when:
  correctness of Redis state needs atomic read-check-write.

Use Pipeline when:
  many commands are independent and you only need speed.

Use MULTI/EXEC when:
  commands should be grouped, but no complex branching is needed.

Use WATCH when:
  optimistic CAS is simple and contention is low.

Use PostgreSQL transaction when:
  business data correctness is involved.
```

---

## 33. Examples by Pattern

| Pattern | Recommended |
|---|---|
| Fixed-window rate limiter | Lua |
| Sliding-window rate limiter | Lua |
| Token bucket | Lua |
| Safe lock release | Lua |
| Semaphore acquire | Lua |
| Idempotency check/start | Lua |
| Bulk cache read | Pipeline |
| Bulk cache warmup | Pipeline |
| SCAN + UNLINK cleanup | Pipeline |
| Simple grouped HSET + EXPIRE | MULTI/EXEC or Lua |
| Low-contention Redis CAS | WATCH or Lua |
| PostgreSQL update case status | PostgreSQL transaction/version |
| External payment call | DB outbound state + provider idempotency |

---

# Bagian E - Java/Jersey Integration

## 34. Package Design

```text
com.company.redis
├── RedisClientProvider.java
├── RedisScriptRegistry.java
├── RedisScriptExecutor.java
├── RedisPipelineExecutor.java
├── RedisTransactionExecutor.java
├── scripts
│   ├── FixedWindowRateLimiterScript.java
│   ├── SlidingWindowRateLimiterScript.java
│   ├── SafeUnlockScript.java
│   ├── IdempotencyCheckOrStartScript.java
│   └── SemaphoreAcquireScript.java
└── keys
    └── RedisKeyFactory.java
```

Rule:

```text
Resource tidak menjalankan script langsung.
Application service memanggil port.
Redis adapter yang tahu Lua/pipeline/transaction.
```

---

## 35. Script Registry

```java
public enum RedisScriptName {
    SAFE_UNLOCK,
    IDEMPOTENCY_CHECK_OR_START,
    FIXED_WINDOW_RATE_LIMIT,
    SLIDING_WINDOW_RATE_LIMIT,
    SEMAPHORE_ACQUIRE
}
```

```java
public record RedisScript(
    RedisScriptName name,
    String version,
    String source,
    String sha
) {}
```

Script harus versioned:

```text
safe_unlock:v1
idempotency_check_or_start:v2
sliding_window_rate_limit:v1
```

Jangan edit script behavior tanpa menaikkan version jika return shape berubah.

---

## 36. Script Return Contract

Lua return harus stabil.

Bad:

```lua
return "OK"
-- sometimes return number
-- sometimes return array
```

Better:

```lua
return {"COMPLETED", resultRef, httpStatus}
```

Java mapping:

```java
public IdempotencyScriptResult map(List<Object> raw) {
    String outcome = asString(raw.get(0));

    return switch (outcome) {
        case "STARTED" -> IdempotencyScriptResult.started();
        case "EXISTS" -> IdempotencyScriptResult.exists(
            asString(raw.get(1)),
            asString(raw.get(2))
        );
        case "CONFLICT" -> IdempotencyScriptResult.conflict();
        default -> throw new IllegalStateException("Unknown script outcome: " + outcome);
    };
}
```

---

## 37. Script Governance

Checklist untuk setiap script:

```text
[ ] Name jelas
[ ] Version jelas
[ ] KEYS dan ARGV terdokumentasi
[ ] Return shape terdokumentasi
[ ] Semua key lewat KEYS[]
[ ] Tidak ada dynamic hidden key
[ ] TTL behavior jelas
[ ] Cluster hash slot kompatibel
[ ] Unit test dengan Redis real/integration
[ ] Load test jika hot path
[ ] Timeout budget jelas
[ ] Error mapping Java jelas
[ ] Metrics latency/error ada
```

---

## 38. Script Documentation Template

```text
Script:
  sliding_window_rate_limit:v1

Purpose:
  Atomic sliding-window rate limiter using ZSET.

KEYS:
  KEYS[1] = rate limit key

ARGV:
  ARGV[1] = nowMillis
  ARGV[2] = windowMillis
  ARGV[3] = limit
  ARGV[4] = requestId
  ARGV[5] = ttlSeconds

Return:
  {allowed, remainingOrCurrent, retryAfterMillis}

Cluster:
  single key, cluster-safe

TTL:
  EXPIRE key ttlSeconds after successful add

Failure:
  Redis timeout -> policy decides fail-open/fail-closed
```

---

## 39. JAX-RS Use Case: Rate Limit Filter

```text
ContainerRequestFilter
  ↓
RateLimitSubjectResolver
  ↓
RateLimitPolicyResolver
  ↓
RedisRateLimiter
  ↓
Lua sliding-window script
  ↓
ALLOW / 429
```

Resource layer tidak tahu Lua.

---

## 40. JAX-RS Use Case: Idempotency

```text
Resource receives Idempotency-Key
  ↓
CommandService
  ↓
IdempotencyStore.checkOrStart()
  ↓
Lua script
  ↓
STARTED / EXISTS / CONFLICT
  ↓
PostgreSQL transaction if STARTED
  ↓
markCompleted
```

Redis Lua mempercepat state gate. PostgreSQL tetap final guard.

---

# Bagian F - Cluster, Hot Key, dan Performance

## 41. Redis Cluster Hash Tags

Untuk multi-key script:

```text
lock key
fencing counter key
```

Gunakan hash tag yang sama:

```text
case-svc:prod:{tenant:t1:case:c991}:lock:submit:v1
case-svc:prod:{tenant:t1:case:c991}:fence:submit:v1
```

Redis Cluster hash tags dipakai agar beberapa key dialokasikan ke hash slot yang sama, sehingga multi-key operation bisa dilakukan. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/operate/oss_and_stack/reference/cluster-spec/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 42. Hot Script / Hot Key Problem

Lua script tetap berjalan di Redis server. Jika script dipakai pada hot key:

```text
1 key
100k requests/sec
script moderately expensive
```

Risiko:

```text
Redis latency naik
semua client terdampak
```

Mitigasi:

```text
- shard key by tenant/user/route
- batasi script complexity
- gunakan token bucket per subject, bukan global key
- monitor script latency
- move heavy logic ke application/DB jika perlu
```

---

## 43. Long-Running Script Danger

Bad Lua:

```lua
for i = 1, 10000000 do
  -- heavy loop
end
```

Dampak:

```text
Redis blocked
latency spike
timeouts across app
failover risk
```

Rule:

```text
Lua script harus O(1) atau bounded small O(log N / N kecil).
```

Untuk cleanup banyak key, jangan Lua loop besar. Pakai SCAN di client + pipeline chunk.

---

## 44. Pipeline Hot Path Risk

Pipeline bisa meningkatkan throughput, tapi juga bisa menumpuk banyak response.

Risiko:

```text
- client memory naik
- Redis output buffer membesar
- latency per batch naik
- timeout jika batch terlalu besar
```

Guardrail:

```text
- batch size bounded
- time budget
- value size limit
- metrics per batch
- backpressure
```

---

# Bagian G - Error Handling

## 45. Redis Timeout During Lua

Jika client timeout saat menjalankan Lua:

```text
Script mungkin:
  - belum dieksekusi
  - sedang dieksekusi
  - sudah sukses tapi response hilang
```

Maka untuk operasi penting:

```text
Retry harus memakai key/idempotency yang sama.
Jangan membuat operasi baru dengan token baru tanpa cek state.
```

Contoh idempotency:

```text
Redis timeout on checkOrStart
  ↓
retry checkOrStart with same idempotency key + requestHash
```

---

## 46. Redis Timeout During Pipeline

Pipeline timeout lebih rumit:

```text
Sebagian command mungkin sudah diproses,
sebagian mungkin belum,
client tidak selalu tahu.
```

Karena itu pipeline cocok untuk:

```text
- cache write best-effort
- bulk read
- cleanup best-effort
```

Tidak cocok untuk:

```text
- critical multi-step business effect
```

---

## 47. Redis Transaction Error

Redis transaction bisa mengalami:

```text
- error saat command di-queue sebelum EXEC
- error runtime setelah EXEC
- WATCH abort
```

Redis transaction tidak rollback seperti RDBMS. Untuk business state final, gunakan PostgreSQL transaction. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/using-commands/transactions/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# Bagian H - Observability

## 48. Metrics

```text
redis.lua.execution.count
redis.lua.execution.latency_ms
redis.lua.error.count
redis.lua.noscript.count
redis.lua.reload.count
redis.lua.timeout.count
redis.lua.script_evicted.count

redis.pipeline.batch.count
redis.pipeline.command.count
redis.pipeline.latency_ms
redis.pipeline.error.count
redis.pipeline.batch_size

redis.transaction.exec.count
redis.transaction.abort.count
redis.transaction.error.count
redis.watch.abort.count
```

Labels:

```text
service
env
script_name
script_version
operation
result
exception_class
```

Jangan label:

```text
raw key
tenant ID raw
user ID raw
request hash
idempotency key
lock token
```

---

## 49. Logging

Good:

```json
{
  "event": "redis_lua_failed",
  "script": "idempotency_check_or_start",
  "version": "v2",
  "result": "timeout",
  "operation": "submit-case",
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

Good NOSCRIPT log:

```json
{
  "event": "redis_lua_noscript_reload",
  "script": "safe_unlock",
  "version": "v1",
  "action": "script_load_then_retry"
}
```

Bad:

```json
{
  "scriptArgs": ["raw-idempotency-key", "raw-token", "raw-user-email"]
}
```

---

# Bagian I - Testing Matrix

## 50. Lua Tests

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Counter + TTL first increment | TTL selalu ada |
| Counter second increment | TTL tidak hilang |
| Safe unlock correct token | Lock terhapus |
| Safe unlock wrong token | Lock tidak terhapus |
| Idempotency first request | `STARTED` |
| Idempotency same hash | `EXISTS` |
| Idempotency different hash | `CONFLICT` |
| Sliding window under limit | Allow |
| Sliding window over limit | Deny + retryAfter |
| Semaphore max permits | Deny saat penuh |
| Lua cluster keys same slot | Script sukses |
| Lua keys different slot | Test fail fast |
| NOSCRIPT reload | Adapter reload script |
| Redis 7.4 script eviction metric | Observability |

---

## 51. Pipeline Tests

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Batch GET preserves order | Mapping benar |
| Batch SET applies TTL | Cache tidak permanen |
| Partial missing keys | Response benar |
| Large batch bounded | Tidak overload |
| Pipeline timeout | Failure policy |
| SCAN + UNLINK dry-run | Operational safety |
| Error response per command | Tidak dianggap all-success |

---

## 52. Transaction Tests

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| MULTI/EXEC grouped commands | Basic |
| WATCH abort on concurrent change | Optimistic concurrency |
| Retry after WATCH abort | Correct behavior |
| Runtime command error | No false rollback assumption |
| DISCARD clears transaction | Safety |
| Redis Cluster multi-key transaction | Slot compatibility |

---

# Bagian J - Anti-Patterns

## 53. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| GET lalu SET untuk CAS | Race condition | Lua / WATCH |
| INCR lalu EXPIRE terpisah | Counter tanpa TTL | Lua |
| Lock release `DEL` biasa | Hapus lock owner lain | Lua compare-delete |
| Pipeline dianggap atomic | Partial/race bug | Lua/MULTI |
| MULTI/EXEC dianggap PostgreSQL transaction | Salah rollback assumption | PostgreSQL untuk business |
| Lua script terlalu panjang | Redis blocking | Keep script small |
| Dynamic key di ARGV | Cluster/routing bug | Semua key di KEYS |
| Multi-key script beda slot | Cluster error | Hash tag |
| Tidak handle NOSCRIPT | Fail after restart/failover | SCRIPT LOAD retry |
| Tidak version script | Rolling deploy risk | Script versioning |
| Store raw secret in ARGV logs | Security leak | Mask/hash |
| Pipeline batch terlalu besar | Memory/latency spike | Bounded batch |
| WATCH around external call | Side effect before CAS | Redesign/idempotency |

---

## 54. Senior Engineer Checklist

```text
[ ] Apakah operasi butuh atomic read-check-write?
[ ] Jika ya, kenapa bukan Lua?
[ ] Apakah command hanya independent bulk operation?
[ ] Jika ya, pipeline cukup?
[ ] Apakah transaction Redis dipakai dengan asumsi rollback?
[ ] Apakah business correctness tetap di PostgreSQL?
[ ] Apakah semua Lua key ada di KEYS[]?
[ ] Apakah Redis Cluster hash slot aman?
[ ] Apakah script punya version?
[ ] Apakah return shape terdokumentasi?
[ ] Apakah NOSCRIPT ditangani?
[ ] Apakah Redis 7.4 script eviction dimonitor?
[ ] Apakah script bounded dan tidak heavy loop?
[ ] Apakah pipeline batch size bounded?
[ ] Apakah timeout behavior jelas?
[ ] Apakah metric/log aman dari PII/token?
[ ] Apakah integration test memakai Redis real?
```

---

## 55. Ringkasan Praktis

```text
Lua:
  atomic conditional multi-command logic

Pipeline:
  network batching for many independent commands

MULTI/EXEC:
  grouped Redis command execution

WATCH:
  optimistic CAS for Redis keys

Redis Cluster:
  multi-key script/transaction needs same hash slot

Script governance:
  version, KEYS/ARGV contract, return contract, tests, metrics

PostgreSQL:
  business transaction and final invariant

Jersey service:
  call intent-level ports, not raw Redis scripts from Resource
```

Kalimat kunci:

> Gunakan Lua saat Redis state harus berubah secara atomic. Gunakan pipeline saat Anda hanya ingin lebih cepat mengirim banyak command. Gunakan PostgreSQL saat yang dipertaruhkan adalah kebenaran data bisnis.

# Seri 18 - Session, Token, OTP & Authorization Cache

Status: **Seri 18 dari 22 seri lanjutan**.  
Setelah seri ini selesai, tersisa **4 seri**.

---

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas penggunaan Redis untuk security-adjacent state:

```text id="redis-sec-01"
- session store
- token blacklist / denylist
- token allowlist
- refresh token state
- OTP
- nonce / replay protection
- login attempt temporary state
- authorization snapshot cache
- permission cache
- policy version cache
```

Ini area yang lebih sensitif daripada cache biasa. Salah TTL, salah key design, atau salah failure policy bisa menyebabkan:

```text id="redis-sec-02"
- session tetap valid setelah logout
- token yang sudah dicabut tetap diterima
- permission lama masih berlaku
- OTP bisa ditebak/replay
- user tenant A melihat data tenant B
- sistem fail-open pada endpoint security-critical
```

Redis cocok karena key bisa diberi TTL; Redis `EXPIRE` membuat key otomatis dihapus setelah timeout, dan Redis `SET` mendukung expiry seperti `EX`/`PX` serta conditional write seperti `NX`. Ini berguna untuk session, OTP, nonce, dan replay protection. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/expire/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="redis-sec-03"
Redis security state =
  fast temporary security state
  with strict TTL
  with clear fail policy
  with minimal sensitive data
  with tenant/user boundary
```

Batas penting:

```text id="redis-sec-04"
Identity Provider / Auth Service:
  source of identity truth

PostgreSQL:
  source of user/account/permission truth

JWT:
  signed claim container

Redis:
  temporary state, revocation helper, session cache, OTP/nonce store, authz snapshot cache

JAX-RS Filter:
  enforcement point

Application Service:
  domain authorization and business validation
```

Kalimat kunci:

> Redis boleh mempercepat dan menyimpan state keamanan sementara, tetapi tidak boleh membuat keputusan keamanan final tanpa fallback/source-of-truth yang jelas.

---

## 3. Diagram Besar

```text id="redis-sec-05"
HTTP Request
  ↓
JAX-RS ContainerRequestFilter
  ↓
Authentication Filter
  ├── validate session cookie / bearer token
  ├── check Redis session/token state
  ├── validate JWT signature/claims if JWT
  └── create Principal/UserContext
  ↓
Authorization Filter / Service
  ├── load permission snapshot from Redis
  ├── fallback to PostgreSQL/Authz service
  ├── check policy version
  └── enforce route/action permission
  ↓
JAX-RS Resource
  ↓
Application Service
  ↓
Domain-level authorization
```

---

## 4. Core Design Principle

Untuk security Redis, selalu jawab 6 pertanyaan:

```text id="redis-sec-06"
1. Apa source of truth?
2. Apakah data Redis boleh hilang?
3. Kalau Redis down, fail-open atau fail-closed?
4. TTL berapa dan kenapa?
5. Apakah key/value mengandung PII/secret?
6. Bagaimana revocation/invalidation bekerja?
```

Jika tidak bisa menjawab, jangan production-kan pattern itu.

---

## 5. Use Case Map

| Use case | Redis cocok? | Source of truth | Failure default |
|---|---:|---|---|
| Server-side session | Ya | Redis + DB/user service | Biasanya fail-closed |
| JWT denylist | Ya | Token issuer/auth service | Tergantung endpoint; sering fail-closed |
| Refresh token state | Ya, hati-hati | Auth DB/token table | Fail-closed |
| OTP | Ya | Redis temporary state | Fail-closed |
| Nonce/replay protection | Ya | Redis temporary state | Fail-closed |
| Login attempt counter | Ya | Redis temporary state | Fail-safe/security-biased |
| Permission snapshot | Ya | PostgreSQL/Authz service | Fallback source or fail-closed |
| Role/ACL cache | Ya | PostgreSQL/Authz service | Fallback source or fail-closed |
| Policy version cache | Ya | DB/config/Authz service | Fallback source |
| Final entitlement state | Tidak sebagai primary | DB/Authz service | N/A |
| Audit/security event | Tidak primary | DB/SIEM/Kafka | N/A |

---

# Bagian A - Session Store

## 6. Server-side Session dengan Redis

Server-side session berarti client hanya membawa session ID, sedangkan session data disimpan di server.

```text id="redis-sec-07"
Browser/API client
  ↓
Cookie: SESSION_ID=random
  ↓
Server
  ↓
Redis session:{hash(sessionId)} -> session data
```

OWASP menyarankan session identifier memiliki entropy minimal 64 bit agar sulit ditebak lewat brute force; session ID juga tidak boleh mengandung data sensitif. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Session_Management_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. Session Key Design

Jangan:

```text id="redis-sec-08"
session:john.doe@example.com
session:+628123456789
session:raw-session-token
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="redis-sec-09"
auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:session:v1:sid:sha256-session-id
```

Value:

```json id="redis-sec-10"
{
  "schemaVersion": 1,
  "tenantId": "t1",
  "userId": "u123",
  "authTime": "2026-07-13T17:00:00+07:00",
  "lastSeenAt": "2026-07-13T17:10:00+07:00",
  "absoluteExpiresAt": "2026-07-13T21:00:00+07:00",
  "mfaLevel": "MFA",
  "policyVersion": "p17",
  "deviceIdHash": "sha256-device",
  "ipHash": "sha256-ip"
}
```

Jangan simpan:

```text id="redis-sec-11"
- password
- raw token
- raw OTP
- full PII kalau tidak perlu
- access token third-party
- refresh token plaintext
```

---

## 8. Session TTL: Idle vs Absolute Expiry

Ada dua expiry:

```text id="redis-sec-12"
Idle timeout:
  expire jika user tidak aktif selama N menit

Absolute timeout:
  expire walau user aktif terus, misalnya setelah 8 jam
```

Redis TTL cocok untuk idle timeout:

```text id="redis-sec-13"
EXPIRE sessionKey 1800
```

Tetapi absolute expiry harus disimpan di value dan divalidasi:

```java id="redis-sec-14"
if (Instant.now(clock).isAfter(session.absoluteExpiresAt())) {
    sessionStore.delete(sessionKey);
    throw new UnauthorizedException();
}
```

Kenapa?

```text id="redis-sec-15"
Sliding TTL bisa terus diperpanjang.
Absolute expiry mencegah session hidup selamanya.
```

---

## 9. Sliding Session TTL

Flow:

```text id="redis-sec-16"
Request authenticated
  ↓
session valid
  ↓
if lastSeenAt older than refresh threshold:
    update lastSeenAt
    EXPIRE sessionKey idleTimeout
```

Jangan update TTL setiap request jika traffic sangat tinggi. Gunakan threshold:

```text id="redis-sec-17"
refresh TTL only if remaining TTL < idleTimeout / 2
```

Redis `TTL` dapat dipakai untuk memeriksa sisa lifetime key, dan `EXPIRE` untuk memperbarui expiry key. ([Redis](https://redis.io/docs/latest/commands/ttl/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10. Session Logout

Logout harus menghapus session key.

```text id="redis-sec-18"
DELETE /session
  ↓
UNLINK auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:session:v1:sid:hash
  ↓
client cookie expired
```

Jika multiple device session:

```text id="redis-sec-19"
auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:set:user-sessions:v1:user:uHash
  members = session key hashes
```

Logout all devices:

```text id="redis-sec-20"
SMEMBERS userSessionsSet
UNLINK each session key
UNLINK userSessionsSet
```

Guardrail:

```text id="redis-sec-21"
- user session index harus punya TTL atau cleanup
- session delete failure di logout harus diperlakukan serius
- jangan hanya hapus cookie client; server-side session juga harus invalidated
```

---

## 11. Session Fail Policy

| Scenario | Recommended |
|---|---|
| Redis session store down | Fail-closed for authenticated endpoint |
| Redis timeout during optional lastSeen update | Continue if session already validated |
| Redis timeout before session validation | 401/503 depending API contract |
| Redis delete logout fails | Return warning/error or retry; do not claim fully logged out if not invalidated |
| Redis stale session value malformed | Delete session and require login |

Rule:

```text id="redis-sec-22"
Kalau Redis adalah source session state, Redis down berarti session tidak bisa divalidasi.
```

---

# Bagian B - JWT, Token Denylist, Allowlist

## 12. JWT Mental Model

JWT adalah format compact untuk merepresentasikan claims yang bisa dikirim di HTTP Authorization header dan space-constrained environments. RFC 7519 mendefinisikan JWT sebagai JSON claims representation format. ([datatracker.ietf.org](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7519?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

JWT umum berisi:

```json id="redis-sec-23"
{
  "sub": "user-123",
  "iss": "https://issuer.example",
  "aud": "case-api",
  "exp": 1783948800,
  "iat": 1783945200,
  "jti": "token-id-123"
}
```

Important:

```text id="redis-sec-24"
JWT validation utama:
  signature valid
  issuer valid
  audience valid
  expiration valid
  not-before valid if used
  algorithm expected
```

Redis biasanya dipakai untuk state tambahan:

```text id="redis-sec-25"
- denylist revoked JWT jti
- allowlist active token/session
- refresh token rotation state
- user policy version
```

---

## 13. Token Denylist

Denylist menyimpan token yang sudah dicabut sebelum `exp`.

Key:

```text id="redis-sec-26"
auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:token-deny:v1:jti:sha256-jti
```

Value:

```json id="redis-sec-27"
{
  "revokedAt": "2026-07-13T17:30:00+07:00",
  "reason": "logout",
  "userIdHash": "sha256-user"
}
```

TTL:

```text id="redis-sec-28"
ttl = token.exp - now + smallBuffer
```

Jangan simpan denylist lebih lama dari token validity kecuali untuk audit. Audit sebaiknya di DB/SIEM/Kafka, bukan Redis saja.

Flow:

```text id="redis-sec-29"
Validate JWT signature/claims
  ↓
extract jti
  ↓
GET denylist key
  ├── exists -> reject 401
  └── absent -> continue
```

---

## 14. Denylist Failure Policy

Ini sensitif.

| Endpoint | Redis denylist down |
|---|---|
| Low-risk read | Bisa fallback ke short-lived JWT only, tergantung risk appetite |
| Sensitive read | Fail-closed or fallback auth service |
| Money/admin/regulatory action | Fail-closed |
| Logout/revocation operation | Retry/persist revocation event |

Default aman:

```text id="redis-sec-30"
Jika endpoint high-risk dan Redis denylist tidak bisa dicek:
  fail-closed / 503 / require re-auth
```

Kalau tim memilih fail-open untuk availability, harus documented risk:

```text id="redis-sec-31"
Revoked token mungkin diterima sampai Redis pulih atau token exp.
```

---

## 15. Token Allowlist

Allowlist menyimpan token/session yang masih aktif.

Key:

```text id="redis-sec-32"
auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:token-active:v1:jti:sha256-jti
```

Validation:

```text id="redis-sec-33"
JWT valid
  ↓
Redis active token exists?
  ├── yes -> accept
  └── no -> reject
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="redis-sec-34"
- logout/revocation mudah
- token harus dikenal server
- bisa enforce active session
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="redis-sec-35"
- Redis dependency di setiap request
- Redis down cenderung fail-closed
- lebih stateful
```

Gunakan untuk high-security internal/admin system. Untuk public high-scale API, sering dipilih short-lived access token + refresh token rotation + denylist untuk revocation tertentu.

---

## 16. Refresh Token State

Refresh token lebih sensitif dari access token karena umurnya lebih panjang.

Pattern:

```text id="redis-sec-36"
refresh token raw:
  never store plaintext

store:
  hash(refreshToken)
  userId
  sessionId
  rotationFamilyId
  status
  expiresAt
  lastUsedAt
```

Redis key:

```text id="redis-sec-37"
auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:refresh:v1:rt:sha256-refresh-token
```

Untuk sistem penting, simpan refresh token state di PostgreSQL/Auth DB juga. Redis bisa jadi cache/fast lookup.

Rotation flow:

```text id="redis-sec-38"
Client submits refresh token
  ↓
hash token
  ↓
check status active
  ↓
mark old token used/rotated
  ↓
issue new refresh token
  ↓
store new hash
```

Replay detection:

```text id="redis-sec-39"
old refresh token used again
  ↓
possible theft
  ↓
revoke token family/session
```

---

# Bagian C - OTP

## 17. OTP Store

OTP cocok di Redis karena short-lived.

Key:

```text id="redis-sec-40"
auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:otp:v1:purpose:login:user:uHash
```

Value:

```json id="redis-sec-41"
{
  "otpHash": "hmac/argon/hash",
  "attemptCount": 0,
  "maxAttempts": 5,
  "createdAt": "2026-07-13T17:40:00+07:00",
  "expiresAt": "2026-07-13T17:45:00+07:00"
}
```

TTL:

```text id="redis-sec-42"
3-10 minutes depending channel/risk
```

Jangan simpan OTP plaintext.

```text id="redis-sec-43"
BAD:
  otp = "123456"

BETTER:
  otpHash = HMAC(serverSecret, otp + context)
```

---

## 18. OTP Generate Pattern

```text id="redis-sec-44"
generate random OTP
  ↓
store hash in Redis with TTL
  ↓
send OTP via SMS/email/app
```

Command:

```text id="redis-sec-45"
SET otpKey otpState NX EX 300
```

Kenapa `NX`?

```text id="redis-sec-46"
Mencegah spam regenerate overwrite tanpa policy.
```

Alternatif:

```text id="redis-sec-47"
Allow regenerate only after cooldown
```

Cooldown key:

```text id="redis-sec-48"
auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:otp-cooldown:v1:user:uHash
```

---

## 19. OTP Verify Pattern

OTP verify harus atomic:

```text id="redis-sec-49"
read otp state
check attemptCount
compare hash
if valid:
  delete OTP key
if invalid:
  increment attemptCount
  maybe delete/lock after max attempts
```

Gunakan Lua agar tidak ada race:

```lua id="redis-sec-50"
local key = KEYS[1]
local submittedHash = ARGV[1]
local maxAttempts = tonumber(ARGV[2])

local storedHash = redis.call("HGET", key, "otpHash")
if not storedHash then
  return {"MISSING"}
end

local attempts = tonumber(redis.call("HGET", key, "attemptCount") or "0")

if attempts >= maxAttempts then
  redis.call("DEL", key)
  return {"LOCKED"}
end

if storedHash == submittedHash then
  redis.call("DEL", key)
  return {"VERIFIED"}
end

attempts = attempts + 1
redis.call("HSET", key, "attemptCount", attempts)

if attempts >= maxAttempts then
  redis.call("DEL", key)
  return {"LOCKED"}
end

return {"INVALID", attempts}
```

Important:

```text id="redis-sec-51"
OTP successful verification deletes OTP.
OTP cannot be reused.
```

---

## 20. OTP Security Guardrail

```text id="redis-sec-52"
[ ] OTP random, not predictable
[ ] OTP plaintext not stored
[ ] TTL short
[ ] max attempts enforced atomically
[ ] resend cooldown
[ ] verify response does not leak account existence
[ ] phone/email hashed in key
[ ] audit event stored outside Redis
[ ] rate limit per user/IP/device
[ ] Redis failure fail-closed
```

---

# Bagian D - Nonce / Replay Protection

## 21. Replay Protection

Replay protection mencegah request lama dipakai ulang.

Use case:

```text id="redis-sec-53"
Partner API signed request
Webhook receiver
Internal service signed command
OTP challenge
Payment callback
```

Pattern:

```text id="redis-sec-54"
validate signature
validate timestamp skew
check nonce not used
SET nonceKey seen NX EX 300
```

Redis key:

```text id="redis-sec-55"
api-svc:prod:tenant:t1:nonce:v1:partner:pHash:nonce:nHash
```

If `SET NX EX` fails:

```text id="redis-sec-56"
nonce already used
reject replay
```

TTL:

```text id="redis-sec-57"
allowed clock skew window + buffer
```

Example:

```text id="redis-sec-58"
timestamp allowed skew = 5 minutes
nonce TTL = 10 minutes
```

---

## 22. Nonce Failure Policy

```text id="redis-sec-59"
If Redis nonce store unavailable:
  high-risk signed request should fail-closed.
```

Kalau fail-open:

```text id="redis-sec-60"
attacker could replay valid signed request within timestamp window.
```

---

# Bagian E - Authorization Cache

## 23. Authorization Snapshot Cache

Authorization sering mahal:

```text id="redis-sec-61"
user -> roles -> groups -> permissions -> resource ACL -> policy rules
```

Redis bisa cache snapshot:

```text id="redis-sec-62"
authz-svc:prod:tenant:t1:authz-snapshot:v1:user:uHash:policy:p17
```

Value:

```json id="redis-sec-63"
{
  "schemaVersion": 1,
  "tenantId": "t1",
  "userIdHash": "sha256-user",
  "policyVersion": "p17",
  "roles": ["CASE_REVIEWER"],
  "permissions": [
    "case:read",
    "case:submit",
    "case:comment"
  ],
  "dataScopes": [
    "region:west"
  ],
  "sourceUpdatedAt": "2026-07-13T17:50:00+07:00",
  "cachedAt": "2026-07-13T17:51:00+07:00"
}
```

TTL:

```text id="redis-sec-64"
30 seconds - 5 minutes
```

Tergantung risiko stale permission.

---

## 24. Policy Versioning

Permission stale adalah risiko besar.

Gunakan policy version:

```text id="redis-sec-65"
authz snapshot key includes policy:p17
```

Jika policy berubah:

```text id="redis-sec-66"
policy p17 -> p18
```

Aplikasi otomatis baca key baru.

Keuntungan:

```text id="redis-sec-67"
- tidak perlu delete semua old permission keys
- old cache tidak dipakai
- TTL membersihkan old keys
```

Source policy version:

```text id="redis-sec-68"
PostgreSQL/Authz service/config store
```

Bukan Redis saja.

---

## 25. Authorization Cache: Fail Policy

| Scenario | Recommended |
|---|---|
| Redis authz cache hit | Use if policy version current |
| Redis miss | Load from Authz source/DB |
| Redis down | Fallback Authz source if available |
| Redis down + Authz source down | Fail-closed for protected action |
| Policy version unknown | Fail-closed or fetch source |
| Deserialization error | Delete cache + fallback source |

Rule:

```text id="redis-sec-69"
Authorization cache failure should not grant access by default.
```

---

## 26. Authz Cache in JAX-RS Filter

```text id="redis-sec-70"
ContainerRequestFilter
  ↓
extract principal/user/tenant
  ↓
resolve route/action
  ↓
load permission snapshot
  ↓
check permission
  ↓
allow or 403
```

Pseudo-code:

```java id="redis-sec-71"
public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
    UserContext user = userContextResolver.resolve(ctx);
    RouteAction action = routeActionResolver.resolve(ctx);

    AuthorizationDecision decision = authorizationService.authorize(
        user,
        action
    );

    if (!decision.allowed()) {
        ctx.abortWith(Response.status(403)
            .entity(new ErrorResponse("FORBIDDEN", "Access denied"))
            .build());
    }
}
```

Important:

```text id="redis-sec-72"
Route-level authz di filter tidak menggantikan domain-level authz di service.
```

Contoh:

```text id="redis-sec-73"
Filter:
  user has case:approve permission

Service/domain:
  user may approve this specific case?
  case status = WAITING_APPROVAL?
  user not same as submitter?
  tenant/scope matches?
```

---

## 27. ACL / Resource Permission Cache

Untuk resource-specific ACL:

```text id="redis-sec-74"
case-svc:prod:tenant:t1:acl:v1:case:c991:policy:p17
```

Value:

```json id="redis-sec-75"
{
  "caseId": "c991",
  "policyVersion": "p17",
  "allowedUserHashes": ["u1", "u2"],
  "allowedRoleHashes": ["r1"],
  "dataScope": "region:west",
  "sourceVersion": 42
}
```

Hati-hati:

```text id="redis-sec-76"
Resource ACL cache bisa high-cardinality.
Jangan cache semua ACL tanpa hit-rate dan memory plan.
```

---

## 28. Permission Decision Cache

Decision cache menyimpan hasil:

```text id="redis-sec-77"
can user u123 approve case c991?
```

Key:

```text id="redis-sec-78"
authz-svc:prod:tenant:t1:decision:v1:user:uHash:action:approve:res:case:cHash:policy:p17
```

Risiko:

```text id="redis-sec-79"
- key explosion
- stale decision
- permission context kompleks
```

Gunakan hanya untuk:

```text id="redis-sec-80"
- high-cost decision
- high hit ratio
- short TTL
- strong policy versioning
```

Biasanya lebih baik cache **permission snapshot**, bukan setiap decision.

---

# Bagian F - Login Attempt / Account Protection

## 29. Login Attempt Counter

Key:

```text id="redis-sec-81"
auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:login-attempt:v1:user:uHash
auth-svc:prod:tenant:t1:login-attempt-ip:v1:ip:ipHash
```

Pattern:

```text id="redis-sec-82"
INCR key
EXPIRE key 900
```

Gunakan Lua agar `INCR + EXPIRE` atomic.

Policy:

```text id="redis-sec-83"
5 failed attempts / 15 minutes per account
20 failed attempts / 15 minutes per IP
100 failed attempts / 15 minutes per tenant
```

Security guardrail:

```text id="redis-sec-84"
- response tidak leak account exists
- combine user + IP/device dimension
- audit failed login di durable store
- Redis down: fail-safe sesuai risk
```

---

## 30. Account Lockout vs Risk-based Protection

Jangan terlalu mudah lock account hanya karena IP/user counter, karena attacker bisa melakukan denial-of-service dengan sengaja gagal login banyak kali.

Lebih aman:

```text id="redis-sec-85"
- temporary delay
- CAPTCHA/challenge
- MFA step-up
- risk score
- notify user
- rate limit by IP/device/user
```

Redis menyimpan temporary counters. Keputusan account security final sebaiknya melibatkan Auth service/DB/risk engine.

---

# Bagian G - Secure Key & Value Design

## 31. Jangan Simpan Secret Mentah

Jangan di key:

```text id="redis-sec-86"
session:rawSessionId
otp:628123456789
token:jti-raw-if-sensitive
apiKey:raw-api-key
email:john@example.com
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="redis-sec-87"
session:sid:sha256-session
otp:phone-hash:sha256-phone
api-key:sha256-key
user:uHash
```

Value juga minimal:

```text id="redis-sec-88"
- store hash/reference
- not full token
- not password
- not unnecessary PII
```

---

## 32. Hashing Strategy

```text id="redis-sec-89"
hash = HMAC-SHA256(serverSecret, identifier)
```

Kenapa HMAC, bukan plain SHA-256?

```text id="redis-sec-90"
Jika identifier low-entropy seperti phone/email,
plain hash bisa ditebak lewat dictionary.
HMAC dengan secret membuat offline guessing lebih sulit.
```

Gunakan key rotation strategy untuk HMAC secret jika perlu.

---

## 33. TTL Strategy Security

| Data | TTL |
|---|---:|
| OTP | 3-10 min |
| Nonce | 5-15 min |
| Login attempt counter | 15 min-24 h |
| Access token denylist | until token exp + buffer |
| Session idle | 15-60 min |
| Session absolute | stored in value, e.g. 4-12 h |
| Refresh token state | days/weeks, often DB-backed |
| Authz snapshot | 30 sec-5 min |
| Policy version | short cache or event invalidated |
| Device trust marker | depends risk, DB-backed preferred |

Rule:

```text id="redis-sec-91"
TTL security tidak boleh asal default.
TTL adalah bagian dari security policy.
```

---

## 34. Redis ACL / Network Security

Untuk Redis yang menyimpan security state:

```text id="redis-sec-92"
- require TLS in transit where supported
- require authentication/ACL
- restrict network access
- separate Redis roles/users if possible
- least privilege command ACL
- no public exposure
- monitor CONFIG/FLUSH/KEYS dangerous usage
- encrypt sensitive data at application layer if needed
```

Jangan treat Redis sebagai internal “aman otomatis”. Jika Redis bocor, session/token/OTP state bisa dieksploitasi.

---

## 35. Redis Eviction Warning

Security keys tidak boleh di-evict sembarangan.

Jika denylist key ter-evict:

```text id="redis-sec-93"
revoked token might become accepted
```

Jika OTP key ter-evict:

```text id="redis-sec-94"
user cannot verify OTP; fail safe but UX broken
```

Jika session key ter-evict:

```text id="redis-sec-95"
user logged out unexpectedly
```

Untuk security state Redis:

```text id="redis-sec-96"
- hindari aggressive eviction
- pertimbangkan separate Redis instance
- monitor memory
- noeviction atau policy yang sesuai
- jangan campur dengan volatile cache besar
```

---

# Bagian H - Integration dengan Jersey/JAX-RS

## 36. Authentication Filter

```java id="redis-sec-97"
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public final class AuthenticationFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private final SessionService sessionService;
    private final TokenService tokenService;

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
        Optional<String> sessionCookie = extractSessionCookie(ctx);
        Optional<String> bearer = extractBearerToken(ctx);

        UserContext user;

        if (sessionCookie.isPresent()) {
            user = sessionService.authenticate(sessionCookie.get());
        } else if (bearer.isPresent()) {
            user = tokenService.authenticate(bearer.get());
        } else {
            throw new NotAuthenticatedException("Authentication required");
        }

        ctx.setProperty("userContext", user);
    }
}
```

---

## 37. Authorization Service

```java id="redis-sec-98"
public final class AuthorizationService {

    private final AuthorizationCache authzCache;
    private final AuthorizationSource authzSource;

    public AuthorizationDecision authorize(UserContext user, Action action) {
        String policyVersion = authzSource.currentPolicyVersion(user.tenantId());

        Optional<AuthzSnapshot> cached = authzCache.getSnapshot(
            user.tenantId(),
            user.userId(),
            policyVersion
        );

        AuthzSnapshot snapshot = cached.orElseGet(() -> {
            AuthzSnapshot loaded = authzSource.loadSnapshot(user);
            authzCache.putSnapshot(loaded, Duration.ofMinutes(2));
            return loaded;
        });

        return snapshot.allows(action);
    }
}
```

---

## 38. Domain-level Authorization

Jangan hanya route-level permission.

```java id="redis-sec-99"
public void approveCase(UserContext user, CaseId caseId) {
    CaseRow row = caseRepository.findById(user.tenantId(), caseId)
        .orElseThrow(NotFoundException::new);

    authorizationPolicy.assertCanApprove(user, row);

    caseRepository.approve(user.tenantId(), caseId, row.version());
}
```

Redis authz cache membantu cepat. Domain tetap validasi konteks spesifik resource.

---

# Bagian I - Observability & Audit

## 39. Metrics

```text id="redis-sec-100"
security.session.lookup.count
security.session.miss.count
security.session.expired.count
security.session.logout.delete.error.count

security.token.denylist.hit.count
security.token.denylist.redis.error.count
security.token.allowlist.miss.count

security.otp.generated.count
security.otp.verified.count
security.otp.invalid.count
security.otp.locked.count

security.nonce.replay.detected.count

security.authz.cache.hit.count
security.authz.cache.miss.count
security.authz.cache.stale.count
security.authz.fallback.count
security.authz.fail_closed.count

security.redis.timeout.count
security.redis.error.count
```

Labels aman:

```text id="redis-sec-101"
service
env
security_family
operation
result
exception_class
```

Jangan label:

```text id="redis-sec-102"
userId
email
phone
sessionId
token
otp
raw Redis key
```

---

## 40. Audit Events

Security audit jangan hanya di Redis.

Audit durable:

```text id="redis-sec-103"
- login success/failure
- logout
- session revoked
- token revoked
- OTP generated/verified/failed
- nonce replay detected
- permission denied
- admin role changed
- policy changed
```

Simpan ke:

```text id="redis-sec-104"
PostgreSQL audit table
Kafka security/audit topic
SIEM/log pipeline
```

Redis boleh mempercepat, bukan audit ledger.

---

## 41. Logging

Good:

```json id="redis-sec-105"
{
  "event": "token_denylist_hit",
  "tenantHash": "sha256-...",
  "userHash": "sha256-...",
  "jtiHash": "sha256-...",
  "action": "reject_request",
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

Bad:

```json id="redis-sec-106"
{
  "sessionId": "raw-session-id",
  "otp": "123456",
  "accessToken": "eyJhbGciOi..."
}
```

---

# Bagian J - Testing Matrix

## 42. Session Tests

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Valid session accepted | Basic |
| Expired TTL rejected | Security |
| Absolute expiry rejected even if TTL refreshed | No immortal session |
| Logout deletes session | Revocation |
| Logout all devices deletes all indexed sessions | Multi-session |
| Redis down fail-closed | Security |
| Malformed session value deleted | Self-healing |

---

## 43. Token Tests

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Valid JWT accepted | Basic |
| Expired JWT rejected | JWT claim |
| Denylisted token rejected | Revocation |
| Token denylist TTL equals token exp | Cleanup |
| Same token after logout rejected | Logout correctness |
| Redis denylist down high-risk endpoint fails closed | Security |
| Policy version mismatch reloads authz | Stale control |

---

## 44. OTP / Nonce Tests

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| OTP valid once | No replay |
| OTP invalid increments attempt | Brute force protection |
| OTP max attempts locks/deletes | Security |
| OTP expires | TTL |
| OTP plaintext not stored | Secret safety |
| Nonce first use accepted | Replay protection |
| Nonce second use rejected | Replay block |
| Redis down fails closed | Security |

---

## 45. Authorization Cache Tests

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Authz cache hit allows permitted action | Fast path |
| Authz cache miss loads source | Fallback |
| Policy version change uses new key | Stale prevention |
| Revoked permission not accepted beyond TTL/version | Security |
| Redis down fallback source | Availability |
| Source down + Redis down fails closed | Security |
| Domain-level authz catches resource-specific denial | Defense in depth |

---

# Bagian K - Anti-Patterns

## 46. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Store raw session ID in Redis key | Session leak risk | Hash/HMAC |
| Store OTP plaintext | OTP compromise | Store hash/HMAC |
| JWT accepted without checking `exp` | Expired token accepted | Validate claims |
| Denylist without TTL | Memory leak | TTL until token expiry |
| Denylist Redis fail-open on admin API | Revoked token accepted | Fail-closed |
| Authz cache TTL too long | Revoked permission still works | Short TTL + policy version |
| Cache permission by user only | Cross-tenant/policy stale | Include tenant/policy/scope |
| Session sliding TTL without absolute expiry | Immortal session | Absolute expiry in value |
| Logout only clears cookie | Server session remains | Delete Redis session |
| Refresh token plaintext in Redis | Token theft impact | Store hash, DB-backed |
| Security audit only in Redis | Audit loss | DB/Kafka/SIEM |
| Security state mixed with evictable cache | Revocation/session loss | Separate/noeviction |
| Raw token/OTP in logs | Severe leak | Mask/hash |

---

## 47. Senior Engineer Checklist

```text id="redis-sec-107"
[ ] Source of truth identity/authz jelas
[ ] Redis role: session/token/OTP/cache/nonce jelas
[ ] TTL per key family documented
[ ] Fail-open/fail-closed per endpoint documented
[ ] Session ID/token/OTP tidak disimpan plaintext
[ ] Redis key tidak mengandung PII/secret
[ ] Session punya idle dan absolute expiry
[ ] Logout invalidates server-side state
[ ] JWT signature/issuer/audience/exp divalidasi
[ ] Denylist/allowlist TTL sesuai token lifetime
[ ] OTP max attempt atomic
[ ] Nonce SET NX EX dipakai untuk replay protection
[ ] Authz cache include tenant/policy/scope
[ ] Permission revocation strategy jelas
[ ] Domain-level authz tetap ada
[ ] Security audit durable di luar Redis
[ ] Redis instance eviction-safe untuk security state
[ ] Metrics/logging tidak bocor PII/token
[ ] Tests mencakup Redis down dan stale permission
```

---

## 48. Ringkasan Praktis

```text id="redis-sec-108"
Session:
  Redis cocok sebagai server-side session store,
  gunakan hashed session ID, TTL, absolute expiry, fail-closed.

JWT:
  Redis cocok untuk denylist/allowlist,
  tapi JWT signature/claims tetap wajib divalidasi.

OTP:
  Redis cocok untuk short-lived OTP,
  simpan hash, TTL pendek, max attempt atomic, delete on success.

Nonce:
  SET NX EX cocok untuk replay protection,
  Redis failure biasanya fail-closed.

Authorization cache:
  cache snapshot/permission dengan short TTL + policy version,
  fallback ke source of truth,
  jangan fail-open untuk protected action.

Security state:
  jangan dicampur sembarangan dengan volatile cache,
  jangan simpan secret plaintext,
  audit harus durable di luar Redis.
```

Kalimat kunci:

> Redis sangat berguna untuk session, token revocation, OTP, nonce, dan authorization cache, tetapi semua pattern ini harus didesain dengan TTL, failure policy, secret handling, dan source-of-truth boundary yang eksplisit. Dalam security path, cache miss boleh lambat; akses salah tidak boleh terjadi.

---

## Seri 18 Selesai

**Seri 18 - Session, Token, OTP & Authorization Cache sudah selesai.**

Sisa: **4 seri lagi**.

Rekomendasi berikutnya: **Seri 19 - Workflow / Camunda / Saga Support Pattern**, membahas Redis sebagai helper untuk workflow correlation, transient process cache, external task dedup, saga timeout helper, dan boundary tegas bahwa Camunda/PostgreSQL tetap menjadi source of truth workflow/business state.
